\widetilde{\mathbb{F}}_{\Gamma_{j}}^{u_{j}}\Phi^{N}_{\mathfrak{r}}
, and

\widetilde{\mathbb{F}}_{\Gamma_{\times}}^{u_{\times}}(\Phi\otimes\Phi)^{N}_{\mathfrak{r}}

are used to control the discrepancies. Similarly, the reader is invited to
disregard boundary terms appearing when integrating by parts: they too are
controlled by the defect sizes. The reader can also suppose that
\beta_{2}=\beta_{3}=\beta
,
\alpha_{2}=\alpha_{3}=\beta^{-1},
and
\gamma_{2}=\gamma_{3}=\frac{1}{2}
. The second assumption is formally not allowed since
we require
1+\alpha_{2}+\alpha_{3}=0
. However, this fact is used only in
the reduction following (
4.6
) and is not of relevance for the
remainder of the proof. Finally, in the proof of (
4.1
) the
reader can assume simply that
\theta=0
instead of having an integral over

\theta\in\Theta
while in the proof of (
4.2
) and of
(
4.3
) the reader can assume that
\theta=-\frac{1}{2}

so that
\theta_{\Gamma_{j}}=0
for
j\in\{2,3\}
.

Proof.

Proof of bound (
4.1
)
We deal only with the size
\mathbb{I}^{(o,l,l)}_{\Theta}\Phi_{\mathfrak{r}}^{N}
appearing
on
\mathrm{LHS}{\eqref{eq:STE:o-l-l:tree}}
; the term
\mathbb{I}^{(l,l,l)}_{\Theta}\Phi_{\mathfrak{r}}^{N}
is dealt with in the same way. We begin by fixing
T\in\mathbb{T}_{\Theta}
and
\varphi\in\Phi_{\mathfrak{r}}^{N}
with
\|\varphi\|_{\Phi_{\mathfrak{r}}^{N}}\leq 1
so that we now need to show bound

(4.14)

\int_{\Theta}\left|\int_{0}^{1-|\zeta|}F_{1}F_{2}F_{3}\circ\pi_{T}(\theta,\zeta,\sigma)[\varphi_{1,\theta}^{o},\varphi_{2,\theta}^{l},\varphi_{3,\theta}^{l}]\mathrm{d}\zeta\frac{\mathrm{d}\sigma}{\sigma}\right|\frac{\mathrm{d}\theta}{|\Theta|}.

Using the notation from the discussion preceding this proof we can rewrite
and bound this expression as follows:

(4.15)

\displaystyle\int_{\Theta}\left|\int_{\mathbb{R}^{2}_{+}}F_{1}F_{2}F_{3}\circ\pi_{T}(\theta,\zeta,\sigma)[\varphi_{1,\theta}^{o},\varphi_{2,\theta}^{l},\varphi_{3,\theta}^{l}]\mathrm{d}\zeta\frac{\mathrm{d}\sigma}{\sigma}\right|\frac{\mathrm{d}\theta}{|\Theta|}

\displaystyle\leq\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}_{+}}\begin{aligned} &|F_{1}^{\ast}(\theta,\zeta,\sigma)[\varphi_{1,\theta}^{o}].\;\mathfrak{d}_{\zeta}(\theta_{\Gamma_{2}})F_{2}^{\ast}(\theta,\zeta,\sigma)[\widetilde{\varphi_{2,\theta}^{l}}]\\
&\qquad\left.\times\mathfrak{d}_{\zeta}(\theta_{\Gamma_{3}})F_{3}^{\ast}(\theta,\zeta,\sigma)[\widetilde{\varphi_{2,\theta}^{l}}]\right|\mathrm{d}\zeta\frac{\mathrm{d}\sigma}{\sigma}\frac{\mathrm{d}\theta}{|\Theta|}\end{aligned}

\displaystyle\lesssim\sup_{\widetilde{\varphi}_{1},\widetilde{\varphi}_{2},\widetilde{\varphi}_{3}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}_{+}}\begin{aligned} &|F_{1}^{\ast}(\theta,\zeta,\sigma)[\widetilde{\varphi_{1}}]|\left|\;\mathfrak{d}_{\zeta}(\theta_{\Gamma_{2}})F_{2}^{\ast}(\theta,\zeta,\sigma)[\widetilde{\varphi_{2}}]\right|\\
&\qquad\times\left|\mathfrak{d}_{\zeta}(\theta_{\Gamma_{3}})F_{3}^{\ast}(\theta,\zeta,\sigma)[\widetilde{\varphi_{3}}]\right|\mathrm{d}\zeta\frac{\mathrm{d}\sigma}{\sigma}\frac{\mathrm{d}\theta}{|\Theta|},\end{aligned}

with the upper bound being taken over wave packets
\widetilde{{\varphi_{j}}}\in\Phi^{\infty}_{2\mathfrak{r}}
with
\|\widetilde{{\varphi_{j}}}\|_{\Phi_{2\mathfrak{r}}^{N-3}}\leq 1
,
j\in\{1,2,3\}
. The second inequality follows
from
Lemma

3.13
according to which we can
decompose each of the wave packets
\widetilde{\varphi}_{1,\theta}^{o},\widetilde{\varphi}_{2,\theta}^{l},\widetilde{\varphi}_{3,\theta}^{l}
into an absolutely
convergent series e.g.

(4.16)

\widetilde{\varphi}_{1,\theta}^{l}\coloneqq\sum_{k}\widetilde{a}^{l}_{1,k}(\theta)\widetilde{\widetilde{\varphi}}_{k}

with
\|\widetilde{\widetilde{\varphi}}_{k}\|_{\Phi_{2\mathfrak{r}}^{N-3}}\lesssim\|\widetilde{\varphi}_{1,\theta}^{l}\|_{\Phi_{\mathfrak{r}}^{N-1}}\lesssim 1
. We bound the

\mathrm{RHS}{}
of the chain of inequalities above Cauchy-Schwartz inequality: we
obtain the bound

(4.17)

\displaystyle\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}_{+}}\begin{aligned} &|F_{1}^{\ast}(\theta,\zeta,\sigma)[\widetilde{\varphi_{1}}]|\left|\;\mathfrak{d}_{\zeta}(\theta_{\Gamma_{2}})F_{2}^{\ast}(\theta,\zeta,\sigma)[\widetilde{\varphi_{2}}]\right|\qquad\\
&\qquad\times\left|\mathfrak{d}_{\zeta}(\theta_{\Gamma_{3}})F_{3}^{\ast}(\theta,\zeta,\sigma)[\widetilde{\varphi_{3}}]\right|\mathrm{d}\zeta\frac{\mathrm{d}\sigma}{\sigma}\frac{\mathrm{d}\theta}{|\Theta|}\end{aligned}

\displaystyle\lesssim\begin{aligned} &\|F_{1}^{\ast}(\theta,\zeta,\sigma)[\widetilde{\varphi_{1}}]\|_{L^{u_{1}}_{\frac{\mathrm{d}\zeta\mathrm{d}\theta}{|\Theta|}}L^{\infty}_{\frac{\mathrm{d}\sigma}{\sigma}}}\left\|\mathfrak{d}_{\zeta}(\theta_{\Gamma_{2}})F_{2}^{\ast}(\theta,\zeta,\sigma)[\widetilde{\varphi_{2}}]\right\|_{L^{u_{2}}_{\frac{\mathrm{d}\zeta\mathrm{d}\theta}{|\Theta|}}L^{2}_{\frac{\mathrm{d}\sigma}{\sigma}}}\\
&\times\left\|\mathfrak{d}_{\zeta}(\theta_{\Gamma_{3}})F^{\ast}_{3}(\theta,\zeta,\rho)[\widetilde{\varphi_{3}}]\right\|_{L_{\frac{\mathrm{d}\zeta\mathrm{d}\theta}{|\Theta|}}^{u_{3}}L_{\frac{\mathrm{d}\rho}{\rho}}^{2}}\end{aligned}

\displaystyle\lesssim\|F_{1}\|_{\mathbb{L}_{\Theta}^{(u_{1},\infty)}\Phi_{2\mathfrak{r}}^{N-2}(T)}\|F_{2}\|_{\mathbb{L}_{\Theta}^{(u_{2},2)}\Gamma_{2}^{\ast}\mathfrak{d}_{2\mathfrak{r}}^{N-3}(T)}\|F_{3}\|_{\mathbb{L}_{\Theta}^{(u_{3},2)}\Gamma_{3}^{\ast}\mathfrak{d}_{2\mathfrak{r}}^{N-3}(T)},

as required.
∎

Proof.

Proof of bound (
4.2
)
We deal only with the size
\mathbb{I}^{(l,l,o)}_{\Theta}\Phi_{\mathfrak{r}}^{N}
appearing
on
\mathrm{LHS}{\eqref{eq:STE:l-l-o:tree}}
; the term with
\mathbb{I}^{(l,o,l)}_{\Theta}\Phi_{\mathfrak{r}}^{N}
is dealt with analogously, by exchanging the role
of the indexes
2
and
3
. We begin by fixing
T\in\mathbb{T}_{\Theta}
and

\varphi\in\Phi_{\mathfrak{r}}^{N}
with
\|\varphi\|_{\Phi_{\mathfrak{r}}^{N}}\leq 1
. Using the
notation from the discussion preceding this proof, we need to bound the
expression

(4.18)

\displaystyle\int_{\Theta}\left|\int_{\mathbb{R}^{2}_{+}}\mathfrak{d}_{\zeta}(\theta)F_{1}^{\ast}(\theta,\zeta,\sigma)[\widetilde{\varphi}_{1,\theta}^{l}]\mathfrak{d}_{\zeta}(\theta_{\Gamma_{2}})F_{2}^{\ast}(\theta,\zeta,\sigma)[\widetilde{\varphi}_{2,\theta}^{l}]F_{3}^{\ast}(\theta,\zeta,\sigma)[\varphi_{3,\theta}^{o}]\mathrm{d}\zeta\frac{\mathrm{d}\sigma}{\sigma}\right|\frac{\mathrm{d}\theta}{|\Theta|}.

We claim that

(4.19)

\displaystyle\eqref{eq:l-l-o-quantity}\leq\int_{\Theta}\begin{aligned} &(|\textrm{M1}(\theta)[\widetilde{\varphi}_{1,\theta}^{l},\widetilde{\varphi}_{2,\theta}^{l},\widetilde{\varphi}_{3,\theta}^{o}]|+|\textrm{M2}(\theta)[\widetilde{\varphi}_{1,\theta}^{l},\widetilde{\varphi}_{2,\theta}^{l},\widetilde{\varphi}_{3,\theta}^{o}]|+.\qquad\\
&+|\textrm{B1}(\theta)[\widetilde{\varphi}_{1,\theta}^{l},\widetilde{\varphi}_{2,\theta}^{l},\varphi_{3,\theta}^{o}]|+|\textrm{B2}(\theta)[\widetilde{\varphi}_{1,\theta}^{l},\widetilde{\varphi}_{2,\theta}^{l},\widetilde{\varphi}_{3,\theta}^{o}]|\\
&\qquad\left.+\left|\text{B3}(\theta)[\widetilde{\varphi}_{1,\theta}^{l},\widetilde{\varphi}_{2,\theta}^{l},\widetilde{\varphi}_{3,\theta}^{o}]\right|+\left|\textrm{B4}(\theta)[\widetilde{\varphi}_{1,\theta}^{l},\widetilde{\varphi}_{2,\theta}^{l},\widetilde{\varphi}_{3,\theta}^{o}]\right|\right)\frac{\mathrm{d}\theta}{|\Theta|}\end{aligned}

\displaystyle\lesssim\sup_{\text{\scriptsize{$\begin{aligned} &\left\|{\varphi_{j}}\right\|_{\Phi_{\mathfrak{r}}^{N}}\leq 1\\
&j\in\{1,2,3\}\end{aligned}$}}}\int_{\Theta}\begin{aligned} &\left(|\textrm{M1}(\theta)[\varphi_{1},\varphi_{2},\varphi_{3}]|+\left|\textrm{M2$(\theta)[\varphi_{1},\varphi_{2},\varphi_{3}]$}\right|+\right.\qquad\\
&+|\textrm{B1}(\theta)[\varphi_{1},\varphi_{2},\varphi_{3}]|+|\textrm{B2}(\theta)[\varphi_{1},\varphi_{2},\varphi_{3}]|\\
&\qquad\left.+\left|\text{B3}(\theta)[\varphi_{1},\varphi_{2},\varphi_{3}]\right|+\left|\textrm{B4}(\theta)[\varphi_{1},\varphi_{2},\varphi_{3}]\right|\right)\frac{\mathrm{d}\theta}{|\Theta|}\end{aligned}

where

(4.20)

\textrm{M1}(\theta)[\varphi_{1},\varphi_{2},\varphi_{3}]:=\begin{aligned} &\int_{\mathbb{R}^{2}_{+}}\mathfrak{d}_{\zeta}(\theta)F_{1}^{\ast}(\theta,\zeta,\sigma)[\varphi_{1}]\mathfrak{d}_{\zeta}(\theta_{\Gamma_{2}})^{2}F_{2}^{\ast}(\theta,\zeta,\sigma)[\varphi_{2}]\qquad\\
&\times\int_{0}^{\sigma}\frac{\rho}{\sigma}\mathfrak{d}_{\zeta}{(\theta_{\Gamma_{3}})}F_{3}^{\ast}(\theta,\zeta,\rho)[\varphi_{3}]\frac{\mathrm{d}\rho}{\rho}\frac{\mathrm{d}\sigma}{\sigma}\mathrm{d}\zeta,\end{aligned}

(4.21)

\textrm{M2}(\theta)[\varphi_{1},\varphi_{2},\varphi_{3}]:=\begin{aligned} &\beta\int_{\mathbb{R}^{2}_{+}}\mathfrak{d}_{\zeta}{(\theta)}^{2}F_{1}^{\ast}(\theta,\zeta,\sigma)[\varphi_{1}]\mathfrak{d}_{\zeta}(\theta_{\Gamma_{2}})F_{2}^{\ast}(\theta,\zeta,\sigma)[\varphi_{2}]\qquad\\
&\times\int_{0}^{\sigma}\frac{\rho}{\sigma}\mathfrak{d}_{\zeta}{(\theta_{\Gamma_{3}})}F_{3}^{\ast}(\theta,\zeta,\rho)[\varphi_{3}]\frac{\mathrm{d}\rho}{\rho}\frac{\mathrm{d}\sigma}{\sigma}\mathrm{d}\zeta,\end{aligned}

and

(4.22)

\displaystyle\textrm{B1}(\theta)[\varphi_{1},\varphi_{2},\varphi_{3}]:=\begin{aligned} &\int_{\mathbb{R}^{2}_{+}}\mathfrak{d}_{\zeta}(\theta)F_{1}^{\ast}(\theta,\zeta,\sigma)[\varphi_{1}]\hskip 1.69998pt\mathfrak{d}_{\zeta}(\theta_{\Gamma_{2}})F_{2}^{\ast}(\theta,\zeta,\sigma)[\varphi_{2}]\\
&\qquad\times\int_{0}^{\sigma}\left(\mathfrak{d}_{\sigma}(\theta_{\Gamma_{3}})-\rho\mathrm{d}_{\rho}\right)F_{3}^{\ast}(\theta,\zeta,\rho)[\varphi_{3}]\frac{\mathrm{d}\rho}{\rho}\frac{\mathrm{d}\sigma}{\sigma}\mathrm{d}\zeta,\end{aligned}

(4.23)

\displaystyle\textrm{B2}(\theta)[\varphi_{1},\varphi_{2},\varphi_{3}]:=\begin{aligned} &\int_{\mathbb{R}^{2}_{+}}\mathfrak{d}_{\zeta}(\theta)F_{1}^{\ast}(\theta,\zeta,\sigma)[\varphi_{1}]\hskip 1.69998pt\mathfrak{d}_{\zeta}(\theta_{\Gamma_{2}})F_{2}^{\ast}(\theta,\zeta,\sigma)[\varphi_{2}]\\
&\qquad\times\int_{0}^{\sigma}\left(\mathfrak{d}_{\zeta_{3}}-\beta\rho d_{\zeta}\right)\mathfrak{d}_{\zeta_{3}}F^{\ast}_{3}(\theta,\zeta,\rho)[\varphi_{3}]\frac{\mathrm{d}\rho}{\rho}\frac{\mathrm{d}\sigma}{\sigma}\mathrm{d}\zeta,\end{aligned}

(4.24)

\displaystyle\textrm{B3}(\theta)[\varphi_{1},\varphi_{2},\varphi_{3}]

\displaystyle:=\begin{aligned} &\int_{\mathbb{R}^{2}_{+}}\mathfrak{d}_{\zeta}(\theta)F_{1}^{\ast}(\theta,\zeta,\sigma)[\varphi_{1}]\left(\mathfrak{d}_{\zeta}(\theta_{\Gamma_{2}})-\beta\sigma\mathrm{d}_{\zeta}\right)\mathfrak{d}_{\zeta}(\theta_{\Gamma_{2}})F_{2}^{\ast}(\theta,\zeta,\sigma)[\varphi_{2}]\qquad\\
&\qquad\times\int_{0}^{\sigma}\frac{\rho}{\sigma}\mathfrak{d}_{\zeta}{(\theta_{\Gamma_{3}})}F_{3}^{\ast}(\theta,\zeta,\rho)[\varphi_{3}]\frac{\mathrm{d}\rho}{\rho}\frac{\mathrm{d}\sigma}{\sigma}\mathrm{d}\zeta,\end{aligned}

(4.25)

\displaystyle\textrm{B4}(\theta)[\varphi_{1},\varphi_{2},\varphi_{3}]

\displaystyle:=\begin{aligned} &\beta\int_{\mathbb{R}^{2}_{+}}\left(\mathfrak{d}_{\zeta}(\theta_{\Gamma_{2}})-\sigma\mathrm{d}_{\zeta}\right)\mathfrak{d}_{\zeta}(\theta)F_{1}^{\ast}(\theta,\zeta,\sigma)[\varphi_{1}]\mathfrak{d}_{\zeta}(\theta_{\Gamma_{2}})F_{2}^{\ast}(\theta,\zeta,\sigma)[\varphi_{2}]\qquad\\
&\qquad\times\int_{0}^{\sigma}\frac{\rho}{\sigma}\mathfrak{d}_{\zeta}{(\theta_{\Gamma_{3}})}F_{3}^{\ast}(\theta,\zeta,\rho)[\varphi_{3}]\frac{\mathrm{d}\rho}{\rho}\frac{\mathrm{d}\sigma}{\sigma}\mathrm{d}\zeta.\end{aligned}

The second inequality in (
4.19
) follows from

Lemma

3.13
according to which we can decompose
each of the wave packets
\widetilde{\varphi}_{1,\theta}^{l},\widetilde{\varphi}_{2,\theta}^{l},\widetilde{\varphi}_{3,\theta}^{o},\varphi_{3,\theta}^{o}
into an
absolutely convergent series e.g.

(4.26)

\widetilde{\varphi}_{1,\theta}^{l}\coloneqq\sum_{k}\widetilde{a}^{l}_{1,k}(\theta)\widetilde{\widetilde{\varphi}}_{k}

with
\|\widetilde{\widetilde{\varphi}}_{k}\|_{\Phi_{\mathfrak{r}}^{N}}\leq 1
.

Let us now show that each of the 6 terms in

\mathrm{RHS}{\eqref{eq:single-tree:l-l-o:decomp}}
contributes a quantity bounded
by
\mathrm{RHS}{\eqref{eq:STE:l-l-o}}
. We record Young’s convolution inequality in
a form useful for us. A direct computation gives

(4.27)

\bigl\|\rho\mathbbm{1}_{\rho<1}\bigr\|_{L^{1}_{\frac{\mathrm{d}\rho}{\rho}}(\mathbb{R})}+\bigl\|\rho\mathbbm{1}_{\rho<1}\bigr\|_{L^{\infty}_{\frac{\mathrm{d}\rho}{\rho}}(\mathbb{R})}\lesssim 1,

so it follows that for any
q\in[1,\infty]
and any
h\in L^{q}_{\mathrm{d}\rho/\rho}(\mathbb{R}_{+})
it holds that

(4.28)

\left\|\int_{0}^{\sigma}\frac{\rho}{\sigma}h(\rho)\frac{\mathrm{d}\rho}{\rho}\right\|_{L^{q}_{\frac{\mathrm{d}\rho}{\rho}}(\mathbb{R})+L^{\infty}_{\frac{\mathrm{d}\rho}{\rho}}(\mathbb{R})}\lesssim\|h\|_{L^{q}_{\frac{\mathrm{d}\rho}{\rho}}(\mathbb{R})}.

Note that the integral above is a multiplicative convolution with respect to
the multiplicative Haar measure
\frac{\mathrm{d}\rho}{\rho}
. For
\left\|{\varphi_{j}}\right\|_{\Phi_{\mathfrak{r}}^{N}}\leq 1
,
j\in\{1,2,3\}
, the
following bounds thus hold:

(4.29)

\displaystyle\int_{\Theta}|\textrm{M1}(\theta)[\varphi_{1},\varphi_{2},\varphi_{3}]|\mathrm{d}\theta

\displaystyle\lesssim\begin{aligned} &\left\|\mathfrak{d}_{\zeta}(\theta)F_{1}^{\ast}(\theta,\zeta,\sigma)[\varphi_{1}]\right\|_{L^{u_{1}}_{\frac{\mathrm{d}\zeta\mathrm{d}\theta}{|\Theta|}}L^{\infty}_{\frac{\mathrm{d}\sigma}{\sigma}}}\left\|\mathfrak{d}_{\zeta}(\theta_{\Gamma_{2}})^{2}F_{2}^{\ast}(\theta,\zeta,\sigma)[\varphi_{2}]\right\|_{L^{u_{2}}_{\frac{\mathrm{d}\zeta\mathrm{d}\theta}{|\Theta|}}L^{2}_{\frac{\mathrm{d}\sigma}{\sigma}}}\qquad\\
&\times\left\|\mathfrak{d}_{\zeta}(\theta_{\Gamma_{3}})F_{3}^{\ast}(\theta,\zeta,\rho)[\varphi_{3}]\right\|_{L_{\frac{\mathrm{d}\zeta\mathrm{d}\theta}{|\Theta|}}^{u_{3}}L_{\frac{\mathrm{d}\sigma}{\sigma}}^{2}}\end{aligned}

\displaystyle\lesssim\|F_{1}\|_{\mathbb{L}_{\Theta}^{(u_{1},\infty)}\Phi_{\mathfrak{r}}^{N}(T)}\|F_{2}\|_{\mathbb{L}_{\Theta}^{(u_{2},2)}\Gamma_{2}^{\ast}\mathfrak{d}_{\mathfrak{r}}^{N}(T)}\|F_{3}\|_{\mathbb{L}_{\Theta}^{(u_{3},2)}\Gamma_{3}^{\ast}\mathfrak{d}_{\mathfrak{r}}^{N}(T)};

(4.30)

\displaystyle\int_{\Theta}|\textrm{M2}(\theta)[\varphi_{1},\varphi_{2},\varphi_{3}]|\mathrm{d}\theta

\displaystyle\lesssim\begin{aligned} &\beta\left\|\mathfrak{d}_{\zeta}(\theta)^{2}F_{1}^{\ast}(\theta,\zeta,\sigma)[\varphi_{1}]\right\|_{L^{u_{1}}_{\frac{\mathrm{d}\zeta\mathrm{d}\theta}{|\Theta|}}L^{\infty}_{\frac{\mathrm{d}\sigma}{\sigma}}}\left\|\mathfrak{d}_{\zeta}(\theta_{\Gamma_{2}})F_{2}^{\ast}(\theta,\zeta,\sigma)[\varphi_{2}]\right\|_{L^{u_{2}}_{\frac{\mathrm{d}\zeta\mathrm{d}\theta}{|\Theta|}}L^{2}_{\frac{\mathrm{d}\sigma}{\sigma}}}\qquad\\
&\times\left\|\mathfrak{d}_{\zeta}(\theta_{\Gamma_{3}})F_{3}^{\ast}(\theta,\zeta,\rho)[\varphi_{3}]\right\|_{L_{\frac{\mathrm{d}\zeta\mathrm{d}\theta}{|\Theta|}}^{u_{3}}L_{\frac{\mathrm{d}\sigma}{\sigma}}^{2}}\end{aligned}

\displaystyle\lesssim\beta\|F_{1}\|_{\mathbb{L}_{\Theta}^{(u_{1},\infty)}\Phi_{\mathfrak{r}}^{N}(T)}\|F_{2}\|_{\mathbb{L}_{\Theta}^{(u_{2},2)}\Gamma_{2}^{\ast}\mathfrak{d}_{\mathfrak{r}}^{N}(T)}\|F_{3}\|_{\mathbb{L}_{\Theta}^{(u_{3},2)}\Gamma_{3}^{\ast}\mathfrak{d}_{\mathfrak{r}}^{N}(T)}.

Bounding the boundary terms
B1
,
B2
,
B3
, and

B4
requires the use of defect sizes:

(4.31)

\displaystyle\int_{\Theta}|\textrm{B1}(\theta)[\varphi_{1},\varphi_{2},\varphi_{3}]|\mathrm{d}\theta

\displaystyle\lesssim\begin{aligned} &\left\|\mathfrak{d}_{\zeta}(\theta)F_{1}^{\ast}(\theta,\zeta,\sigma)[\varphi_{1}]\right\|_{L^{u_{1}}_{\frac{\mathrm{d}\zeta\mathrm{d}\theta}{|\Theta|}}L^{2}_{\frac{\mathrm{d}\sigma}{\sigma}}}\left\|\mathfrak{d}_{\zeta}(\theta_{\Gamma_{2}})F_{2}^{\ast}(\theta,\zeta,\sigma)[\varphi_{2}]\right\|_{L^{u_{2}}_{\frac{\mathrm{d}\theta\mathrm{d}\zeta}{|\Theta|}}L^{2}_{\frac{\mathrm{d}\sigma}{\sigma}}}\\
&\times\left\|\left(\mathfrak{d}_{\sigma}(\theta_{\Gamma_{3}})-\rho\mathrm{d}_{\rho}\right)F_{3}^{\ast}(\theta,\zeta,\rho)[\varphi_{3}]\right\|_{L_{\frac{\mathrm{d}\theta\mathrm{d}\zeta}{|\Theta|}}^{u_{3}}L_{\frac{\mathrm{d}\rho}{\rho}}^{1}}\end{aligned}\qquad

\displaystyle\lesssim\|F_{1}\|_{\mathbb{L}_{\Theta}^{(u_{1},\infty)}\mathfrak{d}_{\mathfrak{r}}^{N}(T)}\|F_{2}\|_{\mathbb{L}_{\Theta}^{(u_{2},2)}\Gamma_{2}^{\ast}\mathfrak{d}_{\mathfrak{r}}^{N}(T)}\|F_{3}\|_{\mathbb{L}_{\Theta}^{(u_{3},1)}\Gamma_{3}^{\ast}{\Box}_{\mathfrak{r}}^{N}(T)},

(4.32)

\displaystyle\int_{\Theta}\left|\textrm{B2}(\theta)[\varphi_{1},\varphi_{2},\varphi_{3}]\right|\mathrm{d}\theta

\displaystyle\lesssim\begin{aligned} &\left\|\mathfrak{d}_{\zeta}(\theta)F_{1}^{\ast}(\theta,\zeta,\sigma)[\varphi_{1}]\right\|_{L^{u_{1}}_{\frac{\mathrm{d}\zeta\mathrm{d}\theta}{|\Theta|}}L^{2}_{\frac{\mathrm{d}\sigma}{\sigma}}}\left\|\mathfrak{d}_{\zeta}(\theta_{\Gamma_{2}})F_{2}^{\ast}(\theta,\zeta,\sigma)[\varphi_{2}]\right\|_{L^{u_{2}}_{\frac{\mathrm{d}\theta\mathrm{d}\zeta}{|\Theta|}}L^{2}_{\frac{\mathrm{d}\sigma}{\sigma}}}\\
&\times\left\|\left(\mathfrak{d}_{\zeta_{3}}-\beta\rho d_{\zeta}\right)\mathfrak{d}_{\zeta}(\theta_{\Gamma_{3}})F^{\ast}_{3}(\theta,\zeta,\rho)[\varphi_{3}]\right\|_{L_{\frac{\mathrm{d}\theta\mathrm{d}\zeta}{|\Theta|}}^{u_{3}}L_{\frac{\mathrm{d}\rho}{\rho}}^{1}}\end{aligned}\qquad

\displaystyle\lesssim\|F_{1}\|_{\mathbb{L}_{\Theta}^{(u_{1},\infty)}\mathfrak{d}_{\mathfrak{r}}^{N}(T)}\|F_{2}\|_{\mathbb{L}_{\Theta}^{(u_{2},2)}\Gamma_{2}^{\ast}\mathfrak{d}_{\mathfrak{r}}^{N}(T)}\|F_{3}\|_{\mathbb{L}_{\Theta}^{(u_{3},1)}\Gamma_{3}^{\ast}\Box_{\mathfrak{r}}^{N}(T)},

(4.33)

\displaystyle\int_{\Theta}|\textrm{B3}(\theta)[\varphi_{1},\varphi_{2},\varphi_{3}]|\mathrm{d}\theta

\displaystyle\lesssim\begin{aligned} &\left\|\mathfrak{d}_{\zeta}(\theta)F_{1}^{\ast}(\theta,\zeta,\sigma)[\varphi_{1}]\right\|_{L^{u_{1}}_{\frac{\mathrm{d}\zeta\mathrm{d}\theta}{|\Theta|}}L^{\infty}_{\frac{\mathrm{d}\sigma}{\sigma}}}\qquad\\
&\qquad\times\left\|\left(\mathfrak{d}_{\zeta}(\theta_{\Gamma_{2}})-\beta\sigma\mathrm{d}_{\zeta}\right)\mathfrak{d}_{\zeta}(\theta_{\Gamma_{2}})F_{2}^{\ast}(\theta,\zeta,\sigma)[\varphi_{2}]\right\|_{L^{u_{2}}_{\frac{\mathrm{d}\theta\mathrm{d}\zeta}{|\Theta|}}L^{1}_{\frac{\mathrm{d}\sigma}{\sigma}}}\\
&\qquad\times\left\|\mathfrak{d}_{\zeta}(\theta_{\Gamma_{3}})F^{\ast}_{3}(\theta,\zeta,\rho)[\varphi_{3}]\right\|_{L_{\frac{\mathrm{d}\theta\mathrm{d}\zeta}{|\Theta|}}^{u_{3}}L_{\frac{\mathrm{d}\rho}{\rho}}^{2}}\end{aligned}

\displaystyle\lesssim\|F_{1}\|_{\mathbb{L}_{\Theta}^{(u_{1},\infty)}\Phi_{\mathfrak{r}}^{N}(T)}\|F_{2}\|_{\Gamma_{2}^{\ast}\mathbb{L}_{\Theta}^{(u_{2},1)}\Box_{\mathfrak{r}}^{N}(T)}\|F_{3}\|_{\Gamma_{3}^{\ast}\mathbb{L}_{\Theta}^{(u_{3},2)}\mathfrak{d}_{\mathfrak{r}}^{N}(T)},

(4.34)

\displaystyle\int_{\Theta}\left|\textrm{B4}(\theta)[\varphi_{1},\varphi_{2},\varphi_{3}]\right|\mathrm{d}\theta

\displaystyle\lesssim\begin{aligned} &\beta\left\|\left(\mathfrak{d}_{\zeta}(\theta_{\Gamma_{2}})-\sigma\mathrm{d}_{\zeta}\right)\mathfrak{d}_{\zeta}(\theta)F_{1}^{\ast}(\theta,\zeta,\sigma)\right\|_{L^{u_{1}}_{\frac{\mathrm{d}\zeta\mathrm{d}\theta}{|\Theta|}}L^{1}_{\frac{\mathrm{d}\sigma}{\sigma}}}\qquad\\
&\times\left\|\mathfrak{d}_{\zeta}(\theta_{\Gamma_{2}})F_{2}^{\ast}(\theta,\zeta,\sigma)[\varphi_{2}]\right\|_{L^{u_{2}}_{\frac{\mathrm{d}\zeta\mathrm{d}\theta}{|\Theta|}}L^{\infty}_{\frac{\mathrm{d}\sigma}{\sigma}}}\left\|\mathfrak{d}_{\zeta}(\theta_{\Gamma_{3}})F^{\ast}_{3}(\theta,\zeta,\rho)[\varphi_{3}]\right\|_{L_{\frac{\mathrm{d}\zeta\mathrm{d}\theta}{|\Theta|}}^{u_{3}}L_{\frac{\mathrm{d}\rho}{\rho}}^{2}}\end{aligned}

\displaystyle\lesssim\beta\|F_{1}\|_{\mathbb{L}_{\Theta}^{(u_{1},1)}\Box_{\mathfrak{r}}^{N}(T)}\|F_{2}\|_{\mathbb{L}_{\Theta}^{(u_{2},\infty)}\Phi_{\mathfrak{r}}^{N}(T)}\|F_{3}\|_{\mathbb{L}_{\Theta}^{(u_{3},2)}\Gamma_{3}^{\ast}\mathfrak{d}_{\mathfrak{r}}^{N}(T)}

We conclude the proof by showing that (
4.19
)
holds. Our manipulations rely on using the fundamental theorem of calculus
in the scale parameter on
F_{3}^{\ast}
. To exploit certain cancellations we
need to integrate by parts in the spatial variable. Every integration causes
“defect” terms to appear that have to be accounted for; they are grouped
into the terms
\textrm{B1}-\textrm{B4}
.

We crucially rely on the following algebraic identity that follows directly
from definitions (
3.74
) and (
3.76
):

(4.35)

\mathfrak{d}_{\sigma}(\theta)F(\theta,\zeta,\sigma)[\varphi]=\mathfrak{d}_{\zeta}(\theta)F(\theta,\zeta,\sigma)[z\varphi(z)].

In what follows we drop
\theta
from the notation and keep the dependence
on this variable implicit: we write
\mathfrak{d}_{\zeta_{j}}
in place of

\mathfrak{d}_{\zeta}(\theta_{\Gamma_{j}})
,
\mathfrak{d}_{\sigma_{j}}
in place of

\mathfrak{d}_{\sigma}(\theta_{\Gamma_{j}})
,
F^{\ast}_{j}(\zeta,\sigma)
in place
of
F^{\ast}_{j}(\theta,\zeta,\sigma)
,
\widetilde{\varphi}_{j}^{l/o}
in place
of
\widetilde{\varphi}_{j,\theta}^{l/o}
, and so on.

It holds that (using the convention of keeping
\theta
implicit)

(4.36)

\displaystyle\rho\mathrm{d}_{\rho}F^{\ast}_{3}(\zeta,\rho)[\varphi_{3}^{o}]=\mathfrak{d}_{\sigma_{3}}F^{\ast}_{3}(\zeta,\rho)[\varphi_{3}^{o}]-\left(\mathfrak{d}_{\sigma_{3}}-\rho\mathrm{d}_{\rho}\right)F^{\ast}_{3}(\zeta,\rho)[\varphi_{3}^{o}]

\displaystyle=\mathfrak{d}_{\zeta_{3}}^{2}F^{\ast}_{3}(\zeta,\rho)[\widetilde{\varphi}_{3}^{o}]-\left(\mathfrak{d}_{\sigma_{3}}-\rho\mathrm{d}_{\rho}\right)F^{\ast}_{3}(\zeta,\rho)[\varphi_{3}^{o}]

\displaystyle=\begin{aligned} &\beta\rho d_{\zeta}\mathfrak{d}_{\zeta_{3}}F^{\ast}_{3}(\zeta,\rho)[\widetilde{\varphi}_{3}^{o}]+\left(\mathfrak{d}_{\zeta_{3}}-\beta\rho d_{\zeta}\right)\mathfrak{d}_{\zeta_{3}}F^{\ast}_{3}(\zeta,\rho)[\widetilde{\varphi}_{3}^{o}]\\
&-\left(\mathfrak{d}_{\sigma_{3}}-\rho\mathrm{d}_{\rho}\right)F^{\ast}_{3}(\zeta,\rho)[\varphi_{3}^{o}]\end{aligned}

We now concentrate on the inner
\mathbb{R}^{2}_{+}
integral in

\mathrm{LHS}{\eqref{eq:single-tree:l-l-o:decomp}}
. Applying the fundamental
theorem of calculus in the scale variable to term
F^{\ast}_{3}(\zeta,\sigma)[\varphi_{3}^{o}]
and using identity
(
4.36
) we get

(4.37)

\displaystyle\int_{\mathbb{R}^{2}_{+}}{\mathfrak{d}_{\zeta_{1}}}F_{1}^{\ast}(\zeta,\sigma)[\widetilde{\varphi}_{1}^{l}]\mathfrak{d}_{\zeta_{2}}F_{2}^{\ast}(\zeta,\sigma)[\widetilde{\varphi}_{2}^{l}]F_{3}^{\ast}(\theta,\zeta,\sigma)[\varphi_{2}^{o}]\mathrm{d}\zeta\frac{\mathrm{d}\sigma}{\sigma}

\displaystyle=\int_{\mathbb{R}^{2}_{+}}{\mathfrak{d}_{\zeta_{1}}}F^{\ast}_{1}(\zeta,\sigma)[\widetilde{\varphi}_{1}^{l}]\hskip 1.69998pt\mathfrak{d}_{\zeta_{2}}F^{\ast}_{2}(\zeta,\sigma)[\widetilde{\varphi}_{2}^{l}]\int_{0}^{\sigma}\rho\mathrm{d}_{\rho}F^{\ast}_{3}(\zeta,\rho)[\varphi_{3}^{o}]\frac{\mathrm{d}\rho}{\rho}\mathrm{d}\zeta\frac{\mathrm{d}\sigma}{\sigma}.

\displaystyle=\begin{aligned} &\int_{\mathbb{R}^{2}_{+}}\mathfrak{d}_{\zeta_{1}}F^{\ast}_{1}(\zeta,\sigma)[\widetilde{\varphi}_{1}^{l}]\mathfrak{d}_{\zeta_{2}}F^{\ast}_{2}(\zeta,\sigma)[\widetilde{\varphi}_{2}^{l}]\int_{0}^{\sigma}\beta\rho\mathrm{d}_{\zeta}\mathfrak{d}_{\zeta_{3}}F^{\ast}_{3}(\zeta,\rho)[\widetilde{\varphi}_{3}^{o}]\frac{\mathrm{d}\rho}{\rho}\mathrm{d}\zeta\frac{\mathrm{d}\sigma}{\sigma}\\
&-\textrm{B1}[\widetilde{\varphi}_{1}^{l},\widetilde{\varphi}_{2}^{l},\varphi_{3}^{o}]+\textrm{B2}[\widetilde{\varphi}_{1}^{l},\widetilde{\varphi}_{2}^{l},\widetilde{\varphi}_{3}^{o}].\end{aligned}

Integrating the first summand on the
\mathrm{RHS}{}
by parts in
\zeta
gives

(4.38)

\displaystyle\int_{\mathbb{R}^{2}_{+}}\mathfrak{d}_{\zeta_{1}}F^{\ast}_{1}(\zeta,\sigma)[\widetilde{\varphi}_{1}^{l}]\mathfrak{d}_{\zeta_{2}}F^{\ast}_{2}(\zeta,\sigma)[\widetilde{\varphi}_{2}^{l}]\int_{0}^{\sigma}\beta\rho\mathrm{d}_{\zeta}\mathfrak{d}_{\zeta_{3}}F^{\ast}_{3}(\zeta,\rho)[\widetilde{\varphi}_{3}^{o}]\frac{\mathrm{d}\rho}{\rho}\mathrm{d}\zeta\frac{\mathrm{d}\sigma}{\sigma}

\displaystyle=\begin{aligned} &\int_{\mathbb{R}^{2}_{+}}\mathfrak{d}_{\zeta_{1}}F^{\ast}_{1}(\zeta,\sigma)[\widetilde{\varphi}_{1}^{l}]\hskip 1.69998pt\beta\sigma\mathrm{d}_{\zeta}\mathfrak{d}_{\zeta_{2}}F^{\ast}_{2}(\zeta,\sigma)[\widetilde{\varphi}_{2}^{l}]\int_{0}^{\sigma}\frac{\rho}{\sigma}\mathfrak{d}_{\zeta_{3}}F^{\ast}_{3}(\zeta,\rho)[\widetilde{\varphi}_{3}^{o}]\frac{\mathrm{d}\rho}{\rho}\frac{\mathrm{d}\sigma}{\sigma}\mathrm{d}\zeta\\
&\qquad+\beta\int_{\mathbb{R}^{2}_{+}}\sigma\mathrm{d}_{\zeta}\mathfrak{d}_{\zeta_{1}}F^{\ast}_{1}(\zeta,\sigma)[\widetilde{\varphi}_{1}^{l}]\mathfrak{d}_{\zeta_{2}}F^{\ast}_{2}(\zeta,\sigma)[\widetilde{\varphi}_{2}^{l}]\int_{0}^{\sigma}\frac{\rho}{\sigma}\mathfrak{d}_{\zeta_{3}}F^{\ast}_{3}(\zeta,\rho)[\widetilde{\varphi}_{3}^{o}]\frac{\mathrm{d}\rho}{\rho}\frac{\mathrm{d}\sigma}{\sigma}\mathrm{d}\zeta\end{aligned}

\displaystyle=\textrm{M1}[\widetilde{\varphi}_{1}^{l},\widetilde{\varphi}_{2}^{l},\widetilde{\varphi}_{3}^{o}]+\textrm{B3}[\widetilde{\varphi}_{1}^{l},\widetilde{\varphi}_{2}^{l},\widetilde{\varphi}_{3}^{o}]+\textrm{M2}[\widetilde{\varphi}_{1}^{l},\widetilde{\varphi}_{2}^{l},\widetilde{\varphi}_{3}^{o}]+\textrm{B4}[\widetilde{\varphi}_{1}^{l},\widetilde{\varphi}_{2}^{l},\widetilde{\varphi}_{3}^{o}].

This concludes the proof of the decomposition
(
4.19
).
∎

Proof.

Proof of bound (
4.3
)
Fix
\varphi_{j}\in\Phi_{\mathfrak{r}/4}^{N+3}
with
\|\varphi_{j}\|_{\Phi_{\mathfrak{r}/4}^{N+3}}\leq 1
so that
\|\varphi_{j,\theta}^{o/l}\|_{\Phi^{N+3}_{\mathfrak{r}/2}}\lesssim 1
. Using the notation from the discussion preceding
this proof, we need to bound the expression

(4.39)

\int_{\Theta}\left|\int_{\mathbb{R}^{2}_{+}}\mathfrak{d}_{\zeta}(\theta)F_{1}^{\ast}(\theta,\zeta,\sigma)[\widetilde{\varphi}_{1,\theta}^{l}]F_{\times}^{\ast}(\theta,\zeta,\rho)[\varphi_{2,\theta}^{o},\varphi_{3,\theta}^{o}]\mathrm{d}\zeta\frac{\mathrm{d}\sigma}{\sigma}\right|\frac{\mathrm{d}\theta}{|\Theta|}

for every
\varphi_{j}\in\Phi_{\mathfrak{r}/4}^{N+3}
with
\|\varphi_{j}\|_{\Phi_{\mathfrak{r}/4}^{N+3}}\leq 1
. We claim that

(4.40)

\eqref{eq:l-o-o-quantity}\begin{aligned} &\leq\int_{\Theta}\begin{aligned} &(|\textrm{M1}(\theta)[\widetilde{\varphi}_{1,\theta}^{l},\widetilde{\varphi}_{2,\theta}^{o},\varphi_{3,\theta}^{o}]|+|\textrm{M1}(\theta)[\widetilde{\varphi}_{1,\theta}^{l},\varphi_{2,\theta}^{o},\widetilde{\varphi}_{3}^{o}]|.\qquad\\
&+\left|\textrm{M2}_{\left(\mathfrak{d}_{\zeta_{2}}\otimes\mathrm{Id}\right)}[\widetilde{\varphi}_{1,\theta}^{l},\widetilde{\varphi}_{2,\theta}^{o},\varphi_{3,\theta}^{o}]\right|+\left|\textrm{M2}_{\left(\mathrm{Id}\otimes\mathfrak{d}_{\zeta_{3}}\right)}[\widetilde{\varphi}_{1,\theta}^{l},\varphi_{2,\theta}^{o},\widetilde{\varphi}_{3,\theta}^{o}]\right|\\
&+|\textrm{B1}(\theta)[\widetilde{\varphi}_{1,\theta}^{l},\varphi_{2,\theta}^{o},\varphi_{3,\theta}^{o}]|\\
&+\left|\textrm{B2}_{\left(\mathfrak{d}_{\zeta_{2}}\otimes\mathrm{Id}\right)}(\theta)[\widetilde{\varphi}_{1,\theta}^{l},\widetilde{\varphi}_{2,\theta}^{o},\varphi_{3,\theta}^{o}]\right|+\left|\textrm{B2}_{\left(\mathrm{Id}\otimes\mathfrak{d}_{\zeta_{3}}\right)}(\theta)[\widetilde{\varphi}_{1,\theta}^{l},\varphi_{2,\theta}^{o},\widetilde{\varphi}_{3,\theta}^{o}]\right|\\
&+\left|\textrm{B3}_{\left(\mathfrak{d}_{\zeta_{2}}\otimes\mathrm{Id}\right)}(\theta)[\widetilde{\varphi}_{1,\theta}^{l},\widetilde{\varphi}_{2,\theta}^{o},\varphi_{3,\theta}^{o}]\right|\\
&\qquad\left.+\left|\textrm{B3}_{\left(\mathrm{Id}\otimes\mathfrak{d}_{\zeta_{3}}\right)}(\theta)[\widetilde{\varphi}_{1,\theta}^{l},\varphi_{2,\theta}^{o},\widetilde{\varphi}_{3,\theta}^{o}]\right|\right)\frac{\mathrm{d}\theta}{|\Theta|}\end{aligned}\\
&\lesssim_{N}\sup_{\text{\scriptsize{$\begin{aligned} &\left\|{\varphi_{j}}\right\|_{\Phi_{\mathfrak{r}}^{N}}\leq 1\\
&j\in\{1,2,3\}\end{aligned}$}}}\int_{\Theta}\begin{aligned} &\left(|\textrm{M1}(\theta)[\varphi_{1},\varphi_{2},\varphi_{3}]|+\left|\textrm{M2$(\theta)[\varphi_{1},\varphi_{2},\varphi_{3}]$}\right|+\right.\qquad\\
&\qquad+|\textrm{B1}(\theta)[\varphi_{1},\varphi_{2},\varphi_{3}]|+|\textrm{B2}(\theta)[\varphi_{1},\varphi_{2},\varphi_{3}]|\\
&\qquad\left.+\left|\text{B3}(\theta)[\varphi_{1},\varphi_{2},\varphi_{3}]\right|\right)\frac{\mathrm{d}\theta}{|\Theta|}\end{aligned}\end{aligned}

where

(4.41)

\textrm{M1}(\theta)[\varphi_{1},\varphi_{2},\varphi_{3}]:=\begin{aligned} &\int_{\mathbb{R}^{2}_{+}}\mathfrak{d}_{\zeta}(\theta)F_{1}^{\ast}(\theta,\zeta,\sigma)[\varphi_{1}]\qquad\\
&\qquad\times\int_{0}^{\sigma}\left(\mathfrak{d}_{\zeta}(\theta_{\Gamma_{2}})\otimes\mathfrak{d}_{\zeta}(\theta_{\Gamma_{3}})\right)F_{\times}^{\ast}(\theta,\zeta,\sigma)[\varphi_{2},\varphi_{3}]\frac{\mathrm{d}\rho}{\rho}\frac{\mathrm{d}\sigma}{\sigma}\mathrm{d}\zeta,\end{aligned}

(4.42)

\textrm{$\textrm{M2}_{\left(\mathfrak{d}_{\zeta}\otimes\mathrm{Id}\right)}$}(\theta)[\varphi_{1},\varphi_{2},\varphi_{3}]:=\begin{aligned} &\beta\int_{\mathbb{R}^{2}_{+}}\mathfrak{d}_{\zeta}(\theta)^{2}F_{1}^{\ast}(\theta,\zeta,\sigma)[\varphi_{1}]\qquad\\
&\qquad\times\int_{0}^{\sigma}\frac{\rho}{\sigma}\left(\mathfrak{d}_{\zeta}(\theta_{\Gamma_{2}})\otimes\operatorname{Id}\right)F_{\times}^{\ast}(\theta,\zeta,\sigma)[\varphi_{2},\varphi_{3}]\frac{\mathrm{d}\rho}{\rho}\frac{\mathrm{d}\sigma}{\sigma}\mathrm{d}\zeta,\end{aligned}

(4.43)

\textrm{$\textrm{M2}_{\left(\mathrm{Id}\otimes\mathfrak{d}_{\zeta}\right)}$}(\theta)[\varphi_{1},\varphi_{2},\varphi_{3}]:=\begin{aligned} &\beta\int_{\mathbb{R}^{2}_{+}}\mathfrak{d}_{\zeta}(\theta)^{2}F_{1}^{\ast}(\theta,\zeta,\sigma)[\varphi_{1}]\qquad\\
&\qquad\times\int_{0}^{\sigma}\frac{\rho}{\sigma}\left(\operatorname{Id}\otimes\mathfrak{d}_{\zeta}(\theta_{\Gamma_{3}})\right)F_{\times}^{\ast}(\theta,\zeta,\sigma)[\varphi_{2},\varphi_{3}]\frac{\mathrm{d}\rho}{\rho}\frac{\mathrm{d}\sigma}{\sigma}\mathrm{d}\zeta,\end{aligned}

and

(4.44)

\displaystyle\textrm{B1}(\theta)[\varphi_{1},\varphi_{2},\varphi_{3}]

\displaystyle:=\begin{aligned} &\int_{\mathbb{R}^{2}_{+}}\mathfrak{d}_{\zeta}(\theta)F_{1}^{\ast}(\theta,\zeta,\sigma)[\varphi_{1}]\\
&\qquad\times\int_{0}^{\sigma}\left(\mathfrak{d}_{\sigma}(\theta_{\Gamma_{2}})\otimes\mathrm{Id}+\mathrm{Id}\otimes\mathfrak{d}_{\sigma}(\theta_{\Gamma_{3}})-\rho\mathrm{d}_{\rho}\right)F_{\times}^{\ast}(\theta,\zeta,\sigma)[\varphi_{2},\varphi_{3}]\frac{\mathrm{d}\rho}{\rho}\frac{\mathrm{d}\sigma}{\sigma}\mathrm{d}\zeta,\end{aligned}

(4.45)

\displaystyle\textrm{B2}_{\left(\mathfrak{d}_{\zeta}\otimes\mathrm{Id}\right)}[\varphi_{1},\varphi_{2},\varphi_{3}]

\displaystyle:=\begin{aligned} &\int_{\mathbb{R}^{2}_{+}}\mathfrak{d}_{\zeta}(\theta)F_{1}^{\ast}(\theta,\zeta,\sigma)[\varphi_{1}]\int_{0}^{\sigma}\left(\mathfrak{d}_{\zeta}(\theta_{\Gamma_{2}})\otimes\mathrm{Id}+\mathrm{Id}\otimes\mathfrak{d}_{\zeta}(\theta_{\Gamma_{3}})-\beta\rho\mathrm{d}_{\zeta}\right)\\
&\qquad\left(\mathfrak{d}_{\zeta}(\theta_{\Gamma_{2}})\otimes\mathrm{Id}\right)F_{\times}^{\ast}(\theta,\zeta,\sigma)[\varphi_{2},\varphi_{3}]\frac{\mathrm{d}\rho}{\rho}\frac{\mathrm{d}\sigma}{\sigma}\mathrm{d}\zeta,\end{aligned}

(4.46)

\displaystyle\textrm{B2}_{\left(\mathrm{Id}\otimes\mathfrak{d}_{\zeta}\right)}[\varphi_{1},\varphi_{2},\varphi_{3}]

\displaystyle:=\begin{aligned} &\int_{\mathbb{R}^{2}_{+}}\mathfrak{d}_{\zeta}(\theta)F_{1}^{\ast}(\theta,\zeta,\sigma)[\varphi_{1}]\int_{0}^{\sigma}\left(\mathfrak{d}_{\zeta}(\theta_{\Gamma_{2}})\otimes\mathrm{Id}+\mathrm{Id}\otimes\mathfrak{d}_{\zeta}(\theta_{\Gamma_{3}})-\beta\rho\mathrm{d}_{\zeta}\right)\\
&\qquad\left(\mathrm{Id}\otimes\mathfrak{d}_{\zeta}(\theta_{\Gamma_{3}})\right)F_{\times}^{\ast}(\theta,\zeta,\sigma)[\varphi_{2},\varphi_{3}]\frac{\mathrm{d}\rho}{\rho}\frac{\mathrm{d}\sigma}{\sigma}\mathrm{d}\zeta,\end{aligned}

(4.47)

\textrm{B3}_{\left(\mathfrak{d}_{\zeta}\otimes\mathrm{Id}\right)}[\varphi_{1},\varphi_{2},\varphi_{3}]:=\begin{aligned} &\beta\int_{\mathbb{R}^{2}_{+}}\left(\mathfrak{d}_{\zeta}(\theta)-\sigma d_{\zeta}\right)F_{1}^{\ast}(\theta,\zeta,\sigma)[\varphi_{1}]\\
&\qquad\times\int_{0}^{\sigma}\frac{\rho}{\sigma}\left(\mathfrak{d}_{\zeta}(\theta_{\Gamma_{2}})\otimes\mathrm{Id}\right)F_{\times}^{\ast}(\theta,\zeta,\sigma)[\varphi_{2},\varphi_{3}]\frac{\mathrm{d}\rho}{\rho}\frac{\mathrm{d}\sigma}{\sigma}\mathrm{d}\zeta,\end{aligned}

(4.48)

\textrm{B3}_{\left(\mathrm{Id}\otimes\mathfrak{d}_{\zeta}\right)}[\varphi_{1},\varphi_{2},\varphi_{3}]:=\begin{aligned} &\beta\int_{\mathbb{R}^{2}_{+}}\left(\mathfrak{d}_{\zeta}(\theta)-\sigma d_{\zeta}\right)F_{1}^{\ast}(\theta,\zeta,\sigma)[\varphi_{1}]\qquad\\
&\qquad\times\int_{0}^{\sigma}\frac{\rho}{\sigma}\left(\mathrm{Id}\otimes\mathfrak{d}_{\zeta}(\theta_{\Gamma_{3}})\right)F_{\times}^{\ast}(\theta,\zeta,\sigma)[\varphi_{2},\varphi_{3}]\frac{\mathrm{d}\rho}{\rho}\frac{\mathrm{d}\sigma}{\sigma}\mathrm{d}\zeta,\end{aligned}

Let us now show that each of the 6 terms in

\mathrm{RHS}{\eqref{eq:single-tree:l-o-o:decomp}}
contributes a quantity bounded
by
\mathrm{RHS}{\eqref{eq:STE:l-o-o}}
. For
\left\|{\varphi_{j}}\right\|_{\Phi_{\mathfrak{r}}^{N}}\leq 1
,
j\in\{1,2,3\}
, the following bounds
thus hold:

(4.49)

\displaystyle\int_{\Theta^{\operatorname{in}}}|\textrm{M1}(\theta)[\varphi_{1},\varphi_{2},\varphi_{3}]|\mathrm{d}\theta

\displaystyle\lesssim\begin{aligned} &\left\|\mathfrak{d}_{\zeta}(\theta)F_{1}^{\ast}(\theta,\zeta,\sigma)[\varphi_{1}]\right\|_{L^{u_{1}}_{\frac{\mathrm{d}\zeta\mathrm{d}\theta}{|\Theta|}}L^{\infty}_{\frac{\mathrm{d}\sigma}{\sigma}}}\\
&\qquad\times\left\|\left(\mathfrak{d}_{\zeta}(\theta_{\Gamma_{2}})\otimes\mathfrak{d}_{\zeta}(\theta_{\Gamma_{3}})\right)F_{\times}^{\ast}(\theta,\zeta,\sigma)[\varphi_{2},\varphi_{3}]\right\|_{L^{u_{\times}}_{\frac{\mathrm{d}\zeta\mathrm{d}\theta}{|\Theta|}}L^{1}_{\frac{\mathrm{d}\sigma}{\sigma}}}\end{aligned}

\displaystyle\lesssim\|F_{1}\|_{\mathbb{L}_{\Theta}^{(u_{1},\infty)}\Phi_{\mathfrak{r}}^{N}(T)}\|F_{2}F_{3}\|_{\mathbb{L}_{\Theta}^{(u_{\times},1)}\Gamma_{\times}^{\ast}\left(\mathfrak{d}\otimes\mathfrak{d}\right)_{\mathfrak{r}}^{N}(T)};

(4.50)

\displaystyle\int_{\Theta^{\operatorname{in}}}\left|\textrm{M2}_{\left(\mathfrak{d}_{\zeta}\otimes\mathrm{Id}\right)}(\theta)[\varphi_{1},\varphi_{2},\varphi_{3}]\right|\mathrm{d}\theta

\displaystyle\lesssim\begin{aligned} &\beta\left\|\mathfrak{d}_{\zeta}(\theta)^{2}F_{1}^{\ast}(\theta,\zeta,\sigma)[\varphi_{1}]\right\|_{L^{u_{1}}_{\frac{\mathrm{d}\zeta\mathrm{d}\theta}{|\Theta|}}L^{2}_{\frac{\mathrm{d}\sigma}{\sigma}}}\\
&\qquad\times\left\|\left(\mathfrak{d}_{\zeta}(\theta_{\Gamma_{2}})\otimes\mathrm{Id}\right)F_{\times}^{\ast}(\theta,\zeta,\sigma)[\varphi_{2},\varphi_{3}]\right\|_{L^{u_{\times}}_{\frac{\mathrm{d}\zeta\mathrm{d}\theta}{|\Theta|}}L^{2}_{\frac{\mathrm{d}\sigma}{\sigma}}}\end{aligned}

\displaystyle\lesssim\beta\|F_{1}\|_{\mathbb{L}_{\Theta}^{(u_{1},2)}\mathfrak{d}_{\mathfrak{r}}^{N}(T)}\|F_{2}F_{3}\|_{\Gamma_{\times}^{\ast}\mathbb{L}_{\Theta}^{(u_{\times},2)}\left(\mathrm{Id}\otimes\mathfrak{d}\right)_{\mathfrak{r}}^{N}(T)};

similarly

(4.51)

\displaystyle\int_{\Theta}\left|\textrm{M2}_{\left(\mathrm{Id}\otimes\mathfrak{d}_{\zeta}\right)}(\theta)[\varphi_{1},\varphi_{2},\varphi_{3}]\right|\mathrm{d}\theta

\displaystyle\lesssim\beta\|F_{1}\|_{\mathbb{L}_{\Theta}^{(u_{1},2)}\mathfrak{d}_{\mathfrak{r}}^{N}(T)}\|F_{2}F_{3}\|_{\Gamma_{\times}^{\ast}\mathbb{L}_{\Theta}^{(u_{\times},2)}\left(\mathfrak{d}\otimes\mathrm{Id}\right)_{\mathfrak{r}}^{N}(T)}.

Bounding the boundary terms
B1
,
B2
, and
B3
,
requires the use of defect sizes:

(4.52)

\displaystyle\int_{\Theta^{\operatorname{in}}}|\textrm{B1}(\theta)[\varphi_{1},\varphi_{2},\varphi_{3}]|\mathrm{d}\theta

\displaystyle\lesssim\begin{aligned} &\left\|\int_{\rho}^{+\infty}\mathfrak{d}_{\zeta}(\theta)F_{1}^{\ast}(\theta,\zeta,\sigma)[\varphi_{1}]\frac{\mathrm{d}\sigma}{\sigma}\right\|_{L^{u_{1}}_{\frac{\mathrm{d}\zeta\mathrm{d}\theta}{|\Theta|}}L^{\infty}_{\frac{\mathrm{d}\rho}{\rho}}}\\
&\times\left\|\left(\mathfrak{d}_{\sigma}(\theta_{\Gamma_{2}})\otimes\mathrm{Id}+\mathrm{Id}\otimes\mathfrak{d}_{\sigma}(\theta_{\Gamma_{3}})-\rho\mathrm{d}_{\rho}\right)F_{\times}^{\ast}(\theta,\zeta,\rho)[\varphi_{2},\varphi_{3}]\right\|_{L^{u_{\times}}_{\frac{\mathrm{d}\zeta\mathrm{d}\theta}{|\Theta|}}L^{1}_{\frac{\mathrm{d}\rho}{\rho}}}\end{aligned}

\displaystyle\lesssim\|F_{1}\|_{\mathbb{J}^{(u_{1},\infty)}_{\Theta}\Phi_{\mathfrak{r}}^{N}(T)}\|F_{2}F_{3}\|_{\Gamma_{\times}^{\ast}\mathbb{L}_{(\Theta,\Theta^{\operatorname{in}})}^{(u_{\times},1)}\Box_{\mathfrak{r}}^{N}(T)};

(4.53)

\displaystyle\int_{\Theta}\left|\textrm{B2}_{\left(\mathfrak{d}_{\zeta_{2}}\otimes\mathrm{Id}\right)}[\varphi_{1},\varphi_{2},\varphi_{3}]\right|\mathrm{d}\theta

\displaystyle\lesssim\begin{aligned} &\left\|\mathfrak{d}_{\zeta}(\theta)F_{1}^{\ast}(\theta,\zeta,\sigma)[\varphi_{1}]\frac{\mathrm{d}\sigma}{\sigma}\right\|_{L^{u_{1}}_{\frac{\mathrm{d}\zeta\mathrm{d}\theta}{|\Theta|}}L^{2}_{\frac{\mathrm{d}\sigma}{\sigma}}}\\
&\times\begin{aligned} &\left\|\left(\mathfrak{d}_{\zeta}(\theta_{\Gamma_{2}})\otimes\mathrm{Id}+\mathrm{Id}\otimes\mathfrak{d}_{\zeta}(\theta_{\Gamma_{3}})-\beta\rho\mathrm{d}_{\zeta}\right)\right.\\
&\qquad\times\left.\left(\mathfrak{d}_{\zeta}(\theta_{\Gamma_{2}})\otimes\mathrm{Id}\right)F_{\times}^{\ast}(\theta,\zeta,\rho)[\varphi_{2},\varphi_{3}]\right\|_{L^{u_{\times}}_{\frac{\mathrm{d}\zeta\mathrm{d}\theta}{|\Theta|}}L^{1}_{\frac{\mathrm{d}\rho}{\rho}}}\end{aligned}\end{aligned}

\displaystyle\lesssim\|F_{1}\|_{\mathbb{L}^{(u_{1},2)}_{\Theta}\mathfrak{d}_{\mathfrak{r}}^{N}(T)}\|F_{2}F_{3}\|_{\Gamma_{\times}^{\ast}\mathbb{L}_{(\Theta,\Theta^{\operatorname{in}})}^{(u_{\times},1)}\Box_{\mathfrak{r}}^{N}(T)}

and similarly

(4.54)

\int_{\Theta}\left|\textrm{B2}_{\left(\mathfrak{d}_{\zeta_{2}}\otimes\mathrm{Id}\right)}[\varphi_{1},\varphi_{2},\varphi_{3}]\right|\mathrm{d}\theta\lesssim\|F_{1}\|_{\mathbb{L}^{(u_{1},2)}_{\Theta}{\mathfrak{d}_{\mathfrak{r}}^{N}}^{\prime}(T)}\|F_{2}F_{3}\|_{\Gamma_{\times}^{\ast}\mathbb{L}_{(\Theta,\Theta^{\operatorname{in}})}^{(u_{\times},1)}\Box_{\mathfrak{r}}^{N}(T)};

(4.55)

\displaystyle\int_{\Theta}\left|\textrm{B3}_{\left(\mathfrak{d}_{\zeta_{2}}\otimes\mathrm{Id}\right)}[\varphi_{1},\varphi_{2},\varphi_{3}]\right|\mathrm{d}\theta\begin{aligned} &\lesssim\begin{aligned} &\beta\left\|\left(\mathfrak{d}_{\zeta}(\theta)-\sigma d_{\zeta}\right)F_{1}^{\ast}(\theta,\zeta,\sigma)[\varphi_{1}]\frac{\mathrm{d}\sigma}{\sigma}\right\|_{L^{u_{1}}_{\frac{\mathrm{d}\zeta\mathrm{d}\theta}{|\Theta|}}L^{1}_{\frac{\mathrm{d}\sigma}{\sigma}}}\\
&\qquad\times\left\|\left(\mathfrak{d}_{\zeta}(\theta_{\Gamma_{2}})\otimes\mathrm{Id}\right)F_{\times}^{\ast}(\theta,\zeta,\rho)[\varphi_{2},\varphi_{3}]\right\|_{L^{u_{\times}}_{\frac{\mathrm{d}\zeta\mathrm{d}\theta}{|\Theta|}}L^{2}_{\frac{\mathrm{d}\rho}{\rho}}}\end{aligned}\\
&\lesssim\beta\|F_{1}\|_{\mathbb{L}^{(u_{1},1)}_{\Theta}\Box_{\mathfrak{r}}^{N}(T)}\|F_{2}F_{3}\|_{\Gamma_{\times}^{\ast}\mathbb{L}_{\Theta}^{(u_{\times},1)}{\left(\mathfrak{d}\otimes\mathrm{Id}\right)}_{\mathfrak{r}}^{N}(T)}\end{aligned}

and similarly

(4.56)

\displaystyle\int_{\Theta}\left|\textrm{B3}_{\left(\mathrm{Id}\otimes\mathfrak{d}_{\zeta_{3}}\right)}[\varphi_{1},\varphi_{2},\varphi_{3}]\right|\mathrm{d}\theta\lesssim\begin{aligned} &\beta\left\|\left(\mathfrak{d}_{\zeta}(\theta)-\sigma d_{\zeta}\right)F_{1}^{\ast}(\theta,\zeta,\sigma)[\varphi_{1}]\frac{\mathrm{d}\sigma}{\sigma}\right\|_{L^{u_{1}}_{\frac{\mathrm{d}\zeta\mathrm{d}\theta}{|\Theta|}}L^{1}_{\frac{\mathrm{d}\sigma}{\sigma}}}\\
&\qquad\times\left\|\left(\mathrm{Id}\otimes\mathfrak{d}_{\zeta}(\theta_{\Gamma_{3}})\right)F_{\times}^{\ast}(\theta,\zeta,\rho)[\varphi_{2},\varphi_{3}]\right\|_{L^{u_{\times}}_{\frac{\mathrm{d}\zeta\mathrm{d}\theta}{|\Theta|}}L^{2}_{\frac{\mathrm{d}\rho}{\rho}}}\end{aligned}

\displaystyle\lesssim\beta\|F_{1}\|_{\mathbb{L}^{(u_{1},1)}_{\Theta}\Box_{\mathfrak{r}}^{N}(T)}\|F_{2}F_{3}\|_{\Gamma_{\times}^{\ast}\mathbb{L}_{\Theta}^{(u_{\times},1)}{\left(\mathrm{Id}\otimes\mathfrak{d}\right)}_{\mathfrak{r}}^{N}(T)}.

We conclude the proof by showing that (
4.40
)
holds. Our manipulations rely on using the fundamental theorem of calculus
in the scale parameter on the product
F_{2}^{\ast}F_{3}^{\ast}
. To exploit
certain cancellations we need to integrate by parts in the spatial variable.
Every integration causes “defect” terms to appear that have to be
accounted for; they are grouped into the terms
\textrm{B1}-\textrm{B3}
.

In what follows we drop
\theta
from the notation and keep the dependence
on this variable implicit: we write
\mathfrak{d}_{\zeta_{j}}
in place of

\mathfrak{d}_{\zeta}(\theta_{\Gamma_{j}})
,
\mathfrak{d}_{\sigma_{j}}
in place of

\mathfrak{d}_{\sigma}(\theta_{\Gamma_{j}})
,
\widetilde{\varphi}_{j}^{l/o}
in place of

\widetilde{\varphi}_{j,\theta}^{l/o}
, and so on. For conciseness, we also
write
F_{\times}^{\ast}(\zeta,\rho)
for
F^{\ast}_{2}(\zeta,\rho)F^{\ast}_{3}(\zeta,\rho)
. It holds that (using the convention of keeping

\theta
implicit)

(4.57)

\displaystyle\rho\mathrm{d}_{\rho}(F^{\ast}_{2}(\zeta,\rho)[\varphi_{2}^{o}]F^{\ast}_{3}(\zeta,\rho)[\varphi_{3}^{o}])=\rho\mathrm{d}_{\rho}F_{\times}^{\ast}(\zeta,\rho)[\varphi_{2}^{o},\varphi_{3}^{o}]

\displaystyle=\begin{aligned} &+\left(\mathfrak{d}_{\sigma_{2}}\otimes\mathrm{Id}+\mathrm{Id}\otimes\mathfrak{d}_{\sigma_{3}}\right)F_{\times}^{\ast}(\zeta,\rho)[\varphi_{2}^{o},\varphi_{3}^{o}]\\
&-\left(\mathfrak{d}_{\sigma_{2}}\otimes\mathrm{Id}+\mathrm{Id}\otimes\mathfrak{d}_{\sigma_{3}}-\rho\mathrm{d}_{\rho}\right)F_{\times}^{\ast}(\zeta,\rho)[\varphi_{2}^{o},\varphi_{3}^{o}]\end{aligned}

\displaystyle=\begin{aligned} &+\left(\mathfrak{d}_{\zeta_{2}}\otimes\mathrm{Id}\right)^{2}F_{\times}^{\ast}(\zeta,\rho)[\widetilde{\varphi}_{2}^{o},\varphi_{3}^{o}]+\left(\mathrm{Id}\otimes\mathfrak{d}_{\zeta_{3}}\right)^{2}F_{\times}^{\ast}(\zeta,\rho)[\varphi_{2}^{o},\widetilde{\varphi}_{3}^{o}]\\
&-\left(\mathfrak{d}_{\sigma_{2}}\otimes\mathrm{Id}+\mathrm{Id}\otimes\mathfrak{d}_{\sigma_{3}}-\rho\mathrm{d}_{\rho}\right)F_{\times}^{\ast}(\zeta,\rho)[\varphi_{2}^{o},\varphi_{3}^{o}]\end{aligned}

\displaystyle=\begin{aligned} &+\left(\mathfrak{d}_{\zeta_{2}}\otimes\mathrm{Id}+\mathrm{Id}\otimes\mathfrak{d}_{\zeta_{3}}\right)\left(\mathfrak{d}_{\zeta_{2}}\otimes\mathrm{Id}\right)F_{\times}^{\ast}(\zeta,\rho)[\widetilde{\varphi}_{2}^{o},\varphi_{3}^{o}]\\
&+\left(\mathfrak{d}_{\zeta_{2}}\otimes\mathrm{Id}+\mathrm{Id}\otimes\mathfrak{d}_{\zeta_{3}}\right)\left(\mathrm{Id}\otimes\mathfrak{d}_{\zeta_{3}}\right)F^{\ast}_{\times}(\zeta,\rho)[\varphi_{2}^{o},\widetilde{\varphi}_{3}^{o}]\\
&-\left(\mathfrak{d}_{\zeta_{2}}\otimes\mathfrak{d}_{\zeta_{3}}\right)F_{\times}^{\ast}(\zeta,\rho)[\widetilde{\varphi}_{2}^{o},\varphi_{3}^{o}]-\left(\mathfrak{d}_{\zeta_{2}}\otimes\mathfrak{d}_{\zeta_{3}}\right)F_{\times}^{\ast}(\zeta,\rho)[\varphi_{2}^{o},\widetilde{\varphi}_{3}^{o}]\\
&-\left(\mathfrak{d}_{\sigma_{2}}\otimes\mathrm{Id}+\mathrm{Id}\otimes\mathfrak{d}_{\sigma_{3}}-\rho\mathrm{d}_{\rho}\right)F_{\times}^{\ast}(\zeta,\rho)[\varphi_{2}^{o},\varphi_{3}^{o}]\end{aligned}

\displaystyle=\begin{aligned} &+\beta\rho d_{\zeta}\left(\mathfrak{d}_{\zeta_{2}}\otimes\mathrm{Id}\right)F_{\times}^{\ast}(\zeta,\rho)[\widetilde{\varphi}_{2}^{o},\varphi_{3}^{o}]\\
&+\beta\rho d_{\zeta}\left(\mathrm{Id}\otimes\mathfrak{d}_{\zeta_{3}}\right)F_{\times}^{\ast}(\zeta,\rho)[\varphi_{2}^{o},\widetilde{\varphi}_{3}^{o}]\\
&+\left(\mathfrak{d}_{\zeta_{2}}\otimes\mathrm{Id}+\mathrm{Id}\otimes\mathfrak{d}_{\zeta_{3}}-\beta\rho d_{\zeta}\right)\left(\mathfrak{d}_{\zeta_{2}}\otimes\mathrm{Id}\right)F_{\times}^{\ast}(\zeta,\rho)[\widetilde{\varphi}_{2}^{o},\varphi_{3}^{o}]\\
&+\left(\mathfrak{d}_{\zeta_{2}}\otimes\mathrm{Id}+\mathrm{Id}\otimes\mathfrak{d}_{\zeta_{3}}-\beta\rho d_{\zeta}\right)\left(\mathrm{Id}\otimes\mathfrak{d}_{\zeta_{3}}\right)F^{\ast}_{\times}(\zeta,\rho)[\varphi_{2}^{o},\widetilde{\varphi}_{3}^{o}]\\
&-\left(\mathfrak{d}_{\zeta_{2}}\otimes\mathfrak{d}_{\zeta_{3}}\right)F_{\times}^{\ast}(\zeta,\rho)[\widetilde{\varphi}_{2}^{o},\varphi_{3}^{o}]-\left(\mathfrak{d}_{\zeta_{2}}\otimes\mathfrak{d}_{\zeta_{3}}\right)F_{\times}^{\ast}(\zeta,\rho)[\varphi_{2}^{o},\widetilde{\varphi}_{3}^{o}]\\
&-\left(\mathfrak{d}_{\sigma_{2}}\otimes\mathrm{Id}+\mathrm{Id}\otimes\mathfrak{d}_{\sigma_{3}}-\rho\mathrm{d}_{\rho}\right)F_{\times}^{\ast}(\zeta,\rho)[\varphi_{2}^{o},\varphi_{3}^{o}].\end{aligned}

We now concentrate on the inner
\mathbb{R}^{2}_{+}
integral in

\mathrm{LHS}{\eqref{eq:single-tree:l-o-o:decomp}}
. Applying the fundamental
theorem Differentiating and integrating back in scale the term
F^{\ast}_{3}(\zeta,\sigma)[\varphi_{3}^{o}]
in the first summand gives and using identity
(
4.57
) we get

(4.58)

\displaystyle\int_{\mathbb{R}^{2}_{+}}\mathfrak{d}_{\zeta}F_{1}^{\ast}(\zeta,\sigma)[\widetilde{\varphi}_{1}^{l}]F_{2}^{\ast}(\zeta,\sigma)[\varphi_{2}^{o}]F_{3}^{\ast}(\zeta,\sigma)[\varphi_{3}^{o}]\mathrm{d}\zeta\frac{\mathrm{d}\sigma}{\sigma}

\displaystyle=\int_{\mathbb{R}^{2}_{+}}\mathfrak{d}_{\zeta}F_{1}^{\ast}(\zeta,\sigma)[\widetilde{\varphi}_{1}^{l}]\int_{0}^{\sigma}\rho\mathrm{d}_{\rho}F_{\times}^{\ast}(\zeta,\rho)[\varphi_{2}^{o},\varphi_{3}^{o}]\frac{\mathrm{d}\rho}{\rho}\mathrm{d}\zeta\frac{\mathrm{d}\sigma}{\sigma}

\displaystyle=\begin{aligned} &\int_{\mathbb{R}^{2}_{+}}\mathfrak{d}_{\zeta}F_{1}^{\ast}(\zeta,\sigma)[\widetilde{\varphi}_{1}^{l}]\int_{0}^{\sigma}\beta\rho d_{\zeta}\left(\mathfrak{d}_{\zeta_{2}}\otimes\mathrm{Id}\right)F_{\times}^{\ast}(\zeta,\rho)[\widetilde{\varphi}_{2}^{o},\varphi_{3}^{o}]\frac{\mathrm{d}\rho}{\rho}\mathrm{d}\zeta\frac{\mathrm{d}\sigma}{\sigma}\\
&+\int_{\mathbb{R}^{2}_{+}}\mathfrak{d}_{\zeta}F_{1}^{\ast}(\zeta,\sigma)[\widetilde{\varphi}_{1}^{l}]\int_{0}^{\sigma}\beta\rho d_{\zeta}\left(\mathrm{Id}\otimes\mathfrak{d}_{\zeta_{3}}\right)F_{\times}^{\ast}(\zeta,\rho)[\varphi_{2}^{o},\widetilde{\varphi}_{3}^{o}]\frac{\mathrm{d}\rho}{\rho}\mathrm{d}\zeta\frac{\mathrm{d}\sigma}{\sigma}\\
&-\textrm{B1}[\widetilde{\varphi}_{1}^{l},\varphi_{2}^{o},\varphi_{3}^{o}]-\textrm{M1}[\widetilde{\varphi}_{1}^{l},\widetilde{\varphi}_{2}^{o},\varphi_{3}^{o}]-\textrm{M1}[\widetilde{\varphi}_{1}^{l},\varphi_{2}^{o},\widetilde{\varphi}_{3}^{o}]\\
&+\textrm{B2}_{\left(\mathfrak{d}_{\zeta}\otimes\mathrm{Id}\right)}[\widetilde{\varphi}_{1}^{l},\widetilde{\varphi}_{2}^{o},\varphi_{3}^{o}]+\textrm{B2}_{\left(\mathrm{Id}\otimes\mathfrak{d}_{\zeta}\right)}[\widetilde{\varphi}_{1}^{l},\varphi_{2}^{o},\widetilde{\varphi}_{3}^{o}].\end{aligned}

Integrating the first two summands on the
\mathrm{RHS}{}
by parts in
\zeta
gives

(4.59)

\displaystyle\int_{\mathbb{R}^{2}_{+}}\mathfrak{d}_{\zeta}F_{1}^{\ast}(\zeta,\sigma)[\widetilde{\varphi}_{1}^{l}]\int_{0}^{\sigma}\beta\rho d_{\zeta}\left(\mathfrak{d}_{\zeta_{2}}\otimes\mathrm{Id}\right)F_{\times}^{\ast}(\zeta,\rho)[\widetilde{\varphi}_{2}^{o},\varphi_{3}^{o}]\frac{\mathrm{d}\rho}{\rho}\mathrm{d}\zeta\frac{\mathrm{d}\sigma}{\sigma}

\displaystyle=-\beta\int_{\mathbb{R}^{2}_{+}}\sigma d_{\zeta}\mathfrak{d}_{\zeta}F_{1}^{\ast}(\zeta,\sigma)[\widetilde{\varphi}_{1}^{l}]\int_{0}^{\sigma}\frac{\rho}{\sigma}\left(\mathfrak{d}_{\zeta_{2}}\otimes\mathrm{Id}\right)F_{\times}^{\ast}(\zeta,\rho)[\widetilde{\varphi}_{2}^{o},\varphi_{3}^{o}]\frac{\mathrm{d}\rho}{\rho}\mathrm{d}\zeta\frac{\mathrm{d}\sigma}{\sigma}

\displaystyle=\begin{aligned} &-\beta\int_{\mathbb{R}^{2}_{+}}\mathfrak{d}_{\zeta}^{2}F_{1}^{\ast}(\zeta,\sigma)[\widetilde{\varphi}_{1}^{l}]\int_{0}^{\sigma}\frac{\rho}{\sigma}\left(\mathfrak{d}_{\zeta_{2}}\otimes\mathrm{Id}\right)F_{\times}^{\ast}(\zeta,\rho)[\widetilde{\varphi}_{2}^{o},\varphi_{3}^{o}]\frac{\mathrm{d}\rho}{\rho}\mathrm{d}\zeta\frac{\mathrm{d}\sigma}{\sigma}\\
&+\beta\int_{\mathbb{R}^{2}_{+}}\left(\mathfrak{d}_{\zeta}-\sigma d_{\zeta}\right)\mathfrak{d}_{\zeta}F_{1}^{\ast}(\zeta,\sigma)[\widetilde{\varphi}_{1}^{l}]\int_{0}^{\sigma}\frac{\rho}{\sigma}\left(\mathfrak{d}_{\zeta_{2}}\otimes\mathrm{Id}\right)F_{\times}^{\ast}(\zeta,\rho)[\widetilde{\varphi}_{2}^{o},\varphi_{3}^{o}]\frac{\mathrm{d}\rho}{\rho}\mathrm{d}\zeta\frac{\mathrm{d}\sigma}{\sigma}\end{aligned}

\displaystyle=-\textrm{M2}_{\left(\mathfrak{d}_{\zeta_{2}}\otimes\mathrm{Id}\right)}[\widetilde{\varphi}_{1}^{l},\widetilde{\varphi}_{2}^{o},\varphi_{3}^{o}]+\textrm{B3}_{\left(\mathfrak{d}_{\zeta_{2}}\otimes\mathrm{Id}\right)}[\widetilde{\varphi}_{1}^{l},\widetilde{\varphi}_{2}^{o},\varphi_{3}^{o}]

and

(4.60)

\displaystyle\int_{\mathbb{R}^{2}_{+}}\mathfrak{d}_{\zeta}F_{1}^{\ast}(\zeta,\sigma)[\widetilde{\varphi}_{1}^{l}]\int_{0}^{\sigma}\beta\rho d_{\zeta}\left(\mathfrak{d}_{\zeta_{2}}\otimes\mathrm{Id}\right)F_{\times}^{\ast}(\zeta,\rho)[\widetilde{\varphi}_{2}^{o},\varphi_{3}^{o}]\frac{\mathrm{d}\rho}{\rho}\mathrm{d}\zeta\frac{\mathrm{d}\sigma}{\sigma}

\displaystyle=-\textrm{M2}_{\left(\mathrm{Id}\otimes\mathfrak{d}_{\zeta}\right)}[\widetilde{\varphi}_{1}^{l},\varphi_{2}^{o},\widetilde{\varphi}_{3}^{o}]+\textrm{B3}_{\left(\mathrm{Id}\otimes\mathfrak{d}_{\zeta}\right)}[\widetilde{\varphi}_{1}^{l},\varphi_{2}^{o},\widetilde{\varphi}_{3}^{o}].

This concludes the proof of the decomposition
(
4.40
).
∎

5.
Forest estimates

In this section we prove bounds (
1.15
),
(
1.17
), and
(
1.21
). These bounds are proven for
general functions
F_{j}\in L^{\infty}(\mathbb{R}^{3}_{+})\otimes\Phi^{\infty}_{\mathfrak{r}}
in place of
\mathrm{Emb}[f_{j}](\eta,y,t)
specifically. The
results are generally obtained using interpolation using geometric
considerations on the support of the functions
F_{j}
and on properties of the
outer Lebesgue norms in play. The single tree estimates of

Section

4
serve as the
L^{\infty}
endpoint when proving bounds
(
1.17
), and
(
1.21
). Bound
(
1.15
), on the other hand relies on the correctness of

Definition

3.15
of the size
\mathbb{I}_{\Theta}
.

We begin this section by showing in
Proposition

5.1
that
(
1.15
) holds. The role of this estimate allows us to pass
from trying to bound the trilinear form on
\mathrm{LHS}{\eqref{eq:BHT-dual-bounds}}

directly to bounding appropriate outer Lebesgue norms of the integrand

(5.1)

\mathrm{Emb}[f_{1}](\eta,y,t)[\varphi_{0}]\prod_{j=2}^{3}\mathrm{Emb}[f_{j}]\circ\Gamma_{j}(\eta,y,t)[\varphi_{0}].

Subsequently, our goal is to prove bounds
(
1.17
) and
(
1.21
) that we do in

Proposition

5.4
. This relies first on proving a
non-iterated version of these bounds in
Lemma

5.2
. This is
just a straight-forward application of the outer Hölder inequality. Then we
deducing an atomic estimate for iterated outer Lebesgue spaces in

Lemma

5.3
. This is the more involved procedure and involves. Then

Proposition

5.4
follows by interpolation as a corollary of

Lemma

5.3
.

Proposition 5.1
.

Given any
\varphi_{0}\in\Phi^{N}_{\mathfrak{r}}
, the bound

(5.2)

\left|\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}_{+}}H(\eta,y,t)[\varphi_{0},\varphi_{0},\varphi_{0}]\mathrm{d}\eta\mathrm{d}y\mathrm{d}t\right|\lesssim\|\varphi_{0}\|_{\Phi^{N}_{\mathfrak{r}}}^{3}\|H(\eta,y,t)\|_{L^{1}_{\nu_{1}}\not{L}^{1}_{\mu^{1}_{\Theta},\nu_{1}}\mathbb{I}_{\Theta}\Phi_{\mathfrak{r}}^{N}}

holds for any compactly supported
H\in L^{\infty}(\mathbb{R}^{3}_{+})\otimes^{3}{\Phi^{\infty}_{\mathfrak{r}}}^{\prime}
. As a consequence, the bound
(
1.15
) holds for all functions
f_{j}\in\mathscr{S}(\mathbb{R}),j\in\{1,2,3\}
.

Proof.

Bound (
1.15
) follows from (
5.2
) by applying
it to
H=\mathbbm{1}_{\mathbb{K}_{n}}\prod_{j=1}^{3}\mathrm{Emb}[f_{j}]\circ\Gamma_{j}

where
\mathbb{K}_{n}\subset\mathbb{R}^{3}_{+}
are compact subsets as specified in
Remark
Remark

3.12
. Convergence follows by

Proposition

2.1
: the integral on the

\mathrm{LHS}{\eqref{eq:RN-domination-BHT}}
is absolutely convergent.

The bound (
5.2
) follows by a two-fold application of

Proposition

3.5
. At the first level we obtain that

(5.3)

\left|\int H(\eta,y,t)[\varphi_{0},\varphi_{0},\varphi_{0}]\mathrm{d}\eta\mathrm{d}y\mathrm{d}t\right|\lesssim\|H(\eta,y,t)\|_{L^{1}_{\mu^{1}_{\Theta}}\mathbb{I}_{\Theta}\Phi_{\mathfrak{r}}^{N}}

as long as for any fixed tree
T\in\mathbb{T}_{\Theta}
one has

(5.4)

\left|\int_{T}H(\eta,y,t)[\varphi_{0},\varphi_{0},\varphi_{0}]\mathrm{d}\eta\mathrm{d}y\mathrm{d}t\right|\lesssim_{N}\mu_{\Theta}^{1}(T)\|H(\eta,y,t)\|_{\mathbb{I}_{\Theta}\Phi_{\mathfrak{r}}^{N}}.

To show the latter bound it is sufficient to decompose each
\varphi_{j}=\varphi_{0}
into
\varphi_{j}=\varphi_{j,\theta}^{o}+\varphi_{j,\theta}^{l}
as described
in (
3.114
) to obtain that

(5.5)

H(\eta,y,t)[\varphi_{0},\varphi_{0},\varphi_{0}]=\sum_{(\star_{1},\star_{2},\star_{3})\in\{l,o\}^{3}}H(\eta,y,t)[\varphi_{1,\theta}^{\star_{1}},\varphi_{2,\theta}^{\star_{2}},\varphi_{3,\theta}^{\star_{3}}].

The terms with
(\star_{1},\star_{2},\star_{3})=(o,o,l)
and
(\star_{1},\star_{2},\star_{3})=(o,l,o)
vanish because if
\widehat{\varphi_{0}}(\theta_{\Gamma_{1}})\neq 0
then
\varphi_{j,\theta}^{o}(z)=0
for
j\in\{2,3\}
. This is because if
\theta_{\Gamma_{1}}=\theta\in B_{\mathfrak{r}}
then

\theta_{\Gamma_{2}}\in\alpha_{2}\beta_{2}\left(B_{\mathfrak{r}}+\gamma_{2}\right)=B_{\mathfrak{r}}-1
and
\theta_{\Gamma_{3}}\in\alpha_{3}\beta_{3}\left(B_{\mathfrak{r}}+\gamma_{3}\right)\in B_{(1+\beta)\mathfrak{r}}+1
; in particular

\theta_{\Gamma_{2}}\notin B_{\mathfrak{r}}
and
\theta_{\Gamma_{3}}\notin B_{\mathfrak{r}}
.
The claim then follows by directly integrating each term and recalling

Definition

3.15
of
\mathbb{I}_{\Theta}\Phi_{\mathfrak{r}}^{N}
.

From (
5.3
) it follows that that for any
V^{+}\in\mathbb{D}^{\cup}
it holds that

(5.6)

\displaystyle\left|\int\mathbbm{1}_{V^{+}}H(\eta,y,t)[\varphi_{0},\varphi_{0},\varphi_{0}]\mathrm{d}\eta\mathrm{d}y\mathrm{d}t\right|\lesssim\left\|\mathbbm{1}_{V^{+}}H(\eta,y,t)\right\|_{L^{1}_{\mu^{1}_{\Theta},\nu_{1}}\mathbb{I}_{\Theta}\Phi_{\mathfrak{r}}^{N}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\nu_{1}(V^{+})\left\|\mathbbm{1}_{V^{+}}H(\eta,y,t)\right\|_{\not{L}^{1}_{\mu^{1}_{\Theta},\nu_{1}}\mathbb{I}_{\Theta}\Phi_{\mathfrak{r}}^{N}}.

This allows us to apply
Proposition

3.5
again and obtain bound
(
5.2
).
∎

Lemma 5.2
.

The bound

(5.7)

\displaystyle\|F_{1}F_{2}F_{3}\|_{L^{q}_{\mu^{1}_{\Theta}}\left(\mathbb{I}^{(l,l,l)}_{\Theta}\Phi_{\mathfrak{r}}^{N}+\mathbb{I}^{(o,l,l)}_{\Theta}\Phi_{\mathfrak{r}}^{N}+\mathbb{I}^{(l,l,o)}_{\Theta}\Phi_{\mathfrak{r}}^{N}+\mathbb{I}^{(l,o,l)}_{\Theta}\Phi_{\mathfrak{r}}^{N}\right)}\qquad

\displaystyle\lesssim\|F_{1}\|_{L^{q_{1}}_{\mu^{1}_{\Theta}}\mathbb{F}_{\Theta}^{u_{1}}\Phi_{4\mathfrak{r}}^{N-3}}\|F_{2}\|_{L^{q_{2}}_{\mu^{1}_{\Theta}}\widetilde{\mathbb{F}}_{\Gamma_{2}}^{u_{2}}\Phi_{4\mathfrak{r}}^{N-3}}\|F_{3}\|_{L^{q_{3}}_{\mu^{1}_{\Theta}}\widetilde{\mathbb{F}}_{\Gamma_{3}}^{u_{3}}\Phi_{4\mathfrak{r}}^{N-3}}

holds for any three functions
F_{1},F_{2},F_{3}\in L^{\infty}_{\operatorname{loc}}(\mathbb{R}^{3}_{+}){\otimes\Phi_{4\mathfrak{r}}^{\infty}}^{\prime}
as long as
(q_{1},q_{2},q_{3})\in[1,\infty]^{3}
with
q^{-1}=\sum_{j=1}^{3}q_{j}^{-1}
and
as long as
(u_{1},u_{2},u_{3})\in[1,\infty]^{3}
are such that
\sum_{j=1}^{3}u_{j}^{-1}\leq 1
. Similarly, it holds that

(5.8)

\|F_{1}F_{2}F_{3}\|_{L^{q}_{\mu^{1}_{\Theta}}\mathbb{I}^{(l,o,o)}_{\Theta}\Phi_{\mathfrak{r}}^{N}}\lesssim\|F_{1}\|_{L^{q_{1}}_{\mu^{1}_{\Theta}}\mathbb{F}_{\Theta}^{u_{1}}\Phi_{4\mathfrak{r}}^{N-3}}\|F_{2}F_{3}\|_{\widetilde{\mathbb{F}}_{\Gamma_{\times}}^{u_{\times}}(\Phi\otimes\Phi)_{4\mathfrak{r}}^{N-3}}

as long as
(q_{1},q_{\times})\in[1,\infty]^{2}
with
q^{-1}=q_{1}^{-1}+q_{\times}^{-1}
and as long as
(u_{1},u_{\times})\in[1,\infty]^{2}
are
such that
u_{1}^{-1}+u_{\times}^{-1}\leq 1
. The implicit constants are
independent of
F_{1},F_{2},F_{3}
.

Proof.

The claim follows from
Theorem

2
by the outer Hölder inequality
(
Corollary

3.4
). To apply the outer Hölder notice that
the bound (
3.47
) corresponds to the bounds
(
1.25
) and (
1.26
) of
Theorem

2
. Bound
(
3.48
), on the other hand, follows directly from
the fact that
F_{1}F_{2}F_{3}
is a point-wise product: if
W_{j}\in\mathbb{T}^{\cup}_{\Theta}
are such that

(5.9)

\mathbbm{1}_{\mathbb{R}^{3}_{+}\setminus W_{j}}F_{j}=0

then

(5.10)

\mathbbm{1}_{\mathbb{R}^{3}_{+}\setminus W_{\cap}}F_{1}F_{2}F_{3}=0

where
W_{\cap}=W_{j_{0}}
for
j_{0}
such that
\mu^{1}_{\Theta}(W_{j_{0}})=\min_{j\in\{1,2,3\}}(\mu^{1}_{\Theta}(W_{j}))
.
∎

Lemma 5.3
.

Let
(q_{1},q_{2},q_{3})\in[1,\infty)^{3}
and let

(5.11)

q_{\times}\coloneqq(q_{2}^{-1}+q_{3}^{-1})^{-1},\qquad q\coloneqq(q_{1}^{-1}+q_{\times}^{-1})^{-1}=\Big(\sum_{j=1}^{3}q_{j}^{-1}\Big)^{-1}.

Then the bound

(5.12)

\displaystyle\|F_{1}F_{2}F_{3}\|_{L^{p}_{\mu_{\Theta}^{1}}\left(\mathbb{I}^{(l,l,l)}_{\Theta}\Phi_{\mathfrak{r}}^{N}+\mathbb{I}^{(o,l,l)}_{\Theta}\Phi_{\mathfrak{r}}^{N}+\mathbb{I}^{(l,l,o)}_{\Theta}\Phi_{\mathfrak{r}}^{N}+\mathbb{I}^{(l,o,l)}_{\Theta}\Phi_{\mathfrak{r}}^{N}\right)}

\displaystyle\lesssim\begin{aligned} &\nu_{1}(\|F_{1}\|_{\mathbb{F}_{\Theta}^{u_{1}}\Phi_{4\mathfrak{r}}^{N-3}}>0)^{\frac{1}{p_{1}}}\min_{j\in\{2,3\}}\Big(\nu_{\beta}(\|{F_{j}}\|_{\widetilde{\mathbb{F}}_{\Gamma_{j}}^{u_{j}}\Phi_{4\mathfrak{r}}^{N-3}}>0\Big)\Big)^{\frac{1}{p_{\times}}}\\
&\qquad\times\|F_{1}\|_{\not{L}^{q_{1},+}_{\mu_{\Theta}^{\infty},\nu_{1}}\mathbb{F}_{\Theta}^{u_{1}}\Phi_{4\mathfrak{r}}^{N-3}}\Big(\prod_{j=2}^{3}\|F_{j}\|_{X_{\mu_{\Theta}^{1},\nu_{\beta}}^{q_{j},r_{j},+}\widetilde{\mathbb{F}}_{\Gamma_{j}}^{u_{j}}\Phi_{4\mathfrak{r}}^{N-3}}\Big)\end{aligned}

holds for any
F_{j}\in L^{\infty}_{\operatorname{loc}}(\mathbb{R}^{3}_{+})\otimes\Phi_{4\mathfrak{r}}^{\infty}
as long as
(p_{1},p_{\times},p)\in{(q,+\infty]^{3}}
with
p^{-1}=p_{1}^{-1}+p_{\times}^{-1}
, as long as
r_{j}\in(0,q_{j}]
,
j\in\{2,3\}
, are such that
r_{2}^{-1}+r_{3}^{-1}\geq p_{\times}^{-1}
, and as long as
(u_{1},u_{2},u_{3})\in[1,\infty]^{3}

satisfy
\sum_{j=1}^{3}u_{j}^{-1}\leq 1
.

Similarly, let
(q_{1},q_{\times})\in[1,\infty)^{2}
and let
q\coloneqq(q_{1}^{-1}+q_{\times}^{-1})^{-1}
. The bound

(5.13)

\displaystyle\|F_{1}F_{2}F_{3}\|_{L^{p}_{\mu_{\Theta}^{1}}\mathbb{I}^{(l,o,o)}_{\Theta}\Phi^{N}_{\mathfrak{r}}}\lesssim\begin{aligned} &\nu_{1}(\|F_{1}\|_{\mathbb{F}_{\Gamma_{1}}^{u_{1}}\Phi_{4\mathfrak{r}}^{(N-3)}}>0)^{\frac{1}{p_{1}}}\nu_{\beta}(\|F_{2}F_{3}\|_{\widetilde{\mathbb{F}}^{u_{\times}}_{\Gamma_{2}\times\Gamma_{3}}\Phi_{4\mathfrak{r}}^{N-3}}>0)^{\frac{1}{p_{\times}}}\\
&\qquad\times\|F_{1}\|_{\not{L}^{q_{1}}_{\mu_{\Theta}^{\infty},\nu_{1}}\mathbb{F}_{\Theta}^{u_{1}}\Phi_{4\mathfrak{r}}^{N-3}}\|F_{2}F_{3}\|_{X_{\mu_{\Theta}^{1},\nu_{\beta}}^{q_{\times},r_{\times}}\widetilde{\mathbb{F}}^{u_{\times}}_{\Gamma_{2}\times\Gamma_{3}}\Phi_{4\mathfrak{r}}^{N-3}}\end{aligned}

holds for any
F_{j}\in L^{\infty}_{\operatorname{loc}}(\mathbb{R}^{3}_{+})\otimes\Phi_{4\mathfrak{r}}^{\infty}
as long as
(p_{1},p_{\times},p)\in{(q,+\infty]^{3}}
with
p^{-1}=p_{1}^{-1}+p_{\times}^{-1}
, as long as

r_{\times}\in(0,q_{\times}]
, is such that
r_{\times}\leq p_{\times}
,
and as long as
(u_{1},u_{\times})\in[1,\infty]^{3}
satisfy
u_{1}^{-1}+u_{\times}^{-1}\leq 1
.

Proof.

Proof of
Lemma

5.3

We first prove bound (
5.12
). The condition
r_{j}\leq q_{j}
for
j\in\{2,3\}
is required for the sizes
X_{\mu_{\Theta}^{1},\nu_{\beta}}^{q_{j},r_{j},+}\widetilde{\mathbb{F}}_{\Gamma_{j}}^{u_{j}}\Phi_{4\mathfrak{r}}^{N-3}
to even be defined. We may assume that all terms on

\mathrm{RHS}{\eqref{eq:atomic-trilinear}}
are finite; otherwise there is nothing
to prove. Let
V_{1}\in\mathbb{D}^{\cup}_{1}
and
V_{\times}\in\mathbb{D}^{\cup}_{\beta}
be such that
\mathbbm{1}_{\mathbb{R}^{3}_{+}\setminus V_{1}}F_{1}=0
,

\mathbbm{1}_{\mathbb{R}^{3}_{+}\setminus V_{\times}}F_{2}F_{3}=0
, and

(5.14)

\displaystyle\nu_{1}(V_{1})\leq 2\nu_{1}(\|F_{1}\|_{\mathbb{F}_{\Theta}^{u_{1}}\Phi_{4\mathfrak{r}}^{N-3}}>0),

\displaystyle\nu_{\beta}(V_{\times})\leq 2\min_{j\in\{2,3\}}\nu_{\beta}(\|F_{j}\|_{\widetilde{\mathbb{F}}_{\Gamma_{j}}^{u_{j}}\Phi_{4\mathfrak{r}}^{N-3}}>0).

Let us first reason under the assumption that
q<p_{\times}\leq q_{\times}
. Since
r_{2}^{-1}+r_{3}^{-1}\geq p_{\times}^{-1}\geq q_{\times}^{-1}
let us choose
p_{2}\in[r_{2},q_{2}]
and
p_{3}\in[r_{3},q_{2}]
such that
p_{\times}^{-1}=p_{2}^{-1}+p_{3}^{-1}
.

By homogeneity, we may assume that
\|F_{1}\|_{\not{L}^{q_{1},+}_{\mu_{\Theta}^{\infty}}\mathbb{F}_{\Theta}^{u_{1}}\Phi_{4\mathfrak{r}}^{N-3}}=1
and

\|F_{j}\|_{X_{\mu_{\Theta}^{1},\nu_{\beta}}^{q_{j},r_{j},+}\widetilde{\mathbb{F}}_{\Gamma_{j}}^{u_{j}}\Phi_{4\mathfrak{r}}^{N-3}}=1
for
j\in\{2,3\}
. Let us enact the atomic decomposition
(
Proposition

3.2
) of
F_{1}
to obtain a sequence of sets

W_{k}\in\mathbb{T}_{\Theta}^{\cup}
so that

(5.15)

\displaystyle\left\|\mathbbm{1}_{\Delta W_{k}}F_{1}\right\|_{\mathbb{F}_{\Theta}^{u_{1}}\Phi_{4\mathfrak{r}}^{N-3}}\lesssim 2^{\frac{k}{q_{1}}},\qquad\mu_{\Theta}^{\infty}(\Delta W_{k})\lesssim 2^{-k},\qquad\Delta W_{k}=W_{k-1}\setminus W_{k}.

Since
\|F_{1}\|_{\mathbb{F}_{\Theta}^{u_{1}}\Phi_{4\mathfrak{r}}^{N-3}}=\|F_{1}\|_{L^{\infty}_{\mu^{\infty}_{\Theta}}\mathbb{F}_{\Theta}^{u_{1}}\Phi_{4\mathfrak{r}}^{N-3}}\lesssim\|F_{1}\|_{\not{L}^{q_{1},+}_{\mu_{\Theta}^{\infty},\nu_{1}}\mathbb{F}_{\Theta}^{u_{1}}\Phi_{4\mathfrak{r}}^{N-3}}
it holds that
W_{k}=\emptyset

for
k\geq 1
. As a consequence we will only be concerned with
k\leq 1
.
Turning our attention to
F_{j}
, according to the definition of the size
\|\cdot\|_{X_{\mu_{\Theta}^{1},\nu_{\beta}}^{q_{j},r_{j},+}\widetilde{\mathbb{F}}_{\Gamma_{j}}^{u_{j}}\Phi_{4\mathfrak{r}}^{N-3}}
(given in
(
3.132
) and (
3.133
)) we have that

(5.16)

\left\|\mathbbm{1}_{V_{\times}}\mathbbm{1}_{\Delta W_{k}}F_{j}\right\|_{L^{\overline{r}_{j}}_{\mu^{1}_{\Theta}}\widetilde{\mathbb{F}}_{\Gamma_{j}}^{u_{j}}\Phi_{4\mathfrak{r}}^{N-3}}\lesssim\nu_{\beta}(V_{\times})^{\frac{1}{p_{j}}}\mu^{\infty}_{\Theta_{j}}(\Delta W_{k})^{\frac{1}{p_{j}}-\frac{1}{q_{j}}}

since
p_{j}\in[r_{j},q_{j}]
. Using
Lemma

5.2
we get that

(5.17)

\displaystyle\Big\|\mathbbm{1}_{V_{1}}\mathbbm{1}_{V_{\times}}\mathbbm{1}_{\Delta W_{k}}\prod_{j=1}^{3}F_{j}\Big\|{{}_{L^{\overline{r}}_{\mu_{\Theta}^{1}}\left(\mathbb{I}^{(l,l,l)}_{\Theta}\Phi_{\mathfrak{r}}^{N}+\mathbb{I}^{(o,l,l)}_{\Theta}\Phi_{\mathfrak{r}}^{N}+\mathbb{I}^{(l,l,o)}_{\Theta}\Phi_{\mathfrak{r}}^{N}+\mathbb{I}^{(l,o,l)}_{\Theta}\Phi_{\mathfrak{r}}^{N}\right)}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\left\|\mathbbm{1}_{V_{1}}\mathbbm{1}_{\Delta W_{k}}F_{1}\right\|_{L^{p_{1}}_{\mu^{1}_{\Theta}}\mathbb{F}_{\Theta}^{u_{1}}\Phi_{4\mathfrak{r}}^{N-3}}\prod_{j=1}^{3}\left\|\mathbbm{1}_{V_{\times}}\mathbbm{1}_{\Delta W_{k}}F_{j}\right\|_{L^{p_{j}}_{\mu^{1}_{\Theta}}\widetilde{\mathbb{F}}_{\Gamma_{j}}^{u_{j}}\Phi_{4\mathfrak{r}}^{N-3}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\nu_{1}(V_{1})^{\frac{1}{p_{1}}}\mu_{\Theta}^{\infty}(\Delta W_{k})^{\frac{1}{p_{1}}}\left\|\mathbbm{1}_{\Delta W_{k}}F_{1}\right\|_{\mathbb{F}_{\Theta}^{u_{1}}\Phi_{4\mathfrak{r}}^{N-3}}\prod_{j=2}^{3}\nu_{1}(V_{\times})^{\frac{1}{\overline{r}_{j}}}\mu^{\infty}_{\Theta_{j}}(\Delta W_{k+1})^{\frac{1}{p_{j}}-\frac{1}{q_{j}}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\nu_{1}(V_{1})^{\frac{1}{p_{1}}}\nu_{\beta}(V_{\times})^{\frac{1}{p_{2}}+\frac{1}{p_{3}}}2^{k\sum_{j=1}^{3}\frac{1}{q_{j}}-\frac{1}{p_{j}}}=\nu_{1}(V_{1})^{\frac{1}{p_{1}}}\nu_{\beta}(V_{\times})^{\frac{1}{p_{\times}}}2^{k\left(\frac{1}{q}-\frac{1}{p}\right)}.

Using the triangle inequality (
3.14
) we get that

(5.18)

\displaystyle{\displaystyle\Big\|\prod_{j=1}^{3}F_{j}\Big\|_{L^{p}_{\mu_{\Theta}^{1}}\left(\mathbb{I}^{(l,l,l)}_{\Theta}\Phi_{\mathfrak{r}}^{N}+\mathbb{I}^{(o,l,l)}_{\Theta}\Phi_{\mathfrak{r}}^{N}+\mathbb{I}^{(l,l,o)}_{\Theta}\Phi_{\mathfrak{r}}^{N}+\mathbb{I}^{(l,o,l)}_{\Theta}\Phi_{\mathfrak{r}}^{N}\right)}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\nu_{1}(V_{1})^{\frac{1}{p_{1}}}\nu_{\beta}(V_{\times})^{\frac{1}{p_{\times}}}\sum_{k=-\infty}^{1}k^{C_{\|\cdot\|}}2^{k\left(\frac{1}{q}-\frac{1}{p}\right)}\lesssim\nu_{1}(V_{1})^{\frac{1}{p_{1}}}\nu_{\beta}(V_{\times})^{\frac{1}{p_{\times}}},

as required. The sum is convergent because
\frac{1}{q}>\frac{1}{p}
.

Now let us reason under the assumption that
q_{\times}\leq p_{\times}
.
From the above proof we have obtained that

(5.19)

\displaystyle\|F_{1}F_{2}F_{3}\|_{L^{p}_{\mu_{\Theta}^{1}}\left(\mathbb{I}^{(l,l,l)}_{\Theta}\Phi_{\mathfrak{r}}^{N}+\mathbb{I}^{(o,l,l)}_{\Theta}\Phi_{\mathfrak{r}}^{N}+\mathbb{I}^{(l,l,o)}_{\Theta}\Phi_{\mathfrak{r}}^{N}+\mathbb{I}^{(l,o,l)}_{\Theta}\Phi_{\mathfrak{r}}^{N}\right)}

\displaystyle=\left\|\mathbbm{1}_{V_{1}\cap V_{\times}}F_{1}F_{2}F_{3}\right\|_{L^{p}_{\mu_{\Theta}^{1}}\left(\mathbb{I}^{(l,l,l)}_{\Theta}\Phi_{\mathfrak{r}}^{N}+\mathbb{I}^{(o,l,l)}_{\Theta}\Phi_{\mathfrak{r}}^{N}+\mathbb{I}^{(l,l,o)}_{\Theta}\Phi_{\mathfrak{r}}^{N}+\mathbb{I}^{(l,o,l)}_{\Theta}\Phi_{\mathfrak{r}}^{N}\right)}

\displaystyle\lesssim\nu_{1}(V_{1})^{\frac{1}{p_{1}}}\nu_{\beta}(V_{\times})^{\frac{1}{p_{\times}}}\|F_{1}\|_{\not{L}^{q_{1},+}_{\mu_{\Theta}^{\infty},\nu_{1}}\mathbb{F}_{\Theta}^{u_{1}}\Phi_{4\mathfrak{r}}^{N-3}}\left(\prod_{j=2}^{3}\|F_{j}\|_{X_{\mu_{\Theta}^{1},\nu_{\beta}}^{q_{j},q_{j},+}\widetilde{\mathbb{F}}_{\Gamma_{j}}^{u_{j}}\Phi_{4\mathfrak{r}}^{N-3}}\right)

for any
p_{1}\in(0,+\infty]
such that
p^{-1}=p_{1}^{-1}+q_{\times}^{-1}<q^{-1}
. We may assume that
V_{\times}\subseteq V_{1}

by carrying out the proof with
\widetilde{V}_{\times}=V_{\times}\cap V_{1}\in\mathbb{D}_{\beta}^{\cup}
in place of
V_{\times}
. It holds that
\nu_{\beta}(V_{1})\leq\nu_{1}(V_{1})
. Thus we obtain that for any
p_{\times}>q_{\times}
it holds that

(5.20)

\displaystyle\|F_{1}F_{2}F_{3}\|_{L^{p}_{\mu_{\Theta}^{1}}\left(\mathbb{I}^{(l,l,l)}_{\Theta}\Phi_{\mathfrak{r}}^{N}+\mathbb{I}^{(o,l,l)}_{\Theta}\Phi_{\mathfrak{r}}^{N}+\mathbb{I}^{(l,l,o)}_{\Theta}\Phi_{\mathfrak{r}}^{N}+\mathbb{I}^{(l,o,l)}_{\Theta}\Phi_{\mathfrak{r}}^{N}\right)}

\displaystyle\lesssim\nu_{1}(V_{1})^{\frac{1}{p}}\left(\frac{\nu_{\beta}(V_{\times}\cap V_{1})}{\nu_{\beta}(V_{1})}\right)^{\frac{1}{q_{\times}}}\|F_{1}\|_{\not{L}^{q_{1},+}_{\mu_{\Theta}^{\infty},\nu_{1}}\mathbb{F}_{\Theta}^{u_{1}}\Phi_{4\mathfrak{r}}^{N-3}}\left(\prod_{j=2}^{3}\|F_{j}\|_{X_{\mu_{\Theta}^{1},\nu_{\beta}}^{q_{j},q_{j},+}\widetilde{\mathbb{F}}_{\Gamma_{j}}^{u_{j}}\Phi_{4\mathfrak{r}}^{N-3}}\right)

\displaystyle\lesssim\nu_{1}(V_{1})^{\frac{1}{p}}\left(\frac{\nu_{\beta}(V_{\times}\cap V_{1})}{\nu_{\beta}(V_{1})}\right)^{\frac{1}{p_{\times}}}\|F_{1}\|_{\not{L}^{q_{1},+}_{\mu_{\Theta}^{\infty},\nu_{1}}\mathbb{F}_{\Theta}^{u_{1}}\Phi_{4\mathfrak{r}}^{N-3}}\left(\prod_{j=2}^{3}\|F_{j}\|_{X_{\mu_{\Theta}^{1},\nu_{\beta}}^{q_{j},q_{j},+}\widetilde{\mathbb{F}}_{\Gamma_{j}}^{u_{j}}\Phi_{4\mathfrak{r}}^{N-3}}\right)

\displaystyle\lesssim\nu_{1}(V_{1})^{\frac{1}{p}-\frac{1}{p_{\times}}}\nu_{\beta}(V_{\times}\cap V_{1})^{\frac{1}{p_{\times}}}\|F_{1}\|_{\not{L}^{q_{1},+}_{\mu_{\Theta}^{\infty},\nu_{1}}\mathbb{F}_{\Theta}^{u_{1}}\Phi_{4\mathfrak{r}}^{N-3}}\left(\prod_{j=2}^{3}\|F_{j}\|_{X_{\mu_{\Theta}^{1},\nu_{\beta}}^{q_{j},q_{j},+}\widetilde{\mathbb{F}}_{\Gamma_{j}}^{u_{j}}\Phi_{4\mathfrak{r}}^{N-3}}\right).

Thus we have shown the claim for any
p_{\times}>q_{\times}
and any
p_{1}\in(q,+\infty]
with the cited restrictions.

Bound (
5.13
) is proved analogously with the following
natural modifications. We require
r_{\times}\leq q_{\times}
for the size

\widetilde{\mathbb{F}}^{u_{\times}}_{\Gamma_{2}\times\Gamma_{3}}\Phi_{4\mathfrak{r}}^{N-3}
to be defined. By homogeneity we assume that
\|F_{2}F_{3}\|_{X_{\mu_{\Theta}^{1},\nu_{\beta}}^{q_{\times},r_{\times},+}\widetilde{\mathbb{F}}^{u_{\times}}_{\Gamma_{2}\times\Gamma_{3}}\Phi_{4\mathfrak{r}}^{N-3}}=1
. Assuming
p_{\times}\leq q_{\times}
and taking the sets
\Delta W_{k}
to be the same as before, we obtain that

(5.21)

\left\|\mathbbm{1}_{V_{\times}}\mathbbm{1}_{\Delta W_{k}}F_{2}F_{3}\right\|_{L^{p_{\times}}_{\mu^{1}_{\Theta}}\widetilde{\mathbb{F}}_{j}^{u_{j}}}\lesssim\nu_{\beta}(V_{\times})^{\frac{1}{p_{\times}}}\mu^{\infty}_{\Theta_{j}}(\Delta W_{k})^{\frac{1}{p_{\times}}-\frac{1}{q_{\times}}}

and we conclude that

(5.22)

{\left\|\mathbbm{1}_{V_{1}}\mathbbm{1}_{V_{\times}}\mathbbm{1}_{\Delta W_{k}}F_{1}F_{2}{F_{3}}_{j}\right\|_{L^{p}_{\mu_{\Theta}^{1}}\mathbb{I}^{(l,o,o)}_{\Theta}\Phi^{N}_{\mathfrak{r}}}}\lesssim\nu_{1}(V_{1})^{\frac{1}{p_{1}}}\nu_{\beta}(V_{\times})^{\frac{1}{p_{\times}}}2^{k\left(\frac{1}{q}-\frac{1}{p}\right)}.

Summing up in
k\in\mathbb{Z},k\leq 1
yields the claim. To address the case

p_{\times}>q_{\times}
the procedure is the same as above and relies on
noticing that one may assume
\nu_{\beta}(V_{\times})\leq\nu_{\beta}(V_{1})\leq\nu_{1}(V_{1})
.
∎

Proposition 5.4
.

Let
(q_{1},q_{2},q_{3})\in[1,\infty)^{3}

and let

(5.23)

q_{\times}\coloneqq(q_{2}^{-1}+q_{3}^{-1})^{-1},\qquad q\coloneqq(q_{1}^{-1}+q_{\times}^{-1})^{-1}=\Big(\sum_{j=1}^{3}q_{j}^{-1}\Big)^{-1},

with
q<1
. Then the bounds

(5.24)

\displaystyle\|F_{1}F_{2}F_{3}\|_{L^{1}_{\mu_{\Theta}^{1}}\left(\mathbb{I}^{(l,l,l)}_{\Theta}\Phi_{\mathfrak{r}}^{N}+\mathbb{I}^{(o,l,l)}_{\Theta}\Phi_{\mathfrak{r}}^{N}+\mathbb{I}^{(l,l,o)}_{\Theta}\Phi_{\mathfrak{r}}^{N}+\mathbb{I}^{(l,o,l)}_{\Theta}\Phi_{\mathfrak{r}}^{N}\right)}\qquad

\displaystyle\lesssim\|F_{1}\|_{L^{p_{1}}_{\nu_{1}}\not{L}^{q_{1},+}_{\mu_{\Theta}^{\infty},\nu_{1}}\mathbb{F}_{\Gamma_{1}}^{u_{1}}\Phi_{4\mathfrak{r}}^{N-3}}\Big(\prod_{j=2}^{3}\|F_{j}\|_{L^{p_{j}}_{\nu_{\beta}}X_{\mu_{\Theta}^{1},\nu_{\beta}}^{q_{j},r_{j},+}\widetilde{\mathbb{F}}_{\Gamma_{j}}^{u_{j}}\Phi_{4\mathfrak{r}}^{N-3}}\Big)

holds for any
F_{j}\in L^{\infty}_{\operatorname{loc}}(\mathbb{R}^{3}_{+})\otimes\Phi_{4\mathfrak{r}}^{\infty}
as long as
(p_{1},p_{2},p_{3})\in[1,\infty]^{3}
with

\sum_{j=1}^{3}p_{j}^{-1}=1
, as long as
(u_{1},u_{2},u_{3})\in[1,\infty]^{3}
with
\sum_{j=1}^{3}u_{j}^{-1}\leq 1
, as long as
r_{j}\in(0,q_{j}]
,
j\in\{2,3\}
, are such that
r_{2}^{-1}+r_{3}^{-1}\geq p_{2}^{-1}+p_{3}^{-1}
.

In particular, bound (
1.17
) holds
under the above assumptions on the exponents.

Similarly, the bound

(5.25)

\displaystyle\|F_{1}F_{2}F_{3}\|_{L^{1}_{\mu_{\Theta}^{1}}\mathbb{I}^{(l,o,o)}_{\Theta}\Phi^{N}_{\mathfrak{r}}}\lesssim\|F_{1}\|_{L^{p_{1}}_{\nu_{1}}\not{L}^{q_{1},+}_{\mu_{\Theta}^{\infty},\nu_{1}}\mathbb{F}_{\Theta}^{u_{1}}\Phi_{4\mathfrak{r}}^{N-3}}\|F_{2}F_{3}\|_{L^{p_{\times}}_{\nu_{\beta}}X_{\mu_{\Theta}^{1},\nu_{\beta}}^{q_{\times},r_{\times},+}\widetilde{\mathbb{F}}^{u_{\times}}_{\Gamma_{2}\times\Gamma_{3}}\Phi_{4\mathfrak{r}}^{N-3}}

holds for any
F_{j}\in L^{\infty}_{\operatorname{loc}}(\mathbb{R}^{3}_{+})\otimes\Phi_{4\mathfrak{r}}^{\infty}
as long as
(p_{1},p_{\times})\in[1,\infty]^{2}
with

p^{-1}\coloneqq p_{1}^{-1}+p_{\times}^{-1}
, as long as
r_{\times}\in[1,\infty]
and
(q_{1},q_{\times})\in[1,\infty]^{2}
are such that

(5.26)

r_{\times}<p_{\times}<q_{\times},

and

(5.27)

q_{1}^{-1}+q_{\times}^{-1}>1,

and as long as
(u_{1},u_{\times})\in[1,\infty]^{2}
with
u_{1}^{-1}+u_{\times}^{-1}\leq 1
.

In particular, bound (
1.21
) holds
under the above assumptions on the exponents.

Proof.

Proposition

3.3
can be applied to the bounds
(
5.12
) to yield bound
(
5.24
). As a matter of fact the conditions of

Lemma

5.3
are open, the condition
q<1
and
p_{1}^{-1}+p_{2}^{-1}+p_{3}^{-1}=1
imply that
p_{\times}^{-1}\coloneqq p_{2}^{-1}+p_{3}^{-1}

satisfies
p_{\times}^{-1}<q^{-1}
.

Similarly
Proposition

3.3
can be applied to the
bounds (
5.13
) to obtain bounds
(
5.25
).
∎

6.
Non-uniform embedding bounds

In this section we prove
Theorem

5
. We will
start by recording the following embedding bounds that have been shown in

[
AU20a
]
, the proof of which we will not
repeat here.

Theorem 6

(
[
AU20a
, Theorem 1.3]
)
.

The bounds

(6.1)

\left\|\mathrm{Emb}[f]\right\|_{L^{p}_{\nu_{1}}\not{L}^{q,+}_{\mu_{\Theta}^{1},\nu_{1}}\left(\mathbb{L}^{\infty,\infty}_{\Theta}\Phi_{\mathfrak{r}}^{N}+\mathbb{L}^{u,2}_{\Theta}\mathfrak{d}_{\mathfrak{r}}^{N}+{\mathbb{L}_{\Theta}^{(u,1)}}\Box_{\mathfrak{r}}^{N}\right)}\lesssim\|f\|_{L^{p}(\mathbb{R})}

hold for any
f\in\mathscr{S}(\mathbb{R})
as long as
p\in(1,\infty]
,

q\in(\max(p^{\prime},2),\infty)
, and
u\in(1,\infty)
.

The statement
[
AU20a
, Theorem 1.3]
covers
more general embeddings for functions valued in Banach spaces. For our
purposes, it is sufficient to apply it for scalar-valued functions. That
corresponds to setting
r=2
the statement of
[
AU20a
, Theorem
1.3]
. This corresponds to the case of
functions valued in Hilbert spaces.

Bounds (
1.19
) of

Theorem

5
are an improvement over

Theorem

6
in two ways. First of all, the
size
\mathbb{F}^{u}_{\Theta}\Phi_{\mathfrak{r}}^{N}
appearing in

Theorem

5
contains the term
\mathbb{J}_{\Theta}^{u}\Phi_{\mathfrak{r}}^{N}
, absent in the bounds of

Theorem

6
. Next, we show that the
embedding holds with localized outer Lebesgue norm
\not{L}^{q,+}_{\mu_{\Theta}^{\infty},\nu_{1}}
in place of
\not{L}^{q,+}_{\mu_{\Theta}^{1},\nu_{1}}
. According to
Lemma

3.18
, for
\beta=1

this is a strengthening of the result since we have that

(6.2)

\nu(V)^{-\frac{1}{q}}\left\|\mathbbm{1}_{V}F\right\|_{L^{q}_{\mu^{1}_{\Theta}}\mathbb{S}}\lesssim\left\|\mathbbm{1}_{V}F\right\|_{L^{q}_{\mu^{\infty}_{\Theta}}\mathbb{S}}

for any
F\in\mathscr{R}(\mathbb{R}^{3}_{+})\otimes\Phi^{\infty}_{\mathfrak{r}}
and, in
particular,

(6.3)

\|F\|_{\not{L}^{q,+}_{\mu_{\Theta}^{1},\nu_{1}}\mathbb{F}^{u}_{\Theta}\Phi^{N}_{\mathfrak{r}}}\lesssim\left\|\mathbbm{1}_{V}F\right\|_{L^{q,+}_{\mu^{\infty}_{\Theta},\nu_{1}}\mathbb{F}^{u}_{\Theta}\Phi^{N}_{\mathfrak{r}}}.

This strengthening costs us the
p=\infty
endpoint for the embedding.

Section

6.1
is dedicated to showing the bound

(6.4)

\left\|\mathrm{Emb}[f]\right\|_{L^{p}_{\nu_{1}}\not{L}^{q,+}_{\mu_{\Theta}^{1},\nu_{1}}\mathbb{F}_{\Theta}^{u}\Phi_{\mathfrak{r}}^{N}}\lesssim\|f\|_{L^{p}(\mathbb{R})}

by appropriately controlling the
\mathbb{J}_{\Theta}^{u}\Phi_{\mathfrak{r}}^{N}
size using
the sizes appearing on the
\mathrm{LHS}{}
of the bound in

Theorem

6
.

Section

6.2
is dedicated to deducing
bounds (
1.19
) of

Theorem

5
from
(
6.4
) by showing that
super-level sets of
\mathrm{Emb}[f]
can be selected to satisfy appropriate bounds
in the
\mu_{\Theta}^{\infty}
outer measure.

6.1.
SIO truncation size control

This section concentrates on proving the bound below..

Proposition 6.1
.

Given any sets
V^{+},V^{-}\in\mathbb{D}_{1}^{\cup}
, and
W^{+},W^{-}\in\mathbb{T}^{\cup}_{\Theta}
, the bounds

(6.5)

\displaystyle\left\|\mathbbm{1}_{(V^{+}\cap W^{+})\setminus\left(V^{-}{\cup W^{-}}\right)}\mathrm{Emb}[f]\right\|_{\mathbb{J}^{u}_{\Theta}\Phi_{\mathfrak{r}}^{N}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\begin{aligned} &\left\|\mathbbm{1}_{(V^{+}\cap W^{+})\setminus\left(V^{-}{\cup W^{-}}\right)}\mathrm{Emb}[f]\right\|_{\mathbb{L}^{(2,2)}_{\Theta}\mathfrak{d}_{8\mathfrak{r}}^{N-8}}\\
&\qquad+\left\|\mathbbm{1}_{(V^{+}\cap W^{+})\setminus\left(V^{-}{\cup W^{-}}\right)}\mathrm{Emb}[f]\right\|_{\mathbb{L}^{(\infty,\infty)}_{\Theta}\Phi_{8\mathfrak{r}}^{N-8}}\end{aligned}

hold for all
f\in\mathscr{S}(\mathbb{R})
as long as
u\in[1,\infty)
.
The implicit constant is independent of
f
and of the sets
V^{\pm},W^{\pm}
.

This is sufficient to show
(
6.4
).

Corollary 6.2
.

The bounds
(
6.4
) hold for all
f\in\mathscr{S}(\mathbb{R})
as long as
p\in(1,\infty]
,
q\in(\max(p^{\prime},2),\infty)
, and
u\in[1,\infty]
.

Proof.

According to Remark
Remark

3.19
,

Theorem

6
provides us with a choice of

V^{-}(\lambda)\in\mathbb{D}^{\cup}_{1}
and
W_{q}(\lambda,\tau,V^{+})\in\mathbb{T}_{\Theta}^{\cup}
satisfying (
3.143
) and
(
3.144
) with

(6.6)

\displaystyle F=\mathrm{Emb}[f],\qquad A=\|f\|_{L^{p}},\qquad\beta=1,

\displaystyle\mathbb{S}=\left(\mathbb{L}^{(\infty,\infty)}_{\Theta}\Phi_{8\mathfrak{r}}^{N-8}+\mathbb{L}^{(u,2)}_{\Theta}\mathfrak{d}_{8\mathfrak{r}}^{N-8}+{\mathbb{L}_{\Theta}^{(u,1)}}\Box_{8\mathfrak{r}}^{N-8}\right).

Bound (
6.5
) shows that this same choice also
satisfies conditions (
3.143
) and
(
3.144
) with
\mathbb{S}=\mathbb{F}_{\Theta}^{u}\Phi_{\mathfrak{r}}^{N}
, instead. Again, by Remark

Remark

3.19
, this is exactly what is claimed
by bound (
6.4
).
∎

Proof.

Proof of
Proposition

6.1

After some preliminary geometric reductions, this proof is divided into two
parts. First we reduce the claim of the proposition to the case
u=2
. We
do so by assuming that bound (
6.5
) holds with
u=2
for any function
f\in\mathscr{S}(\mathbb{R})
and any sets
V^{+},V^{-}\in\mathbb{D}_{1}^{\cup}
, and
W^{+},W^{-}\in\mathbb{T}^{\cup}_{\Theta}
, and then we
deduce that bound (
6.5
) also holds for any
u\in[1,+\infty)
. We then conclude the proof by showing bound
(
6.5
) holds with
u=2
.

We start by introducing some notation. Let us fix
T\in\mathbb{T}_{\Theta}
and
use the shorthand

(6.7)

F^{\ast}_{T}(\theta,\zeta,\sigma)\coloneqq\left(\mathbbm{1}_{(V^{+}\cap W^{+})\setminus\left(V^{-}{\cup W^{-}}\right)}\mathrm{Emb}[f]\right)\circ\pi_{T}(\theta,\zeta,\sigma).

Let
E^{\ast+}\coloneqq\pi_{T}^{-1}\left(\mathfrak{T}_{\Theta}\cap V^{+}\cap W^{+}\right)
and
E^{\ast-}\coloneqq\pi_{T}^{-1}(V^{-}\cup W^{-})
and let
E^{\ast}\coloneqq E^{\ast+}\setminus E^{\ast-}
. Let
\mathfrak{b}_{\pm}:\mathbb{R}^{2}\rightarrow\mathbb{R}_{+}
be two functions such that their graphs are the boundary of
E^{\pm}

and let
\mathfrak{b^{\ast}_{\pm}}:\Theta\times B_{1}\rightarrow\mathbb{R}_{+}
be two
functions such that their graphs are the boundaries of
E^{\ast\pm}\cap\Theta\times B_{1}\times\mathbb{R}_{+}
. These functions are obtained from

Lemma

3.20
. Let

(6.8)

\displaystyle\widetilde{\mathfrak{b}}^{\ast}_{-}(\zeta)=\max_{\theta\in\Theta}\mathfrak{b}^{\ast}_{-}(\zeta,\theta)\qquad\widetilde{\mathfrak{b}}^{\ast}_{+}(\zeta)=\min_{\theta\in\Theta}\mathfrak{b}^{\ast}_{+}(\zeta,\theta).

Let us begin by showing that for any
T\in\mathbb{T}_{\Theta}
it holds that

(6.9)

\displaystyle\left\|\mathbbm{1}_{(V^{+}\cap W^{+})\setminus\left(V^{-}{\cup W^{-}}\right)}\mathrm{Emb}[f]\right\|_{\mathbb{J}^{u}_{\Theta}\Phi_{\mathfrak{r}}^{N}(T)}

\displaystyle\lesssim\begin{aligned} &\sup_{\varphi}\Big\|\sup_{\sigma>\widetilde{\mathfrak{b}}^{\ast}_{-}(\zeta)}\Big|\int_{\sigma}^{\widetilde{\mathfrak{b}}^{\ast}_{+}(\zeta)}\mathfrak{d}_{\zeta}(\theta)F^{\ast}(\theta,\zeta,\rho)[\varphi]\frac{\mathrm{d}\rho}{\rho}\Big|\Big\|_{L^{u}_{\frac{\mathrm{d}\theta\mathrm{d}\zeta}{|\Theta|}}(\Theta\times B_{1})}\\
&+\left\|\mathbbm{1}_{(V^{+}\cap W^{+})\setminus\left(V^{-}{\cup W^{-}}\right)}\mathrm{Emb}[f]\right\|_{\mathbb{L}{}^{(\infty,\infty)}_{\Theta}\Phi_{\mathfrak{r}}^{N}(T)}.\end{aligned}

As a matter of fact it holds that

(6.10)

\displaystyle\left\|\mathbbm{1}_{(V^{+}\cap W^{+})\setminus\left(V^{-}{\cup W^{-}}\right)}\mathrm{Emb}[f]\right\|_{\mathbb{J}^{u}_{\Theta}\Phi_{\mathfrak{r}}^{N}(T)}

\displaystyle\lesssim\begin{aligned} &\sup_{\varphi}\Big\|\sup_{\sigma>\widetilde{\mathfrak{b}}^{\ast}_{-}(\zeta)}\Big|\int_{\sigma}^{\widetilde{\mathfrak{b}}^{\ast}_{+}(\zeta)}\mathfrak{d}_{\zeta}(\theta)F^{\ast}(\theta,\zeta,\rho)[\varphi]\frac{\mathrm{d}\rho}{\rho}\Big|\Big\|_{L^{u}_{\frac{\mathrm{d}\theta\mathrm{d}\zeta}{|\Theta|}}(\Theta\times B_{1})}\quad\\
&\qquad+\begin{aligned} &\Big\|\Big|\log\Big(\frac{\mathfrak{b}^{\ast}_{-}(\zeta,\theta)}{\widetilde{\mathfrak{b}}^{\ast}_{-}(\zeta)}\Big)\Big|+\Big|\log\Big(\frac{\mathfrak{b}^{\ast}_{+}(\zeta,\theta)}{\widetilde{\mathfrak{b}}^{\ast}_{+}(\zeta)}\Big)\Big|\Big\|_{L^{u}_{\frac{\mathrm{d}\theta\mathrm{d}\zeta}{|\Theta|}}(\Theta\times B_{1})}\\
&\times\sup_{\varphi}\Big\|\mathfrak{d}_{\zeta}(\theta)F^{\ast}(\theta,\zeta,\rho)[\varphi]\Big\|_{L^{\infty}_{\frac{\mathrm{d}\theta\mathrm{d}\zeta}{|\Theta|}\frac{\mathrm{d}\rho}{\rho}}}\end{aligned}\end{aligned}

where the upper bound is taken over
\varphi\in\Phi_{\mathfrak{r}}^{\infty}
with
\|\varphi\|_{\Phi_{\mathfrak{r}}^{N}}\leq 1
. We assume that the integrand vanishes when

\widetilde{\mathfrak{b}}^{\ast}_{+}(\zeta)\leq\widetilde{\mathfrak{b}}^{\ast}_{-}(\zeta)
. Since
\|(-d_{z}+2\pi i\theta)\varphi(z)\|_{\Phi_{\mathfrak{r}}^{N}}\leq\|\varphi\|_{\Phi_{\mathfrak{r}}^{N}}
, we have that

(6.11)

\operatorname{ssup}_{\varphi}\left\|\mathfrak{d}_{\zeta}(\theta)F^{\ast}(\theta,\zeta,\rho)[\varphi]\right\|_{L^{\infty}_{\frac{\mathrm{d}\theta\mathrm{d}\zeta}{|\Theta|}\frac{\mathrm{d}\rho}{\rho}}}\lesssim\left\|\mathbbm{1}_{(V^{+}\cap W^{+})\setminus\left(V^{-}{\cup W^{-}}\right)}\mathrm{Emb}[f]\right\|_{\mathbb{L}^{(\infty,\infty)}_{\Theta}\Phi_{\mathfrak{r}}^{N}}.

On the other hand, let
\overline{\theta}
be the point where
