\max_{\theta\in\Theta}\mathfrak{b}^{\ast}_{-}(\zeta,\theta)
is attained. From
(
3.153
) we get for
\theta>\overline{\theta}
that

(6.12)

1\geq\frac{\mathfrak{b}^{\ast}(\theta,\zeta)}{\widetilde{\mathfrak{b}}^{\ast}_{-}(\zeta)}\geq\frac{\theta_{+}-\theta}{\theta_{+}-\overline{\theta}}

and for
\theta<\overline{\theta}
that

(6.13)

1\geq\frac{\mathfrak{b}^{\ast}(\theta,\zeta)}{\widetilde{\mathfrak{b}}^{\ast}_{-}(\zeta)}\geq\frac{\theta-\theta_{-}}{\overline{\theta}-\theta_{-}}

where we denote
\Theta=(\theta_{-},\theta_{+})
. This shows that

(6.14)

\Big\|\log\Big(\frac{\mathfrak{b}^{\ast}_{-}(\zeta,\theta)}{\widetilde{\mathfrak{b}}^{\ast}_{-}(\zeta)}\Big)\Big\|_{L^{u}_{\frac{\mathrm{d}\theta\mathrm{d}\zeta}{|\Theta|}}(\Theta\times B_{1})}+\Big\|\log\Big(\frac{\mathfrak{b}^{\ast}_{+}(\zeta,\theta)}{\widetilde{\mathfrak{b}}^{\ast}_{+}(\zeta)}\Big)\Big\|_{L^{u}_{\frac{\mathrm{d}\theta\mathrm{d}\zeta}{|\Theta|}}(\Theta\times B_{1})}\lesssim 1.

Reduction to the case
u=2
:

Recall that we assume bound (
6.5
) holds with
u=2
. For
u<2
, bound (
6.5
) follows by the
classical Hölder inequality that shows that for any
T\in\mathbb{T}_{\Theta}

one has

(6.15)

\left\|\mathbbm{1}_{(V^{+}\cap W^{+})\setminus\left(V^{-}{\cup W^{-}}\right)}\mathrm{Emb}[f]\right\|_{\mathbb{J}^{u}_{\Theta}\Phi_{\mathfrak{r}}^{N}(T)}\lesssim\left\|\mathbbm{1}_{(V^{+}\cap W^{+})\setminus\left(V^{-}{\cup W^{-}}\right)}\mathrm{Emb}[f]\right\|_{\mathbb{J}^{2}_{\Theta}\Phi_{\mathfrak{r}}^{N}(T)}.

We henceforth fix
u\in(2,+\infty)
. The subsequent proof relies mainly
on the fact that the size over trees is a
\operatorname{BMO}
-style quantity and
thus any two integrability exponents
u_{1},u_{2}
can be compared. We adopt a
procedure inspired by the proof of the John-Nirenberg inequality: we prove
the reduction by induction on the scales of the trees
T\in\mathbb{T}_{\Theta}

in play. According to (
6.9
) we need to show that for any

T\in\mathbb{T}_{\Theta}
it holds that

(6.16)

\displaystyle\sup_{\varphi}\left\|\sup_{\sigma>\widetilde{\mathfrak{b}}^{\ast}_{-}(\zeta)}\left|\int_{\sigma}^{\widetilde{\mathfrak{b}}^{\ast}_{+}(\zeta)}\mathfrak{d}_{\zeta}(\theta)F^{\ast}(\theta,\zeta,\rho)[\varphi]\frac{\mathrm{d}\rho}{\rho}\right|\right\|_{L^{u}_{\frac{\mathrm{d}\theta\mathrm{d}\zeta}{|\Theta|}}(\Theta\times B_{1})}\lesssim\mathrm{RHS}{}\eqref{prop:SIO-size-domination}

Let us begin by showing that for any
T\in\mathbb{T}_{\Theta}
and for any
\varphi\in\Phi_{\mathfrak{r}}^{\infty}
with
\|\varphi\|_{\Phi_{\mathfrak{r}}^{N}}\leq 1
it holds
that

(6.17)

\displaystyle\left\|\sup_{\sigma>\widetilde{\mathfrak{b}}^{\ast}_{-}(\zeta)}\left|\int_{\sigma}^{\widetilde{\mathfrak{b}}^{\ast}_{+}(\zeta)}\mathfrak{d}_{\zeta}(\theta)F^{\ast}(\theta,\zeta,\rho)[\varphi]\frac{\mathrm{d}\rho}{\rho}\right|\right\|_{L^{u}_{\frac{\mathrm{d}\theta\mathrm{d}\zeta}{|\Theta|}}(\Theta\times B_{1})}

\displaystyle\lesssim\sup_{\varphi^{\prime}}\left\|\left(\int_{\Theta}\sup_{\sigma>\widetilde{\mathfrak{b}}^{\ast}_{-}(\zeta)}\left|\int_{\sigma}^{\widetilde{\mathfrak{b}}^{\ast}_{+}(\zeta)}\mathfrak{d}_{\zeta}(\theta)F^{\ast}(\theta,\zeta,\rho)[\varphi]\frac{\mathrm{d}\rho}{\rho}\right|^{2}\frac{\mathrm{d}\theta}{|\Theta|}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}\right\|_{L^{u}(B_{1})}

where the upper bound gets taken over
\varphi^{\prime}\in\Phi_{4\mathfrak{r}}^{\infty}
with

\|\varphi^{\prime}\|_{\Phi_{4\mathfrak{r}}^{N-4}}\leq 1
. This amounts to reducing the
exponent of the integral in the frequency variable
\theta
. According to
(
1.12
) (that defines
\mathrm{Emb}
), to (
3.74
)
(that defines
\mathfrak{d}_{\zeta}(\theta)
), and to the symmetry property
(
3.65
), we have that

(6.18)

\mathfrak{d}_{\zeta}(\theta)F^{\ast}(\theta,\zeta,\rho)[\varphi]=e^{2\pi i\xi_{T}s_{T}\zeta}\rho\mathrm{d}_{\zeta}\left(f_{T}\ast\mathrm{Dil}_{\rho}\mathrm{Mod}_{\theta}\varphi^{\vee}(\zeta)\right)

with
f_{T}=s_{T}\mathrm{Dil}_{s_{T}^{-1}}\mathrm{Mod}_{-\xi_{T}}\mathrm{Tr}_{-x_{T}}f
. This
allows us to write that

(6.19)

\mathfrak{d}_{\zeta}(\theta)F^{\ast}(\theta,\zeta,\rho)[\varphi]=\mathfrak{d}_{\zeta}(\theta^{\prime})F^{\ast}(\theta^{\prime},\zeta,\rho)\left[\mathrm{Mod}_{(\theta-\theta^{\prime})}\varphi\right]

for any
\theta^{\prime}\in B_{\mathfrak{r}}(\theta)
with
\widetilde{\mathfrak{b}}^{\ast}_{-}(\zeta)<\theta^{\prime}<\widetilde{\mathfrak{b}}^{\ast}_{+}(\zeta)
since then

(\theta^{\prime},\zeta,\sigma)\in E^{\ast}
. Note also that
\mathrm{Mod}_{(\theta-\theta^{\prime})}\varphi\in\Phi_{2\mathfrak{r}}^{\infty}
with
\left\|\mathrm{Mod}_{(\theta-\theta^{\prime})}\varphi\right\|_{\Phi_{\mathfrak{r}}^{N}}\leq\|\varphi\|_{\Phi_{2\mathfrak{r}}^{N}}
. We
thus have that

(6.20)

\displaystyle\sup_{\sigma\in\mathbb{R}_{+}}\left|\int_{\sigma}^{\widetilde{\mathfrak{b}}^{\ast}_{+}(\zeta)}\mathfrak{d}_{\zeta}(\theta)F^{\ast}(\theta,\zeta,\rho)[\varphi]\frac{\mathrm{d}\rho}{\rho}\right|\leq

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\mathfrak{r}}\int_{B_{\mathfrak{r}}(\theta)\cap\Theta}\sup_{\sigma\in\mathbb{R}_{+}}\left|\int_{\sigma}^{\widetilde{\mathfrak{b}}^{\ast}_{+}(\zeta)}\mathfrak{d}_{\zeta}(\theta^{\prime})F^{\ast}(\theta^{\prime},\zeta,\rho)\left[\mathrm{Mod}_{(\theta-\theta^{\prime})}\varphi\right]\frac{\mathrm{d}\rho}{\rho}\right|\mathrm{d}\theta^{\prime}.

Note that
\mathfrak{r}<\left|B_{\mathfrak{r}}(\theta)\cap\Theta\right|<2\mathfrak{r}
. Using

Lemma

3.13
we decompose
\mathrm{Mod}_{(\theta-\theta^{\prime})}\varphi\in\Phi_{2\mathfrak{r}}^{N}
into an absolutely convergent sum:

(6.21)

\mathrm{Mod}_{(\theta-\theta^{\prime})}\varphi=\sum_{k\in\mathbb{Z}}a_{k}(\theta,\theta^{\prime},\varphi)\varphi_{k}^{\prime}.

with
\|\varphi_{k}^{\prime}\|_{\Phi_{4\mathfrak{r}}^{N-2}}\leq 1
with uniformly summable
coefficients. This shows that

(6.22)

\displaystyle\left\|\sup_{\sigma\in\mathbb{R}_{+}}\left|\int_{\sigma}^{\widetilde{\mathfrak{b}}^{\ast}_{+}(\zeta)}\mathfrak{d}_{\zeta}(\theta)F^{\ast}(\theta,\zeta,\rho)[\varphi]\frac{\mathrm{d}\rho}{\rho}\right|\right\|_{L^{u}_{\frac{\mathrm{d}\theta\mathrm{d}\zeta}{|\Theta|}}(\Theta\times B_{1})}

\displaystyle\lesssim\sup_{\varphi^{\prime}}\int_{\Theta\times B_{1}}\left(\frac{1}{\mathfrak{r}}\int_{B_{\mathfrak{r}}(\theta)\cap\Theta}\sup_{\sigma\in\mathbb{R}_{+}}\left|\int_{\sigma}^{\widetilde{\mathfrak{b}}^{\ast}_{+}(\zeta)}\mathfrak{d}_{\zeta}(\theta^{\prime})F^{\ast}(\theta^{\prime},\zeta,\rho)[\varphi^{\prime}]\frac{\mathrm{d}\rho}{\rho}\right|^{2}\mathrm{d}\theta^{\prime}\right)^{\frac{u}{2}}\mathrm{d}\zeta\frac{\mathrm{d}\theta}{|\Theta|}

\displaystyle\lesssim\sup_{\varphi^{\prime}}\left\|\left(\int_{\Theta}\sup_{\sigma\in\mathbb{R}_{+}}\left|\int_{\sigma}^{\mathfrak{\widetilde{b}}^{\ast}_{+}(\zeta)}\mathfrak{d}_{\zeta}(\theta)F^{\ast}(\theta^{\prime},\zeta,\rho)[\varphi^{\prime}]\frac{\mathrm{d}\rho}{\rho}\right|^{2}\frac{\mathrm{d}\theta^{\prime}}{|\Theta|}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}\right\|_{L^{u}(B_{1})}

with the upper bounds being taken over
\varphi^{\prime}\in\Phi^{\infty}_{\mathfrak{r}}
with

\|\varphi^{\prime}\|_{\Phi_{4\mathfrak{r}}^{N-2}}\leq 1
. This shows that bound
(
6.17
) holds.

With bound (
6.17
) in hand‚ to deduce bound
(
6.5
) we now need to show that for every
T\in\mathbb{T}_{\Theta}
it holds that

(6.23)

\displaystyle\sup_{\varphi}\left\|\left(\int_{\Theta}\sup_{\sigma\in\mathbb{R}_{+}}\left|\int_{\sigma}^{\widetilde{\mathfrak{b}}^{\ast}_{+}(\zeta)}\mathfrak{d}_{\zeta}(\theta)F^{\ast}(\theta,\zeta,\rho)[\varphi]\frac{\mathrm{d}\rho}{\rho}\right|^{2}\frac{\mathrm{d}\theta}{|\Theta|}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}\right\|_{L^{u}(B_{1})}

\displaystyle\lesssim\sup_{\text{\scriptsize{$\begin{aligned} &T^{\prime}\in\mathbb{T}_{\Theta}\\
&s_{T^{\prime}}\leq s_{T}\end{aligned}$}}}\begin{aligned} &\left(\left\|\mathbbm{1}_{(V^{+}\cap W^{+})\setminus\left(V^{-}{\cup W^{-}}\right)}\mathrm{Emb}[f]\right\|_{\mathbb{L}^{(2,2)}_{\Theta}\mathfrak{d}_{8\mathfrak{r}}^{N-8}(T^{\prime})}\right.\qquad\\
&\qquad+\left.\left\|\mathbbm{1}_{(V^{+}\cap W^{+})\setminus\left(V^{-}{\cup W^{-}}\right)}\mathrm{Emb}[f]\right\|_{\mathbb{L}^{(\infty,\infty)}_{\Theta}\Phi_{8\mathfrak{r}}^{N-8}(T^{\prime})}\right)\end{aligned}

where the upper bound is taken over
\varphi\in\Phi_{4\mathfrak{r}}^{\infty}
with

\|\varphi\|_{\Phi_{4\mathfrak{r}}^{N-4}}\leq 1
. We do an induction on scales
argument. Suppose we have shown that
(
6.23
)
holds with
implicit constant
C_{\ast}
for fixed
V^{+}{,V^{-}}
, for any
\widetilde{W}^{+},\widetilde{W}^{-}\in\mathbb{T}_{\Theta}^{\cup}
, and for all
T\in\mathbb{T}_{\Theta}
with
s_{T}<S
for some
S>0
. Let us show that it holds for
those same
V^{+},V^{-},
and any
W^{+},W^{-}\in\mathbb{T}_{\Theta}^{\cup}
,
and all
T\in\mathbb{T}_{\Theta}
with
s_{T}<C_{\operatorname{ind}}S
for some

C_{\operatorname{ind}}>1
. This is sufficient to conclude that

(
6.23
)
holds for all
T\in\mathbb{T}_{\Theta}
. As a matter
of fact, first check that it holds for all
V^{-}\supset\{(\eta,y,t):t<\varepsilon\}
for a fixed
\varepsilon>0
because
(
6.23
) holds trivially for
s_{T}<S<\varepsilon
and
thus, by induction,
(
6.23
)
holds for all
T\in\mathbb{T}_{\Theta}
. Since the implicit constant in (
6.23
) is
independent of
\varepsilon>0
and
\mathrm{RHS}{\eqref{eq:tree-SIO-size}}
then
we can pass to the limit and conclude that (
6.23
) holds
for any
V^{+},V^{-}
, as required.

We will fix the constants
C_{\ast}>1
and
C_{\operatorname{ind}}>1
at the end
of this discussion. By symmetry (identity (
3.94
) and
subsequent discussion), we can suppose, without loss of generality, that
T=T_{\Theta}(0,0,1)
and
S>C_{\operatorname{ind}}^{-1}
. Consider the
function

(6.24)

\mathfrak{F}(\zeta)\coloneqq\left(\int_{\Theta}\sup_{\sigma\in\mathbb{R}_{+}}\left|\int_{\sigma}^{\widetilde{\mathfrak{b}}^{\ast}_{+}(\zeta)}F^{\ast}(\theta,\zeta,\rho)[\varphi_{\theta}]\frac{\mathrm{d}\rho}{\rho}\right|^{2}\frac{\mathrm{d}\theta}{|\Theta|}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}

with some fixed
\varphi\in\Phi_{4\mathfrak{r}}^{\infty}
with
\|\varphi\|_{\Phi_{4\mathfrak{r}}^{N-4}}\leq 1
and with
\varphi_{\theta}(z)\coloneqq(-d_{z}+2\pi i\theta)\varphi(z)
. We need to show that

(6.25)

\int_{B_{1}}\mathfrak{F}(\zeta)^{u}\mathrm{d}\zeta\leq C_{\ast}\mathrm{RHS}{\eqref{eq:tree-SIO-size}}^{u}.

We separate
B_{1}
into a part where
\mathfrak{F}(\zeta)
is bounded and an
exceptional set

(6.26)

E\coloneqq\left\{{}\zeta:M\mathfrak{F}(\zeta)\geq C_{2}\left\|\mathfrak{\mathfrak{F}}(\zeta)\right\|_{L^{2}(B_{1})}\right\}

for some
C_{2}\gg 1
to be chosen later.
E
is open and we can represent
E=\bigcup_{n\in\mathbb{N}}B_{s_{n}}(x_{n})
with pairwise disjoint
B_{s_{n}}(x_{n})
.
Let us set
\mathfrak{\widetilde{b}}_{+,n}^{\ast}(\zeta)\coloneqq 10s_{n}-|\zeta-x_{n}|
so that
9s_{n}<\mathfrak{\widetilde{b}}_{+,n}(\zeta){\leq 10s_{n}}
on

B_{s_{n}}(x_{n})
. By the boundedness of the Hardy Littlewood maximal function
it holds that
\sum_{n\in\mathbb{N}}2s_{n}\leq C_{M}C_{2}^{-1}.
We can now write
that

(6.27)

\mathfrak{F}(\zeta)^{u}\leq\mathbbm{1}_{B_{1}\setminus E}(\zeta)\mathfrak{F}(\zeta)^{u}+2^{u}\sum_{n\in\mathbb{N}}\left(\mathfrak{F}^{\uparrow}_{n}(\zeta)^{u}+\mathfrak{F}^{\downarrow}_{n}(\zeta)^{u}\right)

where

(6.28)

\displaystyle\mathfrak{F}^{\uparrow}_{n}(\zeta)\coloneqq\mathbbm{1}_{B_{s_{n}}(x_{n})}(\zeta)\left(\int_{\Theta}\sup_{\sigma>s_{n}}\left|\int_{\sigma}^{\widetilde{\mathfrak{b}}^{\ast}_{+}(\zeta)}F^{\ast}(\theta,\zeta,\rho)[\varphi_{\theta}]\frac{\mathrm{d}\rho}{\rho}\right|^{2}\frac{\mathrm{d}\theta}{|\Theta|}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}},

\displaystyle\mathfrak{F}^{\downarrow}_{n}(\zeta)\coloneqq\mathbbm{1}_{B_{s_{n}}(x_{n})}(\zeta)\left(\int_{\Theta}\sup_{\sigma<\mathfrak{\widetilde{b}}_{+,n}^{\ast}(\zeta)}\left|\int_{\sigma}^{\mathfrak{\widetilde{b}}_{+,n}^{\ast}(\zeta)}F^{\ast}(\theta,\zeta,\rho)[\varphi_{\theta}]\frac{\mathrm{d}\rho}{\rho}\right|^{2}\frac{\mathrm{d}\theta}{|\Theta|}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}.

We will prove that

(6.29)

\int_{B_{1}}\mathfrak{F}(\zeta)^{u}\mathrm{d}\zeta\leq(1+2^{u})(C_{2}C_{u=2}+C_{M}C_{5}+C_{M}C_{2}^{-1}C_{\ast})\mathrm{RHS}{\eqref{eq:tree-SIO-size}}^{u}

that will allow us to conclude that

(6.30)

\int_{B_{1}}\mathfrak{F}(\zeta)^{u}\mathrm{d}\zeta\leq C_{\ast}\mathrm{RHS}{\eqref{eq:tree-SIO-size}}^{u},

as required, as long as
C_{2}
is chosen to be satisfy
2(1+2^{u})<C_{2}

and
C_{\ast}
satisfies
2(1+2^{u})(C_{2}C_{u=2}+C_{M}C_{5})\leq C_{\ast}
. Let us now show that (
6.29
) by bounding
integrals of terms in (
6.27
).

The term
\mathbbm{1}_{B_{1}\setminus E}(\zeta)\mathfrak{F}(\zeta)^{u}
on

\mathrm{RHS}{\eqref{eq:SIO:stopping-time-decomp}}
can be bound since
M\mathfrak{F}(\zeta)\geq\mathfrak{F}(\zeta)
; thus

(6.31)

\int_{B_{1}\setminus E}\mathfrak{F}(\zeta)^{u}\mathrm{d}\zeta\leq C_{2}\left\|\mathfrak{\mathfrak{F}}(\zeta)\right\|_{L^{2}(B_{1})}\leq C_{2}C_{u=2}\mathrm{RHS}{\eqref{eq:tree-SIO-size}}^{u}

for some
C_{u=2}>0
that comes from assuming that bound
(
6.5
) holds with
u=2
.

Let us now concentrate on the term
\sum_{n\in\mathbb{N}}\mathfrak{F}^{\uparrow}_{n}(\zeta)^{u}
on
\mathrm{RHS}{\eqref{eq:SIO:stopping-time-decomp}}
. We will show that

(6.32)

\int_{B_{1}}\sum_{n\in\mathbb{N}}\mathfrak{F}^{\uparrow}_{n}(\zeta)^{u}\mathrm{d}\zeta=\sum_{n\in\mathbb{N}}\int_{B_{s_{n}}(x_{n})}\mathfrak{F}^{\uparrow}_{n}(\zeta)^{u}\mathrm{d}\zeta\leq C_{M}C_{5}C_{u=2}\mathrm{RHS}{\eqref{eq:tree-SIO-size}}^{u}

for some fixed
C_{5}>1
by showing that for each
n\in\mathbb{N}
we have

(6.33)

\int_{B_{s_{n}}(x_{n})}\mathfrak{F}^{\uparrow}_{n}(\zeta)^{u}\mathrm{d}\zeta\leq s_{n}C_{2}C_{5}C_{u=2}\mathrm{RHS}{\eqref{eq:tree-SIO-size}}^{u}

and using that
\sum_{n\in\mathbb{N}}2s_{n}\leq C_{M}C_{2}^{-1}
, i.e. the
boundedness of the Hardy Littlewood maximal function. To do so we will argue
that
\mathfrak{F}^{\uparrow}_{n}(\zeta)
is essentially constant on
B_{s_{n}}(x_{n})
. Since
\left\|\widetilde{\mathfrak{b}}^{\ast}_{+}(\zeta)\right\|_{\operatorname{Lip}}\leq 1
this shows that
\min_{\zeta\in B_{s_{n}}(x_{n})}\widetilde{\mathfrak{b}}^{\ast}_{+}(\zeta)\geq\min_{\zeta\in B_{s_{n}}(x_{n})}\widetilde{\mathfrak{b}}^{\ast}_{+}(\zeta)-2s_{n}
. If

\mathfrak{F}^{\uparrow}_{n}
is not constantly vanishing, then

\widetilde{\mathfrak{b}}^{\ast}_{+}(\overline{\zeta})>9s_{n}
for some
\overline{\zeta}\in B_{s_{n}}(x_{n})
and thus for any
\zeta,\zeta^{\prime}\in B_{s_{n}}(x_{n})
it
holds that
5/7\leq\widetilde{\mathfrak{b}}^{\ast}_{+}(\zeta^{\prime})/\widetilde{\mathfrak{b}}^{\ast}_{+}(\zeta)\leq 9/7
. Similarly, since
9s_{n}<\mathfrak{\widetilde{b}}_{+,n}{\leq 10s_{n}}
on
B_{s_{n}}(x_{n})
it holds that
9/10\leq\widetilde{\mathfrak{b}}^{\ast}_{+,n}(\zeta^{\prime})/\widetilde{\mathfrak{b}}^{\ast}_{+}(\zeta)\leq 10/9
for any
\zeta,\zeta^{\prime}\in B_{s_{n}}(x_{n})
. Finally either
\mathfrak{\widetilde{b}}_{-}<7s_{n}
on
B_{s_{n}}(x_{n})

or
5/7\leq\widetilde{\mathfrak{b}}^{\ast}_{-}(\zeta^{\prime})/\widetilde{\mathfrak{b}}^{\ast}_{-}(\zeta)\leq 9/7
for any
\zeta,\zeta^{\prime}\in B_{s_{n}}(x_{n})
. Let us use this to compare
\mathfrak{F}^{\uparrow}_{n}(\zeta)

with
\mathfrak{F}^{\uparrow}_{n}(\zeta^{\prime})
for any
\zeta,\zeta^{\prime}\in B_{s_{n}}(x_{n})
. We have

(6.34)

\left|\mathfrak{F}^{\uparrow}_{n}(\zeta)-\mathfrak{F}^{\uparrow}_{n}(\zeta^{\prime})\right|\leq\begin{aligned} &\left(\int_{\Theta}\left|\int_{\widetilde{\mathfrak{b}}^{\ast}_{+}(\zeta)/2}^{2\widetilde{\mathfrak{b}}^{\ast}_{+}(\zeta)}|F^{\ast}(\theta,\zeta,\rho)[\varphi_{\theta}]|+|F^{\ast}(\theta,\zeta^{\prime},\rho)[\varphi_{\theta}]|\frac{\mathrm{d}\rho}{\rho}\right|^{2}\frac{\mathrm{d}\theta}{|\Theta|}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}\\
&+\left(\int_{\Theta}\left|\int_{\mathfrak{\widetilde{b}}_{+,n}^{\ast}(\zeta)/2}^{2\mathfrak{\widetilde{b}}_{+,n}^{\ast}(\zeta)}|F^{\ast}(\theta,\zeta,\rho)[\varphi_{\theta}]|+|F^{\ast}(\theta,\zeta^{\prime},\rho)[\varphi_{\theta}]|\frac{\mathrm{d}\rho}{\rho}\right|^{2}\frac{\mathrm{d}\theta}{|\Theta|}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}\\
&+\begin{aligned} &\left(\int_{\Theta}\left|\int_{0}^{1}\mathbbm{1}_{\widetilde{\mathfrak{b}}^{\ast}_{-}(\zeta)/2<\rho<2\widetilde{\mathfrak{b}}^{\ast}_{-}(\zeta)}\right.\right.\qquad\\
&\qquad\times\left.\left.|F^{\ast}(\theta,\zeta,\rho)[\varphi_{\theta}]|+|F^{\ast}(\theta,\zeta^{\prime},\rho)[\varphi_{\theta}]|\frac{\mathrm{d}\rho}{\rho}\right|^{2}\frac{\mathrm{d}\theta}{|\Theta|}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}.\end{aligned}\\
&+\begin{aligned} &\left(\int_{\Theta}\left|\int_{2\mathfrak{\widetilde{b}}_{+,n}^{\ast}(\zeta^{\prime})}^{\widetilde{\mathfrak{b}}^{\ast}_{+}(\zeta^{\prime})/2}\mathbbm{1}_{\widetilde{\mathfrak{b}}^{\ast}_{-}(\zeta^{\prime})\vee\widetilde{\mathfrak{b}}^{\ast}_{-}(\zeta)<\rho<\widetilde{\mathfrak{b}}^{\ast}_{+}(\zeta^{\prime})\wedge\widetilde{\mathfrak{b}}^{\ast}_{+}(\zeta)}\right.\right.\qquad\\
&\qquad\times\left.\left.|F^{\ast}(\theta,\zeta,\rho)[\varphi_{\theta}]-F^{\ast}(\theta,\zeta^{\prime},\rho)[\varphi_{\theta}]|\frac{\mathrm{d}\rho}{\rho}\right|^{2}\frac{\mathrm{d}\theta}{|\Theta|}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}.\end{aligned}\end{aligned}

Using
Lemma

3.13
to decompose
\varphi_{\theta}
we
can bound all but the last summand on
\mathrm{RHS}{}
above by
\left\|\mathbbm{1}_{(V^{+}\cap W^{+})\setminus\left(V^{-}{\cup W^{-}}\right)}\mathrm{Emb}[f]\right\|_{\mathbb{L}{}^{(\infty,\infty)}_{\Theta}\Phi_{8\mathfrak{r}}^{N-6}(T)}
. To
bound the last summand notice that as long as
\widetilde{\mathfrak{b}}^{\ast}_{-}(\zeta^{\prime})\vee\widetilde{\mathfrak{b}}^{\ast}_{-}(\zeta)<\rho<\widetilde{\mathfrak{b}}^{\ast}_{+}(\zeta^{\prime})\wedge\widetilde{\mathfrak{b}}^{\ast}_{+}(\zeta)
we have that

(6.35)

\displaystyle F^{\ast}(\theta,\zeta,\rho)[\varphi_{\theta}]=f\ast\mathrm{Dil}_{\rho}\mathrm{Mod}_{\theta}\varphi^{\vee}(\zeta)

\displaystyle=f\ast\mathrm{Tr}_{(\zeta^{\prime}-\zeta)}\mathrm{Dil}_{\rho}\mathrm{Mod}_{\theta}\varphi^{\vee}(\zeta^{\prime})=F^{\ast}(\theta,\zeta^{\prime},\rho)\left[e^{2\pi i\theta\frac{(\zeta-\zeta^{\prime})}{\rho}}\mathrm{Tr}_{\frac{(\zeta^{\prime}-\zeta)}{\rho}}\varphi_{\theta}\right]

Since
\left\|e^{2\pi i\frac{(\zeta-\zeta^{\prime})\theta}{\rho}}\mathrm{Tr}_{\left(\frac{\zeta-\zeta^{\prime}}{\rho}\right)}\varphi_{\theta}-\varphi_{\theta}\right\|_{\Phi_{4\mathfrak{r}}^{N-4}}\lesssim\frac{|\zeta-\zeta^{\prime}|}{\rho}\|\varphi_{\theta}\|_{\Phi_{4\mathfrak{r}}^{N-5}}\lesssim\frac{|\zeta-\zeta^{\prime}|}{\rho}\|\varphi\|_{\Phi_{4\mathfrak{r}}^{N-5}}
for
\frac{|\zeta-\zeta^{\prime}|}{\rho}\lesssim 1
we obtain that

(6.36)

\displaystyle\begin{aligned} &\left(\int_{\Theta}\left|\int_{2\mathfrak{\widetilde{b}}_{+,n}^{\ast}(\zeta^{\prime})}^{\widetilde{\mathfrak{b}}^{\ast}_{+}(\zeta^{\prime})/2}\mathbbm{1}_{\widetilde{\mathfrak{b}}^{\ast}_{-}(\zeta^{\prime})\vee\widetilde{\mathfrak{b}}^{\ast}_{-}(\zeta)<\rho<\widetilde{\mathfrak{b}}^{\ast}_{+}(\zeta^{\prime})\wedge\widetilde{\mathfrak{b}}^{\ast}_{+}(\zeta)}\right.\right.\qquad\\
&\qquad\times\left.\left.|F^{\ast}(\theta,\zeta,\rho)[\varphi_{\theta}]-F^{\ast}(\theta,\zeta^{\prime},\rho)[\varphi_{\theta}]|\frac{\mathrm{d}\rho}{\rho}\right|^{2}\frac{\mathrm{d}\theta}{|\Theta|}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}\end{aligned}

\displaystyle=\begin{aligned} &\left(\int_{\Theta}\left|\int_{2\mathfrak{\widetilde{b}}_{+,n}^{\ast}(\zeta^{\prime})}^{\widetilde{\mathfrak{b}}^{\ast}_{+}(\zeta^{\prime})/2}\mathbbm{1}_{\widetilde{\mathfrak{b}}^{\ast}_{-}(\zeta^{\prime})\vee\widetilde{\mathfrak{b}}^{\ast}_{-}(\zeta)<\rho<\widetilde{\mathfrak{b}}^{\ast}_{+}(\zeta^{\prime})\wedge\widetilde{\mathfrak{b}}^{\ast}_{+}(\zeta)}\right.\right.\qquad\\
&\qquad\times\left.\left.\left|F^{\ast}(\theta,\zeta^{\prime},\rho)\left[e^{2\pi i\frac{(\zeta-\zeta^{\prime})\theta}{\rho}}\mathrm{Tr}_{\left(\frac{\zeta-\zeta^{\prime}}{\rho}\right)}\varphi_{\theta}-\varphi_{\theta}\right]\right|\frac{\mathrm{d}\rho}{\rho}\right|^{2}\frac{\mathrm{d}\theta}{|\Theta|}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}\end{aligned}

\displaystyle\lesssim\begin{aligned} &\left(\int_{\Theta}\left(\int_{2\mathfrak{\widetilde{b}}_{+,n}^{\ast}(\zeta^{\prime})}^{\widetilde{\mathfrak{b}}^{\ast}_{+}(\zeta^{\prime})/2}\mathbbm{1}_{\widetilde{\mathfrak{b}}^{\ast}_{-}(\zeta^{\prime})\vee\widetilde{\mathfrak{b}}^{\ast}_{-}(\zeta)<\rho<\widetilde{\mathfrak{b}}^{\ast}_{+}(\zeta^{\prime})\wedge\widetilde{\mathfrak{b}}^{\ast}_{+}(\zeta)}\frac{|\zeta-\zeta^{\prime}|}{\rho}\frac{\mathrm{d}\rho}{\rho}\right)^{2}\frac{\mathrm{d}\theta}{|\Theta|}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}\\
&\times\left\|\mathbbm{1}_{(V^{+}\cap W^{+})\setminus\left(V^{-}{\cup W^{-}}\right)}\mathrm{Emb}[f]\right\|_{\mathbb{L}{}^{(\infty,\infty)}_{\Theta}\Phi_{4\mathfrak{r}}^{N-5}(T)}\end{aligned}

\displaystyle\lesssim\left\|\mathbbm{1}_{(V^{+}\cap W^{+})\setminus\left(V^{-}{\cup W^{-}}\right)}\mathrm{Emb}[f]\right\|_{\mathbb{L}{}^{(\infty,\infty)}_{\Theta}\Phi_{4\mathfrak{r}}^{N-5}(T)}

where the last bound holds because integration happens over
\rho\geq s_{n}

and
|\zeta-\zeta^{\prime}|\lesssim s_{n}
. We can now average in
\zeta\in B_{s_{n}}(x_{n})
to obtain that

(6.37)

\int_{B_{s_{n}}(x_{n})}\mathfrak{F}^{\uparrow}_{n}(\zeta)^{u}\mathrm{d}\zeta\begin{aligned} &\lesssim\int_{B_{s_{n}}(x_{n})}\left(\frac{1}{2s_{n}}\int_{B_{s_{n}}(x_{n})}\left(\left|\mathfrak{F}^{\uparrow}_{n}(\zeta^{\prime})\right|+\left|\mathfrak{F}^{\uparrow}_{n}(\zeta)-\mathfrak{F}^{\uparrow}_{n}(\zeta^{\prime})\right|\right)\mathrm{d}\zeta^{\prime}\right)^{u}\mathrm{d}\zeta\\
&\lesssim\begin{aligned} &s_{n}\left\|\mathbbm{1}_{(V^{+}\cap W^{+})\setminus\left(V^{-}{\cup W^{-}}\right)}\mathrm{Emb}[f]\right\|_{\mathbb{L}{}^{(\infty,\infty)}_{\Theta}\Phi_{8\mathfrak{r}}^{N-6}(T)}^{u}\\
&+s_{n}\left(\frac{1}{2s_{n}}\int_{B_{s_{n}}(x_{n})}\left|\mathfrak{\mathfrak{\mathfrak{F}}}^{\uparrow}_{n}(\zeta^{\prime})\right|\mathrm{d}\zeta^{\prime}\right)^{u}\end{aligned}\end{aligned}

By maximality of
B_{s_{n}}(x_{n})\subset E
it holds that

(6.38)

\frac{1}{2s_{n}}\int_{B_{s_{n}}(x_{n})}\left|\mathfrak{\mathfrak{\mathfrak{F}}}^{\uparrow}_{n}(\zeta^{\prime})\right|\mathrm{d}\zeta^{\prime}\leq\frac{1}{2s_{n}}\int_{B_{s_{n}}(x_{n})}\left|\mathfrak{\mathfrak{\mathfrak{F}}}(\zeta^{\prime})\right|\mathrm{d}\zeta^{\prime}\lesssim C_{2}\left\|\mathfrak{\mathfrak{\mathfrak{F}}}\right\|_{L^{2}(B_{1})}.

As a matter of fact

(6.39)

\frac{1}{2s_{n}}\int_{B_{s_{n}}(x_{n})}\left|\mathfrak{\mathfrak{\mathfrak{F}}}(\zeta^{\prime})\right|\mathrm{d}\zeta^{\prime}\lesssim M\mathfrak{\mathfrak{\mathfrak{F}}}(x_{n}+s_{n})\leq C_{2}\left\|\mathfrak{\mathfrak{\mathfrak{F}}}\right\|_{L^{2}(B_{1})}

because
x_{n}+s_{n}\notin E
. Thus

(6.40)

\displaystyle\sum_{n}\int_{B_{s_{n}}(x_{n})}\mathfrak{F}^{\uparrow}_{n}(\zeta)^{u}\mathrm{d}\zeta\leq C_{M}C_{5}\left(\mathrm{RHS}{\eqref{eq:tree-SIO-size}}^{u}+C_{2}\left\|\mathfrak{\mathfrak{\mathfrak{F}}}\right\|_{L^{2}(B_{1})}^{u}\right)

\displaystyle\leq C_{M}C_{5}(1+C_{2}C_{u=2})\mathrm{RHS}{\eqref{eq:tree-SIO-size}}^{u},

as required.

Finally, let us concentrate on the term
\sum_{n\in\mathbb{N}}\mathfrak{F}^{\downarrow}_{n}(\zeta)^{u}
on

\mathrm{RHS}{\eqref{eq:SIO:stopping-time-decomp}}
. Setting
T_{n}=T_{\Theta}(0,x_{n},10s_{n})
it holds that

(6.41)

\displaystyle\frac{1}{s_{n}}{\int_{B_{s_{n}}(x_{n})}}\mathfrak{F}^{\downarrow}_{n}(\zeta)^{u}\mathrm{d}\zeta

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{s_{n}}{\int_{B_{s_{n}}(x_{n})}}\left(\int_{\Theta}\sup_{\sigma<\mathfrak{\widetilde{b}}_{+,n}^{\ast}(\zeta)}\left|\int_{\sigma}^{\mathfrak{\widetilde{b}}_{+,n}^{\ast}(\zeta)}F^{\ast}(\theta,\zeta,\rho)[\varphi_{\theta}]\frac{\mathrm{d}\rho}{\rho}\right|^{2}\frac{\mathrm{d}\theta}{|\Theta|}\right)^{\frac{u}{2}}\mathrm{d}\zeta

\displaystyle\lesssim\begin{aligned} &\sup_{\varphi^{\prime}}\left\|\left(\int_{\Theta}\sup_{\sigma>0}\left|\int_{\sigma}^{1-|\zeta|}\mathfrak{d}_{\zeta}(\theta)\left(\mathbbm{1}_{(V^{+}\cap W^{+})\setminus\left(V^{-}{\cup W^{-}}\right)}\right.\right.\right.\right.\qquad\\
&\times\left.\left.\left.\left.\mathrm{Emb}[f]\right)\circ\pi_{T_{n}}(\theta,\zeta,\rho)[\varphi^{\prime}]\frac{\mathrm{d}\rho}{\rho}\right|^{2}\frac{\mathrm{d}\theta}{|\Theta|}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}\right\|_{L^{u}(B_{1})}^{u}\end{aligned}

where the upper bound is taken over
\varphi^{\prime}\in\Phi_{4\mathfrak{r}}^{\infty}
with

\|\varphi^{\prime}\|_{\Phi_{4\mathfrak{r}}^{N-4}}\leq 1
. Since
s_{n}\leq C_{M}C_{2}^{-1}

by the boundedness of the Hardy Littlewood maximal function, we can choose

C_{2}
sufficiently large so that
10s_{n}\leq C_{\operatorname{ind}}^{-1}
and we
can apply the induction hypothesis to the bound above to get

(6.42)

\frac{1}{s_{n}}{\int_{B_{s_{n}}(x_{n})}}\mathfrak{F}^{\downarrow}_{n}(\zeta)^{u}\mathrm{d}\zeta\lesssim C_{\ast}\mathrm{RHS}{\eqref{eq:tree-SIO-size}}^{u}.

Summing up, this gives

(6.43)

\sum_{n\in\mathbb{N}}{\int_{B_{s_{n}}(x_{n})}}\mathfrak{F}^{\downarrow}_{n}(\zeta)^{u}\mathrm{d}\zeta\leq C_{M}C_{2}^{-2}C_{\ast}\mathrm{RHS}{\eqref{eq:tree-SIO-size}}^{u}.

This concludes showing (
6.29
).

The case
u=2
:

The final step is showing that (
6.23
) holds for
u=2
.
By symmetry considerations (identity (
3.20
) and
subsequent discussion) we can suppose, without loss of generality, that
T=T_{\Theta}(0,0,1)
. We need to show that given any smooth
\sigma:\Theta\times B_{1}\rightarrow[0,1]
it holds that

(6.44)

\left\|\int_{\sigma(\theta,\zeta)}^{\mathfrak{\widetilde{b}^{\ast}_{+}(\zeta)}}\mathfrak{d}_{\zeta}(\theta)F^{\ast}(\theta,\zeta,\rho)[\varphi]\frac{\mathrm{d}\rho}{\rho}\right\|_{L^{2}_{\frac{\mathrm{d}\theta\mathrm{d}\zeta}{|\Theta|}}(\Theta\times B_{1})}\lesssim\mathrm{RHS}{\eqref{eq:tree-SIO-size}}

with
\varphi\in\Phi_{4\mathfrak{r}}^{\infty}
being any wave packet with
\|\varphi\|_{\Phi_{4\mathfrak{r}}^{N-4}}\leq 1
. Let
\varphi_{\theta}(z)\coloneqq(-d_{z}+2\pi i\theta)\varphi(z)
so that
\mathfrak{d}_{\zeta}(\theta)F^{\ast}(\theta,\zeta,\rho)[\varphi]=F^{\ast}(\theta,\zeta,\rho)[\varphi_{\theta}]
. Next,
we will show that we can assume that
\sigma(\theta,\zeta)=\widetilde{\mathfrak{b}}_{-}(\zeta)
and, in particular, we may assume that
\|\zeta\mapsto\sigma(\theta,\zeta)\|_{\operatorname{Lip}}\leq 1
. For any
C\geq 1
it holds that

(6.45)

\displaystyle\left\|\int_{\sigma(\theta,\zeta)}^{\min\left(C\sigma(\theta,\zeta),\mathfrak{\widetilde{b}}^{\ast}_{+}(\zeta)\right)}F^{\ast}(\theta,\zeta,\rho)[\varphi_{\theta}]\frac{\mathrm{d}\rho}{\rho}\right\|_{L^{2}_{\frac{\mathrm{d}\theta\mathrm{d}\zeta}{|\Theta|}}(\Theta\times B_{1})}\qquad

\displaystyle\lesssim\left\|\mathbbm{1}_{(V^{+}\cap W^{+})\setminus\left(V^{-}{\cup W^{-}}\right)}\mathrm{Emb}[f]\right\|_{\mathbb{L}^{(\infty,\infty)}_{\Theta}\Phi_{4\mathfrak{r}}^{N-4}}

so we can assume without loss of generality that that
\sigma(\theta,\zeta)\in\left(C\mathfrak{\widetilde{b}}^{\ast}_{-}(\zeta),C^{-1}\mathfrak{\widetilde{b}}^{\ast}_{+}(\zeta)\right)
for some large
C\gg 1
. Let us
freeze
\theta\in\Theta
and, for the sake of the subsequent argument,
assume that
\zeta\geq 0
; the case
\zeta<0
is handled symmetrically.
Since
\left\|\zeta\mapsto\widetilde{\mathfrak{b}}(\zeta)\right\|_{\operatorname{Lip}}\leq 1
it holds that
\rho\in\left(\mathfrak{\widetilde{b}}^{\ast}_{-}(\zeta^{\prime}),\mathfrak{\widetilde{b}}^{\ast}_{+}(\zeta^{\prime})\right)

if
\rho\in\left(\sigma(\theta,\zeta),C^{-1}\mathfrak{\widetilde{b}}^{\ast}_{+}(\zeta)\right)
and
|\zeta^{\prime}-\zeta|\leq\sigma(\theta,\zeta)
. We
decompose

(6.46)

\int_{\sigma(\theta,\zeta)}^{\mathfrak{\widetilde{b}}^{\ast}_{+}(\zeta)}F^{\ast}(\theta,\zeta,\rho)[\varphi_{\theta}]\frac{\mathrm{d}\rho}{\rho}=\mathrm{I}(\theta,\zeta)+\mathrm{II}(\theta,\zeta)+\mathrm{III}(\theta,\zeta)+\mathrm{IV}(\theta,\zeta)

with

(6.47)

\displaystyle\mathrm{I}(\theta,\zeta):=\int_{\sigma(\zeta,\theta)}^{\widetilde{\mathfrak{b}}^{\ast}_{+}(\zeta)/C}\left(F^{\ast}(\theta,\zeta,\rho)[\varphi_{\theta}]\frac{\mathrm{d}\rho}{\rho}-\frac{1}{\sigma(\theta,\zeta)}\int_{\zeta-\sigma(\theta,\zeta)}^{\zeta}F^{\ast}(\theta,\zeta^{\prime},\rho)[\varphi_{\theta}]\mathrm{d}\zeta^{\prime}\right)\frac{\mathrm{d}\rho}{\rho},

\displaystyle\mathrm{II}(\theta,\zeta):=-\frac{1}{\sigma(\theta,\zeta)}\int_{\zeta-\sigma(\theta,\zeta)}^{\zeta}\int_{\mathfrak{\widetilde{b}}^{\ast}_{+}(\zeta)/C}^{\mathfrak{\widetilde{b}}^{\ast}_{+}(\zeta^{\prime})}F^{\ast}(\theta,\zeta^{\prime},\rho)[\varphi_{\theta}]\frac{\mathrm{d}\rho}{\rho}\mathrm{d}\zeta^{\prime},

\displaystyle\mathrm{III}(\theta,\zeta):=-\frac{1}{\sigma(\theta,\zeta)}\int_{\zeta-\sigma(\theta,\zeta)}^{\zeta}\int_{\widetilde{\mathfrak{b}}^{\ast}_{-}(\zeta^{\prime})}^{\sigma(\theta,\zeta)}F^{\ast}(\theta,\zeta^{\prime},\rho)[\varphi_{\theta}]\frac{\mathrm{d}\rho}{\rho}\mathrm{d}\zeta^{\prime},

\displaystyle\mathrm{IV}(\theta,\zeta):=\frac{1}{\sigma(\theta,\zeta)}\int_{\zeta-\sigma(\theta,\zeta)}^{\zeta}\int_{\widetilde{\mathfrak{b}}^{\ast}_{-}(\zeta^{\prime})}^{\mathfrak{\widetilde{\mathfrak{b}}}^{\ast}_{+}(\zeta^{\prime})}F^{\ast}(\theta,\zeta^{\prime},\rho)[\varphi_{\theta}]\frac{\mathrm{d}\rho}{\rho}\mathrm{d}\zeta^{\prime}.

Terms
\mathrm{I}(\theta,\zeta)
and
\mathrm{II}(\theta,\zeta)
are
“correction” terms since we will show that they can be bounded by the size

\left\|\mathbbm{1}_{(V^{+}\cap W^{+})\setminus\left(V^{-}{\cup W^{-}}\right)}\mathrm{Emb}[f]\right\|_{\mathbb{L}{}^{(\infty,\infty)}_{\Theta}\Phi_{8\mathfrak{r}}^{N-8}(T)}
.
We will then proceed to bound the latter
2
terms.

For any value of
(\zeta,\rho)
in the integral expression defining

\mathrm{I}(\theta,\zeta)
, we have that

(6.48)

F^{\ast}(\theta,\zeta,\rho)[\varphi_{\theta}]-\frac{1}{\sigma(\theta,\zeta)}\int_{\zeta-\sigma(\theta,\zeta)}^{\zeta}F^{\ast}(\theta,\zeta^{\prime},\rho)[\varphi_{\theta}]\mathrm{d}\zeta^{\prime}=F^{\ast}(\theta,\zeta,\rho)[\varphi_{(\theta,\zeta)}]

where

(6.49)

\varphi_{(\theta,\zeta)}=\frac{1}{\sigma(\theta,\zeta)}\int_{\zeta-\sigma(\theta,\zeta)}^{\zeta}\left(\varphi_{\theta}-e^{2\pi i\theta\frac{(\zeta^{\prime}-\zeta)}{\rho}}\mathrm{Tr}_{\frac{(\zeta-\zeta^{\prime})}{\rho}}\varphi_{\theta}\right)\mathrm{d}\zeta^{\prime}

satisfies

(6.50)

\|\varphi_{(\theta,\zeta)}(z)\|_{\Phi_{4\mathfrak{r}}^{N-4}}\lesssim\left|\frac{\sigma(\theta,\zeta)}{\rho}\right|\|\varphi_{\theta}(z)\|_{\Phi_{4\mathfrak{r}}^{N-5}}.

This holds since

(6.51)

F^{\ast}(\theta,\zeta^{\prime},\rho)[\varphi_{\theta}]=F^{\ast}(\theta,\zeta,\rho)\left[e^{2\pi i\theta\frac{(\zeta^{\prime}-\zeta)}{\rho}}\mathrm{Tr}_{\frac{(\zeta-\zeta^{\prime})}{\rho}}\varphi_{\theta}\right]

for any
\rho\in\left(\widetilde{\mathfrak{b}}^{\ast}_{-}(\zeta^{\prime})\vee\widetilde{\mathfrak{b}}^{\ast}_{-}(\zeta),\widetilde{\mathfrak{b}}^{\ast}_{+}(\zeta^{\prime})\wedge\widetilde{\mathfrak{b}}^{\ast}_{+}(\zeta)\right)
. Thus

(6.52)

\displaystyle|\mathrm{I}(\theta,\zeta)|\lesssim\int_{\sigma(\theta,\zeta)}^{\mathfrak{\widetilde{b}}^{\ast}_{+}(\zeta)/C}\left|\frac{\sigma(\theta,\zeta)}{\rho}\right|\frac{\mathrm{d}\rho}{\rho}(\sup_{\widetilde{\varphi}}\sup_{\rho\in(0,1)}|F^{\ast}(\theta,\zeta,\rho)[\widetilde{\varphi}]|)

with the upper bound taken over
\widetilde{\varphi}\in\Phi^{\infty}_{4\mathfrak{r}}

with
\|\widetilde{\varphi}\|_{\Phi_{4\mathfrak{r}}^{N-5}}\leq 1
. Integrating and
using
Lemma

3.13
this gives the bound

(6.53)

\|\mathrm{I}(\theta,\zeta)\|_{L^{2}_{\frac{\mathrm{d}\theta\mathrm{d}\zeta}{|\Theta|}}(\Theta\times B_{1})}\lesssim\left\|\mathbbm{1}_{(V^{+}\cap W^{+})\setminus\left(V^{-}{\cup W^{-}}\right)}\mathrm{Emb}[f]\right\|_{\mathbb{L}{}^{(\infty,\infty)}_{\Theta}\Phi_{8\mathfrak{r}}^{N-7}(T)}.

The term
\mathrm{II}(\zeta,\theta)
, again using

Lemma

3.13
, satisfies

(6.54)

\displaystyle|\operatorname{II}(\theta,\zeta)|\lesssim\frac{1}{\sigma(\theta,\zeta)}\int_{\zeta-\sigma(\theta,\zeta)}^{\zeta}\int_{\mathfrak{b}^{\ast}_{+}(\zeta)/C}^{\mathfrak{b}^{\ast}_{+}(\zeta^{\prime})}(\sup_{\widetilde{\varphi}}\sup_{\rho\in(0,1)}|F^{\ast}(\theta,\zeta,\rho)[\widetilde{\varphi}]|)\frac{\mathrm{d}\rho}{\rho}\mathrm{d}\zeta^{\prime}

with the upper bound taken over
\widetilde{\varphi}\in\Phi^{\infty}_{4\mathfrak{r}}

with
\|\widetilde{\varphi}\|_{\Phi_{4\mathfrak{r}}^{N-5}}\leq 1
. Integrating and
using
Lemma

3.13
gives the bound

(6.55)

\|\mathrm{II}(\theta,\zeta)\|_{L^{2}_{\frac{\mathrm{d}\theta\mathrm{d}\zeta}{|\Theta|}}(\Theta\times B_{1})}\lesssim\left\|\mathbbm{1}_{(V^{+}\cap W^{+})\setminus\left(V^{-}{\cup W^{-}}\right)}\mathrm{Emb}[f]\right\|_{\mathbb{L}{}^{(\infty,\infty)}_{\Theta}\Phi_{8\mathfrak{r}}^{N-7}(T)}.

Let us now deal with the term
\mathrm{III}(\theta,\zeta)
. Recall (see
(
3.98
)) that

(6.56)

F^{\ast}(\theta,\zeta^{\prime},\rho)[\varphi_{\theta}]=\mathfrak{d}(\theta)F^{\ast}(\theta,\zeta^{\prime},\rho)[\varphi]=\rho\mathrm{d}_{\zeta^{\prime}}F^{\ast}(\theta,\zeta^{\prime},\rho)[\varphi]

as long as
\rho\in\left(\mathfrak{b_{-}}(\theta,\zeta^{\prime}),\mathfrak{b_{+}}(\theta,\zeta^{\prime})\right)
, which is the case for
\zeta^{\prime}\in(\zeta-\sigma(\theta,\zeta),\zeta)
. Integration by parts gives that

(6.57)

\mathrm{III}(\theta,\zeta)=\begin{aligned} &-\int_{\widetilde{\mathfrak{b}}^{\ast}_{-}(\zeta)}^{\sigma(\theta,\zeta)}\frac{\rho}{\sigma(\theta,\zeta)}F^{\ast}(\theta,\zeta,\rho)[\varphi]\frac{\mathrm{d}\rho}{\rho}\\
&+\int_{\widetilde{\mathfrak{b}}^{\ast}_{-}(\zeta-\sigma(\theta,\zeta))}^{\sigma(\theta,\zeta)}\frac{\rho}{\sigma(\theta,\zeta)}F^{\ast}(\theta,\zeta-\sigma(\theta,\zeta),\rho)[\varphi]\frac{\mathrm{d}\rho}{\rho}\\
&-\frac{1}{\sigma(\theta,\zeta)}\int_{\zeta-\sigma(\theta,\zeta)}^{\zeta}\mathrm{d}_{\zeta^{\prime}}\widetilde{\mathfrak{b}}^{\ast}_{-}(\zeta^{\prime})F^{\ast}\left(\theta,\zeta^{\prime},\widetilde{\mathfrak{b}}^{\ast}_{-}(\zeta^{\prime})\right)[\varphi]\mathrm{d}\zeta^{\prime}\end{aligned}

Since
\left|\mathrm{d}_{\zeta^{\prime}}\widetilde{\mathfrak{b}}^{\ast}_{-}(\zeta^{\prime})\right|\leq 1
it holds that

(6.58)

\displaystyle\left|\frac{1}{\sigma(\theta,\zeta)}\int_{\zeta-\sigma(\theta,\zeta)}^{\zeta}\mathrm{d}_{\zeta^{\prime}}\widetilde{\mathfrak{b}}^{\ast}_{-}(\zeta^{\prime})F^{\ast}\left(\theta,\zeta^{\prime},\widetilde{\mathfrak{b}}^{\ast}_{-}(\zeta^{\prime})\right)[\varphi]\mathrm{d}\zeta^{\prime}\right|

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{\sigma(\theta,\zeta)}\int_{\zeta-\sigma(\theta,\zeta)}^{\zeta}\left|F^{\ast}\left(\theta,\zeta^{\prime},\widetilde{\mathfrak{b}}^{\ast}_{-}(\zeta^{\prime})\right)[\varphi]\right|\mathrm{d}\zeta^{\prime}\lesssim\sup_{\zeta^{\prime}\in B_{1}}\left|F^{\ast}\left(\theta,\zeta^{\prime},\widetilde{\mathfrak{b}}^{\ast}_{-}(\zeta^{\prime})\right)[\varphi]\right|.

The other two summands satisfy

(6.59)

\displaystyle\left|\int_{\widetilde{\mathfrak{b}}^{\ast}_{-}(\zeta)}^{\sigma(\theta,\zeta)}\frac{\rho}{\sigma(\theta,\zeta)}F^{\ast}(\theta,\zeta,\rho)[\varphi]\frac{\mathrm{d}\rho}{\rho}\right|\lesssim\sup_{\rho\in\left(\widetilde{\mathfrak{b}}^{\ast}_{-}(\zeta),\sigma(\theta,\zeta)\right)}|F^{\ast}(\theta,\zeta,\rho)[\varphi]|

and

(6.60)

\displaystyle\left|\int_{\widetilde{\mathfrak{b}}^{\ast}_{-}(\zeta-\sigma(\theta,\zeta))}^{\sigma(\theta,\zeta)}\frac{\rho}{\sigma(\theta,\zeta)}F^{\ast}(\theta,\zeta-\sigma(\theta,\zeta),\rho)[\varphi]\frac{\mathrm{d}\rho}{\rho}\right|

\displaystyle\lesssim\sup_{\zeta^{\prime}\in B_{1}}\sup_{\rho\in\left(\widetilde{\mathfrak{b}}^{\ast}_{-}(\zeta^{\prime}),\widetilde{\mathfrak{b}}^{\ast}_{+}(\zeta^{\prime})\right)}|F^{\ast}(\theta,\zeta^{\prime},\rho)[\varphi]|.

Thus
\|\mathrm{III}(\zeta,\theta)\|_{L^{2}_{\frac{\mathrm{d}\zeta\mathrm{d}\theta}{|\Theta|}}(B_{1}\times\Theta)}\lesssim\left\|\mathbbm{1}_{V^{+}\setminus(V^{-}\cup W)}\mathrm{Emb}[f]\right\|_{\mathbb{L}^{(2,\infty)}_{\Theta}{\Phi_{\mathfrak{r}}^{\widetilde{N}-3}}^{\prime}}
.

It remains to deal with the term
\mathrm{IV}(\theta,\zeta)
. Up to an
application of the Hardy-Littlewood maximal function,
\mathrm{IV}(\theta,\zeta)
can be compared with the original quantity to bound but with
\sigma(\theta,\zeta)=\mathfrak{\widetilde{b}_{-}(\zeta)}
. More explicitly
|\mathrm{IV}(\theta,\zeta)|\leq\mathrm{M}(\widetilde{\mathrm{IV}}(\theta,\cdot))(\zeta)
where

(6.61)

\widetilde{\mathrm{IV}}(\theta,\zeta)=\int_{\mathfrak{\widetilde{b}}^{\ast}_{-}(\zeta)}^{\mathfrak{\widetilde{b}}^{\ast}_{+}(\zeta)}F^{\ast}(\theta,\zeta,\rho)[\varphi_{\theta}]\frac{\mathrm{d}\rho}{\rho}

so, using the boundedness of the Hardy-Littlewood maximal function
M
on

L^{2}
, we need to show that

(6.62)

\|\widetilde{\mathrm{IV}}(\theta,\zeta)\|_{L^{2}_{\frac{\mathrm{d}\theta\mathrm{d}\zeta}{|\Theta|}}(\Theta\times B_{1})}\lesssim\mathrm{RHS}{\eqref{eq:tree-SIO-size}}.

We have

(6.63)

\displaystyle\left|\int_{\mathfrak{\widetilde{b}}^{\ast}_{-}(\zeta)}^{\mathfrak{\widetilde{b}}^{\ast}_{+}(\zeta)}F^{\ast}(\theta,\zeta,\rho)[\varphi_{\theta}]\frac{\mathrm{d}\rho}{\rho}\right|^{2}

\displaystyle=2\Re\left(\int_{\mathfrak{\widetilde{b}}^{\ast}_{-}(\zeta)}^{\mathfrak{\widetilde{b}}^{\ast}_{+}(\zeta)}F^{\ast}(\theta,\zeta,\rho)[\varphi_{\theta}]\int_{\rho}^{\mathfrak{\widetilde{b}}^{\ast}_{+}(\zeta)}\overline{F^{\ast}(\theta,\zeta,\rho^{\prime})[\varphi_{\theta}]}\frac{\mathrm{d}\rho^{\prime}}{\rho}\frac{\mathrm{d}\rho}{\rho}\right).

As before, recall that

(6.64)

F^{\ast}(\theta,\zeta,\rho)[\varphi_{\theta}]=\rho^{\prime}\mathrm{d}_{\zeta^{\prime}}F^{\ast}(\theta,\zeta,\rho^{\prime})[\varphi]

Integrating by parts we obtain that

(6.65)

\displaystyle\int_{B_{1}}\left|\int_{\mathfrak{\widetilde{b}}^{\ast}_{-}(\zeta)}^{\mathfrak{\widetilde{b}}^{\ast}_{+}(\zeta)}F^{\ast}(\theta,\zeta,\rho)[\varphi_{\theta}]\frac{\mathrm{d}\rho}{\rho}\right|^{2}\mathrm{d}\zeta

\displaystyle\begin{aligned} &=2\Re\int_{B_{1}}\left(\int_{\mathfrak{\widetilde{b}}^{\ast}_{-}(\zeta)}^{\mathfrak{\widetilde{b}}^{\ast}_{+}(\zeta)}F^{\ast}(\theta,\zeta,\rho)[\varphi_{\theta}]\int_{\rho}^{\mathfrak{\widetilde{b}}^{\ast}_{+}(\zeta)}\rho^{\prime}\mathrm{d}_{\zeta}\overline{F^{\ast}(\theta,\zeta,\rho^{\prime})[\varphi]}\frac{\mathrm{d}\rho^{\prime}}{\rho^{\prime}}\frac{\mathrm{d}\rho}{\rho}\right)\mathrm{d}\zeta\\
&=\begin{aligned} &-2\Re\int_{B_{1}}\left(\int_{\mathfrak{\widetilde{b}}^{\ast}_{-}(\zeta)}^{\mathfrak{\widetilde{b}}^{\ast}_{+}(\zeta)}\rho\mathrm{d}_{\zeta}F^{\ast}(\theta,\zeta,\rho)[\varphi_{\theta}]\int_{\rho}^{\mathfrak{\widetilde{b}}^{\ast}_{+}(\zeta)}\frac{\rho^{\prime}}{\rho}\overline{F^{\ast}(\theta,\zeta,\rho^{\prime})[\varphi]}\frac{\mathrm{d}\rho^{\prime}}{\rho^{\prime}}\frac{\mathrm{d}\rho}{\rho}\right)\mathrm{d}\zeta\\
&+2\Re\int_{B_{1}}\left(\mathrm{d}_{\zeta}\mathfrak{\widetilde{b}}^{\ast}_{-}(\zeta)F^{\ast}(\theta,\zeta,\rho)[\varphi_{\theta}]\int_{\mathfrak{\widetilde{b}}^{\ast}_{-}(\zeta)}^{\mathfrak{\widetilde{b}}^{\ast}_{+}(\zeta)}\frac{\rho^{\prime}}{\mathfrak{\widetilde{b}}^{\ast}_{-}(\zeta)}\overline{F^{\ast}(\theta,\zeta,\rho^{\prime})[\varphi]}\frac{\mathrm{d}\rho^{\prime}}{\rho^{\prime}}\right)\mathrm{d}\zeta\\
&-2\Re\int_{B_{1}}\left(\int_{\mathfrak{\widetilde{b}}^{\ast}_{-}(\zeta)}^{\mathfrak{\widetilde{b}}^{\ast}_{+}(\zeta)}F^{\ast}(\theta,\zeta,\rho)[\varphi_{\theta}]\frac{\mathrm{d}\rho}{\rho}\mathrm{d}_{\zeta}\mathfrak{\widetilde{b}}^{\ast}_{+}(\zeta)\overline{F^{\ast}\left(\theta,\zeta,\mathfrak{\widetilde{b}}^{\ast}_{+}(\zeta)\right)[\varphi]}\right)\mathrm{d}\zeta.\end{aligned}\end{aligned}

Using the bounds
\left|\mathrm{d}_{\zeta}\mathfrak{\widetilde{b}}^{\ast}_{-}(\zeta)\right|\leq 1
,
\left|\mathrm{d}_{\zeta}\mathfrak{\widetilde{b}}^{\ast}_{+}(\zeta)\right|\leq 1
, the identity

(6.66)

\rho\mathrm{d}_{\zeta}F^{\ast}(\theta,\zeta,\rho)[\varphi_{\theta}]=\mathfrak{d}(\theta)F^{\ast}(\theta,\zeta,\rho)[\varphi_{\theta}]=F^{\ast}(\theta,\zeta,\rho)[\varphi_{\theta}^{\prime}]

with
\varphi^{\prime}_{\theta}(z)=(-d_{z}+2\pi i\theta)\varphi_{\theta}(z)
, the
fact that
\|\varphi_{\theta}^{\prime}\|_{\Phi_{4\mathfrak{r}}^{N-4}}\lesssim\|\varphi_{\theta}\|_{\Phi_{4\mathfrak{r}}^{N-4}}\lesssim\|\varphi\|_{\Phi_{4\mathfrak{r}}^{N-4}}\leq 1
, and Young’s convolution inequality in the multiplicative
measure
\frac{\mathrm{d}\rho^{\prime}}{\rho^{\prime}}
we can integrate in
(\theta,\zeta)\in\Theta\times B_{1}
to obtain that

(6.67)

\displaystyle\int_{\Theta\times B_{1}}\left|\int_{\mathfrak{\widetilde{b}}^{\ast}_{-}(\zeta)}^{\mathfrak{\widetilde{b}}^{\ast}_{+}(\zeta)}\rho\mathrm{d}_{\zeta}F^{\ast}(\theta,\zeta,\rho)[\varphi_{\theta}]\int_{\rho}^{\mathfrak{\widetilde{b}}^{\ast}_{+}(\zeta)}\frac{\rho^{\prime}}{\rho}\overline{F^{\ast}(\theta,\zeta,\rho^{\prime})[\varphi]}\frac{\mathrm{d}\rho^{\prime}}{\rho^{\prime}}\frac{\mathrm{d}\rho}{\rho}\right|\frac{\mathrm{d}\theta\mathrm{d}\zeta}{|\Theta|}

\displaystyle\lesssim\left\|\mathbbm{1}_{(V^{+}\cap W^{+})\setminus\left(V^{-}{\cup W^{-}}\right)}\mathrm{Emb}[f]\right\|_{\mathbb{L}^{(2,2)}_{\Theta}\Phi_{4\mathfrak{r}}^{N-4}}^{2},

(6.68)

\displaystyle\int_{\Theta\times B_{1}}\left|\mathrm{d}_{\zeta}\mathfrak{\widetilde{b}}^{\ast}_{-}(\zeta)F^{\ast}(\theta,\zeta,\rho)[\varphi_{\theta}]\int_{\mathfrak{\widetilde{b}}^{\ast}_{-}(\zeta)}^{\mathfrak{\widetilde{b}}^{\ast}_{+}(\zeta)}\frac{\rho^{\prime}}{\mathfrak{\widetilde{b}}^{\ast}_{-}(\zeta)}\overline{F^{\ast}(\theta,\zeta,\rho^{\prime})[\varphi]}\frac{\mathrm{d}\rho^{\prime}}{\rho^{\prime}}\right|\frac{\mathrm{d}\theta\mathrm{d}\zeta}{|\Theta|}

\displaystyle\lesssim\begin{aligned} &\left\|\mathbbm{1}_{(V^{+}\cap W^{+})\setminus\left(V^{-}{\cup W^{-}}\right)}\mathrm{Emb}[f]\right\|_{\mathbb{L}^{(2,2)}_{\Theta}\Phi_{4\mathfrak{r}}^{N-4}}\\
&\qquad\times\left\|\mathbbm{1}_{(V^{+}\cap W^{+})\setminus\left(V^{-}{\cup W^{-}}\right)}\mathrm{Emb}[f]\right\|_{\mathbb{L}^{(\infty,\infty)}_{\Theta}\Phi_{4\mathfrak{r}}^{N-4}},\end{aligned}

and

(6.69)

\displaystyle\int_{\Theta\times B_{1}}\left|\int_{\mathfrak{\widetilde{b}}^{\ast}_{-}(\zeta)}^{\mathfrak{\widetilde{b}}^{\ast}_{+}(\zeta)}F^{\ast}(\theta,\zeta,\rho)[\varphi_{\theta}]\frac{\mathrm{d}\rho}{\rho}\mathrm{d}_{\zeta}\mathfrak{\widetilde{b}}^{\ast}_{+}(\zeta)\overline{F^{\ast}\left(\theta,\zeta,\mathfrak{\widetilde{b}}^{\ast}_{+}(\zeta)\right)[\varphi]}\right|\frac{\mathrm{d}\theta\mathrm{d}\zeta}{|\Theta|}

\displaystyle\lesssim\begin{aligned} &\left\|\mathbbm{1}_{(V^{+}\cap W^{+})\setminus\left(V^{-}{\cup W^{-}}\right)}\mathrm{Emb}[f]\right\|_{\mathbb{L}^{(\infty,\infty)}_{\Theta}\Phi_{4\mathfrak{r}}^{N-4}}\\
&\qquad\times\|\widetilde{\mathrm{IV}}(\zeta,\theta)\|_{L^{2}_{\frac{\mathrm{d}\zeta\mathrm{d}\theta}{|\Theta|}}(\Theta\times B_{1})}.\end{aligned}

With the above we have shown that

(6.70)

\|\widetilde{\mathrm{IV}}(\zeta,\theta)\|_{L^{2}_{\frac{\mathrm{d}\zeta\mathrm{d}\theta}{|\Theta|}}(\Theta\times B_{1})}\lesssim\mathrm{RHS}{\eqref{eq:tree-SIO-size}}\Big(\mathrm{RHS}{\eqref{eq:tree-SIO-size}}+\|\widetilde{\mathrm{IV}}(\zeta,\theta)\|_{L^{2}_{\frac{\mathrm{d}\zeta\mathrm{d}\theta}{|\Theta|}}(\Theta\times B_{1})}\Big).

This implies that

(6.71)

\|\widetilde{\mathrm{IV}}(\zeta,\theta)\|_{L^{2}_{\frac{\mathrm{d}\zeta\mathrm{d}\theta}{|\Theta|}}(\Theta\times B_{1})}\lesssim\mathrm{RHS}{\eqref{eq:tree-SIO-size}},

as required
∎

6.2.
Counting function
improvement

To conclude that
Theorem

5
holds we now need to
improve the bound (
6.4
). In
particular, we need to bound
\mathrm{Emb}[f]
in the localized outer Lebesgue
quasi-norm
L^{p}_{\nu_{1}}\not{L}^{q,+}_{\mu_{\Theta}^{\infty},\nu_{1}}\mathbb{F}_{\Theta}^{u}\Phi_{\mathfrak{r}}^{N}
in place of
L^{p}_{\nu_{1}}\not{L}^{q,+}_{\mu_{\Theta}^{1},\nu_{1}}\mathbb{F}_{\Theta}^{u}\Phi_{\mathfrak{r}}^{N}
.

Proof.

Proof of
Theorem

5
:

\mu^{1}_{\Theta}
to
\mu^{\infty}_{\Theta}

Fix
q_{0}\in[1,+\infty)
. We aim to show that for any
q\in(q_{0},+\infty]
it holds that

(6.72)

\|F\|_{L^{p}_{\nu_{1}}\not{L}^{q,+}_{\mu^{\infty}_{\Theta},\nu_{1}}\mathbb{F}^{u}_{\Theta}\Phi_{\mathfrak{r}}^{N}(V)}\lesssim\|F\|_{L^{p}_{\nu_{1}}\not{L}^{q_{0},+}_{\mu^{1}_{\Theta},\nu_{1}}\mathbb{F}^{u}_{\Theta}\Phi_{\mathfrak{r}}^{N}}

for any
p\in(1,+\infty)
. To do so let us use the restricted weak
interpolation. To apply
Proposition

3.3
we can
suppose that
F
is supported on
V\in\mathbb{D}^{\cup}_{1}
and we need to check
that

(6.73)

\|F\|_{L^{p}_{\nu_{1}}\not{L}^{q,+}_{\mu^{\infty}_{\Theta},\nu_{1}}\mathbb{F}^{u}_{\Theta}\Phi_{\mathfrak{r}}^{N}}\lesssim\nu_{1}(V)^{\frac{1}{p}}\|F\|_{\not{L}^{q_{0},+}_{\mu^{1}_{\Theta},\nu_{1}}\mathbb{F}^{u}_{\Theta}\Phi_{\mathfrak{r}}^{N}}

for any
p>1
. If
q=+\infty
the claim is immediate because for any

V^{+}

(6.74)

\|F\|_{\not{L}^{\infty}_{\mu^{\infty}_{\Theta},\nu_{1}}\mathbb{F}^{u}_{\Theta}\Phi_{\mathfrak{r}}^{N}(V^{+})}=\left\|\mathbbm{1}_{V^{+}}F\right\|_{\mathbb{F}^{u}_{\Theta}\Phi_{\mathfrak{r}}^{N}}=\|F\|_{\not{L}^{\infty}_{\mu^{1}_{\Theta},\nu_{1}}\mathbb{F}^{u}_{\Theta}\Phi_{\mathfrak{r}}^{N}(V^{+})}\leq\|F\|_{\not{L}^{q_{0},+}_{\mu^{1}_{\Theta},\nu_{\beta}}\mathbb{F}^{u}_{\Theta}\Phi_{\mathfrak{r}}^{N}}.

Thus it is sufficient to show that

(6.75)

\|F\|_{L^{p}_{\nu_{1}}\not{L}^{q}_{\mu^{\infty}_{\Theta},\nu_{1}}\mathbb{F}^{u}_{\Theta}\Phi_{\mathfrak{r}}^{N}}\lesssim\nu_{1}(V)^{\frac{1}{p}}\|F\|_{\not{L}^{q_{0},+}_{\mu^{1}_{\Theta},\nu_{1}}\mathbb{F}^{u}_{\Theta}\Phi_{\mathfrak{r}}^{N}}.

We will show that for any
V^{+}\in\mathbb{D}^{\cup}_{1}
there exists

V^{\operatorname{ecc}}(V^{+})\in\mathbb{D}^{\cup}_{1}
with
\nu_{1}(V^{\operatorname{ecc}}(V^{+}))\leq\frac{1}{2}\nu_{1}(V^{+})
such that

(6.76)

\left\|\mathbbm{1}_{{V^{+}}\setminus V^{\operatorname{ecc}}(V^{+})}F\right\|_{L^{\overline{q},\infty}_{\mu^{\infty}_{\Theta}}\mathbb{F}^{u}_{\Theta}\Phi_{\mathfrak{r}}^{N}}\lesssim\left\|\mathbbm{1}_{V^{+}}F\right\|_{\not{L}^{q_{0},+}_{\mu^{1}_{\Theta},\nu_{1}}\mathbb{F}^{u}_{\Theta}\Phi_{\mathfrak{r}}^{N}}

for any
q_{0}<\overline{q}<q
. Let us show that this is sufficient. Start by
setting
V_{0}=V
and
V_{k+1}=V^{\operatorname{ecc}}(V_{k})
to obtain that

(6.77)

F=\sum_{k=0}^{\infty}\mathbbm{1}_{V_{k}\setminus V_{k+1}}F_{k}

so by quasi-subadditivity for any
\varepsilon>0
it holds that

(6.78)

\|F\|_{L^{p}_{\nu_{1}}\not{L}^{q}_{\mu^{\infty}_{\Theta},\nu_{1}}\mathbb{F}^{u}_{\Theta}\Phi_{\mathfrak{r}}^{N}}\lesssim\sum_{k=0}^{\infty}2^{\varepsilon k}\left\|\mathbbm{1}_{V_{k}\setminus V_{k+1}}F\right\|_{L^{p}_{\nu_{1}}\not{L}^{q}_{\mu^{\infty}_{\Theta},\nu_{1}}\mathbb{F}^{u}_{\Theta}\Phi_{\mathfrak{r}}^{N}}.

By
Definition

3.17
, by the construction of
V_{k}
, and by
log-convexity of outer Lebesgue norms ((
3.19
) in

Proposition

3.1
) we have that

(6.79)

\displaystyle\left\|\mathbbm{1}_{V_{k}\setminus V_{k+1}}F\right\|_{L^{\infty}_{\nu_{1}}\not{L}^{q}_{\mu^{\infty}_{\Theta},\nu_{1}}\mathbb{F}^{u}_{\Theta}\Phi_{\mathfrak{r}}^{N}}\lesssim\left\|\mathbbm{1}_{V_{k}\setminus V_{k+1}}F\right\|_{L^{\overline{q},\infty}_{\mu^{\infty}_{\Theta}}\mathbb{F}^{u}_{\Theta}\Phi_{\mathfrak{r}}^{N}}^{\overline{q}/q}\left\|\mathbbm{1}_{V_{k}\setminus V_{k+1}}F\right\|_{L^{\infty}_{\mu^{\infty}_{\Theta}}\mathbb{F}^{u}_{\Theta}\Phi_{\mathfrak{r}}^{N}}^{1-\overline{q}/q}

\displaystyle\leq\left\|\mathbbm{1}_{V_{k}}F\right\|_{\not{L}^{q_{0}}_{\mu^{1}_{\Theta},\nu_{1}}\mathbb{F}^{u}_{\Theta}\Phi_{\mathfrak{r}}^{N}}^{\overline{q}/q}\left\|\mathbbm{1}_{V_{k}\setminus V_{k+1}}F\right\|_{\not{L}^{\infty}_{\mu^{1}_{\Theta},\nu_{1}}\mathbb{F}^{u}_{\Theta}\Phi_{\mathfrak{r}}^{N}}^{1-\overline{q}/q}\leq\|F\|_{\not{L}^{q_{0},+}_{\mu^{1}_{\Theta},\nu 1}\mathbb{F}^{u}_{\Theta}\Phi_{\mathfrak{r}}^{N}}.

Since
\mathbbm{1}_{V_{k}\setminus V_{k+1}}F
is supported on
V_{k}
that satisfies

\nu_{1}(V_{k})\leq 2^{-k}\nu(V)
it holds that

(6.80)

\displaystyle\|F\|_{L^{p}_{\nu_{1}}\not{L}^{q}_{\mu^{\infty}_{\Theta},\nu_{1}}\mathbb{F}^{u}_{\Theta}\Phi_{\mathfrak{r}}^{N}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\sum_{k=0}^{\infty}2^{\varepsilon k}2^{-k/p}\nu(V)^{1/p}\left\|\mathbbm{1}_{V_{k}}F\right\|_{\not{L}^{q_{0},+}_{\mu^{1}_{\Theta},\nu_{1}}\mathbb{F}^{u}_{\Theta}\Phi_{\mathfrak{r}}^{N}}\lesssim\nu(V)^{1/p}\|F\|_{\not{L}^{q_{0},+}_{\mu^{1}_{\Theta},\nu_{1}}\mathbb{F}^{u}_{\Theta}\Phi_{\mathfrak{r}}^{N}}

as long as
\varepsilon<\frac{1}{p}
. This is what was required by
(
6.75
).

Let us now show that
(
6.76
)
holds. Assume by
homogeneity that
\|F\|_{\not{L}^{q_{0},+}_{\mu^{1}_{\Theta},\nu_{1}}\mathbb{F}^{u}_{\Theta}\Phi_{\mathfrak{r}}^{N}}=1
. Since
\left\|\mathbbm{1}_{V^{+}}F\right\|_{L^{q_{0},\infty}_{\mu^{1}_{\Theta}}\mathbb{F}^{u}_{\Theta}\Phi_{\mathfrak{r}}^{N}}\leq\left\|\mathbbm{1}_{V^{+}}F\right\|_{L^{q_{0}}_{\mu^{1}_{\Theta}}\mathbb{F}^{u}_{\Theta}\Phi_{\mathfrak{r}}^{N}}
, for any
k\in\mathbb{N}
there exists
W_{k}\in\mathbb{T}^{\cup}_{\Theta}
such that

(6.81)

\left\|\mathbbm{1}_{V^{+}\setminus W_{k}}F\right\|_{\mathbb{F}^{u}_{\Theta}\Phi_{\mathfrak{r}}^{N}}\leq 2^{-\frac{k}{q_{0}}},\qquad\mu^{1}_{\Theta}(W_{k})\leq 2^{k}\nu_{1}(V^{+}).

Let
\mathcal{T}_{k}^{1}\subset\mathbb{T}_{\Theta}
be such that
W_{k}\subset\bigcup_{T\in\mathcal{T}_{k}^{1}}T
and
\left\|N_{\mathcal{T}_{k}^{1}}\right\|_{L^{1}}\lesssim 2^{k}\nu_{1}(V^{+})
. Set

(6.82)

\mathbb{V}_{k}=\left\{x\in\mathbb{R}:\mathrm{M}N_{\mathcal{T}_{k}^{1}}>C2^{\frac{\overline{q}}{q_{0}}k}\right\}

and using the fact that
\mathbb{V}_{k}
is open let us represent it as a
countable union of its connected components (open balls):

(6.83)

\mathbb{V}_{k}=\bigcup_{n\in\mathbb{N}}B_{s_{k,n}}(x_{k,n}).

Setting
V_{k}=\bigcup_{n\in\mathbb{N}}D(x_{k,n},100s_{k,n})
and

V^{\operatorname{ecc}}(V^{+})=\bigcup_{k\in\mathbb{N}}V_{k}
, by the boundedness of the
Hardy Littlewood maximal function we have that

(6.84)

\nu_{1}(V^{\operatorname{ecc}}(V^{+}))\leq\sum_{k\in\mathbb{N}}\nu_{1}(V_{k})\lesssim\sum_{k\in\mathbb{N}}C^{-1}2^{k\left(1-\frac{\overline{q}}{q_{0}}\right)}\nu_{1}(V^{+})\lesssim C^{-1}\nu_{1}(V^{+})

as required, as long as
C>1
is chosen large enough. Let us now show that

(6.85)

\left\|\mathbbm{1}_{V^{+}\setminus(V^{\operatorname{ecc}}(V^{+})\cup W_{k}^{\infty})}F\right\|_{\mathbb{F}^{u}_{\Theta}\Phi_{\mathfrak{r}}^{N}}\leq 2^{-\frac{k}{q_{0}}}\text{
and }\mu^{\infty}_{\Theta}(W_{k}^{\infty})\lesssim 2^{\frac{\overline{q}}{q_{0}}k}

where

(6.86)

\displaystyle W^{\infty}_{k}\coloneqq\bigcup_{T\in\mathcal{T}^{\infty}_{k}}T

\displaystyle\mathcal{T}^{\infty}_{k}\coloneqq\begin{aligned} &\left(\bigcup_{n\in\mathbb{N}}\left\{T\in\mathcal{T}^{1}_{k}:B_{s_{T}}(x_{T})\cap B_{s_{k,n}}(x_{k,n})\neq\emptyset,s_{T}>10s_{k,n}\right\}\right)\\
&\qquad\cup\left\{T\in\mathcal{T}^{1}_{k}:B_{s_{T}}(x_{T})\cap V_{k}=\emptyset\right\}.\end{aligned}

The two bounds imply that
\left\|\mathbbm{1}_{V^{+}\setminus V^{\operatorname{ecc}}(V^{+})}F\right\|_{L^{\overline{q},\infty}_{\mu^{\infty}_{\Theta}}}^{\overline{q}}\lesssim 1
, concluding the proof of
(
6.76
)
. The former bound
holds because
W_{k}\subset V_{k}\cup W^{\infty}_{k}
. As a matter of fact,
W_{k}\subset\bigcup_{T\in\mathcal{T}_{k}^{1}}T
and if
T\in\mathcal{T}_{k}^{1}\setminus\mathcal{T}^{\infty}_{k}
then there exists
n\in\mathbb{N}
such that
B_{s_{T}}(x_{T})\cap B_{s_{k,n}}(x_{k,n})
and
s_{T}<10s_{k,n}
but then
B_{s_{T}}(x_{T})\subset B_{100s_{k,n}}(x_{k,n})
and thus
T\subset V_{k}
.

To show that
\mu^{\infty}_{\Theta}(W_{k}^{\infty})\lesssim 2^{\frac{\overline{q}}{q_{0}}k}
it is sufficient to show that

N_{\mathcal{T}_{k}^{\infty}}(x)\lesssim 2^{\frac{\overline{q}}{q_{0}}k}
for all
x\in\mathbb{R}
. If
x\notin\mathbb{V}_{k}
then the claim follows by the definition
of
\mathbb{V}_{k}
and from the fact that
N_{\mathcal{T}_{k}^{\infty}}(x)\leq N_{\mathcal{T}_{k}^{1}}(x)\leq\mathrm{M}N_{\mathcal{T}_{k}^{1}}(x)
. If
x\in\mathbb{V}_{k}
then
x\in B_{s_{k,n}}(x_{k,n})
for some
n\in\mathbb{N}
. Let

(6.87)

\mathcal{T}_{k}^{\infty}(x)\coloneqq\left\{T\in\mathcal{T}_{k}^{\infty}:B_{s_{T}}(x_{T})\ni x\right\}

so that
N_{\mathcal{T}_{k}^{\infty}}(x)
coincides with the cardinality of

\mathcal{T}_{k}^{\infty}(x)
. Given the way
\mathcal{T}_{k}^{\infty}
was defined for
any
T\in\mathcal{T}_{k}^{\infty}(x)
it holds that
s_{T}>10s_{k,n}
and
thus either
B_{s_{T}}(x_{T})\supset(x,x+4s_{T})
or
B_{s_{T}}(x_{T})\supset(x-4s_{T},x)
showing that

(6.88)

\int_{B_{3s_{k,n}}(x_{k,n})}N_{\mathcal{T}^{\infty}_{k}}(y)\mathrm{d}y>3s_{k,n}N_{\mathcal{T}_{k}^{\infty}}(x).

Thus for any
y\in B_{3s_{k,n}}(x_{k,n})
it holds that
\mathrm{M}N_{\mathcal{T}_{k}^{1}}(y)\gtrsim N_{\mathcal{T}_{k}^{\infty}}(x)
while we know from the
maximality of
B_{s_{k,n}}(x_{k,n})
that there exists
y\in B_{3s_{k,n}}(x_{k,n})\setminus B_{s_{k,n}}(x_{k,n})
with
\mathrm{M}N_{\mathcal{T}_{k}^{1}}(y)<C2^{\frac{\overline{q}}{q_{0}}k}
, showing the claimed bound

N_{\mathcal{T}_{k}^{\infty}}(x)\lesssim 2^{\frac{\overline{q}}{q_{0}}k}
.
∎

7.
Uniform embedding bounds - linear case

In this section we prove
Theorem

3
about the
linear embedding bounds (
1.20
). of.
Recall from the setup of
Theorem

3
that

\Gamma=\Gamma_{(\alpha,\beta,\gamma)}
is as described (
3.103
),

\Theta
is an bounded open interval with
B_{4}\subsetneq\Theta\subsetneq\mathbb{R}
, and let
\Theta^{\operatorname{in}}
is a bounded open interval with
B_{2^{-5}}(-\gamma)\subsetneq\Theta^{\operatorname{in}}\subsetneq B_{2^{-3}}(-\gamma)

and let
\Theta^{\operatorname{ex}}\coloneqq\Theta\setminus\Theta^{\operatorname{in}}
. We will
consistently use the notation
\theta_{\Gamma}\coloneqq\alpha\beta(\theta+\gamma)
as introduced in
Section

3.3
. Furthermore

\Theta_{\Gamma}\coloneqq\alpha\beta(\Theta+\gamma)
,

\Theta^{\operatorname{in}}_{\Gamma}\coloneqq\{\theta_{\Gamma}:\theta\in\Theta^{\operatorname{in}}\}
, and
\Theta^{\operatorname{ex}}_{\Gamma}\coloneqq\{\theta_{\Gamma}:\theta\in\Theta^{\operatorname{ex}}\}
. As in the rest of the
paper we assume that the parameter
N
is large enough and
\mathfrak{r}>0
is small
enough. These parameters, that govern the rate of decay and the Fourier
support of wave packets
\varphi
appear in the size
\widetilde{\mathbb{F}}^{u}_{\Gamma}\Phi_{\mathfrak{r}}^{N}
and are ubiquitous in the subsequent section. The values
N>100
and
\mathfrak{r}<2^{-10}
are, in particular, always admissible and the choice
of
N
and
\mathfrak{r}
can be made independently of
\beta\in(0,1]
. All implicit
constants appearing in
Theorem

3
and in this
entire section are uniform in
\beta\in(0,1]
. The constants are also
uniform in
\Theta
and
\mathfrak{r}
as long as
|\Theta|
is uniformly bounded and

\mathfrak{r}>0
is bounded away from
0
.

While
Theorem

3
holds for all
\beta\in(0,1]
, we are mainly interested in the case
0<\beta\ll 1
.

In this section, for simplicity, the reader may assume that
\alpha=\beta^{-1}
and
\gamma=0
. This maintains the important features of the subsequent
discussion and of the proofs but avoids unnecessary complications. Under these
assumption one has that
\theta_{\Gamma}=\theta
,
\Theta_{\Gamma}=\Theta
. There does not, however, seem to be a direct way to deduce the
statements below and
Theorem

3
itself in
their full generality from these restricted assumptions on the parameters

(\alpha,\beta,\gamma)
.

The boundedness properties the map
F\mapsto F\circ\Gamma
are encoded in
the two propositions below. They allow us to control outer Lebesgue
quasi-norms with two auxiliary sizes that do not appear, per se, in the size

\widetilde{\mathbb{F}}^{u}_{\Gamma}\Phi_{\mathfrak{r}}^{N}
. However, for functions of the
form
F=\mathrm{Emb}[f]
these auxiliary sizes can be used to control outer
Lebesgue quasi-norms with size
\widetilde{\mathbb{F}}^{u}_{\Gamma}\Phi_{\mathfrak{r}}^{N}
.

Proposition 7.1

(Uniform bounds for exterior size)
.

The bounds

(7.1)

\displaystyle\|F\circ\Gamma\|_{L^{r}_{\mu_{\Theta}^{1}}\Gamma^{\ast}\mathbb{L}^{(u,2)}_{(\Theta,\Theta^{\operatorname{ex}})}\Phi_{\mathfrak{r}}^{N}}\lesssim\|F\|_{L^{r}_{\mu_{\Theta_{\Gamma}}^{1}}\left(\mathbb{L}^{(\infty,\infty)}_{\Theta_{\Gamma}}+\mathbb{L}^{(\overline{u},2)}_{(\Theta_{\Gamma},\Theta^{\operatorname{ex}}_{\Gamma})}\right)\Phi_{2\mathfrak{r}}^{N-4}},

hold for all
F\in L^{\infty}_{\operatorname{loc}}(\mathbb{R}^{3}_{+}){\otimes\Phi_{2\mathfrak{r}}^{\infty}}^{\prime}
as long as
2\leq u<r\leq+\infty
, and as
long as
\overline{u}\in(u,\infty)
is sufficiently large, depending on
(u,r)
.

Proposition 7.2

(Uniform bounds for singular size)
.

Let
V\in\mathbb{D}^{\cup}_{\beta}
,
W\in\mathbb{T}_{\Theta}^{\cup}
and
E=V\cap W

or
E=V\cup W
. The bounds

(7.2)

\displaystyle\left\|F\circ\Gamma(\eta,y,t)t\mathrm{d}_{t}\mathbbm{1}_{E}(\eta,y,t)\right\|_{L^{r}_{\mu_{\Theta}^{1}}\Gamma^{{}^{\ast}}\mathbb{L}^{(u,1)}_{(\Theta,\Theta^{\operatorname{in}})}\Phi_{\mathfrak{r}}^{N}}\qquad

\displaystyle\lesssim\|F(\eta,y,t)\|_{L^{r}_{\mu_{\Theta_{\Gamma}}^{1}}\mathbb{L}^{(\infty,\infty)}_{\Theta_{\Gamma}}\Phi_{2\mathfrak{r}}^{N-4}}

hold for all
F\in L^{\infty}_{\operatorname{loc}}(\mathbb{R}^{3}_{+}){\otimes\Phi_{2\mathfrak{r}}^{\infty}}^{\prime}
as long as
1<u<r\leq\infty
. The implicit
constant is independent of
E
. The derivative in
t\mathrm{d}_{t}\mathbbm{1}_{E}(\eta,y,t)
is intended in the distributional sense and is a Radon measure on

\mathbb{R}^{3}_{+}
.

These two statements are proved in
Section

7.1
and in

Section

7.2
respectively. The two sections follow a
similar outline: first we prove a geometric covering lemma on
\mathbb{R}^{3}_{+}

(Lemmata
7.4
and
7.6
),
from this we deduce a weak-endpoint result for scalar valued functions
F\in\mathscr{R}(\mathbb{R}^{3}_{+})
. Finally, using restricted weak interpolation and

Lemma

3.13
we deduce the desired claim.

The next lemma shows that sizes
\Gamma^{\ast}\mathbb{L}^{(u,2)}_{(\Theta,\Theta^{\operatorname{ex}})}\Phi_{\mathfrak{r}}^{N}
and
\Gamma^{{}^{\ast}}\mathbb{L}^{(u,1)}_{(\Theta,\Theta^{\operatorname{in}})}\Phi_{\mathfrak{r}}^{N}
can be used to control the
size
\widetilde{\mathbb{F}}^{u}_{\Gamma}\Phi_{\mathfrak{r}}^{N}
, that appears in

Theorem

3
.

Lemma 7.3

(Uniform bounds for the
\widetilde{\mathbb{F}}_{\Gamma}^{u}\Phi_{\mathfrak{r}}^{N}

size)
.

Let
V^{+},V^{-}\in\mathbb{D}^{\cup}_{\beta}
and
W^{+}\in\mathbb{T}_{\Theta}^{\cup}
. The bounds

(7.3)

\displaystyle\Bigl\|\mathbbm{1}_{(V^{+}\cap W^{+})\setminus V^{-}}\mathrm{Emb}[f]\circ\Gamma\Bigr\|_{L^{r}_{\mu^{1}_{\Theta}}\widetilde{\mathbb{F}}^{u}_{\Gamma}{\Phi_{\mathfrak{r}}^{N}}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\begin{aligned} &\Bigl\|\mathbbm{1}_{(V^{+}\cap W^{+})\setminus V^{-}}\mathrm{Emb}[f]\circ\Gamma\Bigr\|_{L^{r}_{\mu^{1}_{\Theta}}\Gamma^{\ast}\mathbb{L}^{(u,2)}_{(\Theta,\Theta^{\operatorname{ex}})}\Phi_{16\mathfrak{r}}^{N-10}}\\
&+\Bigl\|\mathbbm{1}_{\overline{(V^{+}\cap W^{+})}\setminus V^{-}}\mathrm{Emb}[f]\circ\Gamma(\eta,y,t)t\mathrm{d}_{t}\mathbbm{1}_{(V^{+}\cap W^{+})}(\eta,y,t)\Bigr\|_{L^{r}_{\mu^{1}_{\Theta}}{\Gamma^{\ast}}\mathbb{L}^{(u,1)}_{(\Theta,\Theta^{\operatorname{in}})}\Phi_{16\mathfrak{r}}^{N-10}}\\
&+\Bigl\|\mathbbm{1}_{\overline{(V^{+}\cap W^{+})}\setminus V^{-}}\mathrm{Emb}[f]\circ\Gamma(\eta,y,t)t\mathrm{d}_{t}\mathbbm{1}_{\left(\mathbb{R}^{3}_{+}\setminus V^{-}\right)}(\eta,y,t)\Bigr\|_{L^{r}_{\mu^{1}_{\Theta}}{\Gamma^{\ast}}\mathbb{L}^{(u,1)}_{(\Theta,\Theta^{\operatorname{in}})}\Phi_{16\mathfrak{r}}^{N-10}}\end{aligned}

hold for any
f\in\mathscr{S}(\mathbb{R})
and for all
r\in(u,\infty]
.
The implicit constant is independent of
V^{+}
,
V^{-}
, and
W^{+}
.

We prove this lemma in
Section

7.3
. Now we conclude by
showing how
Proposition

7.1
,

Proposition

7.2
, and

Lemma

7.3
can be used to deduce the embedding bounds
(
1.20
) of

Theorem

3
.

Proof.

Proof of
Theorem

3

Using homogeneity let us assume that
\|f_{j}\|_{L^{p_{j}}(\mathbb{R})}=1
. According to the discussion in Remark

Remark

3.19
we need to exhibit a choice of
sets
V^{-}(\lambda)\in\mathbb{D}^{\cup}_{\beta}
such that

(7.4)

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{\infty}\nu_{\beta}\left({V^{-}}(\lambda^{1/p})\right)\mathrm{d}\lambda\lesssim 1,

\displaystyle\left\|\mathbbm{1}_{\mathbb{R}^{3}_{+}\setminus V^{-}(\lambda)}\left(\mathrm{Emb}[f]\circ\Gamma\right)\right\|_{X^{q,r,+}_{\mu^{1}_{\Theta},\nu_{\beta}}\widetilde{\mathbb{F}}^{u}_{\Gamma}\Phi_{\mathfrak{r}}^{N}}\lesssim\lambda.

Using
Theorem

5
. with
N+14
,
32\mathfrak{r}
and
some large enough
\overline{u}\in(2,\infty)
in place of
N
,
\mathfrak{r}
, and
u
,
we can find
\widetilde{V^{-}}(\lambda)\in\mathbb{D}^{\cup}_{1}
such that

(7.5)

\int_{0}^{\infty}\nu_{\beta}(\widetilde{V^{-}}(\lambda^{1/p}))\mathrm{d}\lambda\lesssim 1,\qquad\left\|\mathbbm{1}_{\mathbb{R}^{3}_{+}\setminus\widetilde{V^{-}}(\lambda)}\mathrm{Emb}[f]\right\|_{\not{L}^{q,+}_{\mu^{\textit{}\infty}_{\Theta_{\Gamma}},\nu_{1}}\mathbb{F}^{\overline{u}}_{\Theta}\Phi_{32\mathfrak{r}}^{N+14}}\lesssim\lambda.

Recall that
\mathbb{F}^{\overline{u}}_{\Theta}\Phi_{32\mathfrak{r}}^{N+14}\gtrsim\mathbb{L}^{(\infty,\infty)}_{\Theta_{\Gamma}}\Phi_{32\mathfrak{r}}^{N+14}+\mathbb{L}^{(\overline{u},2)}_{(\Theta_{\Gamma},\Theta^{\operatorname{ex}}_{\Gamma})}\Phi_{32\mathfrak{r}}^{N+14}
because
\mathbb{L}^{(\overline{u},2)}_{\Theta}\mathfrak{d}_{32\mathfrak{r}}^{N+14}\gtrsim\mathbb{L}^{(\overline{u},2)}_{(\Theta_{\Gamma},\Theta^{\operatorname{ex}}_{\Gamma})}\Phi_{32\mathfrak{r}}^{N+14}
since
\Theta^{\operatorname{ex}}_{\Gamma}\cap B_{64\mathfrak{r}}=\emptyset
. We set
V^{-}(\lambda)\coloneqq\Gamma^{-1}(\widetilde{V^{-}}(\lambda))\in\mathbb{D}^{\cup}_{\beta}
and we claim that

(7.6)

\displaystyle\nu_{\beta}(V^{-}(\lambda))=\nu_{1}(\widetilde{V^{-}}(\lambda))

\displaystyle\left\|\mathbbm{1}_{\mathbb{R}^{3}_{+}\setminus V^{-}(\lambda)}\left(\mathrm{Emb}[f]\circ\Gamma\right)\right\|_{X^{q,r,+}_{\mu^{1}_{\Theta},\nu_{\beta}}\widetilde{\mathbb{F}}^{u}_{\Gamma}\Phi_{\mathfrak{r}}^{N}}\lesssim\lambda.

The first bound follows since
\Gamma(D_{\beta}(x,s))=D_{1}(x,s)
and

\nu_{\beta}(D_{\beta}(x,s))=\nu_{1}(D_{1}(x,s))=s
. To show the
latter bound, according to Remark
Remark

3.19
,
we need to prove that for any
V^{+}\in\mathbb{D}^{\cup}_{\beta}
and
W^{+}\in\mathbb{T}_{\Theta}^{\cup}
, and for
\overline{r}\in\{r,q\}
the bounds

(7.7)

\left\|\mathbbm{1}_{(V^{+}\cap W^{+})\setminus V^{-}(\lambda)}\left(\mathrm{Emb}[f]\circ\Gamma\right)\right\|_{L^{\overline{r}}_{\mu^{1}_{\Theta}}\widetilde{\mathbb{F}}^{u}_{\Gamma}\Phi_{\mathfrak{r}}^{N}}\lesssim\lambda\nu_{\beta}(V^{+})^{\frac{1}{\overline{r}}}\mu^{\infty}_{\Theta}(W^{+})^{\frac{1}{\overline{r}}-\frac{1}{q}}

hold.
Lemma

7.3
allows us, instead of working with
the size
\widetilde{\mathbb{F}}^{u}_{\Gamma}\Phi_{\mathfrak{r}}^{N}
directly, to work
with two alternative quantities: specifically those appearing in

\mathrm{LHS}{\eqref{prop:unif-gamma-bounds:Sex}}
and

\mathrm{LHS}{\eqref{prop:unif-gamma-bounds:singular-size}}
. More precisely,
according to
Lemma

7.3
it is sufficient to prove
that

(7.8)

\Bigl\|\mathbbm{1}_{(V^{+}\cap W^{+})\setminus V^{-}(\lambda)}\mathrm{Emb}[f]\circ\Gamma\Bigr\|_{L^{\overline{r}}_{\mu^{1}_{\Theta}}\Gamma^{\ast}\mathbb{L}^{(u,2)}_{(\Theta,\Theta^{\operatorname{ex}})}\Phi_{16\mathfrak{r}}^{N-10}}\lesssim\lambda\nu_{\beta}(V^{+})^{\frac{1}{\overline{r}}}\mu^{\infty}_{\Theta}(W^{+})^{\frac{1}{\overline{r}}-\frac{1}{q}},

and that

(7.9)

\displaystyle\begin{aligned} &\Bigl\|\mathbbm{1}_{\overline{(V^{+}\cap W^{+})}\setminus V^{-}(\lambda)}\mathrm{Emb}[f]\circ\Gamma(\eta,y,t)t\mathrm{d}_{t}\mathbbm{1}_{(V^{+}\cap W^{+})}(\eta,y,t)\Bigr\|_{L^{\overline{r}}_{\mu^{1}_{\Theta}}{\Gamma^{\ast}}\mathbb{L}^{(u,1)}_{(\Theta,\Theta^{\operatorname{in}})}\Phi_{16\mathfrak{r}}^{N-10}}\\
&\qquad+\Bigl\|\mathbbm{1}_{\overline{(V^{+}\cap W^{+})}\setminus V^{-}(\lambda)}\mathrm{Emb}[f]\circ\Gamma(\eta,y,t)t\mathrm{d}_{t}\mathbbm{1}_{\left(\mathbb{R}^{3}_{+}\setminus V^{-}\right)}(\eta,y,t)\Bigr\|_{L^{\overline{r}}_{\mu^{1}_{\Theta}}{\Gamma^{\ast}}\mathbb{L}^{(u,1)}_{(\Theta,\Theta^{\operatorname{in}})}\Phi_{16\mathfrak{r}}^{N-10}}\end{aligned}

\displaystyle\lesssim\lambda\nu_{\beta}(V^{+})^{\frac{1}{\overline{r}}}\mu^{\infty}_{\Theta}(W^{+})^{\frac{1}{\overline{r}}-\frac{1}{q}}.

Since
Proposition

7.1
and

Proposition

7.2
show that

(7.10)

\mathrm{LHS}{}\eqref{eq:ueb:local-claim-Sex}+\mathrm{LHS}{}\eqref{eq:ueb:local-claim-sing}\leq\left\|\mathbbm{1}_{\Gamma((V^{+}\cap W^{+})\setminus V^{-}(\lambda))}\mathrm{Emb}[f]\right\|_{L^{\overline{r}}_{\mu_{\Theta_{\Gamma}}^{1}}\left(\mathbb{L}^{(\infty,\infty)}_{\Theta_{\Gamma}}+\mathbb{L}^{(\overline{u},2)}_{(\Theta_{\Gamma},\Theta^{\operatorname{ex}}_{\Gamma})}\right)\Phi_{32\mathfrak{r}}^{N-14}}

as long as
\overline{u}
is large enough, all we need to do is show that

(7.11)

\displaystyle\left\|\mathbbm{1}_{\Gamma((V^{+}\cap W^{+})\setminus V^{-}(\lambda))}\mathrm{Emb}[f]\right\|_{L^{\overline{r}}_{\mu_{\Theta_{\Gamma}}^{1}}\left(\mathbb{L}^{(\infty,\infty)}_{\Theta_{\Gamma}}+\mathbb{L}^{(\overline{u},2)}_{(\Theta_{\Gamma},\Theta^{\operatorname{ex}}_{\Gamma})}\right)\Phi_{32\mathfrak{r}}^{N-14}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\lambda\nu_{\beta}(V^{+})^{\frac{1}{\overline{r}}}\mu^{\infty}_{\Theta}(W^{+})^{\frac{1}{\overline{r}}-\frac{1}{q}},

which will be the goal for the rest of the proof. This follows essentially
by (
3.18
), up to controlling

\mu^{1}_{\Theta_{\Gamma}}(W^{+})
. More precisely, since
\overline{r}\leq q
, by
(
3.18
) it holds that

(7.12)

\displaystyle\left\|\mathbbm{1}_{\Gamma((V^{+}\cap W^{+})\setminus V^{-}(\lambda))}\mathrm{Emb}[f]\right\|_{L^{\overline{r}}_{\mu_{\Theta_{\Gamma}}^{1}}\left(\mathbb{L}^{(\infty,\infty)}_{\Theta_{\Gamma}}+\mathbb{L}^{(\overline{u},2)}_{(\Theta_{\Gamma},\Theta^{\operatorname{ex}}_{\Gamma})}\right)\Phi_{32\mathfrak{r}}^{N-14}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\mu^{1}_{\Theta_{\Gamma}}(\Gamma(V^{+})\cap\Gamma(W^{+}))^{\frac{1}{\overline{r}}-\frac{1}{q}}\left\|\mathbbm{1}_{\Gamma((V^{+}\cap W^{+})\setminus V^{-}(\lambda))}F\right\|_{L^{q}_{\mu_{\Theta_{\Gamma}}^{1}}\left(\mathbb{L}^{(\infty,\infty)}_{\Theta_{\Gamma}}+\mathbb{L}^{(\overline{u},2)}_{(\Theta_{\Gamma},\Theta^{\operatorname{ex}}_{\Gamma})}\right)\Phi_{32\mathfrak{r}}^{N-14}},

while
Lemma

3.18
applied with
\beta=1
gives
that

(7.13)

\mu^{1}_{\Theta_{\Gamma}}(\Gamma(V^{+})\cap W)\lesssim\nu_{1}(\Gamma(V^{+}))\mu^{\infty}_{\Theta_{\Gamma}}(\Gamma(V^{+})\cap W)\quad\forall W\in\mathbb{T}^{\cup}_{\Theta_{\Gamma}}.

Using the above bound and (
3.16
) we obtain
that

(7.14)

\displaystyle\left\|\mathbbm{1}_{\Gamma((V^{+}\cap W^{+})\setminus V^{-})}F\right\|_{L^{q}_{\mu_{\Theta_{\Gamma}}^{1}}\left(\mathbb{L}^{(\infty,\infty)}_{\Theta_{\Gamma}}+\mathbb{L}^{(\overline{u},2)}_{(\Theta_{\Gamma},\Theta^{\operatorname{ex}}_{\Gamma})}\right)\Phi_{32\mathfrak{r}}^{N-14}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\nu_{1}(\Gamma(V^{+}))^{\frac{1}{q}}\left\|\mathbbm{1}_{\Gamma((V^{+}\cap W^{+})\setminus V^{-})}F\right\|_{L^{q}_{\mu_{\Theta_{\Gamma}}^{\infty}}\left(\mathbb{L}^{(\infty,\infty)}_{\Theta_{\Gamma}}+\mathbb{L}^{(\overline{u},2)}_{(\Theta_{\Gamma},\Theta^{\operatorname{ex}}_{\Gamma})}\right)\Phi_{32\mathfrak{r}}^{N-14}}.

We also have that

(7.15)

\mu^{1}_{\Theta_{\Gamma}}(\Gamma(V^{+})\cap\Gamma(W^{+}))^{\frac{1}{\overline{r}}-\frac{1}{q}}\leq\nu_{\beta}(V^{+})^{\frac{1}{\overline{r}}-\frac{1}{q}}\mu^{\infty}_{\Theta}(W^{+})^{\frac{1}{\overline{r}}-\frac{1}{q}}

because
\Gamma(V^{+})\in\mathbb{D}^{\cup}_{1}
,
\Gamma(W^{+})\in\mathbb{T}_{\Theta_{\Gamma}}^{\cup}
,
\nu_{1}(\Gamma(V^{+}))=\nu_{\beta}(V^{+})
,
and

\mu^{\infty}_{\Theta}(W^{+})=\mu^{\infty}_{\Theta_{\Gamma}}(\Gamma(W^{+}))
. The estimates above, combined, give that

(7.16)

\displaystyle\left\|\mathbbm{1}_{\Gamma((V^{+}\cap W^{+})\setminus V^{-})}\mathrm{Emb}[f]\right\|_{L^{\overline{r}}_{\mu_{\Theta_{\Gamma}}^{1}}\left(\mathbb{L}^{(\infty,\infty)}_{\Theta_{\Gamma}}+\mathbb{L}^{(\overline{u},2)}_{(\Theta_{\Gamma},\Theta^{\operatorname{ex}}_{\Gamma})}\right)\Phi_{32\mathfrak{r}}^{N-14}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\nu_{\beta}(V^{+})^{\frac{1}{\overline{r}}}\mu^{\infty}_{\Theta}(W^{+})^{\frac{1}{\overline{r}}-\frac{1}{q}}\left\|\mathbbm{1}_{\Gamma((V^{+}\cap W^{+})\setminus V^{-})}\mathrm{Emb}[f]\right\|_{L^{q}_{\mu_{\Theta_{\Gamma}}^{\infty}}\left(\mathbb{L}^{(\infty,\infty)}_{\Theta_{\Gamma}}+\mathbb{L}^{(\overline{u},2)}_{(\Theta_{\Gamma},\Theta^{\operatorname{ex}}_{\Gamma})}\right)\Phi_{32\mathfrak{r}}^{N-14}}.

This is the required bound (
7.8
) since
\Gamma(V^{-}(\lambda))=\widetilde{V^{-}}(\lambda)
so

(7.17)

\displaystyle\left\|\mathbbm{1}_{\Gamma((V^{+}\cap W^{+})\setminus V^{-}(\lambda))}\mathrm{Emb}[f]\right\|_{L^{q}_{\mu_{\Theta_{\Gamma}}^{\infty}}\left(\mathbb{L}^{(\infty,\infty)}_{\Theta_{\Gamma}}+\mathbb{L}^{(\overline{u},2)}_{(\Theta_{\Gamma},\Theta^{\operatorname{ex}}_{\Gamma})}\right)\Phi_{32\mathfrak{r}}^{N-14}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\left\|\mathbbm{1}_{\mathbb{R}^{3}_{+}\setminus\widetilde{V^{-}}(\lambda)}\mathrm{Emb}[f]\right\|_{\not{L}^{q,+}_{\mu^{\textit{}\infty}_{\Theta_{\Gamma}},\nu_{1}}\mathbb{F}^{\overline{u}}_{\Theta}\Phi_{32\mathfrak{r}}^{N-14}}\lesssim\lambda

by construction.
∎

7.1.
Uniform bounds for the exterior
size

We now prove
Proposition

7.1
. We begin with a uniform
covering argument.

Lemma 7.4

(Exterior tree covering)
.

Suppose
F\in\mathscr{R}(\mathbb{R}^{3}_{+})
is a compactly
supported Radon measure. For all
\lambda>0
there exists a set

W_{\lambda}\subset\mathbb{T}^{\cup}_{\Theta}
and finite collection of trees

\mathcal{T}\subset\mathbb{T}_{\Theta}
such that

(7.18)

\Bigl\|\mathbbm{1}_{\mathbb{R}^{3}_{+}\setminus W_{\lambda}}F\Bigr\|_{\mathbb{L}^{(1,1)}_{(\Theta,\Theta^{\operatorname{ex}})}}\leq\lambda,\qquad W_{\lambda}\supset\bigcup_{T\in\mathcal{T}}T,\qquad\mu^{1}_{\Theta}(W_{\lambda})\lesssim\sum_{T\in\mathcal{T}}\mu^{1}_{\Theta}(T).

Each
T\in\mathcal{T}
is endowed with a distinguished subset
X_{T}\subset\pi_{T}\left(\mathfrak{T}_{\Theta^{\operatorname{ex}}}\right)
such that

(7.19)

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\mu^{1}_{\Theta}(T)}\int_{X_{T}}|F(\eta,y,t)|\mathrm{d}\eta\mathrm{d}y\mathrm{d}t\geq\lambda.

The subsets
X_{T}
satisfy

(7.20)

\left\|\sum_{T\in\mathcal{T}}\mathbbm{1}_{\Gamma(X_{T})}(\eta,y,t)\right\|_{\mathbb{L}^{(\infty,\infty)}_{\Theta_{\Gamma}}}\lesssim 1

and

(7.21)

\left\|\sum_{T\in\mathcal{T}}\mathbbm{1}_{\Gamma(X_{T})}(\eta,y,t)\right\|_{\mathbb{L}^{(1,1)}_{(\Theta_{\Gamma},\Theta^{\operatorname{in}}_{\Gamma})}}\lesssim 1.

The implicit constants do not depend on
\Gamma
.

Proof.

Let us define local sizes
{\mathbb{L}^{(1,1)}_{(\Theta,\Theta^{\operatorname{ex}+})}}

and
{\mathbb{L}^{(1,1)}_{(\Theta,\Theta^{\operatorname{ex}-})}}
by setting

(7.22)

\displaystyle\Theta^{\operatorname{ex}\pm}\coloneqq\Theta\cap\{\theta:\pm\theta\geq\gamma\}.

It suffices to prove this lemma with
{\mathbb{L}^{(1,1)}_{(\Theta,\Theta^{\operatorname{ex}+})}}
in place of
{\mathbb{L}^{(1,1)}_{(\Theta,\Theta^{\operatorname{ex}})}}
. A symmetric proof handles the size
{\mathbb{L}^{(1,1)}_{(\Theta,\Theta^{\operatorname{ex}-})}}
and the two results can then be
added together.

Without loss of generality we assume that
F
is supported on
\mathbb{K}\subset[-S;S]^{2}\times[S^{-1};S]
for some
S\gg 1
, and by
homogeneity we assume that
\|F\|_{{{\mathbb{L}^{(1,1)}_{(\Theta,\Theta^{\operatorname{ex}+})}}}}=1
. This guarantees a bound on the parameters

(\xi_{T},x_{T},s_{T})
of any tree
T\in\mathbb{T}_{\Theta}
for which

\|F\|_{{{\mathbb{L}^{(1,1)}_{(\Theta,\Theta^{\operatorname{ex}+})}}}(T)}\geq\lambda
. As a matter of fact, it must hold that
s_{T}>S^{-1}
, because
otherwise
T_{\Theta}(\xi_{T},x_{T},s_{T})\cap\mathbb{K}=\emptyset
. It
also holds that
s_{T}\leq 2S\lambda^{-1}
because

(7.23)

\displaystyle s_{T}\|F\|_{{{\mathbb{L}^{(1,1)}_{(\Theta,\Theta^{\operatorname{ex}+})}}}(T_{\Theta}(\xi_{T},x_{T},s_{T}))}\leq 2S\|F\|_{{{\mathbb{L}^{(1,1)}_{(\Theta,\Theta^{\operatorname{ex}+})}}}(T_{\Theta}(\xi_{T},0,2S))}\leq 2S

since
T_{\Theta}(\xi_{T},x_{T},s_{T})\cap\mathbb{K}\subset T_{\Theta}(\xi_{T},0,2S)\cap\mathbb{K}
and since we assumed that

\|F\|_{{{\mathbb{L}^{(1,1)}_{(\Theta,\Theta^{\operatorname{ex}+})}}}}=1
. It also
holds that
|x_{T}|<S+2S\lambda^{-1}
because otherwise, again

T_{\Theta}(\xi_{T},x_{T},s_{T})\cap\mathbb{K}=\emptyset
. For the same
reason we must have that
|\xi_{T}|<4S^{2}(S^{2}+|\Theta|)\lambda^{-1}
.

We want to construct
\mathcal{T}
by an iterative algorithm. The following
preparatory step guarantees that the algorithm will terminate. We discretize
the set of all trees by setting

(7.24)

\displaystyle\mathbb{R}^{3,\operatorname{latt}}_{+}:=\left\{(\eta,y,t):\eta t,yt^{-1}\in\mathbb{Z},t\in 2^{\mathbb{Z}}\right\},

\displaystyle\mathbb{T}_{\Theta}^{\operatorname{latt}}:=\left\{T_{\Theta}(\eta,y,t):(\eta,y,t)\in\mathbb{R}^{3,\operatorname{latt}}_{+}\right\}.

Given any
T\in\mathbb{T}_{\Theta}
we associate to it a collection of
trees
\mathcal{W}(T)\subset\mathbb{T}_{\Theta}^{\operatorname{latt}}
given by

(7.25)

\mathcal{W}(T):=\left\{T_{\Theta}(\xi,x,s)\in\mathbb{T}_{\Theta}^{\operatorname{latt}}:1\leq\frac{s}{s_{T}}<2^{4},|x-x_{T}|\leq 2s_{T},s_{T}|\xi-\xi_{T}|<2^{8}\right\}.

It holds that
\mu_{\Theta}^{1}\bigl(\bigcup_{T^{\prime}\in\mathcal{W}(T)}T^{\prime}\bigr)\lesssim\mu_{\Theta}^{1}(T)
and

\bigcup_{\heartsuit\in\{-1,0,1\}}T_{(\xi_{T}+\heartsuit s_{T}^{-1},x_{T},s_{T})}\subset\bigcup_{T^{\prime}\in\mathcal{W}(T)}T^{\prime}
. Take
\mathcal{K}^{\operatorname{latt}}_{\lambda}\subset\mathbb{T}_{\Theta}^{\operatorname{latt}}
to be

(7.26)

\mathcal{K}^{\operatorname{latt}}_{\lambda}:=\bigcup_{(\xi_{T},x_{T},s_{T})\in A\subset\mathbb{R}^{3}_{+}}\mathcal{W}(T_{\Theta}(\xi_{T},x_{T},s_{T}))\subset\mathbb{T}_{\Theta}^{\operatorname{latt}}

where

(7.27)

A\coloneqq\{(\xi_{T},x_{T},s_{T}):S^{-1}\leq s_{T}\leq 2S\lambda^{-1},|x_{T}|\leq S+2S\lambda^{-1},|\xi_{T}|\leq 4S^{2}(S^{2}+|\Theta|)\lambda^{-1}\},

so that the trees in
\mathcal{K}^{\operatorname{latt}}_{\lambda}
cover

\mathbb{K}
and so that for any tree
T\in\mathbb{T}_{\Theta}
such that

\|F\|_{{{\mathbb{L}^{(1,1)}_{(\Theta,\Theta^{\operatorname{ex}+})}}}(T)}\geq\lambda
it holds that
\mathcal{W}(T)\subset\mathcal{K}^{\operatorname{latt}}_{\lambda}
. The collection

\mathcal{K}^{\operatorname{latt}}_{\lambda}
is finite, since the tops of trees in

\mathcal{K}^{\operatorname{latt}}_{\lambda}
are a bounded subset of the discrete
set
\mathbb{R}^{3,\operatorname{latt}}_{+}
.

Let us now proceed to the actual selection algorithm. Given a collection of
trees
\mathcal{X}\subset\mathbb{T}_{\Theta}
we say that
T\in\mathcal{X}
is
quasi-maximal
if

(7.28)

T^{\prime}\in\mathcal{X}\Longrightarrow\xi_{T^{\prime}}\leq\xi_{T}+\varepsilon_{\max}

with a constant
\varepsilon_{\max}=\varepsilon_{\max}(\lambda,S)
to be
determined later. We start with the collection
\mathcal{T}_{0}=\emptyset

and let
\mathbb{K}_{0}:=\mathbb{K}
. Suppose that at step
n
we have
a collection
\mathcal{T}_{n}=\{T_{1},\ldots,T_{n}\}\subset\mathbb{T}_{\Theta}
and a subset
\mathbb{K}_{n}\subset\mathbb{K}
. At
step
n+1
let

(7.29)

\mathcal{X}_{n+1}:=\Bigl\{T\in\mathbb{T}_{\Theta}:\|\mathbbm{1}_{{\mathbb{K}_{n}}}F\|_{{{\mathbb{L}^{(1,1)}_{(\Theta,\Theta^{\operatorname{ex}+})}}}(T)}\geq\lambda\Bigr\};

if
\mathcal{X}_{n+1}
is empty, we terminate the iteration, otherwise we
choose a quasi-maximal element
T_{n+1}
of
\mathcal{X}_{n+1}
; this
can be done since
\Bigl\{(\xi,x,s)\in\mathbb{R}^{3}_{+}:T_{\Theta}(\xi,x,s)\in\mathcal{X}_{n+1}\Bigr\}
is precompact and non-empty, and
thus has at least one quasi-maximal element. Let
\mathcal{T}_{n+1}=\mathcal{T}_{n}\cup\{T_{n+1}\}
and let
X_{T^{\prime}}:=\mathbb{K}_{n}\cap\pi_{T_{n+1}}\left(\mathfrak{T}_{\Theta^{\operatorname{ex}+}}\right)
. Now set
\mathbb{K}_{n+1}=\mathbb{K}_{n}\setminus\bigcup_{T^{\prime}\in\mathcal{W}(T_{n+1})}T^{\prime}
. This process terminates after finitely many steps: if

T_{n+1}
gets selected then

(7.30)

\mathcal{W}(T_{n+1})\cap\left(\mathcal{K}^{\operatorname{latt}}_{\lambda}\setminus\bigcup_{n^{\prime}=0}^{n}\mathcal{W}(T_{n^{\prime}})\right)\neq\emptyset,

given that
T_{n^{\prime}}\subset\bigcup_{T^{\prime}\in\mathcal{W}(T_{n^{\prime}})}T^{\prime}
for
any
n^{\prime}
and that

(7.31)

\|F\|_{{{\mathbb{L}^{(1,1)}_{(\Theta,\Theta^{\operatorname{ex}+})}}}(T_{n+1})}\geq\|\mathbbm{1}_{{\mathbb{K}_{n}}}F\|_{{{\mathbb{L}^{(1,1)}_{(\Theta,\Theta^{\operatorname{ex}+})}}}(T_{n+1})}>\lambda

and thus
\mathcal{W}(T)\subset\mathcal{K}^{\operatorname{latt}}_{\lambda}
. By
construction,
\mathcal{K}^{\operatorname{latt}}_{\lambda}\setminus\bigcup_{n^{\prime}=0}^{n}\mathcal{W}(T_{n^{\prime}})
is thus a sequence of finite collections of trees
that is strictly decreasing in
n
as long as the algorithm continues. The
collection eventually stabilizes so the algorithm terminates.

The procedure described above yields a collection of trees
\mathcal{T}=\bigcup_{n\in\mathbb{N}}\mathcal{T}_{n}
and distinguished subsets
X_{T^{\prime}}\subset\pi_{T^{\prime}}\left(\mathfrak{T}_{\Theta^{\operatorname{ex}+}}\right)
for any
T^{\prime}\in\mathcal{T}
. According to the selection criterion, it holds that

(7.32)

\frac{1}{\mu^{1}_{\Theta}(T)}\int_{X_{T}}|F(\eta,y,t)|\mathrm{d}\eta\mathrm{d}y\mathrm{d}t\geq\lambda.

We set
W_{\lambda}=\bigcup_{T\in\mathcal{T}}W(T)\supset\bigcup_{T\in\mathcal{T}}T
and thus we have
\mu^{1}_{\Theta}(W_{\lambda})\lesssim\sum_{T\in\mathcal{T}}\mu^{1}_{\Theta}(T)
. Since the procedure terminated
it holds that
\left\|\mathbbm{1}_{\mathbb{K}\setminus W_{\lambda}}F\right\|_{\mathbb{L}^{(1,1)}_{(\Theta,\Theta^{\operatorname{ex}+})}}\leq\lambda
. It
remains to show bounds (
7.20
) and
(
7.21
). The former follows since the
sets
X_{T}
are constructed to be pairwise disjoint. To show the latter bound
let us show that

(7.33)

\int_{\mathbb{R}_{+}}\sum_{T\in\mathcal{T}}\mathbbm{1}_{X_{T}}(\xi+\theta t^{-1},x,t)\frac{\mathrm{d}t}{t}\lesssim 1+\log\left(1+\frac{|\Theta^{\operatorname{ex}+}|}{\mathrm{dist}(\theta,\Theta^{\operatorname{ex}+})}\right)

for any
\xi,x\in\mathbb{R}
and
\theta\in\Theta^{\operatorname{in}}
with some
absolute constant. This is sufficient to deduce
(
7.21
): using a change of variables
we would then rewrite condition
(
7.21
) and get

(7.34)

\displaystyle\left\|\sum_{T\in\mathcal{T}}\mathbbm{1}_{\Gamma(X_{T})}(\eta,y,t)\right\|_{\mathbb{L}^{(1,1)}_{(\Theta_{\Gamma},\Theta^{\operatorname{in}}_{\Gamma})}(T_{\Theta_{\Gamma}}(\alpha\xi,x,s))}

\displaystyle\leq\int_{B_{1}}\int_{\Theta^{\operatorname{in}}_{\Gamma}}\int_{\mathbb{R}_{+}}\sum_{T\in\mathcal{T}}\mathbbm{1}_{\Gamma(X_{T})}(\alpha\xi+\theta(s\sigma)^{-1},x+s\zeta,s\sigma)\frac{\mathrm{d}\sigma}{\sigma}\frac{\mathrm{d}\zeta\mathrm{d}\theta}{|\Theta_{\Gamma}|}

\displaystyle\leq\int_{B_{1}}\int_{\Theta^{\operatorname{in}}}\int_{\mathbb{R}_{+}}\sum_{T\in\mathcal{T}}\mathbbm{1}_{\Gamma(X_{T})}(\alpha\xi+\alpha\beta(\theta^{\prime}+\gamma)(s\beta\sigma^{\prime})^{-1},x+s\zeta,s\beta\sigma^{\prime})\frac{\mathrm{d}\sigma^{\prime}}{\sigma^{\prime}}\frac{\mathrm{d}\zeta\mathrm{d}\theta^{\prime}}{|\Theta|}

\displaystyle\leq\int_{B_{1}}\int_{\Theta^{\operatorname{in}}}\int_{\mathbb{R}_{+}}\sum_{T\in\mathcal{T}}\mathbbm{1}_{X_{T}}(\xi+\theta^{\prime}(s\sigma^{\prime})^{-1},x_{T}+s\zeta,s\sigma^{\prime})\frac{\mathrm{d}\sigma^{\prime}}{\sigma^{\prime}}\frac{\mathrm{d}\zeta\mathrm{d}\theta^{\prime}}{|\Theta|}

\displaystyle\leq\int_{B_{1}}\int_{\Theta^{\operatorname{in}}}\left(1+\log\left(1+\frac{|\Theta^{\operatorname{ex}}|}{\mathrm{dist}(\theta^{\prime},\Theta^{\operatorname{ex}})}\right)\right)\frac{\mathrm{d}\zeta\mathrm{d}\theta^{\prime}}{|\Theta|}\lesssim 1+|\Theta|.

Fix
\xi,x\in\mathbb{R}
and
\theta\in\Theta^{\operatorname{in}}
and let us prove
(
7.33
) by showing that

(7.35)

\frac{\tau_{+}}{\tau_{-}}\leq\frac{2|\Theta^{\operatorname{ex}+}|}{\mathrm{dist}(\theta,\Theta^{\operatorname{ex}+})}+1,

where

(7.36)

\displaystyle\tau_{+}:=\sup\left\{t\in[0,+\infty):(\xi+\theta t^{-1},x,t)\in\bigcup_{T\in\mathcal{T}}X_{T}\right\},

\displaystyle\tau_{-}:=\inf\left\{t\in[0,+\infty):(\xi+\theta t^{-1},x,t)\in\bigcup_{T\in\mathcal{T}}X_{T}\right\}.

The quantity
\tau_{+}
is positive and finite unless
\sum_{T\in\mathcal{T}}\mathbbm{1}_{X_{T}}(\xi+\theta t^{-1},x,t)=0
for all
t
, but
then
\mathrm{LHS}{\eqref{eq:treesel-contra-claim-integral}}
vanishes. According to
the definition of
\tau_{+}
we may find
t_{0}\in\left(\frac{\tau_{+}}{1+\varepsilon},\tau_{+}\right)
and a tree
T_{0}
such that
(\xi+\theta t^{-1},x,t)\in X_{T_{0}}
.

Let
\mathcal{T}_{>}
be the subset of trees in
\mathcal{T}
selected

after

T_{0}
by the algorithm of this proof. It holds that

(7.37)

\inf\left\{t\in[0,+\infty):(\xi+\theta t^{-1},x,t)\in\bigcup_{T\in\mathcal{T}_{>}}X_{T}\right\}\geq t_{0}=\frac{\tau_{+}}{1+\varepsilon_{\max}}.

As a matter of fact
(\xi+\theta\widetilde{t}^{-1},x,\widetilde{t})\in T_{0}

for any
\widetilde{t}<t_{0}
so in particular
(\xi+\theta\widetilde{t}^{-1},x,\widetilde{t})\notin X_{T_{>}}\subset T_{>}\setminus T_{0}
for any
T_{>}\in\mathcal{T}
selected after
T_{0}
.

Let us then focus a tree
