T_{<}\in\mathcal{T}_{<}
selected before
T_{0}
. By
quasi-maximality we have that
-\varepsilon_{\max}+\xi_{T_{0}}\leq\xi_{T_{<}}
and thus

(7.38)

\xi-\xi_{T_{<}}\leq\xi-\xi_{T_{0}}+\varepsilon_{\max}.

If
\xi_{T_{<}}\geq\xi
then we would have that
\pi_{T_{<}}\left(\mathfrak{T}_{\Theta^{\operatorname{ex}+}}\right)\cap\left\{(\xi+\theta t^{-1},x,t):t\in\mathbb{R}_{+}\right\}=\emptyset
so
T_{<}
would not contribute to
defining
\tau^{-}
. Suppose
\xi_{T_{<}}<\xi
; then it holds that

(7.39)

\displaystyle\left\{t:(\xi+\theta t^{-1},x,t)\in\pi_{T_{<}}\left(\mathfrak{T}_{\Theta^{\operatorname{ex}+}}\right)\right\}

\displaystyle\subset\left(\frac{\mathrm{dist}(\theta,\Theta^{\operatorname{ex}+})}{\xi-\xi_{T_{<}}},+\infty\right)

\displaystyle\subset\left(\frac{\mathrm{dist}(\theta,\Theta^{\operatorname{ex}+})}{\xi-\xi_{T_{0}}{+\varepsilon_{\max}}},+\infty\right)

To bound
\frac{\mathrm{dist}(\theta,\Theta^{\operatorname{ex}+})}{\xi-\xi_{T_{0}}{+\varepsilon_{\max}}}
from below we use the fact that
(\xi+\theta t_{0}^{-1},x,t_{0})\in X_{T_{0}}
and, as a consequence,

(7.40)

\frac{\mathrm{dist}(\theta,\Theta^{\operatorname{ex}+})}{t_{0}}<\xi-\xi_{T_{0}}<\frac{|\Theta^{\operatorname{ex}+}|}{t_{0}}

because
X_{T_{0}}\subset\left\{(\xi_{T_{0}}+\overline{\theta}\overline{t}^{-1},\overline{x},\overline{t}):\overline{\theta}\in\Theta^{\operatorname{ex}+},\overline{x}\in B_{s_{T_{0}}}(x_{T_{0}}),\overline{t}<s_{T_{0}}\right\}
. It follows that

(7.41)

\displaystyle\left\{t:(\xi+\theta t^{-1},x,t)\in\pi_{T_{<}}\left(\mathfrak{T}_{\Theta^{\operatorname{ex}+}}\right)\right\}\subset\left(t_{0}\frac{\mathrm{dist}(\theta,\Theta^{\operatorname{ex}+})}{|\Theta^{\operatorname{ex}+}|{+\varepsilon_{\max}}t_{0}},+\infty\right).

Recall that
t_{0}<2S\lambda^{-1}
since all trees
T
in
\mathcal{T}

satisfy
s_{T}<2S\lambda^{-1}
. It is sufficient to take

\varepsilon_{\max}=\frac{\lambda|\Theta^{\operatorname{ex}+}|}{2S}
to yield

(7.42)

\displaystyle\left\{t:(\xi+\theta t^{-1},x,t)\in\pi_{T_{<}}\left(\mathfrak{T}_{\Theta^{\operatorname{ex}+}}\right)\right\}\subset\left(\frac{\tau_{+}}{(1+\varepsilon)}\frac{\mathrm{dist}(\theta,\Theta^{\operatorname{ex}+})}{2|\Theta^{\operatorname{ex}+}|},+\infty\right)

and prove that

(7.43)

\tau_{-}>\frac{\tau_{+}}{(1+\varepsilon)}\min\left(\frac{\mathrm{dist}(\theta,\Theta^{\operatorname{ex}+})}{2|\Theta^{\operatorname{ex}+}|(1+\varepsilon)},1\right)

Since
\varepsilon>0
can be chosen arbitrarily, this proves our claim.
∎

The covering lemma above allows us to obtain the following uniform weak
L^{1}

bound.

Lemma 7.5
.

The bound

(7.44)

\|F\circ\Gamma\|_{L^{1,\infty}_{\mu_{\Theta}^{1}}\Gamma^{\ast}\mathbb{L}^{(1,1)}_{(\Theta,\Theta^{\operatorname{ex}})}}\lesssim\|F\|_{L^{1}_{\mu_{\Theta_{\Gamma}}^{1}}\left(\mathbb{L}^{(\infty,\infty)}_{\Theta_{\Gamma}}+\mathbb{L}^{(1,1)}_{(\Theta_{\Gamma},\Theta^{\operatorname{ex}}_{\Gamma})}\right)}

holds for any
F\in\mathscr{R}(\mathbb{R}^{3}_{+})
.

Proof.

We need to show that for every
\lambda>0
there exists a set
W_{\lambda}=\bigcup_{T\in\mathcal{T}\subset\mathbb{T}_{\Theta}}T
such that

(7.45)

\displaystyle\left\|\mathbbm{1}_{\mathbb{R}^{3}_{+}\setminus W_{\lambda}}F\circ\Gamma\right\|_{\mathbb{L}^{(1,1)}_{(\Theta,\Theta^{\operatorname{ex}})}}\leq\lambda,

\displaystyle\mu_{\Theta}^{1}(W_{\lambda})\lesssim\lambda^{-1}\|F\|_{L^{1}_{\mu_{\Theta_{\Gamma}}^{1}}\left(\mathbb{L}^{(\infty,\infty)}_{{\Theta_{\Gamma}}}+\mathbb{L}^{(1,1)}_{(\Theta_{\Gamma},\Theta_{\Gamma}^{\operatorname{ex}})}\right)}.

We may assume that
F
is supported on a compact set by the discussion at
the end of
Section

3.2.2
. Fix
\lambda>0
and let
W_{\lambda}:=\bigcup_{T\in\mathcal{T}_{\lambda}\subset\mathbb{T}_{\Theta}}T
where

\mathcal{T}_{\lambda}\subset\mathbb{T}_{\Theta}
is obtained by applying
Lemma
7.4
to
F\circ\Gamma
in place of
F
. It
remains to prove the required bounds on
\mu_{\Theta}^{1}(W_{\lambda})
. For
each tree
T\in\mathcal{T}_{\lambda}
, Lemma
7.4

gives the bound

(7.46)

\lambda\mu^{1}_{\Theta}(T)\leq\int_{X_{T}}|F\circ\Gamma(\eta,y,t)|\mathrm{d}\eta\mathrm{d}y\mathrm{d}t.

Summing the bound over all trees
T\in\mathcal{T}_{\lambda}
and applying
a change of variables gives

(7.47)

\displaystyle\lambda\mu^{1}_{\Theta}(W_{\lambda})\begin{aligned} &\lesssim\lambda\sum_{T\in\mathcal{T}_{\lambda}}\mu^{1}_{\Theta}(T)\lesssim\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}_{+}}\left(\sum_{T\in\mathcal{T}_{\lambda}}\mathbbm{1}_{X_{T}}(\eta,y,t)\right)|F\circ\Gamma(\eta,y,t)|\mathrm{d}\eta\mathrm{d}y\mathrm{d}t\\
&=\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}_{+}}\left(\sum_{T\in\mathcal{T}_{\lambda}}\mathbbm{1}_{X_{T}}(\eta,y,t)\right)|F(\alpha(\eta+\gamma t^{-1}),y,\beta t)|\mathrm{d}\eta\mathrm{d}y\mathrm{d}t\\
&=\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}_{+}}\left(\sum_{T\in\mathcal{T}_{\lambda}}\mathbbm{1}_{\Gamma(X_{T})}(\alpha(\eta+\gamma t^{-1}),y,\beta t)\right)|F(\alpha(\eta+\gamma t^{-1}),y,\beta t)|\mathrm{d}\eta\mathrm{d}y\mathrm{d}t\\
&=\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}_{+}}\frac{1}{\alpha\beta}\left(\sum_{T\in\mathcal{T}_{\lambda}}\mathbbm{1}_{\Gamma(X_{T})}(\eta,y,t)\right)|F(\eta,y,t)|\mathrm{d}\eta\mathrm{d}y\mathrm{d}t.\end{aligned}

It remains to bound the
\mathrm{RHS}{}
of this inequality from above. By

Proposition

3.5
we have that

(7.48)

\displaystyle\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}_{+}}\left(\sum_{T\in\mathcal{T}_{\lambda}}\mathbbm{1}_{\Gamma(X_{T})}(\eta,y,t)\right)|F(\eta,y,t)|\mathrm{d}\eta\mathrm{d}y\mathrm{d}t

\displaystyle\lesssim\|F\|_{L^{1}_{\mu_{\Theta_{2}}^{1}}\left(\mathbb{L}^{(\infty,\infty)}_{\Theta_{\Gamma}}+\mathbb{L}^{(1,1)}_{(\Theta_{\Gamma},\Theta_{\Gamma}^{\operatorname{ex}})}\right)}\Bigl\|\sum_{{T\in\mathcal{T}_{\lambda}}}\mathbbm{1}_{\Gamma(X_{T})}(\eta,y,t)\Bigr\|_{L^{\infty}_{\mu_{\Theta}^{1}}\left(\mathbb{L}^{(1,1)}_{(\Theta_{\Gamma},\Theta_{\Gamma}^{\operatorname{in}})}+\mathbb{L}^{(\infty,\infty)}_{\Theta_{\Gamma}}\right)}.

Since

(7.49)

\displaystyle\Bigl\|\sum_{{T\in\mathcal{T}_{\lambda}}}\mathbbm{1}_{\Gamma(X_{T})}(\eta,y,t)\Bigr\|_{L^{\infty}_{\mu_{\Theta}^{1}}\left(\mathbb{L}^{(1,1)}_{(\Theta_{\Gamma},\Theta_{\Gamma}^{\operatorname{in}})}+\mathbb{L}^{(\infty,\infty)}_{(\Theta_{\Gamma},\Theta_{\Gamma}^{\operatorname{ex}})}\right)}\lesssim 1

this concludes the proof.
∎

We are now ready to deduce
Proposition

7.1
from

Lemma

7.5
. We use
Lemma

3.13
to
reduce to the scalar case i.e. to proving bounds for
F\in\mathscr{R}(\mathbb{R}^{3}_{+})
instead of for
F\in\mathscr{R}(\mathbb{R}^{3}_{+})\otimes\Phi^{N}_{\mathfrak{r}}
. Then we conclude using a restricted weak type interpolation
argument.

Proof.

Proof of
Proposition

7.1

We begin by reducing to the scalar bound. In particular, a straightforward
adaptation of
Corollary

3.14
to the case
of the sizes
\Gamma^{\ast}\mathbb{L}^{(u,2)}_{(\Theta,\Theta^{\operatorname{ex}})}

shows that

(7.50)

\|F\circ\Gamma\|_{L^{r}_{\mu_{\Theta}^{1}}\Gamma^{\ast}\mathbb{L}^{(u,2)}_{(\Theta,\Theta^{\operatorname{ex}})}\Phi^{N}_{\mathfrak{r}}}\lesssim\sup_{\varphi}\|F\circ\Gamma(\eta,y,t)[\varphi]\|_{L^{r}_{\mu_{\Theta}^{1}}\Gamma^{\ast}\mathbb{L}^{(u,2)}_{(\Theta,\Theta^{\operatorname{ex}})}}

where the upper bound is taken over all
\varphi\in\Phi^{N^{\prime}}_{2\mathfrak{r}}
with

\|\varphi\|_{\Phi^{N^{\prime}}_{2\mathfrak{r}}}\lesssim 1
, as long as
N\geq N^{\prime}+4
.
Bound (
7.1
) can thus be deduced from

(7.51)

\|F\circ\Gamma\|_{L^{r}_{\mu_{\Theta}^{1}}\Gamma^{\ast}\mathbb{L}^{(u,2)}_{(\Theta,\Theta^{\operatorname{ex}})}}\lesssim\|F\|_{L^{r}_{\mu_{\Theta_{\Gamma}}^{1}}\left(\mathbb{L}^{(\infty,\infty)}_{\Theta_{\Gamma}}+\mathbb{L}^{(\overline{u},2)}_{(\Theta_{\Gamma},\Theta^{\operatorname{ex}}_{\Gamma})}\right)}

for all
F\in\mathscr{R}(\mathbb{R}^{3}_{+})
. The remaining step is to deduce
bound (
7.51
) from bound
(
7.44
) of
Lemma

7.5
. In particular let us
show that

(7.52)

\|F\circ\Gamma\|_{L^{r}_{\mu_{\Theta}^{1}}\Gamma^{\ast}\mathbb{L}^{(u,1)}_{(\Theta,\Theta^{\operatorname{ex}})}}\lesssim\|F\|_{L^{r}_{\mu_{\Theta_{\Gamma}}^{1}}\left(\mathbb{L}^{(\infty,\infty)}_{\Theta_{\Gamma}}+\mathbb{L}^{(\overline{u},1)}_{(\Theta_{\Gamma},\Theta^{\operatorname{ex}}_{\Gamma})}\right)}

for
1\leq u<\overline{u}\leq\infty
and
r\in\left(u\frac{\overline{u}-1}{\overline{u}-u},\infty\right]
from (
7.44
). Bound
(
7.51
) follows from
(
7.52
) by applying it
to
F^{2}
in lieu of
F
and choosing
\overline{u}
appropriately large. For any

\overline{u}>u
the
r=\infty
endpoint for
(
7.52
), given by

(7.53)

\|F\circ\Gamma\|_{L^{\infty}_{\mu_{\Theta}^{1}}\Gamma^{\ast}\mathbb{L}^{(u,1)}_{(\Theta,\Theta^{\operatorname{ex}})}}\leq\|F\|_{L^{\infty}_{\mu_{\Theta}^{1}}\mathbb{L}^{(\overline{u},1)}_{(\Theta_{\Gamma},\Theta_{\Gamma}^{\operatorname{ex}})}},

follows from the fact that
\Gamma(T_{\Theta}(\xi_{T},x_{T},s_{T}))\subset T_{\Theta_{\Gamma}}(\alpha\xi_{T},x_{T},s_{T})
. We can use

Proposition

3.3
to conclude that bound
(
7.52
) holds for all
other
r>u^{\ast}\coloneqq u\frac{\overline{u}-1}{\overline{u}-u}
if we show the

u^{\ast}
restricted weak endpoint

(7.54)

\displaystyle\|F\circ\Gamma\|_{L^{u^{\ast},\infty}_{\mu_{\Theta}^{1}}\Gamma^{\ast}\mathbb{L}^{(u,1)}_{(\Theta,\Theta^{\operatorname{ex}})}}\lesssim\begin{aligned} &\mu_{\Theta}^{1}\left(\|F\|_{\left(\mathbb{L}^{(\infty,\infty)}_{\Theta_{\Gamma}}+\mathbb{L}^{(1,1)}_{(\Theta_{\Gamma},\Theta_{\Gamma}^{\operatorname{ext}})}\right)}>0\right)^{\frac{1}{u^{\ast}}}\\
&\qquad\times\|F\|_{\left(\mathbb{L}^{(\infty,\infty)}_{\Theta_{\Gamma}}+\mathbb{L}^{(\overline{u},1)}_{(\Theta_{\Gamma},\Theta_{\Gamma}^{\operatorname{ex}})}\right)}.\end{aligned}

Fix any
\tau>0
and let us apply
Lemma

7.5
: there exists

W_{\tau}\in\mathbb{T}_{\Theta}^{\cup}
such that

(7.55)

\displaystyle\mu^{1}_{\Theta}(W_{\tau})\lesssim\begin{aligned} &\tau^{-1}\mu_{\Theta}^{1}\left(\|F\|_{\left(\mathbb{L}^{(\infty,\infty)}_{\Theta}+\mathbb{L}^{(\overline{u},1)}_{(\Theta_{\Gamma},\Theta_{\Gamma}^{\operatorname{ext}})}\right)}>0\right)\qquad\\
&\qquad\times\|F\|_{\left(\mathbb{L}^{(\infty,\infty)}_{\Theta}+\mathbb{L}^{(\overline{u},1)}_{(\Theta_{\Gamma},\Theta_{\Gamma}^{\operatorname{ex}})}\right)},\end{aligned}

\displaystyle\|\mathbbm{1}_{\mathbb{R}^{3}_{+}\setminus W_{\tau}}F\circ\Gamma\|_{\Gamma^{\ast}\mathbb{L}^{(1,1)}_{(\Theta,\Theta^{\operatorname{ex}})}}\leq\tau.

By
\log
-convexity of the
\mathbb{L}^{(u,1)}_{(\Theta,\Theta^{\operatorname{ex}})}

sizes in the parameter
u\in[1,\infty]
, we have that

(7.56)

\begin{aligned} &\left\|\mathbbm{1}_{\mathbb{R}^{3}_{+}\setminus W_{\tau}}F\circ\Gamma\right\|_{\Gamma^{\ast}\mathbb{L}^{(u,1)}_{(\Theta,\Theta^{\operatorname{ext}})}}\leq\|\mathbbm{1}_{\mathbb{R}^{3}_{+}\setminus W_{\tau}}F\circ\Gamma\|_{\Gamma^{\ast}\mathbb{L}^{(1,1)}_{(\Theta,\Theta^{\operatorname{ex}})}}^{\frac{1}{u^{\ast}}}\|\mathbbm{1}_{\mathbb{R}^{3}_{+}\setminus W_{\tau}}F\circ\Gamma\|_{\Gamma^{\ast}\mathbb{L}^{(\overline{u},1)}_{(\Theta,\Theta^{\operatorname{ex}})}}^{\frac{u^{\ast}-1}{u^{\ast}}}\\
&\leq\tau^{\frac{1}{u^{\ast}}}\|F\|_{\Gamma^{\ast}\mathbb{L}^{(\overline{u},1)}_{(\Theta,\Theta^{\operatorname{ex}})}}^{\frac{u^{\ast}-1}{u^{\ast}}}\end{aligned}.

Combining the estimate above with the measure bound on
W_{\tau}
we get
that

(7.57)

\mu_{\Theta}^{1}(W_{\tau})\left\|\mathbbm{1}_{\mathbb{R}^{3}_{+}\setminus W_{\tau}}F\circ\Gamma\right\|_{\mathbb{L}^{(u,1)}_{(\Theta,\Theta^{\operatorname{ext}})}}^{u_{\ast}}\lesssim\begin{aligned} &\mu_{\Theta}^{1}\left(\|F\|_{\left(\mathbb{L}^{(\infty,\infty)}_{\Theta_{\Gamma}}+\mathbb{L}^{(\overline{u},1)}_{(\Theta_{\Gamma},\Theta_{\Gamma}^{\operatorname{ex}})}\right)}>0\right)\qquad\\
&\qquad\times\|F\|_{\left(\mathbb{L}^{(\infty,\infty)}_{\Theta_{\Gamma}}+\mathbb{L}^{(\overline{u},1)}_{(\Theta_{\Gamma},\Theta_{\Gamma}^{\operatorname{ex}})}\right)}^{u^{\ast}}.\end{aligned}

Since
\tau>0
is arbitrary we have shown that
(
7.54
) holds.
∎

7.2.
Uniform bounds for the singular
size

We now prove
Proposition

7.2
, proceeding roughly
in the same manner as in
Section

7.1
while proving

Proposition

7.1
. We begin with a uniform covering argument
for the interior part of trees.

Lemma 7.6

(Singular tree covering)
.

Let
V\in\mathbb{D}^{\cup}_{\beta}
,
W\in\mathbb{T}_{\Theta}^{\cup}
and let
E=V\cap W
or
E=V\cup W
. Suppose

F\in L^{\infty}_{\operatorname{loc}}(\mathbb{R}^{3}_{+})
is a compactly supported function.
For all
\lambda>0
there exists a set
W_{\lambda}\subset\mathbb{T}^{\cup}_{\Theta}
and finite collection of trees
\mathcal{T}\subset\mathbb{T}_{\Theta}
such that

(7.58)

\displaystyle\Bigl\|\mathbbm{1}_{\mathbb{R}^{3}_{+}\setminus W_{\lambda}}F(\eta,y,t)t\mathrm{d}_{t}\mathbbm{1}_{E}(\eta,y,t)\Bigr\|_{\mathbb{L}^{(1,1)}_{(\Theta,\Theta^{\operatorname{in}})}}\leq\lambda,

\displaystyle W_{\lambda}\supset\bigcup_{T\in\mathcal{T}}T,\qquad\mu^{1}_{\Theta}(W_{\lambda})\lesssim\sum_{T\in\mathcal{T}}\mu^{1}_{\Theta}(T).

Each
T\in\mathcal{T}
is endowed with a distinguished bounded subset
X_{T}\subset\pi_{T}\left(\mathfrak{T}_{\Theta^{\operatorname{in}}}\right)
such that

(7.59)

\frac{1}{\mu^{1}_{\Theta}(T)}\int_{X_{T}}|F(\eta,y,t)|t\mathrm{d}_{t}\mathbbm{1}_{E}(\eta,y,t)\mathrm{d}\eta\mathrm{d}y\mathrm{d}t\geq\lambda.

and

(7.60)

\left\|\left(\sum_{T\in\mathcal{T}}\mathbbm{1}_{\Gamma(X_{T})}(\eta,y,t)\right)t\mathrm{d}_{t}\mathbbm{1}_{\Gamma(E)}(\eta,y,t)\right\|_{\mathbb{L}^{(1,1)}_{\Theta_{\Gamma}}}\lesssim 1

for any
\Gamma
as in (
3.103
). The implicit constants do not
depend on the set
E
nor on
\Gamma
.

Proof.

Proof of
Lemma

7.6

The proof follows along the same lines of the proof of

Lemma

7.4
. We begin by making a geometric assumption on

W
. If
W=\bigcup_{n\in\mathbb{N}}T_{n}
then let us set
W^{\pm}\coloneqq\bigcup_{n\in\mathbb{N}}T_{n}^{\pm}
where

(7.61)

T^{\pm}:\{(\eta\pm h,y,t),(\eta,y,t)\in T,h\geq 0\};

notice that
T^{+}\in\mathbb{T}^{\cup}_{\Theta}
if
T\in\mathbb{T}^{\cup}_{\Theta}
and
it holds that
T=T^{+}\cap T^{-}
. Thus, if
E=V\cap W
it holds that
E=E^{+}\cap E^{-}
where
E^{\pm}\coloneqq V\cap W^{\pm}
and we have that

(7.62)

\left|F(\eta,y,t)t\mathrm{d}_{t}\mathbbm{1}_{E}(\eta,y,t)\right|\leq F(\eta,y,t)\left|t\mathrm{d}_{t}\mathbbm{1}_{E^{-}}(\eta,y,t)\right|+F(\eta,y,t)\left|t\mathrm{d}_{t}\mathbbm{1}_{E^{+}}(\eta,y,t)\right|

Similarly, if
E=V\cup W
then
E=E^{+}\cup E^{-}
and

(7.63)

\left|F(\eta,y,t)t\mathrm{d}_{t}\mathbbm{1}_{E}(\eta,y,t)\right|\leq F(\eta,y,t)\left|t\mathrm{d}_{t}\mathbbm{1}_{E^{-}}(\eta,y,t)\right|+F(\eta,y,t)\left|t\mathrm{d}_{t}\mathbbm{1}_{E^{+}}(\eta,y,t)\right|.

By the quasi triangle inequality, it is thus sufficient to show the claim
for
E^{+}
in place of
E
. We reason in the case
E^{+}=V\cap W^{+}
and we
write
F
in place of
F(\eta,y,t)\mathbbm{1}_{E^{-}}(\eta,y,t)
.

Without loss of generality we assume that
F
is supported on
\mathbb{K}\subset[-S;S]^{2}\times[S^{-1};S]
for some
S\gg 1
. It follows
from
Lemma

3.20
that

\left\|F\circ\Gamma(\eta,y,t)t\mathrm{d}_{t}\mathbbm{1}_{E}(\eta,y,t)\right\|_{\mathbb{L}^{(1,1)}_{(\Theta,\Theta^{\operatorname{in}})}}<\infty
so by homogeneity we assume that

(7.64)

\left\|F\circ\Gamma(\eta,y,t)t\mathrm{d}_{t}\mathbbm{1}_{E}(\eta,y,t)\right\|_{\mathbb{L}^{(1,1)}_{(\Theta,\Theta^{\operatorname{in}})}}=1.

The parameters
(\xi_{T},x_{T},s_{T})
of any tree
T\in\mathbb{T}_{\Theta}

for which

(7.65)

\left\|F\circ\Gamma(\eta,y,t)t\mathrm{d}_{t}\mathbbm{1}_{E}(\eta,y,t)\right\|_{\mathbb{L}^{(1,1)}_{(\Theta,\Theta^{\operatorname{in}})}(T)}\geq\lambda

are bounded and
s_{T}
is bounded away from
0
. As a matter of fact

(7.66)

\displaystyle s_{T}\|F(\eta,y,t)t\mathrm{d}_{t}\mathbbm{1}_{E^{+}}(\eta,y,t)\|_{{{\mathbb{L}^{(1,1)}_{(\Theta,\Theta^{\operatorname{in}})}}}(T_{\Theta}(\xi_{T},x_{T},s_{T}))}

\displaystyle\leq 2S\|F(\eta,y,t)t\mathrm{d}_{t}\mathbbm{1}_{E^{+}}(\eta,y,t)\|_{{{\mathbb{L}^{(1,1)}_{(\Theta,\Theta^{\operatorname{in}})}}}(T_{\Theta}(\xi_{T},0,2S))}.

We want to construct
\mathcal{T}
by an iterative procedure. We enact the
same preparatory steps that we have done in the proof of

Lemma

7.4
: we introduce

\mathbb{T}_{\Theta}^{\operatorname{latt}}
, the discrete collection of trees; to
any
T\in\mathbb{T}_{\Theta}
we associate a collection of trees

\mathcal{W}(T)\subset\mathbb{T}_{\Theta}^{\operatorname{latt}}
such that
\mu_{\Theta}^{1}\bigl(\bigcup_{T^{\prime}\in\mathcal{W}(T)}T^{\prime}\bigr)\lesssim\mu_{\Theta}^{1}(T)
and
\bigcup_{\heartsuit\in\{-1,0,1\}}T_{(\xi_{T}+\heartsuit s_{T}^{-1},x_{T},s_{T})}\subset\bigcup_{T^{\prime}\in\mathcal{W}(T)}T^{\prime}
. We define a finite collection
\mathcal{K}^{\operatorname{latt}}_{\lambda}\subset\mathbb{T}_{\Theta}^{\operatorname{latt}}
so that the trees in

\mathcal{K}^{\operatorname{latt}}_{\lambda}
cover
\mathbb{K}
and so that for any
tree
T\in\mathbb{T}_{\Theta}
such that

\left\|F\circ\Gamma(\eta,y,t)t\mathrm{d}_{t}\mathbbm{1}_{E}(\eta,y,t)\right\|_{\mathbb{L}^{(1,1)}_{(\Theta,\Theta^{\operatorname{in}})}(T)}\geq\lambda
it holds that
\mathcal{W}(T)\subset\mathcal{K}^{\operatorname{latt}}_{\lambda}
.

Let us now proceed to the selection algorithm. Given a collection of trees

\mathcal{X}\subset\mathbb{T}_{\Theta}
we say that
T\in\mathcal{X}

is
quasi-maximal
if

(7.67)

T^{\prime}\in\mathcal{X}\Longrightarrow\xi_{T^{\prime}}\leq\xi_{T}+\varepsilon_{\max}

with
\varepsilon_{\max}\leq 2^{-100}S.
Let us start with the collection

\mathcal{T}_{0}=\emptyset
and let
\mathbb{K}_{0}:=\mathbb{K}
.
Suppose that at step
n
we have a collection
\mathcal{T}_{n}=\{T_{1},\ldots,T_{n}\}\subset\mathbb{T}_{\Theta}
and a subset
\mathbb{K}_{n}\subset\mathbb{K}
. At step
n+1
let

(7.68)

\mathcal{X}_{n+1}:=\Bigl\{T\in\mathbb{T}_{\Theta}:\|\mathbbm{1}_{{\mathbb{K}_{n}}}F\circ\Gamma(\eta,y,t)t\mathrm{d}_{t}\mathbbm{1}_{E}(\eta,y,t)\|_{{{\mathbb{L}^{(1,1)}_{(\Theta,\Theta^{\operatorname{in}})}}}(T)}\geq\lambda\Bigr\};

if
\mathcal{X}_{n+1}
is empty, we terminate the iteration. Otherwise
choose a quasi-maximal element
T_{n+1}
of
\mathcal{X}_{n+1}
and let

\mathcal{T}_{n+1}=\mathcal{T}_{n}\cup\{T_{n+1}\}
,
X_{T^{\prime}}:=\mathbb{K}_{n}\cap\pi_{T_{n+1}}\left(\mathfrak{T}_{\Theta^{\operatorname{in}}}\right)

and
\mathbb{K}_{n+1}=\mathbb{K}_{n}\setminus\bigcup_{T^{\prime}\in\mathcal{W}(T_{n+1})}T^{\prime}
. This process terminates after finitely many steps. This
procedure yields a collection of trees
\mathcal{T}=\bigcup_{n\in\mathbb{N}}\mathcal{T}_{n}
and distinguished subsets
X_{T^{\prime}}\subset\pi_{T^{\prime}}\left(\mathfrak{T}_{\Theta^{\operatorname{in}}}\right)
for any
T^{\prime}\in\mathcal{T}
. According to
the selection criterion (and Lemma
Lemma

3.20
) we have
that

(7.69)

\frac{1}{\mu^{1}_{\Theta}(T)}\int_{X_{T}}|F(\eta,y,t)|t\mathrm{d}_{t}\mathbbm{1}_{E}(\eta,y,t)\mathrm{d}\eta\mathrm{d}y\mathrm{d}t\geq\lambda.

We set
W_{\lambda}=\bigcup_{T\in\mathcal{T}}W(T)\supset\bigcup_{T\in\mathcal{T}}T
and we have
\mu^{1}_{\Theta}(W_{\lambda})\lesssim\sum_{T\in\mathcal{T}}\mu^{1}_{\Theta}(T)
. Since the procedure terminated
it holds that

(7.70)

\|\mathbbm{1}_{\mathbb{R}^{3}_{+}\setminus W_{\lambda}}F\circ\Gamma(\eta,y,t)t\mathrm{d}_{t}\mathbbm{1}_{E}(\eta,y,t)\|_{{{\mathbb{L}^{(1,1)}_{(\Theta,\Theta^{\operatorname{in}})}}}(T)}\leq\lambda.

It remains to show that (
7.60
) holds. It
holds that
\Gamma(E^{+})=\Gamma(V)\cap\Gamma(W^{+})
with
\Gamma(V)\in\mathbb{D}_{1}^{\cup}
and
\Gamma(W^{+})\in\mathbb{T}_{\Theta_{\Gamma}}^{\cup}
so let

\mathfrak{b}(\eta,y)
be the function whose graph is the boundary of
\Gamma(E^{+})
, as described in
Lemma

3.20
. Denoting

\Theta_{\Gamma}=(\theta_{\Gamma-},\theta_{\Gamma+})
, according to

Lemma

3.20
it holds that

(7.71)

0\leq-\mathrm{d}_{\eta}\left(\frac{1}{\mathfrak{b}(\eta,y)}\right)\leq|\theta_{\Gamma-}|^{-1};

the condition
\mathrm{d}_{\eta}\mathfrak{b}(\eta,y)\geq 0
comes from the fact that

\eta\mapsto\mathfrak{b}(\eta,y)
is non-decreasing: if
(\eta,y,t)\in\Gamma(E^{+})
then
(\eta^{\prime},y,t)\in\Gamma(E^{+})
for any
\eta^{\prime}\geq\eta
. It follows that for any
\eta_{0}\in\mathbb{R}
and any
\eta_{0}<\eta<\frac{|\theta_{\Gamma-}|}{\mathfrak{b}(\eta_{0},y)}
one has

(7.72)

\displaystyle\mathfrak{b}(\eta_{0},y)\leq\mathfrak{b}(\eta,y),\qquad\forall\eta\geq\eta_{0},

\displaystyle\mathfrak{b}(\eta,y)\leq\frac{|\theta_{\Gamma-}|\mathfrak{b}(\eta_{0},y)}{|\theta_{\Gamma-}|-\mathfrak{b}(\eta_{0},y)(\eta-\eta_{0})},\qquad\forall\eta\in\left(\eta_{0},\frac{|\theta_{\Gamma-}|}{\mathfrak{b}(\eta_{0},y)}\right).

We must bound the expression

(7.73)

\displaystyle\Bigl\|\sum_{T\in\mathcal{T}}\mathbbm{1}_{\Gamma(X_{T})}(\eta,y,t)t\mathrm{d}_{t}\mathbbm{1}_{\Gamma(E)}(\eta,y,t)\Bigr\|_{{{\mathbb{L}^{(1,1)}_{\Theta_{\Gamma}}}}(T)}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{s_{T}}\int_{B_{s_{T}}(x_{T})}\left(\int_{\mathbb{R}}\left(\sum_{T\in\mathcal{T}}\mathbbm{1}_{\Gamma(X_{T})}\left(\eta,y,\mathfrak{b}(\eta,y)\right)\right)\mathbbm{1}_{T}\left(\eta,y,\mathfrak{b}(\eta,y)\right)\mathfrak{b}(\eta,y)\frac{\mathrm{d}\eta}{|\Theta|}\right)\mathrm{d}y.

Without loss of generality, let us suppose that
T=T_{\Theta_{\Gamma}}(0,0,1)
. Fix
y\in B_{1}(0)
and fix
\overline{\theta}>\theta_{\Gamma-}
close
to
\theta_{\Gamma-}
; in particular let
\overline{\theta}<-|\theta_{\Gamma-}^{\operatorname{in}}|
.

Let
\overline{\eta}
be the lower bound of
\eta\in\mathbb{R}
such that
\eta\mathfrak{b}(\eta,y)>\overline{\theta}
(or
+\infty
if no such
\eta
exists). The
quantity
\eta\mapsto\eta\mathfrak{b}(\eta,y)
is non-decreasing, according
to (
7.71
). Let us start by showing that

(7.74)

\int_{\eta>\overline{\eta}}\left(\sum_{T\in\mathcal{T}}\mathbbm{1}_{\Gamma(X_{T})}\left(\eta,y,\mathfrak{b}(\eta,y)\right)\right)\mathbbm{1}_{T}\left(\eta,y,\mathfrak{b}(\eta,y)\right)\mathfrak{b}(\eta,y)\frac{\mathrm{d}\eta}{|\Theta|}\lesssim 1.

This is relevant only if
\overline{\eta}<+\infty
. From
(
7.72
) we can deduce that for

\overline{\eta}<\eta<\frac{\overline{\theta}-\theta_{\Gamma-}}{2\mathfrak{b}(\overline{\eta},y)}
it holds that

(7.75)

\mathfrak{b}(\eta,y)<\frac{2|\theta_{\Gamma-}|\mathfrak{b}(\overline{\eta},y)}{\overline{\theta}-\theta_{\Gamma}}.

When the graph
\eta\mapsto\mathfrak{b}(\eta,y)
intersects
T
we have that

\mathfrak{b}(\eta,y)\leq\frac{\theta_{\Gamma_{+}}}{\eta}
. Thus for
\eta>\frac{\overline{\theta}-\theta_{\Gamma}}{2\mathfrak{b}(\overline{\eta},y)}
we have

(7.76)

\mathfrak{b}(\eta,y)<\frac{\theta_{\Gamma_{+}}2\mathfrak{b}(\overline{\eta},y)}{\overline{\theta}-\theta_{\Gamma_{-}}}.

We have shown that for all
\eta>\overline{\eta}
when the graph
\eta\mapsto\mathfrak{b}(\eta,y)
intersects
T
it holds that

(7.77)

\mathfrak{b}(\eta,y)<\frac{(2|\theta_{\Gamma-}|+\theta_{\Gamma_{+}})2\mathfrak{b}(\overline{\eta},y)}{|\Theta|}.

The graph
\eta\mapsto\mathfrak{b}(\eta,y)
intersects
T
only if
\eta<\frac{\theta_{\Gamma+}}{\mathfrak{b}(\overline{\eta},y)}
so

(7.78)

\int_{\eta>\overline{\eta}}\left(\sum_{T\in\mathcal{T}}\mathbbm{1}_{\Gamma(X_{T})}\left(\eta,y,\mathfrak{b}(\eta,y)\right)\right)\mathbbm{1}_{T}\left(\eta,y,\mathfrak{b}(\eta,y)\right)\mathfrak{b}(\eta,y)\frac{\mathrm{d}\eta}{|\Theta|}\leq 2(|\theta_{\Gamma-}|+\theta_{\Gamma_{+}}).

It thus remains to bound the inner integrand of
(
7.73
) restricted to when
\eta<\overline{\eta}

and in particular when
\theta_{\Gamma-}<\eta b(\eta,y)<\overline{\theta}
. Let
A=\{(\eta,y,t):\theta_{\Gamma-}<\eta b(\eta,y)<\overline{\theta}\}.
Let
T_{0}\in\mathcal{T}
be the tree selected earliest
by the algorithm among those for which the graph
\eta\mapsto\mathfrak{b}(\eta,y)
intersects
\Gamma(X_{T_{0}})\cap T\cap A
. We need to show the bound

(7.79)

\int_{\eta=-\infty}^{\overline{\eta}}\left(\sum_{T\in\mathcal{T}_{>0}}\mathbbm{1}_{\Gamma(X_{T})}\left(\eta,y,\mathfrak{b}(\eta,y)\right)\right)\mathbbm{1}_{T\cap A}\left(\eta,y,\mathfrak{b}(\eta,y)\right)\mathfrak{b}(\eta,y)\frac{\mathrm{d}\eta}{|\Theta|}\lesssim 1

where
\mathcal{T}_{>0}
is the sub collection of those trees in
\mathcal{T}

selected after
T_{n_{0}}
. Let
\eta_{0}
be the lower bound of
\eta
for
which the graph
\eta\mapsto\mathfrak{b}(\eta,y)
intersects
\Gamma\left(X_{T_{n_{0}}}\right)\cap T
. It holds that

(7.80)

\alpha\xi_{T_{n_{0}}}<\eta_{0}+|\theta_{\Gamma-}^{\operatorname{in}}|\mathfrak{b}(\eta_{0},y)^{-1}<0

because
\eta_{0}\mathfrak{b}(\eta_{0},y)<\overline{\theta}<-|\theta_{\Gamma-}^{\operatorname{in}}|
. It follows that
\left\{(\eta,y,t)\in T:\eta\leq\eta_{0},t<\mathfrak{b}(\eta_{0},t)\right\}\subset\Gamma(T_{n_{0}})
and thus
the graph
\eta\mapsto\mathfrak{b}(\eta,y)
over
\eta\in(-\infty,\eta_{0}]

cannot intersect
\Gamma(X_{T_{>}})\cap T\cap A
for any
T_{>}\in\mathcal{T}
selected after
T_{n_{0}}
. We are down to bounding

(7.81)

\int_{\eta=\eta_{0}}^{\overline{\eta}}\left(\sum_{T\in\mathcal{T}_{>0}}\mathbbm{1}_{\Gamma(X_{T})}\left(\eta,y,\mathfrak{b}(\eta,y)\right)\right)\mathbbm{1}_{T\cap A}\left(\eta,y,\mathfrak{b}(\eta,y)\right)\mathfrak{b}(\eta,y)\frac{\mathrm{d}\eta}{|\Theta|}

Any tree
T_{>}\in\mathcal{T}_{>0}
satisfies

(7.82)

\alpha\xi_{T^{\prime}}<\alpha\xi_{T_{n_{0}}}+\alpha\varepsilon_{\max}<\eta_{0}+|\theta_{\Gamma-}^{\operatorname{in}}|\mathfrak{b}(\eta_{0},y)^{-1}+\alpha\varepsilon_{\max}<\eta_{0}+2|\theta_{\Gamma-}^{\operatorname{in}}|\mathfrak{b}(\eta_{0},y)^{-1}

by the selection condition, given that
\varepsilon_{\max}<\frac{|\theta_{\Gamma-}^{\operatorname{in}}|}{S}
. The graph
\eta\mapsto\mathfrak{b}(\eta,y)
intersects
\Gamma(X_{T_{>}})\cap T\subset\Gamma\left(\pi_{T_{>}}\left(\mathfrak{T}_{\Theta^{\operatorname{in}}}\right)\right)\cap T
only if

(7.83)

\mathfrak{b}(\eta,y)<\frac{|\theta^{\operatorname{in}}_{\Gamma+}|}{\eta-\alpha\xi_{T_{>}}}.

This shows that if
\eta>\alpha\xi_{T_{>}}+\varepsilon^{\prime}\mathfrak{b}(\eta_{0},y)^{-1}
for some
\varepsilon^{\prime}>0
then

(7.84)

\mathfrak{b}(\eta,y)<\frac{|\theta^{\operatorname{in}}_{\Gamma+}|\mathfrak{b}(\eta_{0},y)}{\varepsilon^{\prime}}.

We have already established that

(7.85)

\mathfrak{b}(\eta,y)\leq\frac{|\theta_{\Gamma-}|\mathfrak{b}(\eta_{0},y)}{|\theta_{\Gamma-}|-\mathfrak{b}(\eta_{0},y)(\eta-\eta_{0})}

so if
\eta<\alpha\xi_{T^{\prime}}+\varepsilon^{\prime}\mathfrak{b}(\eta_{0},y)^{-1}
then

0<\eta-\eta_{0}<(2|\theta_{\Gamma-}^{\operatorname{in}}|+\varepsilon^{\prime})\mathfrak{b}(\eta_{0},y)^{-1}
so

(7.86)

\mathfrak{b}(\eta,y)\leq\frac{|\theta_{\Gamma-}|\mathfrak{b}(\eta_{0},y)}{|\theta_{\Gamma-}|-(2|\theta_{\Gamma-}^{\operatorname{in}}|+\varepsilon^{\prime})}.

Choosing
\varepsilon^{\prime}=1/4
we get that

(7.87)

\mathfrak{b}(\eta,y)\leq(|\theta_{\Gamma-}|+4|\theta^{\operatorname{in}}_{\Gamma+}|)\mathfrak{b}(\eta_{0},y)

when the graph
\eta\mapsto\mathfrak{b}(\eta,y)
intersects
\Gamma(X_{T_{>}})\cap T
. Since
\eta\mapsto\mathfrak{b}(\eta,y)
is non-decreasing
it holds that the graph
\eta\mapsto\mathfrak{b}(\eta,y)
intersects
T
only
if
\eta\mathfrak{b}(\eta_{0},y)<\theta_{\Gamma+}
so we have shown that

(7.88)

\displaystyle\int_{\eta=\eta_{0}}^{\overline{\eta}}\left(\sum_{T\in\mathcal{T}_{>0}}\mathbbm{1}_{\Gamma(X_{T})}\left(\eta,y,\mathfrak{b}(\eta,y)\right)\right)\mathbbm{1}_{T\cap A}\left(\eta,y,\mathfrak{b}(\eta,y)\right)\mathfrak{b}(\eta,y)\frac{\mathrm{d}\eta}{|\Theta|}

\displaystyle\leq\int_{\eta=\theta_{\Gamma-}/\mathfrak{b}(\eta_{0},y)}^{\theta_{\Gamma+}/\mathfrak{b}(\eta_{0},y)}(|\theta_{\Gamma-}|+4|\theta^{\operatorname{in}}_{\Gamma+}|)\mathfrak{b}(\eta_{0},y)\frac{\mathrm{d}\eta}{|\Theta|}\lesssim|\theta_{\Gamma-}|+4|\theta^{\operatorname{in}}_{\Gamma+}|.

The claim follows.
∎

The covering lemma above allows us to obtain the following uniform weak
L^{1}

bound.

Lemma 7.7
.

Let
V\in\mathbb{D}^{\cup}_{\beta}
,
W\in\mathbb{T}_{\Theta}^{\cup}
and
E=V\cap W
or
E=V\cup W
. The bound

(7.89)

\displaystyle\left\|F\circ\Gamma(\eta,y,t)t\mathrm{d}_{t}\mathbbm{1}_{E}(\eta,y,t)\right\|_{L^{1,\infty}_{\mu_{\Theta}^{1}}\Gamma^{\ast}\mathbb{L}^{(1,1)}_{(\Theta,\Theta^{\operatorname{in}})}}\lesssim\|F(\eta,y,t)\|_{L^{1}_{\mu_{\Theta}^{1}}\mathbb{L}^{(\infty,\infty)}_{\Theta_{\Gamma}}},

holds for any function
F\in L^{\infty}_{\operatorname{loc}}(\mathbb{R}^{3}_{+})
. The
implicit constant is independent of the set
E
.

Proof.

We need to show that for every
\lambda>0
there exists a set
W_{\lambda}=\bigcup_{T\in\mathcal{T}\subset\mathbb{T}_{\Theta}}T
such that

(7.90)

\displaystyle\left\|\mathbbm{1}_{\mathbb{R}^{3}_{+}\setminus W_{\lambda}}F\circ\Gamma(\eta,y,t)t\mathrm{d}_{t}\mathbbm{1}_{E}(\eta,y,t)\right\|_{\Gamma^{\ast}\mathbb{L}^{(1,1)}_{(\Theta,\Theta^{\operatorname{in}})}}\leq\lambda,

\displaystyle\mu_{\Theta}^{1}(W_{\lambda})\lesssim\lambda^{-1}\|F\|_{L^{1}_{{\mu_{\Theta}^{1}}_{\Gamma}}\mathbb{L}^{(\infty,\infty)}_{\Theta_{\Gamma}}}.

We may assume that
F
is supported on a compact set by the discussion at
the end of
Section

3.2.2
. Fix
\lambda>0
and let
W_{\lambda}:=\bigcup_{T^{\prime}\in\mathcal{T}_{\lambda}\subset\mathbb{T}_{\Theta}}T^{\prime}

where
\mathcal{T}_{\lambda}\subset\mathbb{T}_{\Theta}
is obtained by
applying
Lemma

7.6
to
F\circ\Gamma
in place of

F
. It remains to prove the required bounds on
\mu_{\Theta}^{1}(W_{\lambda})
. Let
(\eta,y)\mapsto\mathfrak{b}(\eta,y)
be the function
whose graph is the boundary of
E
, as described in

Lemma

3.20
. For each tree
T^{\prime}\in\mathcal{T}_{\lambda}
, Lemma
7.6
gives the
bound

(7.91)

\int_{\mathbb{R}^{2}}\mathbbm{1}_{X_{T^{\prime}}}\left(\eta,y,\mathfrak{b}(\eta,y)\right)\left|F\circ\Gamma(\eta,y,\mathfrak{b}(\eta,y))\right|\mathfrak{b}(\eta,y)\mathrm{d}\eta\mathrm{d}y\geq\lambda\mu^{1}_{\Theta}(T).

and thus

(7.92)

\displaystyle\lambda\mu^{1}_{\Theta}(W_{\lambda})

\displaystyle\lesssim\lambda\sum_{T^{\prime}\in\mathcal{T}_{\lambda}}\mu^{1}_{\Theta}(T^{\prime})

\displaystyle\lesssim\int_{\mathbb{R}^{2}}\left(\sum_{T^{\prime}\in\mathcal{T}_{\lambda}}\mathbbm{1}_{X_{T}}\left(\eta,y,\mathfrak{b}(\eta,y)\right)\right)\left|F\circ\Gamma(\eta,y,\mathfrak{b}(\eta,y))\right|\mathfrak{b}(\eta,y)\mathrm{d}\eta\mathrm{d}y

\displaystyle\lesssim\begin{aligned} &\int_{\mathbb{R}^{2}}\left(\sum_{T^{\prime}\in\mathcal{T}_{\lambda}}\mathbbm{1}_{\Gamma(X_{T^{\prime}})}\left(\alpha\left(\eta+\gamma\mathfrak{b}(\eta,y)^{-1}\right),y,\beta\mathfrak{b}(\eta,y)\right)\right)\\
&\qquad\times\left|F(\alpha\left(\eta+\gamma\mathfrak{b}(\eta,y)^{-1}\right),y,\beta\mathfrak{b}(\eta,y))\right|\mathfrak{b}(\eta,y)\mathrm{d}\eta\mathrm{d}y.\end{aligned}

Let us enact the Lipschitz change of variables
\eta^{\prime}(\eta,y)=\alpha\left(\eta+\gamma\mathfrak{b}(\eta,y)^{-1}\right)
. We have the following
bound on the Jacobian:

(7.93)

\frac{\mathrm{d}\eta^{\prime}(\eta,y)}{\mathrm{d}\eta}=\alpha\left(1-\gamma\frac{\partial_{\eta}\mathfrak{b}(\eta,y)}{\mathfrak{b}(\eta,y)^{2}}\right)\gtrsim\alpha;

this holds since
|\gamma|<1
and
|\theta_{+}|,|\theta_{-}|\geq 2

and thus, according to (
3.150
), it holds that
\left|\gamma\frac{\partial_{\eta}\mathfrak{b}(\eta,y)}{\mathfrak{b}(\eta,y)^{2}}\right|<\frac{1}{2}
. Let
(\eta^{\prime},y)\mapsto\mathfrak{b}_{\Gamma}(\eta^{\prime},y)
be the
function whose graph is the boundary of
\Gamma(E)
.

Lemma

3.20
applies since
\Gamma(E)=\Gamma(V)\cap\Gamma(W)
(or, respectively,
\Gamma(E)=\Gamma(V)\cup\Gamma(W)
)
and it holds that
\Gamma(V)\in\mathbb{D}_{1}^{\cup}
and
\Gamma(W)\in\mathbb{T}^{\cup}_{\Theta_{\Gamma}}.
It follows that

(7.94)

\beta\mathfrak{b}(\eta,y)=\mathfrak{b}_{\Gamma}(\eta^{\prime},y)

since the boundary of
E
is the set

\left\{\left(\eta,y,\mathfrak{b}(\eta,y)\right),(\eta,y)\in\mathbb{R}^{2}\right\}

while the image under
\Gamma
of this set is

(7.95)

\displaystyle\Gamma\left(\left\{\left(\eta,y,\mathfrak{b}(\eta,y)\right),(\eta,y)\in\mathbb{R}^{2}\right\}\right)

\displaystyle\begin{aligned} &=\left\{\left(\left(\eta+\gamma\mathfrak{b}(\eta,y)^{-1}\right),y,\beta\mathfrak{b}(\eta,y)\right):(\eta,y)\in\mathbb{R}^{2}\right\}\\
&=\left\{\left(\eta^{\prime},y,\mathfrak{b}_{\Gamma}(\eta^{\prime},y)\right):(\eta^{\prime},y)\in\mathbb{R}^{2}\right\}.\end{aligned}

We get that

(7.96)

\displaystyle\lambda\mu^{1}_{\Theta}(W_{\lambda})\lesssim\frac{1}{\alpha\beta}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{2}}\begin{aligned} &\left(\sum_{T^{\prime}\in\mathcal{T}_{\lambda}}\mathbbm{1}_{\Gamma(X_{T^{\prime}})}\left(\eta^{\prime},y,\mathfrak{b}_{\Gamma}(\eta^{\prime},y)\right)\right)\\
&\qquad\times\left|F(\eta^{\prime},y,\mathfrak{b}_{\Gamma}(\eta^{\prime},y))\right|\mathfrak{b}_{\Gamma}(\eta^{\prime},y)\mathrm{d}\eta^{\prime}\mathrm{d}y\end{aligned}

It remains to bound the
\mathrm{RHS}{}
of this inequality from above. By

Proposition

3.5
and by the Hölder inequality
(
Corollary

3.4
) we have that

(7.97)

\displaystyle\int_{\mathbb{R}^{2}}\left(\sum_{T^{\prime}\in\mathcal{T}_{\lambda}}\mathbbm{1}_{\Gamma(X_{T^{\prime}})}\left(\eta^{\prime},y,\mathfrak{b}_{\Gamma}(\eta^{\prime},y)\right)\right)\left|F(\eta^{\prime},y,\mathfrak{b}_{\Gamma}(\eta^{\prime},y))\right|\mathfrak{b}_{\Gamma}(\eta^{\prime},y)\mathrm{d}\eta^{\prime}\mathrm{d}y

\displaystyle=\int_{\mathbb{R}^{2}}\left(\sum_{T^{\prime}\in\mathcal{T}_{\lambda}}\mathbbm{1}_{\Gamma(X_{T^{\prime}})}(\eta^{\prime},y,t^{\prime})\right)|F(\eta^{\prime},y,t^{\prime})|t^{\prime}\delta\left(t^{\prime}-\mathfrak{b}_{\Gamma}(\eta^{\prime},y)\right)\mathrm{d}\eta^{\prime}\mathrm{d}y

\displaystyle\leq\left\|\left(\sum_{T^{\prime}\in\mathcal{T}_{\lambda}}\mathbbm{1}_{\Gamma(X_{T^{\prime}})}(\eta^{\prime},y,t^{\prime})\right)F(\eta^{\prime},y,t^{\prime})t^{\prime}\delta\left(t^{\prime}-\mathfrak{b}_{\Gamma}(\eta^{\prime},y)\right)\right\|_{L^{1}_{\mu_{\Theta_{\Gamma}}^{1}}\mathbb{L}^{(1,1)}_{\Theta_{\Gamma}}}

\displaystyle\leq\|F(\eta^{\prime},y,t^{\prime})\|_{L^{1}_{\mu_{\Theta_{\Gamma}}^{1}}\mathbb{L}^{(\infty,\infty)}_{\Theta_{\Gamma}}}\left\|\left(\sum_{T^{\prime}\in\mathcal{T}_{\lambda}}\mathbbm{1}_{\Gamma(X_{T^{\prime}})}(\eta^{\prime},y,t^{\prime})\right)t^{\prime}\delta\left(t^{\prime}-\mathfrak{b}_{\Gamma}(\eta^{\prime},y)\right)\right\|_{L^{\infty}_{\mu_{\Theta_{\Gamma}}^{1}}\mathbb{L}^{(1,1)}_{\Theta_{\Gamma}}}

Using bound (
7.60
) provided by

Lemma

7.6
we have

(7.98)

\displaystyle\left\|\left(\sum_{T^{\prime}\in\mathcal{T}_{\lambda}}\mathbbm{1}_{\Gamma(X_{T^{\prime}})}(\eta^{\prime},y,t^{\prime})\right)t^{\prime}\delta\left(t^{\prime}-\mathfrak{b}_{\Gamma}(\eta^{\prime},y)\right)\right\|_{L^{\infty}_{\mu_{\Theta_{\Gamma}}^{1}}\mathbb{L}^{(1,1)}_{\Theta_{\Gamma}}}

\displaystyle=\left\|\left(\sum_{T^{\prime}\in\mathcal{T}_{\lambda}}\mathbbm{1}_{\Gamma(X_{T^{\prime}})}(\eta^{\prime},y,t^{\prime})\right)t^{\prime}\mathrm{d}_{t}\mathbbm{1}_{\Gamma(E)}(\eta^{\prime},y,t^{\prime})\right\|_{L^{\infty}_{\mu_{\Theta_{\Gamma}}^{1}}\mathbb{L}^{(1,1)}_{\Theta_{\Gamma}}}\lesssim 1

that concludes the proof.
∎

We are now ready to deduce
Proposition

7.2
from

Lemma

7.7
. The procedure is analogous to the deduction of

Proposition

7.1
from
Lemma

7.5
: first we
reduce to a scalar inequality and then we conclude using restricted weak type
interpolation with one endpoint provided by
Lemma

7.7
. The

L^{\infty}
endpoint of this argument is more involved than in the proof of

Proposition

7.1
. The reader is encouraged to refer to the
proof of
Proposition

7.1
for the explanation of the common
steps with the proof below, that here are presented succinctly.

Proof.

Proof of
Proposition

7.2

Bound (
7.2
) follows from the scalar
bound

(7.99)

\left\|F\circ\Gamma(\eta,y,t)t\mathrm{d}_{t}\mathbbm{1}_{E}(\eta,y,t)\right\|_{L^{r}_{\mu_{\Theta}^{1}}\Gamma^{{}^{\ast}}\mathbb{L}^{(u,1)}_{(\Theta,\Theta^{\operatorname{in}})}}\lesssim\|F(\eta,y,t)\|_{L^{r}_{\mu_{\Theta_{\Gamma}}^{1}}\mathbb{L}^{(\infty,\infty)}_{\Theta_{\Gamma}}}

for all
F\in L^{\infty}_{\operatorname{loc}}(\mathbb{R}^{3}_{+})
, as has been
discussed in
Corollary

3.14
and at the
beginning of the proof of
Proposition

7.1
. Next, we deduce
the bound (
7.99
) from the
bound (
7.89
). It is sufficient to prove
(
7.99
) with
u=1
since
F\in L^{\infty}_{\operatorname{loc}}(\mathbb{R}^{3}_{+})
is arbitrary,

(7.100)

\|F(\eta,y,t)\|_{L^{r}_{\mu_{\Theta_{\Gamma}}^{1}}\mathbb{L}^{(\infty,\infty)}_{\Theta_{\Gamma}}}=\||F(\eta,y,t)|^{u}\|_{L^{r/u}_{\mu_{\Theta_{\Gamma}}^{1}}\mathbb{L}^{(\infty,\infty)}_{\Theta_{\Gamma}}}^{1/u},

and by (
3.151
) it holds that

(7.101)

\displaystyle\left\|F\circ\Gamma(\eta,y,t)t\mathrm{d}_{t}\mathbbm{1}_{E}(\eta,y,t)\right\|_{L^{r}_{\mu_{\Theta}^{1}}\Gamma^{{}^{\ast}}\mathbb{L}^{(u,1)}_{(\Theta,\Theta^{\operatorname{in}})}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\left\||F|^{u}\circ\Gamma(\eta,y,t)t\mathrm{d}_{t}\mathbbm{1}_{E}(\eta,y,t)\right\|_{L^{r/u}_{\mu_{\Theta}^{1}}\Gamma^{{}^{\ast}}\mathbb{L}^{(1,1)}_{(\Theta,\Theta^{\operatorname{in}})}}^{1/u}.

Now, supposing
u=1
, we prove the bound by interpolation
(
Proposition

3.3
). The weak
L^{1}
endpoint is
given by
Lemma

7.7
. The
r=\infty
endpoint for
(
7.52
), given by

(7.102)

\left\|F\circ\Gamma(\eta,y,t)t\mathrm{d}_{t}\mathbbm{1}_{E}(\eta,y,t)\right\|_{L^{\infty}_{\mu_{\Theta}^{1}}\Gamma^{{}^{\ast}}\mathbb{L}^{(1,1)}_{(\Theta,\Theta^{\operatorname{in}})}}\leq\|F(\eta,y,t)\|_{L^{r}_{\mu_{\Theta_{\Gamma}}^{1}}\mathbb{L}^{(\infty,\infty)}_{\Theta_{\Gamma}}}.

This bound holds because

(7.103)

\displaystyle\left\|F\circ\Gamma(\eta,y,t)t\mathrm{d}_{t}\mathbbm{1}_{E}(\eta,y,t)\right\|_{L^{\infty}_{\mu_{\Theta}^{1}}\Gamma^{{}^{\ast}}\mathbb{L}^{(1,1)}_{(\Theta,\Theta^{\operatorname{in}})}}

\displaystyle\leq\|F\circ\Gamma(\eta,y,t)\|_{L^{\infty}_{\mu_{\Theta}^{1}}\Gamma^{{}^{\ast}}\mathbb{L}^{(\infty,\infty)}_{\Theta}}\left\|t\mathrm{d}_{t}\mathbbm{1}_{E}(\eta,y,t)\right\|_{L^{\infty}_{\mu_{\Theta}^{1}}\Gamma^{{}^{\ast}}\mathbb{L}^{(1,1)}_{(\Theta,\Theta^{\operatorname{in}})}}

\displaystyle\leq\|F\|_{L^{\infty}_{\mu_{\Theta_{\Gamma}}^{1}}\mathbb{L}^{(\infty,\infty)}_{\Theta_{\Gamma}}}\left\|t\mathrm{d}_{t}\mathbbm{1}_{E}(\eta,y,t)\right\|_{L^{\infty}_{\mu_{\Theta}^{1}}\Gamma^{{}^{\ast}}\mathbb{L}^{(1,1)}_{(\Theta,\Theta^{\operatorname{in}})}}\leq\|F\|_{L^{\infty}_{\mu_{\Theta_{\Gamma}}^{1}}\mathbb{L}^{(\infty,\infty)}_{\Theta_{\Gamma}}}.

The last bound requires us to show that

(7.104)

\left\|t\mathrm{d}_{t}\mathbbm{1}_{E}(\eta,y,t)\right\|_{\Gamma^{{}^{\ast}}\mathbb{L}^{(1,1)}_{(\Theta,\Theta^{\operatorname{in}})}(T)}\lesssim 1\qquad\forall T\in\mathbb{T}_{\Theta},

that we will now show and, allowing us to conclude the proof. Let
(\eta,y)\mapsto\mathfrak{b}(\eta,y)
is the function whose graph is the boundary of

E
, as described by
Lemma

3.20
. The same lemma gives
that

(7.105)

\displaystyle\left\|t\mathrm{d}_{t}\mathbbm{1}_{E}(\eta,y,t)\right\|_{\Gamma^{{}^{\ast}}\mathbb{L}^{(1,1)}_{(\Theta,\Theta^{\operatorname{in}})}(T)}

\displaystyle=\int_{\mathbb{R}^{2}_{+}}\mathbbm{1}_{\pi_{T}\left(\mathfrak{T}_{\Theta^{\operatorname{in}}}\right)}\left(\eta,y,\mathfrak{b}(\eta,y)\right)\mathfrak{b}(\eta,y)\frac{\mathrm{d}y\mathrm{d}\eta}{s_{T}|\Theta|}.

By symmetry, assume
T=T_{\Theta}(0,0,1)
. Let us denote
\Theta=(\theta_{-},\theta_{+})
and
\Theta^{\operatorname{in}}=(\theta_{-}^{\operatorname{in}},\theta^{\operatorname{in}}_{+})
. Integrating bounds (
3.150
),
for
\eta>0
it holds that

(7.106)

\frac{|\theta_{+}|\mathfrak{b}(0,y)}{|\theta_{+}|+\eta\mathfrak{b}(0,y)}\leq\mathfrak{b}(\eta,y),

and if also
\eta<|\theta_{-}|\mathfrak{b}(0,y)^{-1}
then

(7.107)

\mathfrak{b}(\eta,y)\leq\frac{|\theta_{-}|\mathfrak{b}(0,y)}{|\theta_{-}|-\eta\mathfrak{b}(0,y)}.

When
\eta<0
it holds that

(7.108)

\frac{|\theta_{-}|\mathfrak{b}(0,y)}{|\theta_{-}|-\eta\mathfrak{b}(0,y)}\leq\mathfrak{b}(\eta,y)\leq\frac{|\theta_{+}|\mathfrak{b}(0,y)}{|\theta_{+}|+\eta\mathfrak{b}(0,y)}

and if also
\eta>-|\theta_{+}|\mathfrak{b}(0,y)^{-1}
then

(7.109)

\mathfrak{b}(\eta,y)\leq\frac{|\theta_{+}|\mathfrak{b}(0,y)}{|\theta_{+}|+\eta\mathfrak{b}(0,y)}.

Since
\left(\eta,y,\mathfrak{b}(\eta,y)\right)\in\pi_{T}\left(\mathfrak{T}_{\Theta^{\operatorname{in}}}\right)
only if
\left|\eta\mathfrak{b}(\eta,y)\right|<\theta^{\operatorname{in}}_{\ast}\coloneqq\max(|\theta^{\operatorname{in}}_{+}|,|\theta^{\operatorname{in}}_{-}|)
.

Let us show that if the graph of
\mathfrak{b}
intersects
\pi_{T}\left(\mathfrak{T}_{\Theta^{\operatorname{in}}}\right)
then
\mathfrak{b}(\eta,y)\lesssim\mathfrak{b}(0,y)
. Supposing that
\eta>0
, if
\mathfrak{b}
intersects
\pi_{T}\left(\mathfrak{T}_{\Theta^{\operatorname{in}}}\right)
then

(7.110)

\mathfrak{b}(\eta,y)\leq\frac{|\theta_{-}|\mathfrak{b}(0,y)}{|\theta_{-}|-\eta\mathfrak{b}(0,y)}\mathbbm{1}_{\eta<\frac{|\theta_{-}|}{2\mathfrak{b}(0,y)}}+\frac{\theta^{\operatorname{in}}_{\ast}}{\eta}\mathbbm{1}_{\eta>\frac{|\theta_{-}|}{2\mathfrak{b}(0,y)}}\leq 2\left(1+\frac{\theta^{\operatorname{in}}_{+}}{|\theta_{-}|}\right)\mathfrak{b}(0,y)

On the other hand if
\eta<0
then

(7.111)

\mathfrak{b}(\eta,y)\leq\frac{|\theta_{+}|\mathfrak{b}(0,y)}{|\theta_{+}|+\eta\mathfrak{b}(0,y)}\mathbbm{1}_{\eta>-\frac{|\theta_{+}|}{2\mathfrak{b}(0,y)}}+\frac{\theta^{\operatorname{in}}_{\ast}}{|\eta|}\mathbbm{1}_{\eta<-\frac{|\theta_{+}|}{2\mathfrak{b}(0,y)}}\leq 2\left(1+\frac{|\theta^{\operatorname{in}}_{-}|}{|\theta_{+}|}\right)\mathfrak{b}(0,y).

Let us show that if the graph of
\mathfrak{b}
intersects
\pi_{T}\left(\mathfrak{T}_{\Theta^{\operatorname{in}}}\right)
then
|\eta|\lesssim\mathfrak{b}(0,y)^{-1}
. Given the bounds on
\mathfrak{b}
, the graph of
\mathfrak{b}
intersects
\pi_{T}\left(\mathfrak{T}_{\Theta^{\operatorname{in}}}\right)
only if

(7.112)

\displaystyle\frac{|\theta_{+}|\mathfrak{b}(0,y)}{|\theta_{+}|+\eta\mathfrak{b}(0,y)}<\frac{\theta^{\operatorname{in}}_{\ast}}{\eta}\text{ when }\eta>0,

\displaystyle\frac{|\theta_{-}|\mathfrak{b}(0,y)}{|\theta_{-}|+|\eta|\mathfrak{b}(0,y)}<\frac{\theta^{\operatorname{in}}_{\ast}}{|\eta|}\text{ when }\eta<0,

and thus

(7.113)

\displaystyle\mathfrak{b}(0,y)(|\theta_{+}|-\theta^{\operatorname{in}}_{\ast})|\eta|<|\theta_{+}|\theta^{\operatorname{in}}_{\ast}\text{ when }\eta>0,

\displaystyle\mathfrak{b}(0,y)(|\theta_{-}|-\theta^{\operatorname{in}}_{\ast})|\eta|<|\theta_{+}|\theta^{\operatorname{in}}_{\ast}\text{ when }\eta<0.

Since
\theta^{\operatorname{in}}_{\ast}<1<\max(|\theta_{+}|,|\theta_{-}|)
it
follows that
|\eta|\lesssim\mathfrak{b}(0,y)^{-1}
. Together with the
bound
\mathfrak{b}(\eta,y)\lesssim\mathfrak{b}(0,y)
it shows that

(7.114)

\int_{\mathbb{R}^{2}_{+}}\mathbbm{1}_{\pi_{T}\left(\mathfrak{T}_{\Theta^{\operatorname{in}}}\right)}\left(\eta,y,\mathfrak{b}(\eta,y)\right)\mathfrak{b}(\eta,y)\frac{\mathrm{d}y\mathrm{d}\eta}{s_{T}|\Theta|}\lesssim 1

as required.
∎

7.3.
Uniform bounds for the
\widetilde{\mathbb{F}}_{\Gamma}^{u}\Phi_{\mathfrak{r}}^{N}

size

In this section we prove
Lemma

7.3
. The size

\widetilde{\mathbb{F}}_{\Gamma}^{u}\Phi_{\mathfrak{r}}^{N}
, as defined in
(
1.27
), consists of three parts; the
three lemmata
7.8
,

7.9
, and
7.10

below address these parts: the sizes
{\Gamma^{\ast}}\mathbb{L}^{(u,1)}_{(\Theta,\Theta^{\operatorname{in}})}\Box_{\mathfrak{r}}^{N}
,
{\Gamma^{\ast}}\mathbb{L}^{(u,2)}_{\Theta}\mathfrak{d}_{\mathfrak{r}}^{N}
, and
{\beta^{\frac{1}{u}}}\Gamma^{\ast}\mathbb{L}^{(u,\infty)}_{\Theta}\Phi_{\mathfrak{r}}^{N}
respectively.
Lemma

7.8
,
up next, amounts to showing that the defect of
\mathbbm{1}_{(V^{+}\cap W^{+})\setminus V^{-}}\mathrm{Emb}[f]\circ\Gamma
is essentially concentrated on the boundary of the
region
(V^{+}\cap W^{+})\setminus V^{-}
and thus can be controlled by the
quantity on
\mathrm{LHS}{\eqref{prop:unif-gamma-bounds:singular-size}}
, introduced
in
Proposition

7.2
.

Lemma 7.8

(Defect size domination)
.

Let
V^{+},V^{-}\in\mathbb{D}^{\cup}_{\beta}

and
W^{+},W^{-}\in\mathbb{T}_{\Theta}^{\cup}
. The bounds

(7.115)

\displaystyle\Bigl\|\mathbbm{1}_{(V^{+}\cap W^{+})\setminus(V^{-}\cup W^{-})}\left(\mathrm{Emb}[f]\circ\Gamma\right)\Bigr\|_{{\Gamma^{\ast}}\mathbb{L}^{(u,1)}_{(\Theta,\Theta^{\operatorname{in}})}\Box_{\mathfrak{r}}^{N}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\begin{aligned} &\Bigl\|\mathbbm{1}_{\overline{(V^{+}\cap W^{+})}\setminus(V^{-}\cup W^{-})}\left(\mathrm{Emb}[f]\circ\Gamma\right)(\eta,y,t)t\mathrm{d}_{t}\mathbbm{1}_{(V^{+}\cap W^{+})}(\eta,y,t)\Bigr\|_{{\Gamma^{\ast}}\mathbb{L}^{(u,1)}_{(\Theta,\Theta^{\operatorname{in}})}\Phi_{16\mathfrak{r}}^{N-8}}\\
&+\Bigl\|\mathbbm{1}_{\overline{(V^{+}\cap W^{+})}\setminus(V^{-}\cup W^{-})}\left(\mathrm{Emb}[f]\circ\Gamma\right)(\eta,y,t)t\mathrm{d}_{t}\mathbbm{1}_{\left(\mathbb{R}^{3}_{+}\setminus V^{-}\right)}(\eta,y,t)\Bigr\|_{{\Gamma^{\ast}}\mathbb{L}^{(u,1)}_{(\Theta,\Theta^{\operatorname{in}})}\Phi_{16\mathfrak{r}}^{N-8}}\\
&+\Bigl\|\mathbbm{1}_{(V^{+}\cap W^{+})\setminus(V^{-}\cup W^{-})}\left(\mathrm{Emb}[f]\circ\Gamma\right)\Bigr\|_{\Gamma^{\ast}\mathbb{L}^{(u,2)}_{(\Theta,\Theta^{\operatorname{ex}})}\Phi_{16\mathfrak{r}}^{N-8}}\end{aligned}

hold for any
f\in\mathscr{S}(\mathbb{R})
and for all
u\in[1,+\infty]
. The implicit constant is independent of the sets
V^{+}
,
V^{-}
,

W^{+}
, and
W^{-}
.

Proof.

To show (
7.115
) it is sufficient to prove that

(7.116)

\displaystyle\begin{aligned} &\Bigl\|\mathbbm{1}_{(V^{+}\cap W^{+})\setminus(V^{-}\cup W^{-})}\left(\mathrm{Emb}[f]\circ\Gamma\right)\Bigr\|_{{\Gamma^{\ast}}\mathbb{L}^{(u,1)}_{(\Theta,\Theta^{\operatorname{in}})}\Box_{\zeta,\mathfrak{r}}^{N}(T)}\\
&\qquad+\Bigl\|\mathbbm{1}_{(V^{+}\cap W^{+})\setminus(V^{-}\cup W^{-})}\left(\mathrm{Emb}[f]\circ\Gamma\right)\Bigr\|_{{\Gamma^{\ast}}\mathbb{L}^{(u,1)}_{(\Theta,\Theta^{\operatorname{in}})}\Box_{\sigma,\mathfrak{r}}^{N}(T)}\end{aligned}

\displaystyle\lesssim\begin{aligned} &\Bigl\|\mathbbm{1}_{\overline{(V^{+}\cap W^{+})}\setminus(V^{-}\cup W^{-})}\left(\mathrm{Emb}[f]\circ\Gamma\right)(\eta,y,t)t\mathrm{d}_{t}\mathbbm{1}_{(V^{+}\cap W^{+})}(\eta,y,t)\Bigr\|_{{\Gamma^{\ast}}\mathbb{L}^{(u,1)}_{(\Theta,\Theta^{\operatorname{in}})}\Phi_{16\mathfrak{r}}^{N-8}}\\
&+\Bigl\|\mathbbm{1}_{\overline{(V^{+}\cap W^{+})}\setminus(V^{-}\cup W^{-})}\left(\mathrm{Emb}[f]\circ\Gamma\right)(\eta,y,t)t\mathrm{d}_{t}\mathbbm{1}_{\left(\mathbb{R}^{3}_{+}\setminus V^{-}\right)}(\eta,y,t)\Bigr\|_{{\Gamma^{\ast}}\mathbb{L}^{(u,1)}_{(\Theta,\Theta^{\operatorname{in}})}\Phi_{16\mathfrak{r}}^{N-8}}\\
&+\Bigl\|\mathbbm{1}_{\overline{(V^{+}\cap W^{+})}\setminus(V^{-}\cup W^{-})}\left(\mathrm{Emb}[f]\circ\Gamma\right)\Bigr\|_{\Gamma^{\ast}\mathbb{L}^{(u,2)}_{(\Theta,\Theta^{\operatorname{ex}})}\Phi_{16\mathfrak{r}}^{N-8}}\end{aligned}

holds for any
T\in\mathbb{T}_{\Theta}
. Let us fix
f\in\mathscr{S}(\mathbb{R})
. By symmetry, according to (
3.94
) and the
subsequent discussion, we can assume that
{T=T_{\Theta}}(0,0,1)
so
that
\Gamma\circ\pi_{T}(\theta,\zeta,\sigma)=(\alpha\beta(\theta+\gamma)(\beta\sigma)^{-1},\zeta,\beta\sigma)=(\theta_{\Gamma}(\beta\sigma)^{-1},\zeta,\beta\sigma)
. For conciseness we set
F_{\Gamma}=\mathrm{Emb}[f]\circ\Gamma
. By (
3.98
) it holds that

(7.117)

\displaystyle\left(\Gamma^{\ast}\mathfrak{d}_{\zeta}(t\eta)-\beta t\mathrm{d}_{y}\right)F_{\Gamma}(\eta,y,t)=0,

\displaystyle\left(\Gamma^{\ast}\mathfrak{d}_{\sigma}(t\eta)-t\mathrm{d}_{t}+\eta\mathrm{d}_{\eta}\right)F_{\Gamma}(\eta,y,t)=0.

According to definitions (
3.109
) (see also the
discussion below (
3.98
)) it holds that

(7.118)

\displaystyle\|F_{\Gamma}(\eta,y,t)\|_{\Gamma^{\ast}{\mathbb{L}_{(\Theta,\Theta^{\operatorname{in}})}^{(u,1)}}\Box_{\zeta,\mathfrak{r}}^{N}(T)}

\displaystyle\leq\begin{aligned} &{\left\|\left(\beta t\mathrm{d}_{y}\mathbbm{1}_{(W^{+}\cap V^{+})}(\eta,y,t)\right)\left(\mathbbm{1}_{\mathbb{R}^{3}_{+}\setminus(W^{-}\cup V^{-})}F_{\Gamma}\right)(\eta,y,t)\right\|_{\mathbb{L}_{(\Theta,\Theta^{\operatorname{in}})}^{(u,1)}\Phi_{\mathfrak{r}}^{N}(T)}}\\
&+{\left\|\left(\beta t\mathrm{d}_{y}\mathbbm{1}_{\mathbb{R}^{3}_{+}\setminus V^{-}}(\eta,y,t)\right)\left(\mathbbm{1}_{\overline{(W^{+}\cap V^{+})}\setminus(W^{-}\cup V^{-})}F_{\Gamma}\right)(\eta,y,t)\right\|_{\mathbb{L}_{(\Theta,\Theta^{\operatorname{in}})}^{(u,1)}\Phi_{\mathfrak{r}}^{N}(T)}}\\
&+{\left\|\left(\beta t\mathrm{d}_{y}\mathbbm{1}_{\mathbb{R}^{3}_{+}\setminus W^{-}}(\eta,y,t)\right)\left(\mathbbm{1}_{\overline{(W^{+}\cap V^{+})}\setminus(W^{-}\cup V^{-})}F_{\Gamma}\right)(\eta,y,t)\right\|_{\mathbb{L}_{(\Theta,\Theta^{\operatorname{in}})}^{(u,1)}\Phi_{\mathfrak{r}}^{N}(T)}},\end{aligned}

and

(7.119)

\displaystyle\|F_{\Gamma}(\eta,y,t)\|_{\Gamma^{\ast}{\mathbb{L}_{(\Theta,\Theta^{\operatorname{in}})}^{(u,v)}}\Box_{\sigma,\mathfrak{r}}^{N}(T)}

\displaystyle\leq\begin{aligned} &{\left\|\left(\left(t\mathrm{d}_{t}-\eta\mathrm{d}_{\eta}\right)\mathbbm{1}_{(W^{+}\cap V^{+})}(\eta,y,t)\right)\left(\mathbbm{1}_{\mathbb{R}^{3}_{+}\setminus(W^{-}\cup V^{-})}F_{\Gamma}\right)(\eta,y,t)\right\|_{\mathbb{L}_{(\Theta,\Theta^{\operatorname{in}})}^{(u,1)}\Phi_{\mathfrak{r}}^{N}(T)}}\\
&+{\left\|\left(\left(t\mathrm{d}_{t}-\eta\mathrm{d}_{\eta}\right)\mathbbm{1}_{\mathbb{R}^{3}_{+}\setminus V^{-}}(\eta,y,t)\right)\left(\mathbbm{1}_{\overline{(W^{+}\cap V^{+})}\setminus(W^{-}\cup V^{-})}F_{\Gamma}\right)(\eta,y,t)\right\|_{\mathbb{L}_{(\Theta,\Theta^{\operatorname{in}})}^{(u,1)}\Phi_{\mathfrak{r}}^{N}(T)}}\\
&+{\left\|\left(\left(t\mathrm{d}_{t}-\eta\mathrm{d}_{\eta}\right)\mathbbm{1}_{\mathbb{R}^{3}_{+}\setminus W^{-}}(\eta,y,t)\right)\left(\mathbbm{1}_{\overline{(W^{+}\cap V^{+})}\setminus(W^{-}\cup V^{-})}F_{\Gamma}\right)(\eta,y,t)\right\|_{\mathbb{L}_{(\Theta,\Theta^{\operatorname{in}})}^{(u,1)}\Phi_{\mathfrak{r}}^{N}(T).}}\end{aligned}

Let
\mathfrak{b}_{+}
,
\mathfrak{b}_{-}
and
\mathfrak{b}_{W^{-}}
be the functions whose
graphs are the boundaries of
V^{+}\cap W^{+}
,
V^{-}
, and
W^{-}
respectively,
given by
Lemma

3.20
. According to
(
3.150
) we have

(7.120)

\displaystyle\left|\beta t\mathrm{d}_{y}\mathbbm{1}_{V^{+}\cap W^{+}}(\eta,y,t)\right|\lesssim t\delta\left(t-\mathfrak{b}_{+}(\eta,y)\right),

\displaystyle\left|\beta t\mathrm{d}_{y}\mathbbm{1}_{\mathbb{R}^{3}_{+}\setminus V^{-}}(\eta,y,t)\right|\lesssim t\delta\left(t-\mathfrak{b}_{-}(\eta,y)\right),

\displaystyle\left|\beta t\mathrm{d}_{y}\mathbbm{1}_{\mathbb{R}^{3}_{+}\setminus W^{-}}(\eta,y,t)\right|\lesssim t\delta\left(t-\mathfrak{b}_{W^{-}}(\eta,y)\right).

Similarly, when
(\eta,y,t)\in\pi_{T}\left(\mathfrak{T}_{\Theta}\right)
, or in
general when
\eta t
is bounded it holds that

(7.121)

\displaystyle\left|\left(t\mathrm{d}_{t}-\eta\mathrm{d}_{\eta}\right)\mathbbm{1}_{V^{+}\cap W^{+}}(\eta,y,t)\right|\lesssim t\delta\left(t-\mathfrak{b}_{+}(\eta,y)\right),

\displaystyle\left|\left(t\mathrm{d}_{t}-\eta\mathrm{d}_{\eta}\right)\mathbbm{1}_{\mathbb{R}^{3}_{+}\setminus V^{-}}(\eta,y,t)\right|\lesssim t\delta\left(t-\mathfrak{b}_{-}(\eta,y)\right),

\displaystyle\left|\left(t\mathrm{d}_{t}-\eta\mathrm{d}_{\eta}\right)\mathbbm{1}_{\mathbb{R}^{3}_{+}\setminus W^{-}}(\eta,y,t)\right|\lesssim t\delta\left(t-\mathfrak{b}_{W^{-}}(\eta,y)\right).

As a matter of fact, if
\mathfrak{b}
is any of the functions
\mathfrak{b}_{+},\mathfrak{b}_{-},\mathfrak{b}_{W^{-}}
then

(7.122)

\eta\mathrm{d}_{\eta}\mathbbm{1}_{t>\mathfrak{b}(\eta,y)}=-\delta\left(t-\mathfrak{b}(\eta,y)\right)\eta\mathrm{d}_{\eta}\mathfrak{b}(\eta,y)=-t\delta\left(t-\mathfrak{b}(\eta,y)\right)\eta t\frac{\mathrm{d}_{\eta}\mathfrak{b}(\eta,y)}{\mathfrak{b}(\eta,y)^{2}}

and, according to (
3.150
),
\left|\eta t\frac{\mathrm{d}_{\eta}\mathfrak{b}(\eta,y)}{\mathfrak{b}(\eta,y)^{2}}\right|\lesssim 1
.
Thus the first two summands in
\mathrm{RHS}{\eqref{eq:geometry-of-defects:space}}

and in
\mathrm{RHS}{\eqref{eq:geometry-of-defects:scale}}
are bounded by the first
two summands in
\mathrm{RHS}{}
(
7.115
). It remains to show
that the last summand of
\mathrm{RHS}{\eqref{eq:geometry-of-defects:space}}
and
the last summand of
\mathrm{RHS}{\eqref{eq:geometry-of-defects:scale}}
are each
bounded by
\mathrm{RHS}{}
(
7.115
). According to the
discussion above, this follows by showing the bound

(7.123)

{\left\|\left(\mathbbm{1}_{\overline{(W^{+}\cap V^{+})}\setminus(W^{-}\cup V^{-})}F_{\Gamma}\right)(\eta,y,t)t\delta\left(t-\mathfrak{b}_{W^{-}}(\eta,y)\right)\right\|_{\mathbb{L}_{(\Theta,\Theta^{\operatorname{in}})}^{(u,1)}\Phi_{\mathfrak{r}}^{N}(T)}}\lesssim\mathrm{RHS}{}\eqref{eq:geometry-of-defects}.

Let
\mathfrak{b}_{+}^{\ast}
,
\mathfrak{b}_{-}^{\ast}
and
\mathfrak{b}_{W^{-}}^{\ast}
be the
functions
\mathfrak{b}_{+}
,
\mathfrak{b}_{-}
and
\mathfrak{b}_{W^{-}}
expressed in local
coordinates of
\pi_{T}
i.e. such that (
3.152
)
holds for
\mathfrak{b}=\mathfrak{b}_{+}
,
\mathfrak{b}=\mathfrak{b}_{-}
or
\mathfrak{b}=\mathfrak{b}_{W^{-}}
, respectively. By (
3.154
) we
need to show that

(7.124)

\int_{\Theta^{\operatorname{in}}\times B_{1}}\left|\mathbbm{1}_{E^{\ast}}F^{\ast}\left(\theta,\zeta,\mathfrak{b}_{W^{-}}^{\ast}(\theta,\zeta)\right)[\varphi]\right|^{u}\frac{\mathrm{d}\theta\mathrm{d}\zeta}{|\Theta|}\lesssim\mathrm{RHS}{}\eqref{eq:geometry-of-defects}^{u}\|\varphi\|_{\Phi^{N}_{\mathfrak{r}}}

where we use the shorthand
F^{\ast}=\mathbbm{1}_{\mathfrak{T}_{\Theta^{\operatorname{in}}_{\Gamma}}}\left(\mathrm{Emb}[f]\circ\Gamma\circ\pi_{T}\right)
and

E^{\ast}=\left\{(\theta,\zeta,\sigma):\mathfrak{b}_{-}^{\ast}(\theta,\zeta)<\sigma<\mathfrak{b}_{+}^{\ast}(\theta,\zeta)\right\}
. First we record that locally in
\theta
and
\sigma
the
function
F^{\ast}
is almost constant. As a matter of fact, it follows form
the definitions (
1.12
) and (
3.103
) of
\mathrm{Emb}
and

\Gamma
that

(7.125)

F^{\ast}(\theta,\zeta,\sigma)[\varphi]=\mathbbm{1}_{\mathfrak{T}_{\Theta^{\operatorname{in}}_{\Gamma}}}(\theta,\zeta,\sigma)f\ast\mathrm{Dil}_{\sigma}\mathrm{Mod}_{\theta_{\Gamma}}\varphi^{\vee}(\zeta)

where
\varphi^{\vee}(z)=\varphi(-z)
. Thus, if we define
\varphi_{(\theta^{\prime},\sigma^{\prime},\theta,\sigma)}
by setting

(7.126)

\mathrm{Dil}_{\sigma^{\prime}}\mathrm{Mod}_{\theta^{\prime}_{\Gamma}}\varphi_{(\theta^{\prime},\sigma^{\prime},\theta,\sigma)}^{\vee}\coloneqq\mathrm{Dil}_{\sigma}\mathrm{Mod}_{\theta_{\Gamma}}\varphi^{\vee}.

it holds that
F^{\ast}(\theta,\zeta,\sigma)[\varphi]=F^{\ast}(\theta^{\prime},\zeta^{\prime},\sigma^{\prime})[\varphi_{(\theta^{\prime},\sigma^{\prime},\theta,\sigma)}]
as long as

(\theta^{\prime},\zeta^{\prime},\sigma^{\prime})\in\mathfrak{T}_{\Theta^{\operatorname{in}}_{\Gamma}}
.
Furthermore, any
\overline{r}>0
small enough independently of
\Gamma
, if
|\theta^{\prime}-\theta|<\overline{r}
and
|\sigma^{\prime}/\sigma-1|<\overline{r}
one
has that
\|\varphi_{(\theta^{\prime},\sigma^{\prime},\theta,\sigma)}\|_{\Phi^{N}_{2^{l+1}\mathfrak{r}}}\lesssim\|\varphi\|_{\Phi^{N}_{2^{l}\mathfrak{r}}}
for any
l\in\{0,\ldots,10\}
, any
N<N_{0}
for some fixed arbitrary large
N_{0}\in\mathbb{N}
and any

\varphi\in\Phi_{2^{l}\mathfrak{r}}^{N}
. Let us fix such a small
\overline{r}>0
so that
the facts above hold.

Next we want to show that close to any point
\left(\theta,\zeta,\mathfrak{b}_{W^{-}}^{\ast}(\theta,\zeta)\right)\in\mathfrak{T}_{\Theta^{\operatorname{in}}_{\Gamma}}\cap E^{\ast}
there exists a small
neighborhood in
\pi_{T}^{-1}(T\setminus W^{-})
such that the contribution
of
\left|\mathbbm{1}_{E^{\ast}}F^{\ast}\left(\theta,\zeta,\mathfrak{b}_{W^{-}}^{\ast}(\theta,\zeta)\right)[\varphi]\right|^{u}
to the integral we are bounding is
controlled by
F^{\ast}
on that neighborhood. We will then, in turn, bound

F^{\ast}
on that neighborhood by
\mathrm{RHS}{}\eqref{eq:geometry-of-defects}
.
For small enough
r_{\theta},r_{\zeta},r_{\sigma}>0
let

(7.127)

\displaystyle R(\theta,\zeta)\coloneqq\left\{(\theta^{\prime},\zeta^{\prime})\in\Theta^{\operatorname{in}}_{\Gamma}\times B_{1}:|\theta^{\prime}-\theta|<r_{\theta},|\zeta^{\prime}-\zeta|<r_{\zeta}\mathfrak{b}_{W^{-}}^{\ast}(\theta,\zeta)\right\}

\displaystyle R^{0}(\theta,\zeta)\coloneqq\left\{(\theta^{\prime},\zeta^{\prime},\sigma^{\prime}):(\theta^{\prime},\zeta^{\prime})\in R(\theta,\zeta),\frac{\sigma^{\prime}}{\mathfrak{b}_{W^{-}}^{\ast}(\theta,\zeta)}\in[1-10r_{\sigma},1+10r_{\sigma}]\right\},

\displaystyle R^{+}(\theta,\zeta)\coloneqq\left\{(\theta^{\prime},\zeta^{\prime},\sigma^{\prime}):(\theta^{\prime},\zeta^{\prime})\in R(\theta,\zeta),\frac{\sigma^{\prime}}{\mathfrak{b}_{W^{-}}^{\ast}(\theta,\zeta)}\in(1+2r_{\sigma},1+8r_{\sigma})\right\},

\displaystyle R^{++}(\theta,\zeta)\coloneqq\left\{(\theta^{\prime},\zeta^{\prime},\sigma^{\prime}):(\theta^{\prime},\zeta^{\prime})\in R(\theta,\zeta),\frac{\sigma^{\prime}}{\mathfrak{b}_{W^{-}}^{\ast}(\theta,\zeta)}\in(1+4r_{\sigma},1+6r_{\sigma})\right\}.

Clearly
R^{++}(\theta,\zeta)\subset R^{+}(\theta,\zeta)\subset R^{0}(\theta,\zeta)\subset R(\theta,\zeta)\times\mathbb{R}_{+}
. From the bounds
(
3.153
) and in particular from the fact that

W^{-}\in\mathbb{T}_{\Theta}^{\cup}
so
\left|\partial_{\zeta}\mathfrak{b}^{\ast}_{W^{-}}(\theta,\zeta)\right|\leq 1
we can see that

r_{\theta},r_{\zeta},r_{\sigma}
can be chosen small enough so that

(7.128)

\displaystyle\left(\theta^{\prime},\zeta^{\prime},\mathfrak{b}_{W^{-}}^{\ast}(\theta^{\prime},\zeta^{\prime})\right)\subset R^{0}(\theta,\zeta)\qquad\forall(\theta^{\prime},\zeta^{\prime})\in R(\theta,\zeta),

\displaystyle(\theta^{\prime},\zeta^{\prime},\sigma^{\prime}),(\theta^{\prime\prime},\zeta^{\prime},\sigma^{\prime\prime})\in R^{0}(\theta,\zeta)\Longrightarrow|\theta^{\prime}-\theta|<\overline{r},|\sigma^{\prime}/\sigma-1|<\overline{r},

\displaystyle R^{++}(\theta,\zeta)\subset R^{+}(\theta,\zeta)\subset\pi_{T}^{-1}\left(\mathbb{R}^{3}_{+}\setminus W^{-}\right).

As a matter of fact for fixed
r_{\theta}>0
and
r_{\zeta}>0
it holds
that for any
(\theta^{\prime},\zeta^{\prime})\in R(\theta,\zeta)
one has

(7.129)

\mathfrak{b}_{W^{-}}^{\ast}(\theta,\zeta)(e^{-Cr_{\theta}}-r_{\zeta})<\mathfrak{b}_{W^{-}}^{\ast}(\theta^{\prime},\zeta^{\prime})<\mathfrak{b}_{W^{-}}^{\ast}(\theta,\zeta)(e^{Cr_{\theta}}+r_{\zeta})

where
C=\frac{1}{\mathrm{dist}\left(\Theta^{\operatorname{in}},\mathbb{R}\setminus\Theta\right)-r_{\theta}}\lesssim 1
. Thus, choosing
10r_{t}<|(e^{Cr_{\theta}}+r_{\zeta})-1|+|(e^{-Cr_{\theta}}-r_{\zeta})-1|

provides us with the first claim. The other claims can be satisfied
analogously. It holds that

(7.130)

\displaystyle\int_{R(\theta,\zeta)}\left|{\mathbbm{1}_{E^{\ast}}}F^{\ast}\left(\theta^{\prime},\zeta^{\prime},\mathfrak{b}_{W^{-}}^{\ast}(\theta^{\prime},\zeta^{\prime})\right)[\varphi]\right|^{u}\mathrm{d}\zeta^{\prime}\mathrm{d}\theta^{\prime}

\displaystyle\lesssim\begin{aligned} &\int_{R^{+}(\theta,\zeta)}\left|{\mathbbm{1}_{E^{\ast}}}F^{\ast}(\theta^{\prime},\zeta^{\prime},\sigma^{\prime})\left[\varphi_{\left(\theta^{\prime\prime},\sigma^{\prime},\theta^{\prime},\mathfrak{b}_{W^{-}}^{\ast}(\theta^{\prime},\zeta^{\prime})\right)}\right]\right|^{u}\\
&\qquad\times\left(\mathbbm{1}_{L_{(\theta,\zeta)}}(\zeta^{\prime})+\sigma^{\prime}\delta\left(\sigma^{\prime}-\mathfrak{b}_{-}^{\ast}(\theta^{\prime\prime},\zeta^{\prime})\right)+\sigma^{\prime}\delta\left(\sigma^{\prime}-\mathfrak{b}_{+}^{\ast}(\theta^{\prime},\zeta^{\prime})\right)\right)\mathrm{d}\zeta^{\prime}\mathrm{d}\theta^{\prime}\frac{\mathrm{d}\sigma^{\prime}}{\sigma^{\prime}}\end{aligned}

where

(7.131)

L_{(\theta,\zeta)}=\bigcap_{\theta^{\prime}}\left\{\zeta^{\prime}\in B_{1}:\text{}(\theta^{\prime},\zeta^{\prime})\times\left\{\mathfrak{b}_{-}^{\ast}(\theta^{\prime},\zeta^{\prime}),\mathfrak{b}_{+}^{\ast}(\theta^{\prime},\zeta^{\prime})\right\}\cap R^{++}(\theta,\zeta)=\emptyset\right\}

with the intersection taken over all
\theta^{\prime}
with
(\theta^{\prime},\zeta^{\prime})\in R(\theta,\zeta)
. The bound follows because for every fixed
\zeta^{\prime}
either

\mathbbm{1}_{L_{(\theta,\zeta)}}(\zeta^{\prime})\neq 0
and thus

(7.132)

\displaystyle\mathbbm{1}_{R(\theta,\zeta)}(\theta^{\prime},\zeta^{\prime})\left|{\mathbbm{1}_{E^{\ast}}}F^{\ast}\left(\theta^{\prime},\zeta^{\prime},\mathfrak{b}_{W^{-}}^{\ast}(\theta^{\prime},\zeta^{\prime})\right)[\varphi]\right|^{u}\qquad

\displaystyle\lesssim\left|{\mathbbm{1}_{E^{\ast}}}F^{\ast}(\theta^{\prime},\zeta^{\prime},\sigma^{\prime})\left[\varphi_{\left(\theta^{\prime},\sigma^{\prime},\theta^{\prime},\mathfrak{b}_{W^{-}}^{\ast}(\theta^{\prime},\zeta^{\prime})\right)}\right]\right|^{u}

for every
\theta^{\prime}
with
(\theta^{\prime},\zeta^{\prime})\in R(\theta,\zeta)
.
Otherwise, if
\mathbbm{1}_{L_{(\theta,\zeta)}}(\zeta^{\prime})=0
then there exist

\overline{\theta}
with
(\overline{\theta},\zeta^{\prime})\in R(\theta,\zeta)
such that
either
\left(\overline{\theta},\zeta^{\prime},\mathfrak{b}_{-}^{\ast}(\overline{\theta},\zeta^{\prime})\right)\in R^{++}(\theta,\zeta)\neq\emptyset
or
\left(\overline{\theta},\zeta^{\prime},\mathfrak{b}_{+}^{\ast}(\overline{\theta},\zeta^{\prime})\right)\in R^{++}(\theta,\zeta)\neq\emptyset
. In the former case, there exists an absolute

\overline{r}>0
depending on
r_{\theta},r_{\zeta},r_{\sigma}>0
such that

\left(\theta^{\prime\prime},\zeta^{\prime},\mathfrak{b}_{-}^{\ast}(\theta^{\prime},\zeta^{\prime})\right)\in R^{+}(\theta,\zeta)
for all
|\theta^{\prime\prime}-\theta|<\overline{r}
. Similarly,

\left(\theta^{\prime\prime},\zeta^{\prime},\mathfrak{b}_{+}^{\ast}(\theta^{\prime},\zeta^{\prime})\right)\in R^{+}(\theta,\zeta)
in the case
\left(\overline{\theta},\zeta^{\prime},\mathfrak{b}_{+}^{\ast}(\overline{\theta},\zeta^{\prime})\right)\in R^{++}(\theta,\zeta)\neq\emptyset
.
The claim then follows by integrating over
\zeta^{\prime}
and
\theta^{\prime\prime}
.

Our argument then proceeds as follows. We need to show that

(7.133)

\mathrm{RHS}{\eqref{eq:defect-stopped-bound}}\lesssim\mathfrak{b}_{W^{-}}^{\ast}(\theta,\zeta)\mathrm{RHS}{\eqref{eq:geometry-of-defects}}^{u}\|\varphi\|_{\Phi^{N}_{\mathfrak{r}}}

Then we will show the following fact:

(7.134)

\int_{\Theta^{\operatorname{in}}_{\Gamma}\times B_{1}}\frac{1}{\mathfrak{b}_{W^{-}}^{\ast}(\theta,\zeta)}\mathbbm{1}_{R(\theta,\zeta)}(\theta^{\prime},\zeta^{\prime})\mathrm{d}\theta\mathrm{d}\zeta\gtrsim\mathbbm{1}_{\Theta^{\operatorname{in}}\times B_{1}}(\theta^{\prime},\zeta^{\prime}).

From these two bounds we can conclude the proof: using
(
7.134
) we get that

(7.135)

\displaystyle\int_{\Theta^{\operatorname{in}}_{\Gamma}\times B_{1}}\left|{\mathbbm{1}_{E^{\ast}}}F^{\ast}\left(\theta^{\prime},\zeta^{\prime},\mathfrak{b}_{W^{-}}^{\ast}(\theta^{\prime},\zeta^{\prime})\right)[\varphi]\right|^{u}\mathrm{d}\theta^{\prime}\mathrm{d}\zeta^{\prime}

\displaystyle\lesssim\int_{\Theta^{\operatorname{in}}_{\Gamma}\times B_{1}}\frac{1}{\mathfrak{b}_{W^{-}}^{\ast}(\theta,\zeta)}\left(\int_{R(\theta,\zeta)}\left|{\mathbbm{1}_{E^{\ast}}}F^{\ast}\left(\theta^{\prime},\zeta^{\prime},\mathfrak{b}_{W^{-}}^{\ast}(\theta^{\prime},\zeta^{\prime})\right)[\varphi]\right|^{u}\mathrm{d}\theta^{\prime}\mathrm{d}\zeta^{\prime}\right)\mathrm{d}\theta\mathrm{d}\zeta;

using (
7.133
) we obtain that

(7.136)

\displaystyle\int_{\Theta^{\operatorname{in}}_{\Gamma}\times B_{1}}\frac{1}{\mathfrak{b}_{W^{-}}^{\ast}(\theta,\zeta)}\left(\int_{R(\theta,\zeta)}\left|{\mathbbm{1}_{E^{\ast}}}F^{\ast}\left(\theta^{\prime},\zeta^{\prime},\mathfrak{b}_{W^{-}}^{\ast}(\theta^{\prime},\zeta^{\prime})\right)[\varphi]\right|^{u}\mathrm{d}\theta^{\prime}\mathrm{d}\zeta^{\prime}\right)\mathrm{d}\theta\mathrm{d}\zeta

\displaystyle\lesssim\int_{\Theta^{\operatorname{in}}_{\Gamma}\times B_{1}}\mathrm{RHS}{}\eqref{eq:geometry-of-defects}^{u}\|\varphi\|_{\Phi^{N}_{\mathfrak{r}}}\mathrm{d}\theta\mathrm{d}\zeta,

and this concludes the claim. Let us show that
(
7.134
) holds. For any fixed
\left(\theta^{\prime},\zeta^{\prime}\right)\in\Theta^{\operatorname{in}}_{\Gamma}\times B_{1}
and any

(\theta^{\prime\prime},\zeta^{\prime\prime})\in R(\theta^{\prime},\zeta^{\prime})
it holds that

\mathfrak{b}_{W^{-}}^{\ast}(\theta^{\prime\prime},\zeta^{\prime\prime})>\mathfrak{b}_{W^{-}}^{\ast}(\theta,\zeta)(1-10r_{t}).
It follows that if
|\theta^{\prime\prime}-\theta^{\prime}|<r_{\theta}
and
|\zeta^{\prime\prime}-\zeta^{\prime}|<r_{\zeta}(1-10r_{t})\mathfrak{b}_{W^{-}}^{\ast}(\theta^{\prime},\zeta^{\prime})
then
(\theta^{\prime},\zeta^{\prime})\in R(\theta^{\prime\prime},\zeta^{\prime\prime})
. It follows that

(7.137)

\int_{\Theta^{\operatorname{in}}_{\Gamma}\times B_{1}}\mathbbm{1}_{R(\theta^{\prime\prime},\zeta^{\prime\prime})}(\theta^{\prime},\zeta^{\prime})\mathrm{d}\theta^{\prime\prime}\mathrm{d}\zeta^{\prime\prime}\gtrsim\mathfrak{b}_{W^{-}}^{\ast}(\theta^{\prime},\zeta^{\prime})

with an absolute constant depending on
r_{\theta},r_{\zeta},r_{\sigma}

and
r^{\prime\prime}
, as required.

It remains to show that (
7.133
) holds. Note
that
\pi_{T}\left(R^{+}(\theta,\zeta)\cap\mathfrak{T}_{\Theta^{\operatorname{in}}_{\Gamma}}\right)\subset\pi_{T^{\prime}}\left(\mathfrak{T}_{\Theta^{\operatorname{in}}_{\Gamma}}\right)
with
T^{\prime}=T_{\Theta}\left(\xi_{T},x_{T}+\zeta,2\mathfrak{b}_{W^{-}}^{\ast}(\theta,\zeta)\right)
. As a
matter of fact,
(\theta^{\prime},\zeta^{\prime},\sigma^{\prime})\in R^{+}(\theta,\zeta)\cap\mathfrak{T}_{\Theta^{\operatorname{in}}}
only if
\theta^{\prime}\in\Theta^{\operatorname{in}}_{\Gamma}
,

|\zeta^{\prime}-\zeta|<r_{\zeta}\mathfrak{b}_{W^{-}}^{\ast}(\theta,\zeta)
, and

\sigma^{\prime}<(1+8r_{t})\mathfrak{b}_{W^{-}}^{\ast}(\theta,\zeta)
. In particular,
this means that if
(\eta^{\prime},y^{\prime},t^{\prime})=\pi_{T}(\theta^{\prime},\zeta^{\prime},\sigma^{\prime})
then

(7.138)

\displaystyle t^{\prime}<(1+8r_{t})\mathfrak{b}_{W^{-}}^{\ast}(\theta,\zeta),

\displaystyle|y^{\prime}-(x_{T}+\zeta)|<r_{\zeta}\mathfrak{b}_{W^{-}}^{\ast}(\theta,\zeta),

\displaystyle t^{\prime}(\eta^{\prime}-\xi_{T})\in\Theta^{\operatorname{in}}_{\Gamma},

that shows the claimed inclusion. Applying

Lemma

3.13
to
\varphi_{\left(\theta^{\prime},\sigma^{\prime},\theta^{\prime},\mathfrak{b}_{W^{-}}^{\ast}(\theta^{\prime},\zeta^{\prime})\right)}
we get that

(7.139)

\varphi_{\left(\theta^{\prime},\sigma^{\prime},\theta^{\prime},\mathfrak{b}_{W^{-}}^{\ast}(\theta^{\prime},\zeta^{\prime})\right)}=\sum_{k}a_{k}\left(\theta^{\prime},\sigma^{\prime},\theta^{\prime},\mathfrak{b}_{W^{-}}^{\ast}(\theta^{\prime},\zeta^{\prime})\right)\widetilde{\varphi_{k}}

with
\|\widetilde{\varphi_{k}}\|_{\Phi^{N-2}_{4\mathfrak{r}}}\lesssim 1
and

\left|a_{k}\left(\theta^{\prime},\sigma^{\prime},\theta^{\prime},\mathfrak{b}_{W^{-}}^{\ast}(\theta^{\prime},\zeta^{\prime})\right)\right|\lesssim\langle k\rangle^{-2}\|\varphi\|_{\Phi^{N}_{\mathfrak{r}}}
so

(7.140)

\displaystyle\begin{aligned} &\int_{R^{+}(\theta,\zeta)}\left|{\mathbbm{1}_{E^{\ast}}}F^{\ast}(\theta^{\prime},\zeta^{\prime},\sigma^{\prime})\left[\varphi_{\left(\theta^{\prime},\sigma^{\prime},\theta^{\prime},\mathfrak{b}_{W^{-}}^{\ast}(\theta^{\prime},\zeta^{\prime})\right)}\right]\right|^{u}\\
&\qquad\times\left(\mathbbm{1}_{L_{(\theta,\zeta)}}(\zeta^{\prime})+\sigma^{\prime}\delta\left(\sigma^{\prime}-\mathfrak{b}_{-}^{\ast}(\theta^{\prime},\zeta^{\prime})\right)+\sigma^{\prime}\delta\left(\sigma^{\prime}-\mathfrak{b}_{+}^{\ast}(\theta^{\prime},\zeta^{\prime})\right)\right)\mathrm{d}\zeta^{\prime}\mathrm{d}\theta^{\prime}\frac{\mathrm{d}\sigma^{\prime}}{\sigma^{\prime}}\end{aligned}

\displaystyle\lesssim\begin{aligned} &\|\varphi\|_{\Phi^{N}_{\mathfrak{r}}}\sup_{\widetilde{\varphi}}\int_{R^{+}(\theta,\zeta)}\left|{\mathbbm{1}_{E^{\ast}}}F^{\ast}(\theta^{\prime},\zeta^{\prime},\sigma^{\prime})[\widetilde{\varphi}]\right|^{u}\\
&\qquad\times\left(\mathbbm{1}_{L_{(\theta,\zeta)}}(\zeta^{\prime})+\sigma^{\prime}\delta\left(\sigma^{\prime}-\mathfrak{b}_{-}^{\ast}(\theta^{\prime},\zeta^{\prime})\right)+\sigma^{\prime}\delta\left(\sigma^{\prime}-\mathfrak{b}_{+}^{\ast}(\theta^{\prime},\zeta^{\prime})\right)\right)\mathrm{d}\zeta^{\prime}\mathrm{d}\theta^{\prime}\frac{\mathrm{d}\sigma^{\prime}}{\sigma^{\prime}}.\end{aligned}

where the upper bound is taken over
\widetilde{\varphi}\in\Phi^{\infty}_{4\mathfrak{r}}

with
\|\widetilde{\varphi}\|_{\Phi^{N-2}_{4\mathfrak{r}}}\lesssim 1
. By
(
3.154
) we obtain that

(7.141)

\displaystyle\begin{aligned} &\|\varphi\|_{\Phi^{N}_{\mathfrak{r}}}\sup_{\widetilde{\varphi}}\int_{R^{+}(\theta,\zeta)}\left|{\mathbbm{1}_{E^{\ast}}}F^{\ast}(\theta^{\prime},\zeta^{\prime},\sigma^{\prime})[\widetilde{\varphi}]\right|^{u}\\
&\qquad\times\left(\sigma^{\prime}\delta\left(\sigma^{\prime}-\mathfrak{b}_{-}^{\ast}(\theta^{\prime},\zeta^{\prime})\right)+\sigma^{\prime}\delta\left(\sigma^{\prime}-\mathfrak{b}_{+}^{\ast}(\theta^{\prime},\zeta^{\prime})\right)\right)\mathrm{d}\zeta^{\prime}\mathrm{d}\theta^{\prime}\frac{\mathrm{d}\sigma^{\prime}}{\sigma^{\prime}}\end{aligned}

\displaystyle\lesssim\begin{aligned} &\|\varphi\|_{\Phi^{N}_{\mathfrak{r}}}\mu^{1}_{\Theta}(T^{\prime})\left(\left\|\mathbbm{1}_{\overline{(W^{+}\cap V^{+})}\setminus(W^{-}\cup V^{-})}F_{\Gamma}(\eta,y,t)t\delta\left(t-\mathfrak{\mathfrak{b}_{-}}(\eta,y)\right)\right\|_{\mathbb{L}^{(u,1)}_{(\Theta,\Theta^{\operatorname{in}})}(T^{\prime})}\qquad\right.\\
&+\left.\left\|\mathbbm{1}_{\overline{(W^{+}\cap V^{+})}\setminus(W^{-}\cup V^{-})}F_{\Gamma}(\eta,y,t)t\delta\left(t-\mathfrak{\mathfrak{b}_{+}}(\eta,y)\right)\right\|_{\mathbb{L}^{(u,1)}_{(\Theta,\Theta^{\operatorname{in}})}(T^{\prime})}\right)\end{aligned}

\displaystyle\lesssim\|\varphi\|_{\Phi^{N}_{\mathfrak{r}}}\mathrm{RHS}{}\eqref{eq:geometry-of-defects}^{u}\mu^{1}_{\Theta}(T^{\prime})\lesssim\|\varphi\|_{\Phi^{N}_{\mathfrak{r}}}\mathfrak{b}_{W^{-}}^{\ast}(\theta,\zeta)\mathrm{RHS}{}\eqref{eq:geometry-of-defects}^{u},

as required. It remains to show the bound

(7.142)

\left(\frac{1}{\mathfrak{b}_{W^{-}}^{\ast}(\theta,\zeta)}\int_{R^{+}(\theta,\zeta)}\mathbbm{1}_{L_{(\theta,\zeta)}}(\zeta^{\prime})\left|{\mathbbm{1}_{E^{\ast}}}F^{\ast}(\theta^{\prime},\zeta^{\prime},\sigma^{\prime})[\widetilde{\varphi}]\right|^{u}\mathrm{d}\zeta^{\prime}\mathrm{d}\theta^{\prime}\frac{\mathrm{d}\sigma^{\prime}}{\sigma^{\prime}}\right)^{\frac{1}{u}}\lesssim\mathrm{RHS}{}\eqref{eq:geometry-of-defects}

for any fixed
\widetilde{\varphi}\in\Phi^{\infty}_{4\mathfrak{r}}
with
\|\widetilde{\varphi}\|_{\Phi^{N-2}_{4\mathfrak{r}}}\leq 1
. It holds that
\pi_{T}\left(R^{++}(\theta,\zeta)\cap\mathfrak{T}_{\Theta^{\operatorname{in}}_{\Gamma}}\right)\subset\pi_{T^{\prime\prime}}\left(\mathfrak{T}_{\Theta^{\operatorname{in}}_{\Gamma}}\right)
with
T^{\prime\prime}=T_{\Theta}\left(\xi_{T}-\frac{1}{2\mathfrak{b}_{W^{-}}^{\ast}(\theta,\zeta)},x_{T}+\zeta,2\mathfrak{b}_{W^{-}}^{\ast}(\theta,\zeta)\right)
. As a matter of
fact,
(\theta^{\prime},\zeta^{\prime},\sigma^{\prime})\in R^{++}(\theta,\zeta)\cap\mathfrak{T}_{\Theta^{\operatorname{in}}_{\Gamma}}
only if
\theta^{\prime}\in\Theta^{\operatorname{in}}_{\Gamma}
,
|\zeta^{\prime}-\zeta|<r_{\zeta}\mathfrak{b}_{W^{-}}^{\ast}(\theta,\zeta)
, and
(1+4r_{t})\mathfrak{b}_{W^{-}}^{\ast}(\theta,\zeta)<\sigma^{\prime}<(1+6r_{t})\mathfrak{b}_{W^{-}}^{\ast}(\theta,\zeta)
. In particular, this means that if
(\eta^{\prime},y^{\prime},t^{\prime})=\pi_{T}(\theta^{\prime},\zeta^{\prime},\sigma^{\prime})
then

(7.143)

\displaystyle(1+4r_{t})\mathfrak{b}_{W^{-}}^{\ast}(\theta,\zeta)<t^{\prime}<(1+6r_{t})\mathfrak{b}_{W^{-}}^{\ast}(\theta,\zeta),

\displaystyle|y^{\prime}-(x_{T}+\zeta)|<r_{\zeta}\mathfrak{b}_{W^{-}}^{\ast}(\theta,\zeta),

\displaystyle t^{\prime}(\eta^{\prime}-\xi_{T})\in\Theta^{\operatorname{in}}_{\Gamma}.

But then

(7.144)

t^{\prime}\left(\eta^{\prime}-\left(\xi_{T}-\frac{1}{2\mathfrak{b}_{W^{-}}^{\ast}(\theta,\zeta)}\right)\right)\in\Theta^{\operatorname{in}}_{\Gamma}+\left(\frac{1+4r_{t}}{2},\frac{1+6r_{t}}{2}\right)\subset\Theta^{\operatorname{ex}}_{\Gamma},

as required. If
u\leq 2
this is sufficient to conclude: it holds that

(7.145)

\displaystyle\left(\frac{1}{\mathfrak{b}_{W^{-}}^{\ast}(\theta,\zeta)}\int_{R^{+}(\theta,\zeta)}\mathbbm{1}_{L_{(\theta,\zeta)}}(\zeta^{\prime})\left|{\mathbbm{1}_{E^{\ast}}}F^{\ast}(\theta^{\prime},\zeta^{\prime},\sigma^{\prime})[\widetilde{\varphi}]\right|^{u}\mathrm{d}\zeta^{\prime}\mathrm{d}\theta^{\prime}\frac{\mathrm{d}\sigma^{\prime}}{\sigma^{\prime}}\right)^{\frac{1}{u}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\left\|\mathbbm{1}_{\overline{(W^{+}\cap V^{+})}\setminus(W^{-}\cup V^{-})}\mathbbm{1}_{\pi_{T}(R^{+}(\theta,\zeta))}F_{\Gamma}\right\|_{{\Gamma^{\ast}}\mathbb{L}^{(u,u)}_{(\Theta,\Theta^{\operatorname{ex}})}\Phi_{4\mathfrak{r}}^{N-2}(T^{\prime\prime})}

\displaystyle\lesssim\begin{aligned} &\left\|\mathbbm{1}_{\overline{(W^{+}\cap V^{+})}\setminus(W^{-}\cup V^{-})}\mathbbm{1}_{\pi_{T}(R^{+}(\theta,\zeta))}F_{\Gamma}\right\|_{{\Gamma^{\ast}}\mathbb{L}^{(u,2)}_{(\Theta,\Theta^{\operatorname{ex}})}\Phi_{4\mathfrak{r}}^{N-2}(T^{\prime\prime})}\\
&\qquad\times\left\|\mathbbm{1}_{\overline{(W^{+}\cap V^{+})}\setminus(W^{-}\cup V^{-})}\mathbbm{1}_{\pi_{T}(R^{+}(\theta,\zeta))}\right\|_{{\Gamma^{\ast}}\mathbb{L}^{\left(\infty,\frac{2u}{2-u}\right)}_{(\Theta,\Theta^{\operatorname{ex}})}(T^{\prime\prime})}\end{aligned}

and

(7.146)

\displaystyle\left\|\mathbbm{1}_{\overline{(W^{+}\cap V^{+})}\setminus(W^{-}\cup V^{-})}\mathbbm{1}_{\pi_{T}(R^{+}(\theta,\zeta))}\right\|_{{\Gamma^{\ast}}\mathbb{L}^{\left(\infty,\frac{2u}{2-u}\right)}_{(\Theta,\Theta^{\operatorname{ex}})}(T^{\prime\prime})}^{\frac{2u}{2-u}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\sup_{\theta\in\Theta}\sup_{\zeta\in B_{1}}\int_{0}^{1}\left|\mathbbm{1}_{R^{+}(\theta,\zeta)}(\pi_{T}^{-1}\pi_{T^{\prime\prime}}(\theta^{\prime},\zeta^{\prime},\sigma^{\prime}))\right|^{\frac{2u}{2-u}}\frac{\mathrm{d}\sigma^{\prime}}{\sigma^{\prime}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\sup_{\theta\in\Theta}\sup_{\zeta\in B_{1}}\int_{0}^{1}\mathbbm{1}_{(1+2r_{\sigma})/2<\sigma^{\prime}<(1+8r_{\sigma})/2}\frac{\mathrm{d}\sigma^{\prime}}{\sigma^{\prime}}

since
(\theta^{\prime\prime},\zeta^{\prime\prime},\sigma^{\prime\prime})=\pi_{T}^{-1}\pi_{T^{\prime\prime}}(\theta^{\prime},\zeta^{\prime},\sigma^{\prime})
only if
\sigma^{\prime\prime}=2\mathfrak{b}_{W^{-}}^{\ast}(\theta,\zeta)\sigma^{\prime}
. If
u>2
we use that the integrand
{\mathbbm{1}_{E^{\ast}}}F^{\ast}(\theta^{\prime},\zeta^{\prime},\sigma^{\prime})[\widetilde{\varphi}]
is almost constant over
R^{+}(\theta,\zeta)
. As a matter of fact it holds that

(7.147)

\mathbbm{1}_{L_{(\theta,\zeta)}}(\zeta^{\prime})\left|{\mathbbm{1}_{E^{\ast}}}F^{\ast}(\theta^{\prime},\zeta^{\prime},\sigma^{\prime})[\varphi]\right|=\mathbbm{1}_{L_{(\theta,\zeta)}}(\zeta^{\prime})\left|{\mathbbm{1}_{E^{\ast}}}F^{\ast}(\theta^{\prime\prime},\zeta^{\prime},\sigma^{\prime\prime})\left[{\varphi}_{(\theta^{\prime\prime},\sigma^{\prime\prime},\theta^{\prime},\sigma^{\prime})}\right]\right|

as per (
7.126
) for any
(\theta^{\prime\prime},\zeta^{\prime},\sigma^{\prime\prime})\in R^{++}(\theta,\zeta)
. Applying
Lemma

3.13

to
{\varphi}_{(\theta^{\prime\prime},\sigma^{\prime\prime},\theta^{\prime},\sigma^{\prime})}

(7.148)

\displaystyle\begin{aligned} &\mathbbm{1}_{L_{(\theta,\zeta)}}(\zeta^{\prime})\left|{\mathbbm{1}_{E^{\ast}}}F^{\ast}(\theta^{\prime},\zeta^{\prime},\sigma^{\prime})[\widetilde{\varphi}]\right|\\
&\qquad=\sup_{\widetilde{\widetilde{\varphi}}}\left(\frac{\int_{R^{++}(\theta,\zeta)}\mathbbm{1}_{L_{(\theta,\zeta)}}(\zeta^{\prime})\left|{\mathbbm{1}_{E^{\ast}}}F^{\ast}(\theta^{\prime\prime},\zeta^{\prime},\sigma^{\prime\prime})\left[\widetilde{\widetilde{\varphi}}\right]\right|^{2}\frac{\mathrm{d}\sigma^{\prime\prime}}{\sigma^{\prime\prime}}\mathrm{d}\theta^{\prime}}{\int_{R^{++}(\theta,\zeta)}\mathbbm{1}_{L_{(\theta,\zeta)}}(\zeta^{\prime})\frac{\mathrm{d}\sigma^{\prime\prime}}{\sigma^{\prime\prime}}\mathrm{d}\theta^{\prime}}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}\end{aligned}

where the upper bound is taken over
\widetilde{\widetilde{\varphi}}\in\Phi^{\infty}_{16\mathfrak{r}}
with
\|\widetilde{\varphi}\|_{\Phi^{N-4}_{16\mathfrak{r}}}\lesssim 1
. Since

(7.149)

\int_{R^{++}(\theta,\zeta)}\mathbbm{1}_{L_{(\theta,\zeta)}}(\zeta^{\prime})\frac{\mathrm{d}\sigma^{\prime\prime}}{\sigma^{\prime\prime}}\mathrm{d}\theta^{\prime}\gtrsim 1

we have that

(7.150)

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\mathfrak{b}_{W^{-}}^{\ast}(\theta,\zeta)}\int_{R^{+}(\theta,\zeta)}\mathbbm{1}_{L_{(\theta,\zeta)}}(\zeta^{\prime})\left|{\mathbbm{1}_{E^{\ast}}}F^{\ast}(\theta^{\prime},\zeta^{\prime},\sigma^{\prime})[\widetilde{\varphi}]\right|^{u}\mathrm{d}\zeta^{\prime}\mathrm{d}\theta^{\prime}\frac{\mathrm{d}\sigma^{\prime}}{\sigma^{\prime}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\sup_{\widetilde{\widetilde{\varphi}}}\int_{B_{1}}\left(\int_{\Theta^{\operatorname{in}}\times[0,1]}\mathbbm{1}_{L_{(\theta,\zeta)}}(\zeta^{\prime})\left|\mathbbm{1}_{R^{++}(\theta,\zeta)}{\mathbbm{1}_{E^{\ast}}}F^{\ast}(\theta^{\prime\prime},\zeta^{\prime},\sigma^{\prime\prime})\left[\widetilde{\widetilde{\varphi}}\right]\right|^{2}\frac{\mathrm{d}\sigma^{\prime\prime}}{\sigma^{\prime\prime}}\mathrm{d}\theta^{\prime}\right)^{u/2}\mathrm{d}\zeta^{\prime}.

Now, the same procedure as before allows us, using that
u\geq 2
to deduce
that

(7.151)

\displaystyle\left(\frac{1}{\mathfrak{b}_{W^{-}}^{\ast}(\theta,\zeta)}\int_{R^{+}(\theta,\zeta)}\mathbbm{1}_{L_{(\theta,\zeta)}}(\zeta^{\prime})\left|{\mathbbm{1}_{E^{\ast}}}F^{\ast}(\theta^{\prime},\zeta^{\prime},\sigma^{\prime})[\widetilde{\varphi}]\right|^{u}\mathrm{d}\zeta^{\prime}\mathrm{d}\theta^{\prime}\frac{\mathrm{d}\sigma^{\prime}}{\sigma^{\prime}}\right)^{\frac{1}{u}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\begin{aligned} &\left\|\mathbbm{1}_{\overline{(W^{+}\cap V^{+})}\setminus(W^{-}\cup V^{-})}\mathbbm{1}_{\pi_{T}(R^{+}(\theta,\zeta))}F_{\Gamma}\right\|_{{\Gamma^{\ast}}\mathbb{L}^{(u,2)}_{(\Theta,\Theta^{\operatorname{ex}})}\Phi_{4\mathfrak{r}}^{N-2}(T^{\prime\prime})}\\
&\times\left\|\mathbbm{1}_{\overline{(W^{+}\cap V^{+})}\setminus(W^{-}\cup V^{-})}\mathbbm{1}_{\pi_{T}(R^{+}(\theta,\zeta))}\right\|_{{\Gamma^{\ast}}\mathbb{L}^{(\infty,\infty)}_{(\Theta,\Theta^{\operatorname{ex}})}(T^{\prime\prime})}\end{aligned}

\displaystyle\lesssim\left\|\mathbbm{1}_{\overline{(W^{+}\cap V^{+})}\setminus(W^{-}\cup V^{-})}\mathbbm{1}_{\pi_{T}(R^{+}(\theta,\zeta))}F_{\Gamma}\right\|_{{\Gamma^{\ast}}\mathbb{L}^{(u,2)}_{(\Theta,\Theta^{\operatorname{ex}})}\Phi_{4\mathfrak{r}}^{N-2}(T^{\prime\prime})}.

∎

Lemma 7.9

(Lacunary size domination)
.

Given any sets
V^{+},V^{-}\in\mathbb{D}_{\beta}^{\cup}
, and let
W^{+},W^{-}\in\mathbb{T}_{\Theta}^{\cup}
, the bounds

(7.152)

\displaystyle\bigl\|\mathbbm{1}_{(V^{+}\cap W^{+})\setminus(V^{-}\cup W^{-})}\left(\mathrm{Emb}[f]\circ\Gamma\right)\bigr\|_{{\Gamma^{\ast}}\mathbb{L}^{(u,2)}_{\Theta}\mathfrak{d}_{\mathfrak{r}}^{N}}\qquad

\displaystyle\lesssim\begin{aligned} &\bigl\|\mathbbm{1}_{(V^{+}\cap W^{+})\setminus(V^{-}\cup W^{-})}\left(\mathrm{Emb}[f]\circ\Gamma\right)\bigr\|_{{\Gamma^{\ast}}\mathbb{L}^{(u,2)}_{(\Theta,\Theta^{\operatorname{ex}})}\Phi_{16\mathfrak{r}}^{N-10}}\\
&+\bigl\|\mathbbm{1}_{(V^{+}\cap W^{+})\setminus(V^{-}\cup W^{-})}\left(\mathrm{Emb}[f]\circ\Gamma\right)\bigr\|_{{\Gamma^{\ast}}\mathbb{L}^{(u,1)}_{(\Theta,\Theta^{\operatorname{in}})}\Box^{N-10}_{16\mathfrak{r}}}\end{aligned}

hold for all
f\in\mathscr{S}(\mathbb{R})
as long as
u\in[1,\infty]
.
The implicit constant is independent of
f
and of the sets
V^{\pm},W^{\pm}
.

Proof.

Let us fix
T\in\mathbb{T}_{\Theta}
and show that

(7.153)

\displaystyle\bigl\|\mathbbm{1}_{(V^{+}\cap W^{+})\setminus(V^{-}\cup W^{-})}\left(\mathrm{Emb}[f]\circ\Gamma\right)\bigr\|_{{\Gamma^{\ast}}\mathbb{L}^{(u,2)}_{\Theta}\mathfrak{d}_{\mathfrak{r}}^{N}(T)}\lesssim\mathrm{RHS}{\eqref{eq:unif-lac-size-domination}}.

By symmetry, (see (
3.94
) and the subsequent discussion),
we can assume that
{T=T_{\Theta}}(0,0,1)
so that

(7.154)

\Gamma\circ\pi_{T}(\theta,\zeta,\sigma)=(\alpha\beta(\theta+\gamma)(\beta\sigma)^{-1},\zeta,\beta\sigma)=(\theta_{\Gamma}(\beta\sigma)^{-1},\zeta,\beta\sigma)=\pi_{T}(\theta_{\Gamma},\zeta,\beta\sigma).

For the sake of this proof, let us suppose that
\alpha\beta>0
and

\gamma<0
. Any other combination of signs can be dealt with, mutatis
mutandis, following the same argument. We use the shorthand
E_{+}^{\ast}:=\pi_{T}^{-1}(V^{+}\cap W^{+}\cap T)
,
E_{-}^{\ast}:=\pi_{T}^{-1}(V^{-}\cup W^{-})
, and
F^{\ast}\coloneqq\mathbbm{1}_{E_{+}^{\ast}\setminus E_{-}^{\ast}}\mathrm{Emb}{}[f]\circ\Gamma\circ\pi_{T}
. From
Definition

3.9
we have
that

(7.155)

\displaystyle\bigl\|\mathbbm{1}_{(V^{+}\cap W^{+})\setminus(V^{-}\cup W^{-})}\left(\mathrm{Emb}[f]\circ\Gamma\right)\bigr\|_{{\Gamma^{\ast}}\mathbb{L}^{(u,2)}_{\Theta}\mathfrak{d}_{\mathfrak{r}}^{N}(T)}=\sup_{\varphi}\|F^{\ast}(\theta,\zeta,\sigma)[\varphi_{\theta}]\|_{L^{u}_{\frac{\mathrm{d}\theta\mathrm{d}\zeta}{|\Theta|}}L^{2}_{\frac{\mathrm{d}\sigma}{\sigma}}}
