\displaystyle\varphi_{\theta}(z)\coloneqq(-d_{z}+2\pi i\theta_{\Gamma})\varphi(z)

with the upper bound taken over
\varphi\in\Phi^{\infty}_{\mathfrak{r}}
with
\|\varphi\|_{\Phi^{N}_{\mathfrak{r}}}\leq 1
. First we address the case
\theta\in\Theta^{\operatorname{ex}}_{\Gamma}
i.e.
\theta_{\Gamma}\notin\Theta^{\operatorname{in}}
. Since

(7.156)

\displaystyle\mathfrak{d}_{\zeta}(\theta_{\Gamma})F^{\ast}(\theta,\zeta,\sigma)[\varphi]=F^{\ast}(\theta,\zeta,\sigma)[\varphi_{\theta}]

and
\|-d_{z}\varphi\|_{\Phi_{\mathfrak{r}}^{N}}+\|2\pi i\theta_{\Gamma}\varphi\|_{\Phi_{\mathfrak{r}}^{N}}\lesssim\|\varphi\|_{\Phi_{\mathfrak{r}}^{N}}
it follows immediately
that

(7.157)

\displaystyle\sup_{\varphi}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{2}}\left(\int_{\mathbb{R}_{+}}\mathbbm{1}_{\mathfrak{T}_{\Theta_{\Gamma}^{\operatorname{ex}}}}\mathbbm{1}_{E_{+}^{\ast}\setminus E_{-}^{\ast}}\left|\mathfrak{d}_{\zeta}(\theta_{\Gamma})F^{\ast}(\theta,\zeta,\sigma)[\varphi]\right|^{2}\hskip 1.69998pt\frac{\mathrm{d}\sigma}{\sigma}\right)^{\frac{u}{2}}\hskip 1.69998pt\frac{\mathrm{d}\zeta\mathrm{d}\theta}{|\Theta|}

\displaystyle\begin{aligned} &\lesssim\sup_{\varphi}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{2}}\left(\int_{\mathbb{R}_{+}}\mathbbm{1}_{\mathfrak{T}_{\Theta_{\Gamma}^{\operatorname{ex}}}}\mathbbm{1}_{E_{+}^{\ast}\setminus E_{-}^{\ast}}|F^{\ast}(\theta,\zeta,\sigma)[\varphi]|^{2}\hskip 1.69998pt\frac{\mathrm{d}\sigma}{\sigma}\right)^{\frac{u}{2}}\hskip 1.69998pt\frac{\mathrm{d}\zeta\mathrm{d}\theta}{|\Theta|}\\
&\lesssim\|\mathbbm{1}_{(V^{+}\cap W^{+})\setminus(V^{-}\cup W^{-})}\left(\mathrm{Emb}[f]\circ\Gamma\right)\|^{u}_{{\Gamma^{\ast}}\mathbb{L}^{(u,2)}_{(\Theta,\Theta^{\operatorname{ex}})}\Phi_{\mathfrak{r}}^{N}}.\end{aligned}

The rest of the proof is dedicated to dealing with the case
\theta\in\Theta^{\operatorname{in}}_{\Gamma}
. Note that
\widehat{\varphi_{\theta}}
vanishes at
-\theta_{\Gamma}
while for any
\theta\notin\Theta^{\operatorname{in}}_{\Gamma}
,

\widehat{\varphi}
vanishes on a large neighborhood of
-\theta_{\Gamma}
simply
because
|\theta_{\Gamma}|>\mathfrak{r}
and
\mathrm{spt}\widehat{\varphi}\subset B_{\mathfrak{r}}
.
This brings us to compare
F^{\ast}(\theta,\zeta,\sigma)[\varphi_{\theta}]

or
\theta\in\Theta^{\operatorname{in}}_{\Gamma}
with
F^{\ast}(\theta^{\prime},\zeta,\sigma)[\varphi^{\prime}_{\theta^{\prime}}]
for
\theta^{\prime}\in\Theta^{\operatorname{ex}}_{\Gamma}
and
for an appropriate
\varphi^{\prime}_{\theta}\in\Phi^{\infty}_{16\mathfrak{r}}
, and to
expect to be able to bound
\mathrm{LHS}{\eqref{eq:unif-lac-size-domination}}
by
the first addend on
\mathrm{RHS}{\eqref{eq:unif-lac-size-domination}}
. In
practice, we also need to control some remainder terms by the boundary
integral

(7.158)

\bigl\|\mathbbm{1}_{(V^{+}\cap W^{+})\setminus(V^{-}\cup W^{-})}\left(\mathrm{Emb}[f]\circ\Gamma\right)\bigr\|_{{\Gamma^{\ast}}\mathbb{L}^{(u,1)}_{(\Theta,\Theta^{\operatorname{in}})}\Box^{N-10}_{16\mathfrak{r}}}.

This proof goes along the lines of
[
AU20a
, Lemma
5.3]
, however care is taken to

•

make sure the estimates are uniform in
\Gamma
and in particular in
the deformation parameter
\beta\in(0,1]
,

•

use the defect size
{\Gamma^{\ast}}\mathbb{L}^{(u,1)}_{(\Theta,\Theta^{\operatorname{in}})}\Box^{N-10}_{16\mathfrak{r}}
instead of the size

\Gamma^{\ast}\mathbb{L}^{(\infty,\infty)}_{\Theta}\Phi_{16\mathfrak{r}}^{N-10}

that is not available in this setting.

Let
\mathfrak{b}_{+}^{\ast}
,
\mathfrak{b}_{-}^{\ast}
be the two functions whose graphs
are the boundaries of
E_{+}^{\ast}
and
E_{-}^{\ast}
respectively so that

E_{+}^{\ast}\setminus E_{-}^{\ast}=\left\{(\theta,\zeta,\sigma):\mathfrak{b}_{-}^{\ast}(\theta,\zeta)<\sigma<\mathfrak{b}_{+}^{\ast}(\theta,\zeta)\right\}
. The existence of such functions is guaranteed by

Lemma

3.20
. Our goal is to show that

(7.159)

\sup_{\varphi}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{2}}\left(\int_{\mathbb{R}_{+}}\mathbbm{1}_{\mathfrak{T}_{\Theta_{\Gamma}^{\operatorname{in}}}}\mathbbm{1}_{E_{+}^{\ast}\setminus E_{-}^{\ast}}\left|\mathfrak{d}_{\zeta}(\theta_{\Gamma})F^{\ast}(\theta,\zeta,\sigma)[\varphi]\right|^{2}\hskip 1.69998pt\frac{\mathrm{d}\sigma}{\sigma}\right)^{\frac{u}{2}}\hskip 1.69998pt\frac{\mathrm{d}\zeta\mathrm{d}\theta}{|\Theta|}\lesssim\mathrm{RHS}{\eqref{eq:unif-lac-size-domination}}

with
\varphi
as before.

Let us denote
\Theta^{\operatorname{in}}_{\Gamma}=(\theta^{\operatorname{in}}_{\Gamma-},\theta^{\operatorname{in}}_{\Gamma+})
,
\Theta^{\operatorname{in}}=(\theta^{\operatorname{in}}_{-},\theta^{\operatorname{in}}_{+})
with
\theta^{\operatorname{in}}_{\pm}=\alpha\beta\theta^{\operatorname{in}}_{\Gamma\pm}+\gamma
, let

\overline{\Theta}_{\Gamma}\coloneqq(\overline{\theta}_{\Gamma-},\overline{\theta}_{\Gamma+})=(\theta^{\operatorname{in}}_{\Gamma-}-(\alpha\beta)^{-1},\theta^{\operatorname{in}}_{\Gamma+}+(\alpha\beta)^{-1})
be an enlargement of

\Theta^{\operatorname{in}}_{\Gamma}
that still satisfies
\overline{\Theta}_{\Gamma}\subset\Theta
, and let
\overline{\Theta}_{\Gamma}^{\operatorname{ex}}=\overline{\Theta}_{\Gamma}\setminus\Theta^{\operatorname{in}}_{\Gamma}
.

Our main claim is that for any
(\theta,\zeta,\sigma)\in\mathfrak{T}_{\Theta^{\operatorname{in}}_{\Gamma}}\cap(E_{+}^{\ast}\setminus E_{-}^{\ast})

and for any
\varphi\in\Phi_{\mathfrak{r}}^{\infty}
with
\|\varphi\|_{\Phi_{\mathfrak{r}}^{N}}\leq 1
we have that

(7.160)

\displaystyle\left|\mathfrak{d}_{\zeta}(\theta_{\Gamma})F^{\ast}(\theta,\zeta,\sigma)[\varphi]\right|

\displaystyle\lesssim\begin{aligned} &\sup_{\widetilde{\varphi}}\left(\int_{\overline{\Theta}_{\Gamma}^{\operatorname{ex}}}\int_{\mathfrak{b}_{-}^{\ast}(\theta^{\prime},\zeta)}^{\mathfrak{b}_{+}^{\ast}(\theta^{\prime},\zeta)}e^{-\left|\log\left(\frac{\sigma}{\sigma^{\prime}}\right)\right|}|F^{\ast}(\theta^{\prime},\zeta,\sigma^{\prime})[\widetilde{\varphi}]|\frac{\mathrm{d}\sigma^{\prime}}{\sigma^{\prime}}\mathrm{d}\theta^{\prime}\right.\\
&\qquad+\begin{aligned} &\int_{\Theta^{\operatorname{in}}_{\Gamma}}{\mathbbm{1}_{\mathfrak{b}_{-}^{\ast}(\theta^{\prime},\zeta)<\mathfrak{b}_{+}^{\ast}(\theta^{\prime},\zeta)}}e^{-\left|\log\left(\frac{\sigma}{\mathfrak{b}_{-}^{\ast}(\theta^{\prime},\zeta)}\right)\right|}\left|F^{\ast}(\theta^{\prime},\zeta,\mathfrak{b}_{-}^{\ast}(\theta^{\prime},\zeta))[\widetilde{\varphi}]\right|\\
&\qquad\times(1+\delta(\theta-\theta^{\prime}))\mathrm{d}\theta^{\prime}\end{aligned}\\
&\qquad\left.+\int_{\Theta^{\operatorname{in}}_{\Gamma}}{\mathbbm{1}_{\mathfrak{b}_{-}^{\ast}(\theta^{\prime},\zeta)<\mathfrak{b}_{+}^{\ast}(\theta^{\prime},\zeta)}}e^{-\left|\log\left(\frac{\sigma}{\mathfrak{b}_{+}^{\ast}(\theta^{\prime},\zeta)}\right)\right|}\left|F^{\ast}(\theta^{\prime},\zeta,\mathfrak{b}_{+}^{\ast}(\theta^{\prime},\zeta))[\widetilde{\varphi}]\right|\mathrm{d}\theta^{\prime}\right)\end{aligned}

with the upper bound taken over functions
\widetilde{\varphi}\in\Phi_{8\mathfrak{r}}^{N-8}
with
\|\widetilde{\varphi}\|_{\Phi_{8\mathfrak{r}}^{N-8}}\leq 1
. Furthermore,
if
\sigma<C_{\operatorname{sep}}\mathfrak{b}_{-}^{\ast}(\theta,\zeta)
for some fixed
large
C_{\operatorname{sep}}\gg 1
the above statement also holds for any
\varphi\in\Phi_{4\mathfrak{r}}^{\infty}
with
\|\varphi\|_{\Phi_{4\mathfrak{r}}^{N}}\leq 1
on

\mathrm{LHS}{\eqref{eq:ld:full-decomposition-bound}}
while the upper bound is
still taken over
\widetilde{\varphi}\in\Phi_{8\mathfrak{r}}^{N-8}
on

\mathrm{RHS}{\eqref{eq:ld:full-decomposition-bound}}
.

We now show how this would allow us to conclude that

\eqref{eq:ld:local-coordinates:in}\lesssim\mathrm{RHS}{\eqref{eq:unif-lac-size-domination}}^{u}
, as required. Applying

Lemma

3.13
we represent any
\widetilde{\varphi}\in\Phi_{8\mathfrak{r}}^{N-8}
with
\|\widetilde{\varphi}\|_{\Phi_{8\mathfrak{r}}^{N-8}}\leq 1
as

(7.161)

\widetilde{\varphi}=\sum_{k\in\mathbb{Z}}a_{k}\widetilde{\widetilde{\varphi}}_{k}

with
\widetilde{\widetilde{\varphi}}_{k}\in\Phi_{16\mathfrak{r}}^{N-10}
,
\|\widetilde{\widetilde{\varphi}}_{k}\|_{\Phi_{16\mathfrak{r}}^{N-10}}\leq 1
, and
|a_{k}|\lesssim\langle k\rangle^{-2}
. Using
(
7.160
) and the triangle inequality we get
that
\eqref{eq:ld:local-coordinates:in}\lesssim\mathrm{I}+\mathrm{I}^{-}+\mathrm{I}^{+}
where

(7.162)

\displaystyle\mathrm{I}\coloneqq\sup_{\widetilde{\varphi}}\begin{aligned} &\int_{\Theta_{\Gamma}^{\operatorname{in}}\times B_{1}}\left(\int_{[0,1]}{\mathbbm{1}}_{\mathfrak{T}_{\Theta_{\Gamma}^{\operatorname{in}}}\cap(E_{+}^{\ast}\setminus E_{-}^{\ast})}(\theta,\zeta,\sigma)\right.\\
&\qquad\times\left.\left(\int_{\overline{\Theta}_{\Gamma}^{\operatorname{ex}}}\int_{\mathfrak{b}_{-}^{\ast}(\theta^{\prime},\zeta)}^{\mathfrak{b}_{+}^{\ast}(\theta^{\prime},\zeta)}e^{-\left|\log\left(\frac{\sigma}{\sigma^{\prime}}\right)\right|}|F^{\ast}(\theta^{\prime},\zeta,\sigma^{\prime})[\widetilde{\varphi}]|\frac{\mathrm{d}\sigma^{\prime}}{\sigma^{\prime}}\mathrm{d}\theta^{\prime}\right)^{2}\frac{\mathrm{d}\sigma}{\sigma}\right)^{\frac{u}{2}}\frac{\mathrm{d}\zeta\mathrm{d}\theta}{|\Theta|},\end{aligned}

\displaystyle\mathrm{I}^{-}\coloneqq\sup_{\widetilde{\varphi}}\begin{aligned} &\int_{\Theta_{\Gamma}^{\operatorname{in}}\times B_{1}}\left(\int_{[0,1]}{\mathbbm{1}}_{\mathfrak{T}_{\Theta_{\Gamma}^{\operatorname{in}}}\cap(E_{+}^{\ast}\setminus E_{-}^{\ast})}(\theta,\zeta,\sigma)\left(\int_{\Theta^{\operatorname{in}}_{\Gamma}}e^{-\left|\log\left(\frac{\sigma}{\mathfrak{b}_{-}^{\ast}(\theta^{\prime},\zeta)}\right)\right|}\right.\right.\qquad\\
&\qquad\times\left.\left.\left|F^{\ast}(\theta^{\prime},\zeta,\mathfrak{b}_{-}^{\ast}(\theta^{\prime},\zeta))[\widetilde{\varphi}]\right|(1+\delta(\theta-\theta^{\prime}))\mathrm{d}\theta^{\prime}\right)^{2}\frac{\mathrm{d}\sigma}{\sigma}\right)^{\frac{u}{2}}\frac{\mathrm{d}\zeta\mathrm{d}\theta}{|\Theta|},\end{aligned}

\displaystyle\mathrm{I}^{+}\coloneqq\sup_{\widetilde{\varphi}}\begin{aligned} &\int_{\Theta_{\Gamma}^{\operatorname{in}}\times B_{1}}\left(\int_{[0,1]}{\mathbbm{1}}_{\mathfrak{T}_{\Theta_{\Gamma}^{\operatorname{in}}}\cap(E_{+}^{\ast}\setminus E_{-}^{\ast})}(\theta,\zeta,\sigma)\right.\\
&\qquad\times\left.\left(\int_{\Theta^{\operatorname{in}}_{\Gamma}}e^{-\left|\log\left(\frac{\sigma}{\mathfrak{b}_{+}^{\ast}(\theta^{\prime},\zeta)}\right)\right|}\left|F^{\ast}(\theta^{\prime},\zeta,\mathfrak{b}_{+}^{\ast}(\theta^{\prime},\zeta))[\widetilde{\varphi}]\right|\mathrm{d}\theta^{\prime}\right)^{2}\frac{\mathrm{d}\sigma}{\sigma}\right)^{\frac{u}{2}}\frac{\mathrm{d}\zeta\mathrm{d}\theta}{|\Theta|}.\end{aligned}

where the upper bound, this time, is taken over functions
\widetilde{\varphi}\in\Phi_{16\mathfrak{r}}^{N-10}
with

\|\widetilde{\varphi}\|_{\Phi_{16\mathfrak{r}}^{N-10}}\leq 1
. We bound the term
\mathrm{I}
using the Minkowski inequality
and using the Young convolution inequality with respect to the
multiplicative Haar measure
\frac{\mathrm{d}\sigma^{\prime}}{\sigma^{\prime}}
:

(7.163)

\mathrm{I}\textrm{}\begin{aligned} &\lesssim\sup_{\widetilde{\varphi}}\int_{\overline{\Theta}_{\Gamma}^{\operatorname{ex}}\times B_{1}}\left(\int_{\mathfrak{b}_{-}^{\ast}(\theta^{\prime},\zeta)}^{\mathfrak{b}_{+}^{\ast}(\theta^{\prime},\zeta)}|F^{\ast}(\theta^{\prime},\zeta,\sigma^{\prime})[\widetilde{\varphi}]|^{2}\frac{\mathrm{d}\sigma^{\prime}}{\sigma^{\prime}}\right)^{\frac{u}{2}}\hskip 1.69998pt\mathrm{d}\zeta\mathrm{d}\theta^{\prime}\\
&\lesssim\Bigl\|\mathbbm{1}_{V^{+}\setminus V^{-}}\mathbbm{1}_{\mathbb{R}^{3}_{+}\setminus W^{-}}\left(\mathrm{Emb}[f]\circ\Gamma\right)\Bigr\|_{{\Gamma^{\ast}}\mathbb{L}^{(u,2)}_{(\Theta,\Theta^{\operatorname{ex}})}\Phi_{8\mathfrak{r}}^{N-8}(T)}^{u}.\end{aligned}

Bounding the terms
\mathrm{I}^{-}
and
\mathrm{I}^{+}
makes use of the defect
size.

(7.164)

\displaystyle\mathrm{I}^{+}\lesssim\sup_{\widetilde{\varphi}}\begin{aligned} &\int_{\Theta_{\Gamma}^{\operatorname{in}}\times B_{1}}\left.\left(\int_{\Theta^{\operatorname{in}}_{\Gamma}}{\mathbbm{1}_{\mathfrak{b}_{-}^{\ast}(\theta^{\prime},\zeta)<\mathfrak{b}_{+}^{\ast}(\theta^{\prime},\zeta)}}\left|F^{\ast}(\theta^{\prime},\zeta,\mathfrak{b}_{+}^{\ast}(\theta^{\prime},\zeta))[\widetilde{\varphi}]\right|\mathrm{d}\theta^{\prime}\right.\right)^{\frac{u}{2}}\frac{\mathrm{d}\zeta\mathrm{d}\theta}{|\Theta|}\end{aligned}\qquad

\displaystyle\lesssim\sup_{\widetilde{\varphi}}\begin{aligned} &\int_{\Theta^{\operatorname{in}}_{\Gamma}\times B_{1}}{\mathbbm{1}_{\mathfrak{b}_{-}^{\ast}(\theta^{\prime},\zeta)<\mathfrak{b}_{+}^{\ast}(\theta^{\prime},\zeta)}}\left|F^{\ast}(\theta^{\prime},\zeta,\mathfrak{b}_{+}^{\ast}(\theta^{\prime},\zeta))[\widetilde{\varphi}]\right|^{\frac{u}{2}}\frac{\mathrm{d}\zeta\mathrm{d}\theta^{\prime}}{|\Theta|}\end{aligned}\lesssim\mathrm{RHS}{\eqref{eq:unif-lac-size-domination}}^{u}.

Analogously,

(7.165)

\displaystyle\mathrm{I}^{-}\lesssim\sup_{\widetilde{\varphi}}\begin{aligned} &\int_{\Theta_{\Gamma}^{\operatorname{in}}\times B_{1}}\left(\int_{\overline{\Theta}}{\mathbbm{1}_{\mathfrak{b}_{-}^{\ast}(\theta^{\prime},\zeta)<\mathfrak{b}_{+}^{\ast}(\theta^{\prime},\zeta)}}\left|F^{\ast}(\theta^{\prime},\zeta,\mathfrak{b}_{-}^{\ast}(\theta^{\prime},\zeta))[\widetilde{\varphi}]\right|\right.\qquad\\
&\times\left.(1+\delta(\theta-\theta^{\prime}))\mathrm{d}\theta^{\prime}\right)^{\frac{u}{2}}\frac{\mathrm{d}\zeta\mathrm{d}\theta}{|\Theta|}\end{aligned}

\displaystyle\lesssim\sup_{\widetilde{\varphi}}\begin{aligned} &\int_{\overline{\Theta}_{\Gamma}\times B_{1}}{\mathbbm{1}_{\mathfrak{b}_{-}^{\ast}(\theta^{\prime},\zeta)<\mathfrak{b}_{+}^{\ast}(\theta^{\prime},\zeta)}}\left|F^{\ast}(\theta^{\prime},\zeta,\mathfrak{b}_{-}^{\ast}(\theta^{\prime},\zeta))[\widetilde{\varphi}]\right|^{\frac{u}{2}}\frac{\mathrm{d}\zeta\mathrm{d}\theta^{\prime}}{|\Theta|}\end{aligned}

\displaystyle\lesssim\mathrm{RHS}{\eqref{eq:unif-lac-size-domination}}^{u}.

The last inequality in each of the chains above follows from

Lemma

3.20
and in particular from
(
3.154
).

The remaining part of this proof is dedicated to showing that
(
7.160
) holds. In

\mathrm{LHS}{}
(
7.160
) fix
(\theta,\zeta,\sigma)\in\mathfrak{T}_{\Theta}\cap(E_{+}^{\ast}\setminus E_{-}^{\ast})
and
\varphi\in\Phi^{\infty}_{\mathfrak{r}}
with
\|\varphi\|_{\Phi^{N}_{\mathfrak{r}}}\leq 1
. We will
discuss two cases. First we consider when
\sigma\leq C_{\operatorname{sep}}\mathfrak{b}^{\ast}_{-}(\theta,\zeta)
for some very large
C_{\operatorname{sep}}\gg 1
.
This corresponds to the point
(\theta,\zeta,\sigma)
being close to the
boundary of
E^{\ast}_{-}
. We then address the more general case when
\sigma\in\left(C_{\operatorname{sep}}\mathfrak{b}^{\ast}_{-}(\theta,\zeta),\mathfrak{b}^{\ast}_{+}(\theta,\zeta)\right)
.

We start with the former case. In this regime we also allow
\varphi
to have
larger frequency support:
\varphi\in\Phi_{4\mathfrak{r}}^{\infty}
with
\|\varphi\|_{\Phi_{4\mathfrak{r}}^{N}}\leq 1
instead of only
\varphi\in\Phi_{\mathfrak{r}}^{\infty}
;
this will be necessary in the other regime. We further distinguish two
cases:
Case 1.0
and
Case 1.5
. The former is
when
\sigma<\left(1+2^{-10}\mathfrak{r}\right)\mathfrak{b}^{\ast}_{-}(\theta,\zeta)
or if
|\theta_{\Gamma}|<\mathfrak{r}
while the latter is when
\sigma\leq C_{\operatorname{sep}}\mathfrak{b}^{\ast}_{-}(\theta,\zeta)
but the conditions of

Case 1.0
do not hold.

Case 1.0
: Suppose
\sigma<\left(1+2^{-10}\mathfrak{r}\right)\mathfrak{b}^{\ast}_{-}(\theta,\zeta)
or
|\theta_{\Gamma}|<\mathfrak{r}
with
\sigma\leq C_{\operatorname{sep}}\mathfrak{b}^{\ast}_{-}(\theta,\zeta)
. We will use the fact
that

(7.166)

\displaystyle\mathfrak{d}_{\zeta}(\theta)F^{\ast}(\theta,\zeta,\sigma)[\varphi]=F^{\ast}(\theta,\zeta,\mathfrak{b}^{\ast}_{-}(\theta,\zeta))[\widetilde{\varphi}],

\displaystyle\widetilde{\varphi}^{\vee}\coloneqq\mathrm{Mod}_{-\theta_{\Gamma}}{\mathrm{Dil}}_{\sigma/\mathfrak{b}^{\ast}_{-}(\theta,\zeta)}\mathrm{Mod}_{\theta_{\Gamma}}\varphi_{\theta}^{\vee},

\displaystyle\varphi_{\theta}(z)\coloneqq(-d_{z}+2\pi i\theta_{\Gamma})\varphi(z)

This identity follows from (
1.12
) that defines
\mathrm{Emb}
. Since

(7.167)

\mathrm{spt}\left(\widehat{{\mathrm{Dil}}_{\sigma}\mathrm{Mod}_{\theta_{\Gamma}}\varphi_{\theta}^{\vee}}\right)\subset\left(\sigma^{-1}\left(\theta_{\Gamma}-4\mathfrak{r}\right),\sigma^{-1}\left(\theta_{\Gamma}+4\mathfrak{r}\right)\right)

it holds that

(7.168)

\mathrm{spt}\left(\widehat{\widetilde{\varphi}^{\vee}}\right)\subset\left(\frac{\mathfrak{b}^{\ast}_{-}(\theta,\zeta)}{\sigma}\left(\theta_{\Gamma}-4\mathfrak{r}\right)-\theta_{\Gamma},\frac{\mathfrak{b}^{\ast}_{-}(\theta,\zeta)}{\sigma}\left(\theta_{\Gamma}+4\mathfrak{r}\right)-\theta_{\Gamma}\right).

Since
\theta_{\Gamma}\in\Theta
and thus
|\theta_{\Gamma}|<10
and
either
|\theta_{\Gamma}|<\mathfrak{r}
or
\frac{1}{1+2^{-10}\mathfrak{r}}<\frac{\mathfrak{b}^{\ast}_{-}(\theta,\zeta)}{\sigma}<1
we have that
\left|\left(\frac{\mathfrak{b}^{\ast}_{-}(\theta,\zeta)}{\sigma}-1\right)\theta_{\Gamma}\right|<2\mathfrak{r}
. This implies that
\widetilde{\varphi}\in\Phi_{8\mathfrak{r}}^{N}
with
\|\widetilde{\varphi}\|_{\Phi_{8\mathfrak{r}}^{N}}\lesssim_{C_{\operatorname{sep}}}\|\varphi\|_{\Phi_{4\mathfrak{r}}^{N}}\leq 1
. This shows
bound (
7.160
) in this case because we have
obtained that

(7.169)

\left|\mathfrak{d}_{\zeta}(\theta_{\Gamma})F^{\ast}(\theta,\zeta,\sigma)[\varphi]\right|\lesssim\sup_{\widetilde{\varphi}}\begin{aligned} &\int_{\Theta^{\operatorname{in}}_{\Gamma}}{\mathbbm{1}_{\mathfrak{b}_{-}^{\ast}(\theta^{\prime},\zeta)<\mathfrak{b}_{+}^{\ast}(\theta^{\prime},\zeta)}}e^{-\left|\log\left(\frac{\sigma}{\mathfrak{b}_{-}^{\ast}(\theta^{\prime},\zeta)}\right)\right|}\\
&\qquad\times\left|F^{\ast}(\theta^{\prime},\zeta,\mathfrak{b}_{-}^{\ast}(\theta^{\prime},\zeta))[\widetilde{\varphi}]\right|(1+\delta(\theta-\theta^{\prime}))\mathrm{d}\theta^{\prime}\end{aligned}

Case 1.5
: We now suppose that
1+2^{-10}\mathfrak{r}\leq\frac{\sigma}{\mathfrak{b}^{\ast}_{-}(\theta,\zeta)}<C_{\operatorname{sep}}
and
|\theta_{\Gamma}|>\mathfrak{r}
. We assume that
\theta>-\gamma+\frac{\mathfrak{r}}{\alpha\beta}
, the case
\theta<-\gamma-\frac{\mathfrak{r}}{\alpha\beta}
is dealt with symmetrically. Let
\left(\omega,\mathfrak{b}^{\ast}_{-}(\omega,\zeta)\right)
be the point in
\Theta\times\mathbb{R}_{+}
where the
segment connecting the points
(\theta,\sigma)\in\Theta\times\mathbb{R}_{+}
and

(-\gamma,0)\in\Theta\times[0,+\infty)
intersects the graph of

\theta^{\prime}\mapsto\mathfrak{b}^{\ast}_{-}(\theta^{\prime},\zeta)
. We claim that

•

such a point exists,

•

it is unique,

•

it holds that
\mathfrak{b}^{\ast}_{-}(\omega,\zeta)<\mathfrak{b}^{\ast}_{+}(\omega,\zeta)
,

•

there exists a small
\overline{r}>0
such that
(\omega,\omega+\overline{r})\subset(-\gamma,\theta)
and
\mathfrak{b}^{\ast}_{-}(\theta^{\prime},\zeta)<\mathfrak{b}^{\ast}_{+}(\theta^{\prime},\zeta)
for all
\theta^{\prime}\in(\omega,\omega+\overline{r})
.

Assuming for now that this holds, we define
\widetilde{\varphi}_{\theta^{\prime}}
by
setting

(7.170)

{\mathrm{Dil}}_{\mathfrak{b}^{\ast}_{-}(\theta^{\prime},\zeta)}\mathrm{Mod}_{\theta^{\prime}_{\Gamma}}\widetilde{\varphi}_{\theta^{\prime}}^{\vee}\coloneqq{\mathrm{Dil}}_{\sigma}\mathrm{Mod}_{\theta_{\Gamma}}\varphi_{\theta}^{\vee}

so that

(7.171)

\mathfrak{d}_{\zeta}(\theta_{\Gamma})F^{\ast}(\theta,\zeta,\sigma)[\varphi]=\frac{1}{\overline{r}}\int_{\omega}^{\omega+\overline{r}}F^{\ast}(\theta^{\prime},\zeta,\mathfrak{b}^{\ast}_{-}(\theta^{\prime},\zeta))[{\widetilde{\varphi}_{\theta^{\prime}}}]\mathrm{d}\theta^{\prime}.

It holds that
\mathrm{spt}\left(\widehat{\widetilde{\varphi}_{\omega}}\right)\subset B_{4\mathfrak{r}\frac{\mathfrak{b}^{\ast}_{-}(\omega,\zeta)}{\sigma}}\subset B_{4\mathfrak{r}}
;
using bound (
3.153
), and in particular that

(7.172)

e^{-|\theta^{\prime}-\omega|}<\frac{\mathfrak{b}^{\ast}_{-}(\theta^{\prime},\zeta)}{\mathfrak{b}^{\ast}_{-}(\omega,\zeta)}<e^{|\theta^{\prime}-\omega|},

we deduce, by continuity, that
\mathrm{spt}\left(\widehat{\widetilde{\varphi}_{\theta^{\prime}}}\right)\subset B_{8\mathfrak{r}}
if
\overline{r}>0
is chosen small
enough. Furthermore, since
\sigma<C_{\operatorname{sep}}\mathfrak{b}^{\ast}_{-}(\theta,\zeta)
and
\mathfrak{b}^{\ast}_{-}(\omega,\zeta)>e^{-|\theta-\gamma|}\mathfrak{b}^{\ast}_{-}(\theta,\zeta)
it holds that
\sigma\lesssim C_{\operatorname{sep}}\mathfrak{b}^{\ast}_{-}(\omega,\zeta)
. Thus
\widetilde{\varphi}_{\theta^{\prime}}\in\Phi_{8\mathfrak{r}}^{N}
with
\left\|{\widetilde{\varphi}_{\theta^{\prime}}}\right\|_{\Phi_{8\mathfrak{r}}^{N}}\lesssim_{C_{\operatorname{sep}}}\|\varphi\|_{\Phi_{4\mathfrak{r}}^{N}}\leq 1
. An application of
Lemma

3.13

allows us to conclude that

(7.173)

\left|\mathfrak{d}_{\zeta}(\theta_{\Gamma})F^{\ast}(\theta,\zeta,\sigma)[\varphi]\right|=\sup_{\widetilde{\varphi}}\frac{1}{\overline{r}}\int_{\omega}^{\omega+\overline{r}}\left|F^{\ast}(\theta^{\prime},\zeta,\mathfrak{b}^{\ast}_{-}(\theta^{\prime},\zeta))[\widetilde{\varphi}]\right|\mathrm{d}\theta^{\prime}

with the upper bound taken over
\widetilde{\varphi}\in\Phi_{16\mathfrak{r}}^{\infty}
with
\|\widetilde{\varphi}\|_{\Phi_{16\mathfrak{r}}^{N-2}}\lesssim_{C_{\operatorname{sep}}}1.
This would conclude showing bound
(
7.160
) in this case. It remains to prove the
properties about the point
\left(\omega,\mathfrak{b}^{\ast}_{-}(\omega,\zeta)\right)
claimed above.

At least one point of intersection between the segment connecting
(\theta,\sigma)\in\Theta\times[0,+\infty)
and
(-\gamma,0)\in\Theta\times[0,+\infty)
and the graph of
\theta^{\prime}\rightarrow\mathfrak{b}_{-}^{\ast}(\theta^{\prime},\zeta)
exists because
\mathfrak{b}_{-}^{\ast}(-\gamma,\zeta)>0
,
\mathfrak{b}_{-}^{\ast}(\theta,\zeta)<\sigma
, and
\theta^{\prime}\mapsto\mathfrak{b}_{-}^{\ast}(\theta^{\prime},\zeta)
is continuous. Let us call
\left(\omega,\mathfrak{b}_{-}^{\ast}(\omega,\zeta)\right)
one such point. Let us show
that the intersection is unique. As a matter of fact, if
\mathfrak{b}
is any
function satisfying (
3.153
) then its graph can
only cross the segment connecting
(\theta,\sigma)\in\Theta\times[0,+\infty)
and
(-\gamma,0)\in\Theta\times[0,+\infty)
from above to
below. To see this suppose that
\theta_{0}
is the
\Theta
coordinate of a
point of intersection: we then have

(7.174)

\mathfrak{b}(\theta_{0},\zeta)=\sigma\frac{\theta_{0}+\gamma}{\theta+\gamma}.

By (
3.153
) we have that
\mathrm{d}_{\theta^{\prime}}\mathfrak{b}^{\ast}(\theta^{\prime},\zeta)\leq\frac{\mathfrak{b}^{\ast}(\theta^{\prime},\zeta)}{\theta^{\prime}-\theta_{-}}
from which we get that for any
\theta^{\prime}>\theta_{0}

(7.175)

\mathfrak{b}^{\ast}(\theta^{\prime},\zeta)\leq\mathfrak{b}^{\ast}(\theta_{0},\zeta)\frac{\theta^{\prime}-\theta_{-}}{\theta_{0}-\theta_{-}}=\sigma\frac{\theta_{0}+\gamma}{\theta+\gamma}\frac{\theta^{\prime}-\theta_{-}}{\theta_{0}-\theta_{-}}\leq\sigma\frac{\theta^{\prime}+\gamma}{\theta+\gamma};

we used that
\theta_{0}-\theta_{-}>\theta_{0}+\gamma
. This is exactly the
property we claimed. By the same reasoning, if we were to have

\mathfrak{b}^{\ast}_{+}(\omega,\zeta)<\mathfrak{b}^{\ast}_{-}(\omega,\zeta)=\sigma\frac{\omega+\gamma}{\theta+\gamma}
then it would holds that

(7.176)

\mathfrak{b}^{\ast}_{+}(\theta,\zeta)\leq\sigma

which is a contradiction to the fact that
(\theta,\zeta,\sigma)\in\mathfrak{T}_{\Theta}\cap(E_{+}^{\ast}\setminus E_{-}^{\ast})
.

Let us now address the existence of
\overline{r}>0
. Since
\sigma>\mathfrak{b}^{\ast}_{-}(\theta,\zeta)\left(1+2^{-10}\mathfrak{r}\right)
,

\mathfrak{b}^{\ast}_{-}(\theta^{\prime},\zeta)<\mathfrak{b}^{\ast}_{-}(\theta,\zeta)e^{|\theta^{\prime}-\theta|}
and the slope of the segment connecting
(-\gamma,0)

to
(\theta,\sigma)
is bounded from above by
\alpha\beta\frac{\mathfrak{b}^{\ast}_{-}(\theta,\zeta)}{\mathfrak{r}}
it follows that
\omega<\theta-2\overline{r}
for some universal
\overline{r}>0
independent of

\Gamma
,
\theta
and
\zeta
. Finally, for any
\theta^{\prime}\in(\omega,\omega+\overline{r})
it holds that
\mathfrak{b^{\ast}_{-}(\theta^{\prime},\zeta)}\leq\mathfrak{b^{\ast}_{+}(\theta^{\prime},\zeta)}
because otherwise, as before, we would be
able to deduce that
\mathfrak{b}^{\ast}_{+}(\theta,\zeta)\leq\sigma
in
contradiction with the fact that
(\theta,\zeta,\sigma)\in\mathfrak{T}_{\Theta}\cap(E_{+}^{\ast}\setminus E_{-}^{\ast})
.

Case 2:
Now let us discuss the case
\sigma>C_{\operatorname{sep}}\mathfrak{b}^{\ast}_{-}(\theta,\zeta)
. Let

\mathfrak{b}^{\ast}_{-}(\zeta)=\sup_{\theta^{\prime}\in\overline{\Theta}_{\Gamma}}\mathfrak{b}^{\ast}_{-}(\theta^{\prime},\zeta)

and
\mathfrak{b}^{\ast}_{-}(\zeta)=\inf_{\theta^{\prime}\in\overline{\Theta}_{\Gamma}}\mathfrak{b}^{\ast}_{+}(\theta^{\prime},\zeta)
. By (
3.153
)
and in particular from the fact that for any
\theta^{\prime}\in\overline{\Theta}_{\Gamma}
it holds that

(7.177)

e^{-|\overline{\Theta}_{\Gamma}|}\mathfrak{b}^{\ast}_{\pm}(\theta,\zeta)\leq\mathfrak{b}^{\ast}_{\pm}(\theta^{\prime},\zeta)\leq e^{|\overline{\Theta}_{\Gamma}|}\mathfrak{b}^{\ast}_{\pm}(\theta,\zeta)

so we obtain that
\mathfrak{b}^{\ast}_{-}(\theta,\zeta)\mathfrak{<b}^{\ast}_{-}(\zeta)<2\mathfrak{b}^{\ast}_{-}(\theta,\zeta)
,
\mathfrak{b}^{\ast}_{+}(\theta,\zeta)\mathfrak{<b}^{\ast}_{+}(\zeta)<2\mathfrak{b}^{\ast}_{+}(\theta,\zeta)
, and in particular

\mathfrak{b}^{\ast}_{+}(\zeta)>\frac{C_{\operatorname{sep}}}{4}\mathfrak{b}^{\ast}_{-}(\zeta)

and
\sigma>\frac{C_{\operatorname{sep}}}{2}\mathfrak{b}^{\ast}_{-}(\zeta)
. We set

C_{\operatorname{sep}}=2^{11}
. Fix
\chi\in\Phi^{\infty}_{2}
with
\widehat{\chi}\left(\widehat{z}\right)\in[0,1]
and
\widehat{\chi}\left(\widehat{z}\right)=1

for
\widehat{z}\in B_{7/4}
and let
\chi_{\mathfrak{r}}(z)=\mathfrak{r}\chi\left(\mathfrak{r}z\right)
. Let us define

(7.178)

M^{+}\left(\widehat{z}\right)=\int_{\overline{\Theta}_{\Gamma}}\widehat{\chi_{\mathfrak{r}}}\left(\mathfrak{b}^{\ast}_{+}(\theta^{\prime},\zeta)\widehat{z}-\theta^{\prime}_{\Gamma}\right)\mathrm{d}\theta^{\prime}.

It holds that
M^{+}\left(\widehat{z}\right)\geq 0
,
M^{+}\left(\widehat{z}\right)\gtrsim 1
for
\left|\widehat{z}\right|<\frac{1}{2\mathfrak{b}^{\ast}_{+}(\zeta)}
, and
\left|\partial_{\widehat{z}}^{n}M^{+}\left(\widehat{z}\right)\right|\lesssim\mathfrak{b}^{\ast}_{+}(\zeta)^{-n}
. Recall that for
(\theta,\zeta,\sigma)\in\mathfrak{T}_{\Theta}\cap(E_{+}^{\ast}\setminus E_{-}^{\ast})
we
have
\mathfrak{d}_{\zeta}(\theta_{\Gamma})F^{\ast}(\theta,\zeta,\sigma)[\varphi]=f\ast\mathrm{Dil}_{\sigma}\Upsilon
with
\Upsilon:=\mathrm{d}_{z}\operatorname{Mod}_{\theta_{\Gamma}}\varphi^{\vee}(z)
; we keep the dependence on

\theta_{\Gamma}
implicit. It holds that

(7.179)

\mathrm{spt}\left(\widehat{\mathrm{Dil}_{\sigma}\Upsilon}\right)\subset\left(\sigma^{-1}\left(\theta_{\Gamma}-\mathfrak{r}\right),\sigma^{-1}\left(\theta_{\Gamma}+\mathfrak{r}\right)\right).

Let us decompose

(7.180)

\widehat{\mathrm{Dil}_{\sigma}\Upsilon}\left(\widehat{z}\right)\begin{aligned} &=\widehat{\chi}\left(4\mathfrak{b}^{\ast}_{+}(\zeta)\widehat{z}\right)\widehat{\mathrm{Dil}_{\sigma}\Upsilon_{\theta_{\Gamma}}}\left(\widehat{z}\right)+\left(1-\widehat{\chi}\left(4\mathfrak{b}^{\ast}_{+}(\zeta)\widehat{z}\right)\right)\widehat{\mathrm{Dil}_{\sigma}\Upsilon}\left(\widehat{z}\right)\\
&=\widehat{\Upsilon_{\sigma}^{\uparrow}}+\widehat{\Upsilon_{\sigma}^{0}}.\end{aligned}

It holds that

(7.181)

\widehat{\Upsilon_{\sigma}^{\uparrow}}=\int_{\overline{\Theta}_{\Gamma}}\frac{\widehat{\Upsilon_{\sigma}^{\uparrow}}\left(\widehat{z}\right)}{M^{+}\left(\widehat{z}\right)}\widehat{\chi_{\mathfrak{r}}}\left(\mathfrak{b}^{\ast}_{+}(\theta^{\prime},\zeta)\widehat{z}-\theta^{\prime}_{\Gamma}\right)\mathrm{d}\theta^{\prime}

so let us define

(7.182)

\widehat{\varphi_{\theta^{\prime}}}\left(\widehat{z}\right)\coloneqq\frac{\widehat{\Upsilon_{\sigma}^{\uparrow}}\left(\frac{\widehat{z}+\theta^{\prime}_{\Gamma}}{\mathfrak{b}^{\ast}_{+}(\theta^{\prime},\zeta)}\right)}{M^{+}\left(\frac{\widehat{z}+\theta^{\prime}_{\Gamma}}{\mathfrak{b}^{\ast}_{+}(\theta^{\prime},\zeta)}\right)}\widehat{\chi_{\mathfrak{r}}}\left(\widehat{z}\right)

so that
\varphi_{\theta^{\prime}}\in\Phi^{\infty}_{2\mathfrak{r}}
,
\|\varphi_{\theta^{\prime}}\|_{\Phi^{N}_{2\mathfrak{r}}}\lesssim 1
and

(7.183)

\displaystyle\mathfrak{d}_{\zeta}(\theta_{\Gamma})F^{\ast}(\theta,\zeta,\sigma)[\varphi]=\begin{aligned} &\int_{\overline{\Theta}_{\Gamma}^{\operatorname{in}}}F^{\ast}(\theta^{\prime},\zeta,\mathfrak{b}^{\ast}_{+}(\theta^{\prime},\zeta))[\varphi_{\theta^{\prime}}]\mathrm{d}\theta^{\prime}\\
&\qquad+\int_{\overline{\Theta}_{\Gamma}^{\operatorname{ex}}}F^{\ast}(\theta^{\prime},\zeta,\mathfrak{b}^{\ast}_{+}(\theta^{\prime},\zeta))[\varphi_{\theta^{\prime}}]\mathrm{d}\theta^{\prime}.\end{aligned}

An application of
Lemma

3.13
allows us to conclude
that

(7.184)

\left|\int_{\overline{\Theta}_{\Gamma}^{\operatorname{in}}}F^{\ast}(\theta^{\prime},\zeta,\mathfrak{b}^{\ast}_{+}(\theta^{\prime},\zeta))[\varphi_{\theta^{\prime}}]\mathrm{d}\theta^{\prime}\right|\lesssim\sup_{\widetilde{\varphi}}\left|\int_{\overline{\Theta}_{\Gamma}^{\operatorname{in}}}F^{\ast}(\theta^{\prime},\zeta,\mathfrak{b}^{\ast}_{+}(\theta^{\prime},\zeta))[\widetilde{\varphi}]\mathrm{d}\theta^{\prime}\right|

with the upper bound taken over
\widetilde{\varphi}\in\Phi_{4\mathfrak{r}}^{\infty}

with
\|\widetilde{\varphi}\|_{\Phi_{4\mathfrak{r}}^{N-4}}\leq 1.
To bound the second
addend, define
\varphi_{\theta^{\prime},\sigma^{\prime}}
for all
\sigma^{\prime}\in\left(\frac{\mathfrak{b}^{\ast}_{+}(\theta^{\prime},\zeta)}{1+\overline{r}},\mathfrak{b}^{\ast}_{+}(\theta^{\prime},\zeta)\right)
for some sufficiently small
\overline{r}>0
by
setting

(7.185)

\mathrm{Dil}_{\sigma^{\prime}}\mathrm{Mod}_{\theta^{\prime}_{\Gamma}}\varphi_{\theta^{\prime},\sigma^{\prime}}=\mathrm{Dil}_{\mathfrak{b}^{\ast}_{+}(\theta^{\prime},\zeta)}\mathrm{Mod}_{\theta^{\prime}_{\Gamma}}\varphi_{\theta^{\prime}}.

Note that if
\overline{r}>0
is small enough and since
\mathfrak{b}^{\ast}_{+}(\zeta)/\mathfrak{b}^{\ast}_{-}(\zeta)>C_{\operatorname{sep}}/4
we have that
\mathfrak{b}^{\ast}_{-}(\theta^{\prime},\zeta)<\sigma^{\prime}<\mathfrak{b}^{\ast}_{+}(\theta^{\prime},\zeta)
and
\varphi_{\theta^{\prime},\sigma^{\prime}}\in\Phi_{4\mathfrak{r}}^{\infty}
with
\|\varphi_{\theta^{\prime},\sigma^{\prime}}\|_{\Phi_{4\mathfrak{r}}^{N-2}}\lesssim 1
. An
application of
Lemma

3.13
allows us to conclude
that

(7.186)

\displaystyle\left|\int_{\overline{\Theta}_{\Gamma}^{\operatorname{in}}}F^{\ast}(\theta^{\prime},\zeta,\mathfrak{b}^{\ast}_{+}(\theta^{\prime},\zeta))[\varphi_{\theta^{\prime}}]\mathrm{d}\theta^{\prime}\right|\lesssim\left|\int_{\overline{\Theta}_{\Gamma}^{\operatorname{in}}}\int_{\frac{\mathfrak{b}^{\ast}_{+}(\theta^{\prime},\zeta)}{1+\overline{r}}}^{\mathfrak{b}^{\ast}_{+}(\theta^{\prime},\zeta)}F^{\ast}(\theta^{\prime},\zeta,\mathfrak{b}^{\ast}_{+}(\theta^{\prime},\zeta))[\varphi_{\theta^{\prime},\sigma^{\prime}}]\mathrm{d}\theta^{\prime}\frac{\mathrm{d}\sigma^{\prime}}{\sigma^{\prime}}\right|

\displaystyle\sup_{\widetilde{\varphi}}\int_{\overline{\Theta}_{\Gamma}^{\operatorname{in}}}\int_{\frac{\mathfrak{b}^{\ast}_{+}(\theta^{\prime},\zeta)}{1+\overline{r}}}^{\mathfrak{b}^{\ast}_{+}(\theta^{\prime},\zeta)}\left|F^{\ast}(\theta^{\prime},\zeta,\mathfrak{b}^{\ast}_{+}(\theta^{\prime},\zeta))[\widetilde{\varphi}]\right|\mathrm{d}\theta^{\prime}

with the upper bound taken over
\widetilde{\varphi}\in\Phi_{4\mathfrak{r}}^{\infty}

with
\|\widetilde{\varphi}\|_{\Phi_{8\mathfrak{r}}^{N-4}}\leq 1.
It remains to
decompose
\widehat{\Upsilon_{\sigma}^{0}}
. Recall that

(7.187)

\displaystyle\widehat{\Upsilon_{\sigma}^{0}}\left(\widehat{z}\right)=\left(1-\widehat{\chi}\left(4\mathfrak{b}^{\ast}_{+}(\zeta)\widehat{z}\right)\right)\widehat{\mathrm{Dil}_{\sigma}\Upsilon}\left(\widehat{z}\right),

\displaystyle\mathrm{spt}\left(\widehat{\Upsilon_{\sigma}^{0}}\right)\subset B_{\frac{1+4\mathfrak{r}}{4\sigma}}\setminus B_{\frac{7}{16\mathfrak{b}_{+}^{\ast}(\zeta)}}.

For any
\theta^{\prime}\in\overline{\Theta}_{\Gamma}^{\operatorname{ex}}
it holds that

\theta^{\prime}_{\Gamma}>2^{-4}
or
3<\theta^{\prime}_{\Gamma}<-2^{-4}

(7.188)

m_{\theta^{\prime}_{\Gamma}}\left(\widehat{z}\right)=\int_{1}^{\infty}\widehat{\chi}\left(\sigma\widehat{z}-\theta^{\prime}_{\Gamma}\right)\frac{\mathrm{d}\sigma}{\sigma}.

Note that
\mathrm{spt}\left(\widehat{\chi}\left(\sigma\widehat{z}-\theta^{\prime}_{\Gamma}\right)\right)\subset\left(\sigma^{-1}\left(\theta^{\prime}_{\Gamma}-2\mathfrak{r}\right),\sigma^{-1}\left(\theta^{\prime}_{\Gamma}+2\mathfrak{r}\right)\right)
and
\widehat{\chi}\left(\sigma\widehat{z}-\theta^{\prime}_{\Gamma}\right)=1
on
\left(\sigma^{-1}\left(\theta^{\prime}_{\Gamma}-7\mathfrak{r}/4\right),\sigma^{-1}\left(\theta^{\prime}_{\Gamma}+7\mathfrak{r}/4\right)\right)
. It follows that if

\theta^{\prime}_{\Gamma}
then

(7.189)

\mathrm{spt}\left(m_{\theta^{\prime}_{\Gamma}}\left(\widehat{z}\right)\right)\subset\left(0,\theta^{\prime}_{\Gamma}+2\mathfrak{r}\right)

and

(7.190)

m_{\theta^{\prime}_{\Gamma}}\left(\widehat{z}\right)=\int_{0}^{\infty}\widehat{\chi}\left(\widehat{z}-\theta^{\prime}_{\Gamma}\right)\frac{\mathrm{d}\widehat{z}}{\widehat{z}}

for
\widehat{z}\in\left(0,\theta^{\prime}_{\Gamma}-2\mathfrak{r}\right)
;

\int_{0}^{\infty}\widehat{\chi}\left(\widehat{z}-\theta^{\prime}_{\Gamma}\right)\frac{\mathrm{d}\widehat{z}}{\widehat{z}}
is bounded above and away from
0
uniformly in

\theta^{\prime}_{\Gamma}
. It follows that

(7.191)

m\left(\widehat{z}\right)=\int_{\overline{\Theta}^{\operatorname{ex}}_{\Gamma}}m_{\theta^{\prime}_{\Gamma}}\left(\widehat{z}\right)\mathrm{d}\theta^{\prime}

satisfies

(7.192)

\mathrm{spt}\left(m\left(\widehat{z}\right)\right)\subset\left(-2-2\mathfrak{r},2+2\mathfrak{r}\right)

and

(7.193)

m\left(\widehat{z}\right)=\int_{\overline{\Theta}^{\operatorname{ex}}_{\Gamma}}\int_{0}^{\infty}\widehat{\chi}_{\mathfrak{r}}\left(\widehat{z}-\theta^{\prime}_{\Gamma}\right)\frac{\mathrm{d}\widehat{z}}{\widehat{z}}\mathrm{d}\theta^{\prime}

for
\widehat{z}\in B_{2^{-6}-2\mathfrak{r}}
. Let us re-define
m(0)=\lim_{\widehat{z}\rightarrow 0}m\left(\widehat{z}\right)
to obtain a smooth
function
\widehat{z}\rightarrow m\left(\widehat{z}\right).
Now set

M_{\sigma_{-},\sigma_{+}}\left(\widehat{z}\right)\coloneqq\frac{m\left(\sigma_{-}\widehat{z}\right)-m\left(\sigma_{+}\widehat{z}\right)}{m(0)}
for any
\sigma_{-}<\sigma_{+}
. It holds that

(7.194)

\displaystyle M_{\sigma_{-},\sigma_{+}}\left(\widehat{z}\right)\geq 0,

\displaystyle M_{\sigma_{-},\sigma_{+}}\left(\widehat{z}\right)=0\qquad\text{if }\left|\widehat{z}\right|>2\sigma_{-}^{-1}\left(1+\mathfrak{r}\right),

\displaystyle M_{\sigma_{-},\sigma_{+}}\left(\widehat{z}\right)=0\qquad\text{if }\left|\widehat{z}\right|<2\sigma_{+}^{-1}\left(2^{-7}-\mathfrak{r}\right),

\displaystyle M_{\sigma_{-},\sigma_{+}}\left(\widehat{z}\right)=1\qquad\text{if }2\sigma_{+}^{-1}\left(1+\mathfrak{r}\right)<\left|\widehat{z}\right|<2\sigma_{-}^{-1}\left(2^{-7}-\mathfrak{r}\right).

The first claim follows from the fact that

(7.195)

M_{\sigma_{-},\sigma_{+}}\left(\widehat{z}\right)=\frac{1}{m(0)}\int_{\overline{\Theta}^{\operatorname{ex}}_{\Gamma}}\int_{\sigma_{-}}^{\sigma_{+}}\widehat{\chi}_{\mathfrak{r}}\left(\sigma\widehat{z}-\theta^{\prime}_{\Gamma}\right)\frac{\mathrm{d}\sigma}{\sigma}\mathrm{d}\theta^{\prime}

while subsequent claims follow from support considerations on
m
above. Let
us write

(7.196)

\widehat{\Upsilon_{\sigma}^{0}}\left(\widehat{z}\right)=M_{\mathfrak{b}^{\ast}_{-}(\zeta),\mathfrak{b}^{\ast}_{+}(\zeta)}\left(\widehat{z}\right)\widehat{\Upsilon_{\sigma}^{0}}\left(\widehat{z}\right).

This identity holds because of support considerations and since

\frac{\mathfrak{b}^{\ast}_{-}(\zeta)C_{\operatorname{sep}}}{2}<\sigma<\mathfrak{b}_{-}^{\ast}(\zeta)
. We obtain that

(7.197)

\displaystyle\widehat{\Upsilon_{\sigma}^{0}}\left(\widehat{z}\right)=\int_{\overline{\Theta}^{\operatorname{ex}}_{\Gamma}}\int_{0}^{\infty}\widehat{\Upsilon_{\sigma}^{0}}\left(\widehat{z}\right)\widehat{\chi}_{\mathfrak{r}}\left(\sigma^{\prime}\widehat{z}-\theta^{\prime}_{\Gamma}\right)\frac{\mathrm{d}\sigma^{\prime}}{\sigma^{\prime}}\mathrm{d}\theta^{\prime}

\displaystyle=\int_{\overline{\Theta}^{\operatorname{ex}}_{\Gamma}}\int_{0}^{\infty}\left(1-\widehat{\chi}\left(4\mathfrak{b}^{\ast}_{+}(\zeta)\widehat{z}\right)\right)\sigma\widehat{z}\widehat{\varphi}\left(\sigma\widehat{z}-\theta_{\Gamma}\right)\widehat{\chi}_{\mathfrak{r}}\left(\sigma^{\prime}\widehat{z}-\theta^{\prime}_{\Gamma}\right)\frac{\mathrm{d}\sigma^{\prime}}{\sigma^{\prime}}\mathrm{d}\theta^{\prime}

By support considerations it holds that

(7.198)

\widehat{\varphi}\left(\sigma\widehat{z}-\theta_{\Gamma}\right)\widehat{\chi}_{\mathfrak{r}}\left(\sigma^{\prime}\widehat{z}-\theta^{\prime}_{\Gamma}\right)=0

unless
\sigma^{\prime}\gtrsim\sigma
and
\left(1-\widehat{\chi}\left(4\mathfrak{b}^{\ast}_{+}(\zeta)\widehat{z}\right)\right)\widehat{\chi}_{\mathfrak{r}}\left(\sigma^{\prime}\widehat{z}-\theta^{\prime}_{\Gamma}\right)=0
unless
\sigma^{\prime}\gtrsim\mathfrak{b}^{\ast}_{+}(\zeta)\widehat{z}
. It follows that setting

(7.199)

\varphi_{\theta^{\prime},\sigma^{\prime}}\left(\sigma^{\prime}\widehat{z}-\theta^{\prime}_{\Gamma}\right)\coloneqq\left(1-\widehat{\chi}\left(4\mathfrak{b}^{\ast}_{+}(\zeta)\widehat{z}\right)\right)\sigma^{\prime}\widehat{z}\widehat{\varphi}\left(\sigma\widehat{z}-\theta_{\Gamma}\right)\widehat{\chi}_{\mathfrak{r}}\left(\sigma^{\prime}\widehat{z}-\theta^{\prime}_{\Gamma}\right)

we obtain a function
\varphi_{\theta^{\prime},\zeta^{\prime},\sigma^{\prime}}\in\Phi_{2\mathfrak{r}}^{N}

with
{\|\varphi_{\theta^{\prime},\zeta^{\prime},\sigma^{\prime}}\|_{\Phi_{2\mathfrak{r}}^{N}}}\lesssim 1

so

(7.200)

\widehat{\Upsilon_{\sigma}^{0}}\left(\widehat{z}\right)=\int_{\overline{\Theta}^{\operatorname{ex}}_{\Gamma}}\int_{0}^{\infty}\frac{\sigma}{\sigma^{\prime}}\mathbbm{1}_{\sigma^{\prime}>a\sigma}\varphi_{\theta^{\prime},\zeta^{\prime},\sigma^{\prime}}\left(\sigma^{\prime}\widehat{z}-\theta^{\prime}_{\Gamma}\right)\frac{\mathrm{d}\sigma^{\prime}}{\sigma^{\prime}}\mathrm{d}\theta^{\prime}.

or some
a>0
. Making sure to choose
C_{\operatorname{sep}}>\frac{4}{a}
and
applying
Lemma

3.13
allows us to conclude that

(7.201)

|f\ast\Upsilon_{\sigma}^{0}(\zeta)|\lesssim\sup_{\widetilde{\varphi}}\int_{\overline{\Theta}^{\operatorname{ex}}_{\Gamma}}\int_{0}^{\infty}\frac{\sigma}{\sigma^{\prime}}\mathbbm{1}_{\sigma^{\prime}>a\sigma}|F^{\ast}(\theta^{\prime},\zeta^{\prime},\sigma^{\prime})[\widetilde{\varphi}]|\frac{\mathrm{d}\sigma^{\prime}}{\sigma^{\prime}}\mathrm{d}\theta^{\prime}.

with the upper bound taken over
\widetilde{\varphi}\in\Phi_{4\mathfrak{r}}^{\infty}

with
\|\widetilde{\varphi}\|_{\Phi_{8\mathfrak{r}}^{N-4}}\leq 1
, as required.
∎

Lemma 7.10

(Maximal size domination)
.

Let
V^{+},V^{-}\in\mathbb{D}^{\cup}_{\beta}
, and let
W^{+},W^{-}\in\mathbb{T}_{\Theta}^{\cup}
. The
bounds

(7.202)

\displaystyle\|\mathbbm{1}_{(V^{+}\cap W^{+})\setminus(V^{-}\cup W^{-})}\left(\mathrm{Emb}[f]\circ\Gamma\right)\|_{{\Gamma^{\ast}}\mathbb{L}^{(u,\infty)}_{(\Theta,\Theta^{\operatorname{in}})}\Phi_{\mathfrak{r}}^{N}}\qquad

\displaystyle\lesssim\beta^{-\frac{1}{u}}\|\mathbbm{1}_{(V^{+}\cap W^{+})\setminus(V^{-}\cup W^{-})}\left(\mathrm{Emb}[f]\circ\Gamma\right)\|_{\left({\Gamma^{\ast}}\mathbb{L}^{(u,2)}_{(\Theta,\Theta^{\operatorname{ex}})}\Phi_{4\mathfrak{r}}^{N-2}{+\Gamma^{\ast}}\mathbb{L}^{(u,1)}_{\Theta}\Box^{N-2}_{4\mathfrak{r}}\right)},

hold for any
f\in\mathscr{S}(\mathbb{R})
as long as
u\in[2,\infty]
.
The implicit constant is independent of
f
and of the sets
V^{\pm},W^{\pm}
.

Proof.

Let us fix
T\in\mathbb{T}_{\Theta}
and show that

(7.203)

\|\mathbbm{1}_{(V^{+}\cap W^{+})\setminus(V^{-}\cup W^{-})}\left(\mathrm{Emb}[f]\circ\Gamma\right)\|_{{\Gamma^{\ast}}\mathbb{L}^{(u,\infty)}_{(\Theta,\Theta^{\operatorname{in}})}\Phi_{\mathfrak{r}}^{N}(T)}\lesssim\mathrm{RHS}{\eqref{eq:unif-lac-size-domination}.}

By symmetry, (see (
3.94
) and the subsequent discussion),
we can assume that
{T=T_{\Theta}}(0,0,1)
so that

(7.204)

\Gamma\circ\pi_{T}(\theta,\zeta,\sigma)=(\alpha\beta(\theta+\gamma)(\beta\sigma)^{-1},\zeta,\beta\sigma)=(\theta_{\Gamma}(\beta\sigma)^{-1},\zeta,\beta\sigma)=\pi_{T}(\theta_{\Gamma},\zeta,\beta\sigma).

We use the shorthand
E_{+}^{\ast}:=\mathfrak{T}_{\Theta^{\operatorname{in}}}\cap\pi_{T}^{-1}(V^{+}\cap W^{+})
,
E_{-}^{\ast}:=\pi_{T}^{-1}(V^{-}\cup W^{-})
, and
F^{\ast}\coloneqq\mathbbm{1}_{E_{+}^{\ast}\setminus E_{-}^{\ast}}\mathrm{Emb}{}[f]\circ\Gamma\circ\pi_{T}
. We have that

(7.205)

\displaystyle\|\mathbbm{1}_{(V^{+}\cap W^{+})\setminus(V^{-}\cup W^{-})}\left(\mathrm{Emb}[f]\circ\Gamma\right)\|_{{\Gamma^{\ast}}\mathbb{L}^{(u,\infty)}_{(\Theta,\Theta^{\operatorname{in}})}\Phi_{\mathfrak{r}}^{N}(T)}

\displaystyle=\sup_{\varphi}\sup_{\sigma}\int_{\Theta^{\operatorname{in}}\times B_{1}}F^{\ast}(\theta,\zeta,\sigma(\theta,\zeta))[\varphi]^{u}\frac{\mathrm{d}\theta\mathrm{d}\zeta}{|\Theta|}

where the upper bound is taken over all
\varphi\in\Phi^{\infty}_{\mathfrak{r}}
with

\|\varphi\|_{\Phi^{N}_{\mathfrak{r}}}\leq 1
and over all continuous functions
\sigma:\Theta\times B_{1}\rightarrow(0,+\infty)
. Let
\mathfrak{b}_{+}^{\ast}
,

\mathfrak{b}_{-}^{\ast}
be the two functions whose graphs are the boundaries of

E_{+}^{\ast}
and
E_{-}^{\ast}
respectively so that
E_{+}^{\ast}\setminus E_{-}^{\ast}=\left\{(\theta,\zeta,\sigma):\mathfrak{b}_{-}^{\ast}(\theta,\zeta)<\sigma<\mathfrak{b}_{+}^{\ast}(\theta,\zeta)\right\}
. The existence
of such functions is guaranteed by
Lemma

3.20
. Let

\overline{r}>0
be a small absolute constant to depending only on
\mathfrak{r}
be
determined later. We distinguish the points
(\theta,\zeta)
based on how
far
\sigma(\theta,\zeta)
is from the boundary of
E_{+}^{\ast}\setminus E_{-}^{\ast}
. Let

(7.206)

\displaystyle U_{-}\coloneqq\left\{(\theta,\zeta)\in\Theta^{\operatorname{in}}\times B_{1}:\mathfrak{b}_{-}^{\ast}(\theta,\zeta)\leq\sigma(\theta,\zeta)<(1+\overline{r})\mathfrak{b}_{-}^{\ast}(\theta,\zeta)\right\},

\displaystyle U_{+}\coloneqq\left\{(\theta,\zeta)\in\Theta^{\operatorname{in}}\times B_{1}:(1-\overline{r})\mathfrak{b}_{+}^{\ast}(\theta,\zeta)<\sigma(\theta,\zeta)\leq\mathfrak{b}_{+}^{\ast}(\theta,\zeta)\right\},

\displaystyle U_{0}\coloneqq\left\{(\theta,\zeta)\in\Theta^{\operatorname{in}}\times B_{1}:(1+\overline{r})\mathfrak{b}_{-}^{\ast}(\theta,\zeta)\leq\sigma(\theta,\zeta)\leq(1-\overline{r})\mathfrak{b}_{+}^{\ast}(\theta,\zeta)\right\}.

If
(\theta,\zeta)\in U_{-}
we use that

(7.207)

\left(\mathrm{Emb}[f]\circ\Gamma\right)(\theta,\zeta,\sigma(\theta,\zeta))[\varphi]=\left(\mathrm{Emb}[f]\circ\Gamma\right)\left(\theta,\zeta,\mathfrak{b}_{-}^{\ast}(\theta,\zeta)\right)[\varphi_{(\theta,\zeta)}]

with

(7.208)

\varphi_{(\theta,\zeta)}\coloneqq\mathrm{Mod}_{\theta_{\Gamma}}\mathrm{Dil}_{\sigma(\theta,\zeta)/\mathfrak{b}_{-}^{\ast}(\theta,\zeta)}\mathrm{Mod}_{-\theta_{\Gamma}}\varphi.

Since
1\leq\sigma(\theta,\zeta)/\mathfrak{b}_{-}^{\ast}(\theta,\zeta)<1+\overline{r}
we have that
\varphi_{(\theta,\zeta)}\in\Phi_{2\mathfrak{r}}^{\infty}

with
\|\varphi_{(\theta,\zeta)}\|_{\Phi_{2\mathfrak{r}}^{N}}\lesssim 1
. Using

Lemma

3.13
we get that

(7.209)

\displaystyle\int_{\Theta^{\operatorname{in}}\times B_{1}}\mathbbm{1}_{U_{-}}(\theta,\zeta)F^{\ast}(\theta,\zeta,\sigma(\theta,\zeta))[\varphi]^{u}\frac{\mathrm{d}\theta\mathrm{d}\zeta}{|\Theta|}

\displaystyle\lesssim\sup_{\widetilde{\varphi}}\int_{\Theta^{\operatorname{in}}\times B_{1}}\mathbbm{1}_{U_{-}}(\theta,\zeta)F^{\ast}\left(\theta,\zeta,\mathfrak{b}_{-}^{\ast}(\theta,\zeta)\right)[\varphi]^{u}\frac{\mathrm{d}\theta\mathrm{d}\zeta}{|\Theta|}

with the upper bound taken over
\widetilde{\varphi}\in\Phi_{2\mathfrak{r}}^{\infty}

with
\|\widetilde{\varphi}\|_{\Phi_{4\mathfrak{r}}^{N-2}}\leq 1
. The
\mathrm{RHS}{}
of the
entry above is bounded by
\|\mathbbm{1}_{(V^{+}\cap W^{+})\setminus(V^{-}\cup W^{-})}\left(\mathrm{Emb}[f]\circ\Gamma\right)\|_{{\Gamma^{\ast}}\mathbb{L}^{(u,1)}_{\Theta}\Box^{N-2}_{4\mathfrak{r}}(T)}
and thus by

\mathrm{RHS}{\eqref{eq:unif-lac-size-domination}^{u}}
, as required. The same
procedure applies to the case when
(\theta,\zeta)\in U_{+}
: Set

\varphi_{(\theta,\zeta)}\coloneqq\mathrm{Mod}_{\theta_{\Gamma}}\mathrm{Dil}_{\sigma(\theta,\zeta)/\mathfrak{b}_{+}^{\ast}(\theta,\zeta)}\mathrm{Mod}_{-\theta_{\Gamma}}\varphi
to
obtain that

(7.210)

\displaystyle\int_{\Theta^{\operatorname{in}}\times B_{1}}\mathbbm{1}_{U_{+}}(\theta,\zeta)F^{\ast}(\theta,\zeta,\sigma(\theta,\zeta))[\varphi]^{u}\frac{\mathrm{d}\theta\mathrm{d}\zeta}{|\Theta|}

\displaystyle\lesssim\sup_{\widetilde{\varphi}}\int_{\Theta^{\operatorname{in}}\times B_{1}}\mathbbm{1}_{U_{-}}(\theta,\zeta)F^{\ast}\left(\theta,\zeta,\mathfrak{b}_{+}^{\ast}(\theta,\zeta)\right)[\varphi]^{u}\frac{\mathrm{d}\theta\mathrm{d}\zeta}{|\Theta|}\lesssim\mathrm{RHS}{\eqref{eq:unif-lac-size-domination}^{u}}.

In the case
(\theta,\zeta)\in U_{0}
we will just show the pointwise bound

|F^{\ast}(\theta,\zeta,\sigma(\theta,\zeta))[\varphi]|\lesssim\mathrm{RHS}{\eqref{eq:unif-lac-size-domination}}
. Integrating this bound will
yield the claim. Let

\widetilde{W}^{-}\coloneqq W^{-}\cup T_{\Theta}(0,\zeta,\sigma(\theta,\zeta))
; let
\widetilde{E}_{-}^{\ast}=E_{-}^{\ast}\cup\pi_{T}^{-1}(T_{\Theta}(0,\zeta,\sigma(\theta,\zeta)))
and let

\mathfrak{\widetilde{b}_{-}^{\ast}}:\mathbb{R}^{2}\rightarrow\mathbb{R}_{+}
be the function given by

Lemma

3.20
such that its graphs is the boundary of the
set
\widetilde{E}_{-}^{\ast}
. For any
\left(\theta^{\prime},\zeta^{\prime},\widetilde{\mathfrak{b}}_{-}^{\ast}(\theta^{\prime},\zeta^{\prime})\right)\in E^{\ast}_{+}\setminus\widetilde{E}^{\ast}_{-}
it holds that

(7.211)

F^{\ast}(\theta,\zeta,\sigma(\theta,\zeta))[\varphi]=F^{\ast}\left(\theta^{\prime},\zeta^{\prime},\widetilde{\mathfrak{b}}_{-}^{\ast}(\theta^{\prime},\zeta^{\prime})\right)[\varphi_{(\theta^{\prime},\zeta^{\prime})}]

with
\varphi_{(\theta^{\prime},\zeta^{\prime})}
defined by the relation

(7.212)

\mathrm{Tr}_{\zeta^{\prime}}\mathrm{Dil}_{\beta\widetilde{\mathfrak{b}}_{-}^{\ast}(\theta^{\prime},\zeta^{\prime})}\mathrm{Mod}_{-\theta_{\Gamma}}\varphi_{(\theta^{\prime},\zeta^{\prime})}\coloneqq\mathrm{Tr}_{\zeta}\mathrm{Dil}_{\beta\sigma(\theta,\zeta)}\mathrm{Mod}_{-\theta_{\Gamma}}\varphi.

If
|\zeta^{\prime}-\zeta|<\overline{r}^{2}\beta\sigma(\theta,\zeta)
and

\theta^{\prime}\in B_{\overline{r}^{2}}(\theta)\cap\Theta^{\operatorname{in}}
it holds that

\widetilde{\mathfrak{b}}_{-}^{\ast}(\theta^{\prime},\zeta^{\prime})<\mathfrak{b}_{+}^{\ast}(\theta^{\prime},\zeta^{\prime})
given the continuity assumptions
(
3.153
) on
\widetilde{\mathfrak{b}}_{-}^{\ast}
and

\mathfrak{b}_{+}^{\ast}
and since
\mathfrak{b}_{+}^{\ast}(\theta,\zeta)>(1-\overline{r})^{-1}\sigma(\theta,\zeta)=\widetilde{\mathfrak{b}}_{-}^{\ast}(\theta,\zeta)
. Furthermore, we have that
\varphi_{(\theta^{\prime},\zeta^{\prime})}\in\Phi_{2\mathfrak{r}}^{\infty}
with
\|\varphi_{(\theta^{\prime},\zeta^{\prime})}\|_{\Phi^{N}_{2\mathfrak{r}}}\lesssim 1
. It follows that

(7.213)

\displaystyle|F^{\ast}(\theta,\zeta,\sigma(\theta,\zeta))[\varphi]|

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{1}{\beta\sigma(\theta,\zeta)\overline{r}^{4}}\sup_{\widetilde{\varphi}}{\int_{(B_{\overline{r}^{2}}(\theta)\cap\Theta^{\operatorname{in}})\cap\overline{r}^{2}\beta\sigma(\theta,\zeta)}}\left|F^{\ast}\left(\theta^{\prime},\zeta^{\prime},\widetilde{\mathfrak{b}}_{-}^{\ast}(\theta^{\prime},\zeta^{\prime})\right)[\widetilde{\varphi}]\right|\mathrm{d}\theta^{\prime}\mathrm{d}\zeta^{\prime}

\displaystyle\lesssim\bigl\|\mathbbm{1}_{(V^{+}\cap W^{+})\setminus(V^{-}\cup\widetilde{W}^{-})}\left(\mathrm{Emb}[f]\circ\Gamma\right)\bigr\|_{{\Gamma^{\ast}}\mathbb{L}^{(1,\infty)}_{(\Theta,\Theta^{\operatorname{in}})}\Box^{N-2}_{4\mathfrak{r}}(T^{\prime})}

with
T^{\prime}=T_{\Theta}(0,\zeta,10\sigma(\theta,\zeta))
. Since

(7.214)

\displaystyle\bigl\|\mathbbm{1}_{(V^{+}\cap W^{+})\setminus(V^{-}\cup\widetilde{W}^{-})}\left(\mathrm{Emb}[f]\circ\Gamma\right)\bigr\|_{{\Gamma^{\ast}}\mathbb{L}^{(1,\infty)}_{(\Theta,\Theta^{\operatorname{in}})}\Box^{N-2}_{4\mathfrak{r}}(T^{\prime})}

\displaystyle\lesssim\bigl\|\mathbbm{1}_{(V^{+}\cap W^{+})\setminus(V^{-}\cup\widetilde{W}^{-})}\left(\mathrm{Emb}[f]\circ\Gamma\right)\bigr\|_{{\Gamma^{\ast}}\mathbb{L}^{(u,\infty)}_{(\Theta,\Theta^{\operatorname{in}})}\Box^{N-2}_{4\mathfrak{r}}(T^{\prime})}

This concludes the proof.
∎

8.
Uniform embedding bounds - bilinear case

In this section we prove the bilinear embedding bounds
(
1.23
) of

Theorem

4
. It is noteworthy that all but
the last size
\Gamma_{\times}^{\ast}\mathbb{L}_{(\Theta,\Theta^{\operatorname{in}})}^{(u,1)}\Box_{\mathfrak{r}}^{N}
appearing in the expression of
\widetilde{\mathbb{F}}^{u}_{\Gamma_{\times}}\Phi_{\mathfrak{r}}^{N}
can be bound using sizes

\widetilde{\mathbb{F}}^{u}_{\Gamma_{j}}\Phi_{\mathfrak{r}}^{N}
,
j\in\{2,3\}
. Thus, an
application of the outer Hölder inequality
(
Corollary

3.4
) can yields bounds
(
1.23
) with
\mathbb{S}^{u_{\times}}_{\Gamma_{\times}}\Phi_{\mathfrak{r}}^{N}
in place of
\widetilde{\mathbb{F}}^{u}_{\Gamma_{\times}}\Phi_{\mathfrak{r}}^{N}
where

(8.1)

\displaystyle\mathbb{S}^{u_{\times}}_{\Gamma_{\times}}\Phi_{\mathfrak{r}}^{N}\coloneqq\begin{aligned} &\beta^{\frac{1}{u_{\times}}}\Big(\Gamma_{\times}^{\ast}\mathbb{L}_{(\Theta,\Theta^{\operatorname{in}})}^{\left({u_{\times}},2\right)}\left(\Phi\otimes\mathfrak{d}\right)_{\mathfrak{r}}^{N}+\Gamma_{\times}^{\ast}\mathbb{L}_{(\Theta,\Theta^{\operatorname{in}})}^{\left({u_{\times}},2\right)}\left(\mathfrak{d}\otimes\Phi\right)_{\mathfrak{r}}^{N}\Big.\\
&\qquad\Big.+\Gamma_{\times}^{\ast}{\mathbb{L}}^{(u_{\times},\infty)}_{(\Theta,\Theta^{\operatorname{in}})}(\Phi\otimes\Phi)_{\mathfrak{r}}^{N}\Big)\hskip 20.00003pt+\Gamma_{\times}^{\ast}{\mathbb{L}}^{(u_{\times},1)}_{\Theta}\left(\mathfrak{d}\otimes\mathfrak{d}\right)_{\mathfrak{r}}^{N}.\end{aligned}

We show this in
Proposition

8.1
. It then remains to show the
validity of bound (
1.23
) with the size

\Gamma_{\times}^{\ast}\mathbb{L}_{(\Theta,\Theta^{\operatorname{in}})}^{(u,1)}\Box_{\mathfrak{r}}^{N}
in place of
\widetilde{\mathbb{F}}^{u}_{\Gamma_{\times}}\Phi_{\mathfrak{r}}^{N}
. This is done in
Proposition

8.2
.

Proposition 8.1
.

The bound

(8.2)

\|F_{2}F_{3}\|_{L^{p_{\times}}_{\nu_{\beta}}X^{q_{\times},r_{\times},+}_{\mu_{\Theta}^{1},\nu_{\beta}}\mathbb{S}^{u_{\times}}_{\Gamma_{\times}}\Phi_{\mathfrak{r}}^{N}}\lesssim\prod_{j=2}^{3}\|F_{j}\|_{L^{p_{j}}_{\nu_{\beta}}X_{\mu_{\Theta}^{1},\nu_{\beta}}^{q_{j},r_{j},+}\widetilde{\mathbb{F}}^{u_{j}}_{\Gamma_{j}}\Phi_{\mathfrak{r}}^{N}}

holds for any
F_{j}\in L^{p_{j}}_{\nu_{\beta}}X_{\mu_{\Theta}^{1},\nu_{\beta}}^{q_{j},r_{j},+}\widetilde{\mathbb{F}}^{u_{j}}_{\Gamma_{j}}\Phi_{\mathfrak{r}}^{N}
with

(8.3)

\displaystyle p_{\times}^{-1}=p_{2}^{-1}+p_{3}^{-1}\qquad q_{\times}^{-1}=q_{2}^{-1}+q_{3}^{-1}

\displaystyle r_{\times}^{-1}=r_{2}^{-1}+r_{3}^{-1}\qquad u_{\times}^{-1}=u_{2}^{-1}+u_{3}^{-1}

as long as
p_{j}\in(1,\infty]
and
r_{j}\leq q_{j}
,
j\in\{2,3\}
.
As a consequence, the bound

(8.4)

\left\|\mathrm{Emb}[f_{2}]\circ\Gamma_{2}\mathrm{Emb}[f_{3}]\circ\Gamma_{3}\right\|_{L^{p_{\times}}_{\nu_{\beta}}X^{q_{\times},r_{\times},+}_{\mu_{\Theta}^{1},\nu_{\beta}}\mathbb{S}^{u_{\times}}_{\Gamma_{\times}}\Phi_{\mathfrak{r}}^{N}}\lesssim\|f_{2}\|_{L^{p_{2}}(\mathbb{R})}\|f_{2}\|_{L^{p_{3}}(\mathbb{R})}.

holds for any
p_{j}\in(1,\infty]
as long as
q_{\times}^{-1}<\min\left(\frac{1}{2},1-p_{2}^{-1}\right)+\min\left(\frac{1}{2},1-p_{3}^{-1}\right)
and as long as
1\leq u_{\times}<r_{\times}<q_{\times}
. The implicit constant is independent of
\beta\in(0,1]
,
that determines
(\alpha_{j},\beta_{j},\gamma_{j})
and
\Gamma_{j}
,
j\in\{2,3\}
, and of
F_{2},F_{3}
.

Proof.

Bound (
8.4
) follows from bound
(
8.2
) thanks to the linear embedding bounds
(
1.20
). As a matter of fact, fix
1\leq u_{\times}<r_{\times}<q_{\times}
with
q_{\times}^{-1}<\min\left(\frac{1}{2},1-p_{2}^{-1}\right)+\min\left(\frac{1}{2},1-p_{3}^{-1}\right)
and set

(8.5)

\displaystyle q_{j}^{-1}=\min\left(\frac{1}{2},1-p_{j}^{-1}\right)-\varepsilon_{q}/2,

\displaystyle r_{j}^{-1}=q_{j}^{-1}-\varepsilon_{r}/2,

\displaystyle u_{j}=r_{j}-\varepsilon_{u}/2.

with

(8.6)

\displaystyle\varepsilon_{q}\coloneqq q_{\times}^{-1}-\min\left(\frac{1}{2},1-p_{2}^{-1}\right)-\min\left(\frac{1}{2},1-p_{3}^{-1}\right)>0,

\displaystyle\varepsilon_{r}\coloneqq r_{\times}^{-1}-q_{\times}^{-1}>0,

\displaystyle\varepsilon_{r}\coloneqq u_{\times}^{-1}-r_{\times}^{-1}>0.

For such a choice of
(q_{j},r_{j},u_{j})
,

Theorem

3
holds so
\mathrm{RHS}{}\eqref{eq:bound:bilinear-holder}\lesssim\mathrm{RHS}{\eqref{eq:embedding-bound:bilinear-holder}}
proving our claim.

Let us now prove (
8.2
) by appropriately applying
the outer Hölder inequality (
Corollary

3.4
). For any

F_{j}\in\mathscr{R}(\mathbb{R}^{3}_{+})\otimes\Phi^{\infty}_{\mathfrak{r}}
,
j\in\{2,3\}
, it holds that that

(8.7)

\nu_{\beta}(\|F_{2}F_{3}\|_{X^{q_{\times},r_{\times},+}_{\mu_{\Theta}^{1},\nu_{\beta}}\mathbb{S}^{u_{\times},N}_{\mathfrak{r}}}>0)\leq\min_{j=2,3}\nu_{\beta}\bigl(\|F_{j}\|_{X_{\mu_{\Theta}^{1},\nu_{\beta}}^{q_{j},r_{j},+}\widetilde{\mathbb{F}}^{u_{j}}_{\Gamma_{j}}\Phi_{\mathfrak{r}}^{N}}>0\bigr)

since for any
V^{-}\in\mathbb{D}_{\beta}^{\cup}
it holds that if
\mathbbm{1}_{\mathbb{R}^{3}_{+}\setminus V^{-}}F_{j}=0
for
j=2
or
j=3
then, trivially,
\mathbbm{1}_{\mathbb{R}^{3}_{+}\setminus V^{-}}F_{2}F_{3}=0
. To apply the outer Hölder inequality we must
check that for any
F_{j}\in\mathscr{R}(\mathbb{R}^{3}_{+})\otimes\Phi^{\infty}_{\mathfrak{r}}
,
j\in\{2,3\}
it holds that

(8.8)

\|F_{2}F_{3}\|_{X^{q_{\times},r_{\times},+}_{\mu_{\Theta}^{1},\nu_{\beta}}\mathbb{S}^{u_{\times}}_{\Gamma_{\times}}\Phi_{\mathfrak{r}}^{N}}\lesssim\prod_{j=2}^{3}\|F_{j}\|_{X_{\mu_{\Theta}^{1},\nu_{\beta}}^{q_{j},r_{j},+}\widetilde{\mathbb{F}}^{u_{j}}_{\Gamma_{j}}\Phi_{\mathfrak{r}}^{N}}.

The sizes
X^{q_{\times},r_{\times},+}_{\mu_{\Theta}^{1},\nu_{\beta}}\mathbb{S}^{u_{\times}}_{\Gamma_{\times}}\Phi_{\mathfrak{r}}^{N}
are themselves (localized)
outer Lebesgue norms. According to (
3.133
), it is sufficient to
check that for any
V^{+}\in\mathbb{D}_{\beta}^{\cup}
and any
W^{+}\in\mathbb{T}_{\Theta}^{\cup}
one has

(8.9)

\displaystyle\nu_{\beta}(V^{+})^{-\frac{1}{r_{\times}}}\mu^{\infty}_{\Theta}(W^{+})^{\frac{1}{q_{\times}}-\frac{1}{r_{\times}}}\left\|\mathbbm{1}_{(V^{+}\cap W^{+})}F_{2}F_{3}\right\|_{L^{r_{\times}}_{\mu_{\Theta}^{1}}\mathbb{S}^{u_{\times}}_{\Gamma_{\times}}\Phi_{\mathfrak{r}}^{N}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\prod_{j=2}^{3}\left(\nu_{\beta}(V^{+})^{-\frac{1}{r_{j}}}\mu^{\infty}_{\Theta}(W^{+})^{\frac{1}{q_{j}}-\frac{1}{r_{j}}}\left\|\mathbbm{1}_{(V^{+}\cap W^{+})}F_{j}\right\|_{L^{r_{j}}_{\mu_{\Theta}^{1}}\widetilde{\mathbb{F}}^{u_{j}}_{\Gamma_{j}}\Phi_{\mathfrak{r}}^{N}}\right)

that boils down to showing

(8.10)

\left\|\mathbbm{1}_{(V^{+}\cap W^{+})}F_{2}F_{3}\right\|_{L^{r_{\times}}_{\mu_{\Theta}^{1}}\mathbb{S}^{u_{\times}}_{\Gamma_{\times}}\Phi_{\mathfrak{r}}^{N}}\lesssim\prod_{j=2}^{3}\left\|\mathbbm{1}_{(V^{+}\cap W^{+})}F_{j}\right\|_{L^{r_{j}}_{\mu_{\Theta}^{1}}\widetilde{\mathbb{F}}^{u_{j}}_{\Gamma_{j}}\Phi_{\mathfrak{r}}^{N}}.

This amounts to the outer Hölder inequality at the level of the outer
Lebesgue norms
L^{r_{\times}}_{\mu_{\Theta}^{1}}\mathbb{S}^{u_{\times}}_{\Gamma_{\times}}\Phi_{\mathfrak{r}}^{N}
and

L^{r_{j}}_{\mu_{\Theta}^{1}}\widetilde{\mathbb{F}}^{u_{j}}_{\Gamma_{j}}\Phi_{\mathfrak{r}}^{N}
,

j\in\{2,3\}
.

Using that
\mu_{\Theta}^{1}(\|F_{2}F_{3}\|_{\mathbb{S}^{u_{\times}}_{\Gamma_{\times}}\Phi_{\mathfrak{r}}^{N}}>0)\leq\min_{j=2,3}\mu_{\Theta}^{1}\bigl(\|F_{j}\|_{\widetilde{\mathbb{F}}^{u_{j}}_{\Gamma_{j}}\Phi_{\mathfrak{r}}^{N}}>0\bigr)
, we can apply the Hölder inequality to obtain
the above bound as long as it holds that

(8.11)

\left\|\mathbbm{1}_{(V^{+}\cap W^{+})}F_{2}F_{3}\right\|_{\mathbb{S}^{u_{\times}}_{\Gamma_{\times}}\Phi_{\mathfrak{r}}^{N}}\lesssim\prod_{j=2}^{3}\left\|\mathbbm{1}_{(V^{+}\cap W^{+})}F_{j}\right\|_{\widetilde{\mathbb{F}}^{u_{j}}_{\Gamma_{j}}\Phi_{\mathfrak{r}}^{N}}.

This however is a simple consequence of the classical Hölder inequality and
the definitions (
3.119
) of the sizes in play. We
immediately have

(8.12)

\displaystyle\left\|\mathbbm{1}_{(V^{+}\cap W^{+})}F_{2}F_{3}\right\|_{\beta^{\frac{1}{{u_{\times}}}}\Gamma_{\times}^{\ast}{\mathbb{L}}^{(u_{\times},\infty)}_{\Theta}(\Phi\otimes\Phi)_{\mathfrak{r}}^{N}}\lesssim\prod_{j=2}^{3}\beta^{\frac{1}{{u_{j}}}}\left\|\mathbbm{1}_{(V^{+}\cap W^{+})}F_{j}\right\|_{\Gamma_{j}^{\ast}{\mathbb{L}}^{(u_{j},\infty)}_{(\Theta,\Theta^{\operatorname{in}})}\Phi_{\mathfrak{r}}^{N}},

\displaystyle\left\|\mathbbm{1}_{(V^{+}\cap W^{+})}F_{2}F_{3}\right\|_{\beta^{\frac{1}{{u_{\times}}}}\Gamma_{\times}^{\ast}{\mathbb{L}}^{(u_{\times},1)}_{\Theta}\left(\Phi\otimes\mathfrak{d}\right)_{\mathfrak{r}}^{N}}\lesssim\begin{aligned} &\beta^{\frac{1}{u_{2}}}\left\|\mathbbm{1}_{(V^{+}\cap W^{+})}F_{2}\right\|_{\Gamma_{2}^{\ast}{\mathbb{L}}^{(u_{2},\infty)}_{(\Theta,\Theta^{\operatorname{in}})}\Phi_{\mathfrak{r}}^{N}}\\
&\times\beta^{\frac{1}{{u_{3}}}}\left\|\mathbbm{1}_{(V^{+}\cap W^{+})}F_{3}\right\|_{\Gamma_{3}^{\ast}{\mathbb{L}}^{(u_{3},2)}_{\Theta}\mathfrak{d}_{\mathfrak{r}}^{N}},\end{aligned}

\displaystyle\left\|\mathbbm{1}_{(V^{+}\cap W^{+})}F_{2}F_{3}\right\|_{\beta^{\frac{1}{{u_{\times}}}}\Gamma_{\times}^{\ast}{\mathbb{L}}^{(u_{\times},1)}_{\Theta}\left(\Phi\otimes\mathfrak{d}\right)_{\mathfrak{r}}^{N}}\lesssim\begin{aligned} &\beta^{\frac{1}{u_{2}}}\left\|\mathbbm{1}_{(V^{+}\cap W^{+})}F_{2}\right\|_{\Gamma_{2}^{\ast}{\mathbb{L}}^{(u_{2},\infty)}_{\Theta}\mathfrak{d}_{\mathfrak{r}}^{N}}\\
&\times\beta^{\frac{1}{{u_{3}}}}\left\|\mathbbm{1}_{(V^{+}\cap W^{+})}F_{3}\right\|_{\Gamma_{3}^{\ast}{\mathbb{L}}^{(u_{3},\infty)}_{(\Theta,\Theta^{\operatorname{in}})}\Phi_{\mathfrak{r}}^{N}},\end{aligned}

\displaystyle\left\|\mathbbm{1}_{(V^{+}\cap W^{+})}F_{2}F_{3}\right\|_{\Gamma_{\times}^{\ast}{\mathbb{L}}^{(u_{\times},1)}_{\Theta}\left(\mathfrak{d}\otimes\mathfrak{d}\right)_{\mathfrak{r}}^{N}}\lesssim\prod_{j=2}^{3}\left\|\mathbbm{1}_{(V^{+}\cap W^{+})}F_{j}\right\|_{\Gamma_{j}^{\ast}{\mathbb{L}}^{(u_{j},2)}_{\Theta}\mathfrak{d}_{\mathfrak{r}}^{N}}.

∎

Proposition 8.2
.

The bound

(8.13)

\left\|\mathrm{Emb}[f_{2}]\circ\Gamma_{2}\mathrm{Emb}[f_{3}]\circ\Gamma_{3}\right\|_{L^{p_{\times}}_{\nu_{\beta}}X^{q_{\times},r_{\times},+}_{\mu_{\Theta}^{1},\nu_{\beta}}\Gamma_{\times}^{\ast}\mathbb{L}_{(\Theta,\Theta^{\operatorname{in}})}^{\left({u_{\times}},1\right)}\Box_{\mathfrak{r}}^{N}}\lesssim\|f_{2}\|_{L^{p_{2}}(\mathbb{R})}\|f_{2}\|_{L^{p_{3}}(\mathbb{R})}

holds for any
f_{j}\in\mathscr{S}(\mathbb{R})
, and any
p_{j}\in(1,\infty]
,
j\in\{2,3\}
, as long as
p_{\times}^{-1}=p_{2}^{-1}+p_{3}^{-1}
and as long as
q_{\times}\in(0,+\infty]
with
q_{\times}^{-1}<\min\left(\frac{1}{2},1-p_{2}^{-1}\right)+\min\left(\frac{1}{2},1-p_{3}^{-1}\right)
and as long as
1\leq u_{\times}<r_{\times}\leq q_{\times}.
The implicit constant is independent of
\beta\in(0,1]
, that determines
(\alpha_{j},\beta_{j},\gamma_{j})
and
\Gamma_{j}
,

j\in\{2,3\}
, and of
f_{2},f_{3}
.

We believe that the bounds above cannot be obtained as in the case of

Proposition

8.1
from linear embeddings. This is because the
expressions (
3.121
) and
(
3.122
) for the sizes
\Box_{\zeta,\mathfrak{r}}^{N}
and

\Box_{\sigma,\mathfrak{r}}^{N}
are intrinsically bilinear and cannot be
controlled through a classical Hölder inequality. In the subsequent proof we
mimic the parts of
Section

7
related to bounding
the defect sizes.

Proof.

Using homogeneity let us assume that
\|f_{j}\|_{L^{p_{j}}(\mathbb{R})}=1
. Fix
q_{j}\in(\max(2,p_{j}^{\prime}),\infty]
such that
q_{\times}^{-1}=q_{2}^{-1}+q_{3}^{-1}
and select
u_{j}
and
r_{j}
,
j\in\{2,3\}
, so
that
1\leq u_{j}\leq r_{j}\leq q_{j}
and
r_{\times}^{-1}=r_{2}^{-2}+r_{3}^{-2}
and
u_{\times}^{-1}=u_{2}^{-2}+u_{3}^{-2}
. Under these
conditions
Theorem

3
holds for the
exponents
(u_{j},r_{j},q_{j},p_{j})
for
j\in\{2,3\}
. According to the
discussion in Remark
Remark

3.19
this means
that for
j\in\{2,3\}
there exists a choice of sets
V^{-}_{j}(\lambda)\in\mathbb{D}^{\cup}_{\beta}
such that

(8.14)

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{\infty}\nu_{\beta}\left({V^{-}_{j}}(\lambda^{1/p_{j}})\right)\mathrm{d}\lambda\lesssim 1,\qquad\left\|\mathbbm{1}_{\mathbb{R}^{3}_{+}\setminus V^{-}_{j}(\lambda)}\mathrm{Emb}[f_{j}]\right\|_{X^{q_{j},r_{j},+}_{\mu_{\Theta}^{1},\nu_{\beta}}\widetilde{F}^{u_{j}}_{\Gamma_{j}}\Phi_{\mathfrak{r}}^{N}}\lesssim\lambda.

Unwrapping the definition of the size
X^{q_{j},r_{j},+}_{\mu_{\Theta}^{1},\nu_{\beta}}\widetilde{F}^{u_{j}}_{\Gamma_{j}}\Phi_{\mathfrak{r}}^{N}
this means that for
any any
\overline{r}_{j}\in\{r_{j},q_{j}\}
, any
V^{+}\in\mathbb{D}_{\beta}^{\cup}
, any
W^{+}\in\mathbb{T}_{\Theta}^{\cup}
, and
any
\tau>0
there exists a choice of
W^{-}_{j}(\tau)\in\mathbb{T}_{\Theta}^{\cup}
such that

(8.15)

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{\infty}\mu^{1}_{\Theta}(W^{-}_{j}(\tau^{1/\overline{r}_{j}}))\mathrm{d}\tau\lesssim 1,

\displaystyle\left\|\mathbbm{1}_{(V^{+}\cap W^{+})\setminus(V^{-}_{j}(\lambda)\cup W^{-}_{j}(\tau))}\mathrm{Emb}[f_{j}]\right\|_{\widetilde{F}^{u_{j}}_{\Gamma_{j}}\Phi_{\mathfrak{r}}^{N}}\lesssim\tau\lambda\nu_{\beta}(V^{+})^{1/\overline{r}_{j}}\mu^{\infty}_{\Theta}(W^{+})^{\frac{1}{\overline{r}_{j}}-\frac{1}{q_{j}}}.

Note that for any
V^{+},V^{-}\in\mathbb{D}^{\cup}_{\beta}
and
W^{+},W^{-}\in\mathbb{T}_{\Theta}^{\cup}
we have

(8.16)

\displaystyle\left\|\mathbbm{1}_{(V^{{}_{+}}\cap W^{{}_{+}})\setminus(V^{-}\cup W^{-})}\mathrm{Emb}[f_{2}]\circ\Gamma_{2}\mathrm{Emb}[f_{3}]\circ\Gamma_{3}\right\|_{\Gamma_{\times}^{\ast}\mathbb{L}_{(\Theta,\Theta^{\operatorname{in}})}^{\left({u_{\times}},1\right)}\Box_{\mathfrak{r}}^{N}(T)}

\displaystyle\lesssim\begin{aligned} &\left\|\left(\mathrm{Emb}[f_{2}]\circ\Gamma_{2}\mathrm{Emb}[f_{3}]\circ\Gamma_{3}\right)(\eta,y,t)\right.\\
&\qquad\times\left.t\mathrm{d}_{t}\left(\mathbbm{1}_{(V^{{}_{+}}\cap W^{{}_{+}})\setminus(V^{-}\cup W^{-})}(\eta,y,t)\right)\right\|_{\Gamma_{\times}^{\ast}\mathbb{L}_{(\Theta,\Theta^{\operatorname{in}})}^{\left({u_{\times}},1\right)}\Box_{\mathfrak{r}}^{N}(T)}\end{aligned}

\displaystyle\lesssim\begin{aligned} &\left\|\left(\mathrm{Emb}[f_{2}]\circ\Gamma_{2}\mathrm{Emb}[f_{3}]\circ\Gamma_{3}\right)(\eta,y,t)\right.\mathbbm{1}_{(V^{{}_{+}}\cap W^{{}_{+}})\setminus(V^{-}\cup W^{-})}(\eta,y,t)\\
&\qquad\times\left.\left(t\delta\left(t-\mathfrak{b_{+}}(\eta,y)\right)+t\delta\left(t-\mathfrak{b_{-}}(\eta,y)\right)\right.\right\|_{\Gamma_{\times}^{\ast}\mathbb{L}_{(\Theta,\Theta^{\operatorname{in}})}^{\left({u_{\times}},1\right)}(\Phi\otimes\Phi)_{\mathfrak{r}}^{N}(T)}\end{aligned}

\displaystyle\lesssim\prod_{j=2}^{3}\begin{aligned} &\left\|\left(\mathrm{Emb}[f_{j}]\circ\Gamma_{j}\right)(\eta,y,t)\right.\mathbbm{1}_{(V^{{}_{+}}\cap W^{{}_{+}})\setminus(V^{-}\cup W^{-})}(\eta,y,t)\\
&\qquad\times\left.\left(t\delta\left(t-\mathfrak{b_{+}}(\eta,y)\right)+t\delta\left(t-\mathfrak{b_{-}}(\eta,y)\right)\right.\right\|_{\Gamma_{j}^{\ast}\mathbb{L}_{(\Theta,\Theta^{\operatorname{in}})}^{\left({u_{j}},1\right)}\Phi_{\mathfrak{r}}^{N}}.\end{aligned}

On the other hand

(8.17)

\displaystyle\begin{aligned} &\left\|\left(\mathrm{Emb}[f_{j}]\circ\Gamma_{j}\right)(\eta,y,t)\right.\mathbbm{1}_{(V^{{}_{+}}\cap W^{{}_{+}})\setminus(V^{-}\cup W^{-})}(\eta,y,t)\\
&\hskip 40.00006pt\times\left.\left(t\delta\left(t-\mathfrak{b_{+}}(\eta,y)\right)+t\delta\left(t-\mathfrak{b_{-}}(\eta,y)\right)\right.\right\|_{\Gamma_{j}^{\ast}\mathbb{L}_{(\Theta,\Theta^{\operatorname{in}})}^{\left({u_{j}},1\right)}\Phi_{\mathfrak{r}}^{N}}\end{aligned}

\displaystyle\lesssim\left\|\mathbbm{1}_{(V^{+}\cap W^{+})\setminus(V^{-}\cup W^{-})}\mathrm{Emb}[f_{j}]\right\|_{\widetilde{F}^{u_{j}}_{\Gamma_{j}}\Phi_{\mathfrak{r}}^{N}}.

Based on the above two bounds, the rest of the proof is merely an unwrapping
of definitions of iterated outer Lebesgue norms. We set
V^{-}_{\times}(\lambda)=\bigcup_{j\in\{2,3\}}{V^{-}_{j}}(\lambda^{p_{\times}/p_{j}})
so that

(8.18)

\int_{0}^{\infty}\nu_{\beta}\left({V^{-}_{\times}}(\lambda^{1/p_{\times}})\right)\mathrm{d}\lambda\leq\sum_{j\in\{2,3\}}\int_{0}^{\infty}\nu_{\beta}\left({V^{-}_{j}}(\lambda^{1/p_{j}})\right)\mathrm{d}\lambda\lesssim 1.

We claim that

(8.19)

\left\|\mathbbm{1}_{\mathbb{R}^{3}_{+}{\setminus V^{-}_{\times}}(\lambda)}\mathrm{Emb}[f_{2}]\circ\Gamma_{2}\mathrm{Emb}[f_{3}]\circ\Gamma_{3}\right\|_{X^{q_{j},r_{j},+}_{\mu_{\Theta}^{1},\nu_{\beta}}\Gamma_{\times}^{\ast}\mathbb{L}_{(\Theta,\Theta^{\operatorname{in}})}^{\left({u_{\times}},1\right)}\Box_{\mathfrak{r}}^{N}(T)}\lesssim\lambda

i.e. that for
\overline{r}_{\times}\in\{r_{\times},q_{\times}\}
and for
any
V^{+}\in\mathbb{D}_{\beta}^{\cup}
, any
W^{+}\in\mathbb{T}_{\Theta}^{\cup}
, and for any
\tau>0
there exists

W^{-}_{\times}(\tau)\in\mathbb{T}_{\Theta}^{\cup}
such that

(8.20)

\int_{0}^{\infty}\mu^{1}_{\Theta}(W^{-}_{\times}(\tau^{1/\overline{r}_{\times}}))\mathrm{d}\tau\lesssim 1

and

(8.21)

\begin{aligned} &\left\|\mathbbm{1}_{(V^{{}_{+}}\cap W^{{}_{+}})\setminus\left({V^{-}_{\times}}(\lambda)\cup W^{-}_{\times}(\tau)\right)}\mathrm{Emb}[f_{2}]\circ\Gamma_{2}\mathrm{Emb}[f_{3}]\circ\Gamma_{3}\right\|_{\Gamma_{\times}^{\ast}\mathbb{L}_{(\Theta,\Theta^{\operatorname{in}})}^{\left({u_{\times}},1\right)}\Box_{\mathfrak{r}}^{N}(T)}\\
&\qquad\lesssim\tau\lambda\nu_{\beta}(V^{+})^{1/\overline{r}_{\times}}\mu^{\infty}_{\Theta}(W^{+})^{\frac{1}{\overline{r}_{\times}}-\frac{1}{q_{\times}}}\end{aligned}.

It is sufficient to set
W^{-}_{\times}(\tau)=\bigcup_{j\in\{2,3\}}{W^{-}_{j}}(\tau^{\overline{r}_{\times}/\overline{r}_{j}})
where
{W^{-}_{j}}(\tau)\in\mathbb{T}_{\Theta}^{\cup}
is such that

(8.22)

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{\infty}\mu^{1}_{\Theta}(W^{-}_{j}(\tau^{1/\overline{r}_{j}}))\mathrm{d}\tau\lesssim 1,

\displaystyle\begin{aligned} &\left\|\mathbbm{1}_{(V^{+}\cap W^{+})\setminus(V^{-}_{j}(\lambda^{p_{\times}/p_{j}})\cup W^{-}_{j}(\tau))}\mathrm{Emb}[f_{j}]\right\|_{\widetilde{F}^{u_{j}}_{\Gamma_{j}}\Phi_{\mathfrak{r}}^{N}}\\
&\qquad\lesssim\tau\lambda^{p_{\times}/p_{j}}\nu_{\beta}(V^{+})^{1/\overline{r}_{j}}\mu^{\infty}_{\Theta}(W^{+})^{\frac{1}{\overline{r}_{j}}-\frac{1}{q_{j}}}.\end{aligned}

The claim follows.
∎
